--- app/App.tsx ---
import { Component, type ErrorInfo, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import { AppProvider } from './AppProvider';
import { AppShell } from './AppShell';
import { DisclaimerModal } from './DisclaimerModal';

/** Surfaces render errors in the existing loading overlay instead of a blank page. */
class AppErrorBoundary extends Component<{ children: ReactNode }, { error: Error | null }> {
  state = { error: null as Error | null };

  static getDerivedStateFromError(error: Error) {
    return { error };
  }

  componentDidCatch(error: Error, info: ErrorInfo) {
    console.error(error, info);
  }

  render() {
    return this.state.error ? <div className="loading">{this.state.error.message}</div> : this.props.children;
  }
}

export function App() {
  return (
    <AppErrorBoundary>
      <AppProvider>
        <AppShell />
        <DisclaimerModal />
      </AppProvider>
    </AppErrorBoundary>
  );
}

--- app/DisclaimerModal.tsx ---
import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { createPortal } from 'react-dom';

export const DISCLAIMER_STORAGE_KEY = 'transit-simulator:disclaimer-acknowledged:v1';

function wasDisclaimerAcknowledged() {
  try {
    return window.localStorage.getItem(DISCLAIMER_STORAGE_KEY) === 'true';
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

/** One-time notice that makes the simulator's unofficial purpose explicit. */
export function DisclaimerModal() {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(() => !wasDisclaimerAcknowledged());

  const close = useCallback(() => {
    try {
      window.localStorage.setItem(DISCLAIMER_STORAGE_KEY, 'true');
    } catch {
      // Still allow the current session to continue when storage is unavailable.
    }
    setOpen(false);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    const onKeyDown = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (event.key === 'Escape') close();
    };
    window.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
  }, [close, open]);

  if (!open) return null;

  return createPortal(
    <div className="disclaimer-backdrop" onClick={close}>
      <section
        className="disclaimer-modal"
        role="dialog"
        aria-modal="true"
        aria-labelledby="disclaimer-title"
        aria-describedby="disclaimer-description"
        onClick={(event) => event.stopPropagation()}
      >
        <button className="disclaimer-close" aria-label="Close disclaimer" onClick={close}>
          ×
        </button>
        <div className="disclaimer-kicker">Before you continue</div>
        <h2 id="disclaimer-title">Unofficial transit simulator</h2>
        <div id="disclaimer-description" className="disclaimer-copy">
          <p>
            This is an independent application and is not an official service of VVS, any other
            transport association (Verkehrsverbund), or any transport operator.
          </p>
          <p>
            Do not use it to plan journeys, search for transit connections, or make travel
            decisions. Routes, stops, schedules, and other information may be incomplete,
            fictional, or out of date.
          </p>
          <p>This non-commercial project is intended solely for exploratory purposes.</p>
        </div>
        <button className="disclaimer-acknowledge" onClick={close} autoFocus>
          I understand
        </button>
      </section>
    </div>,
    document.body
  );
}

--- app/AppUrlState.ts ---
import { isKnownTransportMode, type TransportMode } from '../domain/transport';
import type { CameraMode } from '../world/CameraController';
import type { AppState } from './AppState';
import type { EndpointValue } from '../features/realtime/LocationField';
import type { Mode, Season } from './types';

export type AppUrlState = {
  city: string;
  transportMode: TransportMode;
  routeId: string;
  startStationIndex: number;
  rideMode: Mode;
  season: Season;
  time: 'auto' | number;
  cameraMode: CameraMode;
  // Live-traffic workspace flag (own key; `view` above is ride/driver mode).
  liveView?: boolean;
  realtime?: { origin: EndpointValue; destination: EndpointValue; journeyId: string };
};

const CAMERA_MODES = new Set<CameraMode>(['chase', 'passenger', 'cab']);
const RIDE_MODES = new Set<Mode>(['ride', 'driver']);
const SEASONS = new Set<Season>(['spring', 'summer', 'autumn', 'winter']);

export function readUrlState(): Partial<AppUrlState> {
  const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
  const state: Partial<AppUrlState> = {};

  const city = params.get('city');
  if (city) state.city = city;

  const transportMode = params.get('transport');
  if (transportMode && isKnownTransportMode(transportMode)) {
    state.transportMode = transportMode;
  }

  const routeId = params.get('route');
  if (routeId) state.routeId = routeId;

  const station = params.get('station');
  if (station) {
    const value = Number.parseInt(station, 10);
    if (Number.isInteger(value) && value >= 0) state.startStationIndex = value;
  }

  const rideMode = params.get('view');
  if (rideMode && RIDE_MODES.has(rideMode as Mode)) state.rideMode = rideMode as Mode;

  const season = params.get('season');
  if (season && SEASONS.has(season as Season)) state.season = season as Season;

  const time = params.get('time');
  if (time === 'auto') {
    state.time = 'auto';
  } else if (time) {
    const value = Number.parseFloat(time);
    if (Number.isFinite(value) && value >= 0 && value <= 24) state.time = value;
  }

  const cameraMode = params.get('camera');
  if (cameraMode && CAMERA_MODES.has(cameraMode as CameraMode)) state.cameraMode = cameraMode as CameraMode;

  if (params.get('live') === '1') state.liveView = true;

  const endpoint = (prefix: string): EndpointValue | null => {
    const name = params.get(`${prefix}n`);
    const lat = Number.parseFloat(params.get(`${prefix}a`) ?? '');
    const lon = Number.parseFloat(params.get(`${prefix}o`) ?? '');
    if (!name || !Number.isFinite(lat) || !Number.isFinite(lon)) return null;
    return { id: params.get(`${prefix}i`) ?? undefined, name, lat, lon };
  };
  if (params.get('rt') === '1') {
    const origin = endpoint('rfo');
    const destination = endpoint('rto');
    const journeyId = params.get('rtj');
    if (origin && destination && journeyId) state.realtime = { origin, destination, journeyId };
  }

  return state;
}

export function writeUrlState(state: AppState): void {
  const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);

  params.set('city', state.selectedCityId);
  params.set('transport', state.selectedTransportMode);
  params.set('route', state.selectedRouteId);
  params.set('station', String(state.selectedStartStation));
  params.set('view', state.selectedMode);
  params.set('season', state.selectedSeason);
  params.set('time', state.selectedTime === 'auto' ? 'auto' : String(state.selectedTime));
  params.set('camera', state.selectedCameraMode);

  if (state.view === 'live') params.set('live', '1');
  else params.delete('live');

  const realtimeKeys = ['rt', 'rfoi', 'rfon', 'rfoa', 'rfoo', 'rtoi', 'rton', 'rtoa', 'rtoo', 'rtj'];
  if (state.realtimeJourney) {
    const writeEndpoint = (prefix: string, endpoint: EndpointValue) => {
      if (endpoint.id) params.set(`${prefix}i`, endpoint.id);
      else params.delete(`${prefix}i`);
      params.set(`${prefix}n`, endpoint.name);
      params.set(`${prefix}a`, String(endpoint.lat));
      params.set(`${prefix}o`, String(endpoint.lon));
    };
    params.set('rt', '1');
    params.set('rtj', state.realtimeJourney.journey.id);
    writeEndpoint('rfo', state.realtimeJourney.origin);
    writeEndpoint('rto', state.realtimeJourney.destination);
  } else {
    realtimeKeys.forEach((key) => params.delete(key));
  }

  const query = params.toString();
  const nextUrl = `${window.location.pathname}${query ? `?${query}` : ''}${window.location.hash}`;
  history.replaceState(history.state, '', nextUrl);
}

--- app/useAppState.ts ---
import { useContext } from 'react';
import { AppDispatchContext, AppStateContext } from './AppProvider';

/** Accesses the app state + dispatcher; throws if used outside AppProvider. */
export function useAppState() {
  const state = useContext(AppStateContext);
  const dispatch = useContext(AppDispatchContext);
  if (!state || !dispatch) throw new Error('useAppState must be used inside AppProvider');
  return { state, dispatch };
}

--- app/SimulatorLoop.ts ---
import type { SimulatorState } from '../simulator/simulator-types';

// Deterministic simulation advances in fixed 1/60 s steps regardless of frame
// rate; the per-frame visual/render pass stays variable. MAX_FRAME_DELTA caps
// catch-up after a tab stall so the sim can't spiral.
const FIXED_STEP = 1 / 60;
const MAX_FRAME_DELTA = 0.05;
// Preserve the prior per-frame visual dt clamp for camera smoothing/doors.
const MAX_VISUAL_DELTA = 0.05;

type LoopOptions = {
  turnaroundMs: number;
  getPaused: () => boolean;
  getSelectedTime: () => 'auto' | number;
  getCurrentLocalHour: () => number;
  step: (dt: number) => SimulatorState;
  onAutoTimeHour: (hour: number) => void;
  onState: (state: SimulatorState, dt: number) => void;
  onTerminalTurnaround: () => void;
};

/** Owns the requestAnimationFrame loop that advances simulation and HUD/world updates. */
export class SimulatorLoop {
  private frameHandle = 0;
  private lastFrame = performance.now();
  private lastClockUpdateMs = 0;
  private terminalReachedAt: number | null = null;
  private state: SimulatorState | null = null;
  private previousState: SimulatorState | null = null;
  private visualState: SimulatorState | null = null;
  // Leftover simulation time not yet consumed by a fixed step.
  private accumulator = 0;

  constructor(private readonly options: LoopOptions) {}

  /** Starts the animation loop if it is not already running. */
  start(): void {
    if (this.frameHandle) return;
    this.lastFrame = performance.now();
    this.accumulator = 0;
    this.frameHandle = requestAnimationFrame(this.frame);
  }

  /** Stops the animation loop and clears the active RAF handle. */
  stop(): void {
    if (!this.frameHandle) return;
    cancelAnimationFrame(this.frameHandle);
    this.frameHandle = 0;
  }

  /** Replaces the active simulator state and clears any terminal turnaround timer. */
  setState(state: SimulatorState | null): void {
    this.state = state;
    this.previousState = state;
    this.visualState = state ? { ...state } : null;
    this.terminalReachedAt = null;
    this.accumulator = 0;
  }

  /** Returns the latest simulator state known to the loop. */
  currentState(): SimulatorState | null {
    return this.state;
  }

  /** Per-frame callback used by requestAnimationFrame. */
  private readonly frame = (now: number): void => {
    const frameDelta = Math.min((now - this.lastFrame) / 1000, MAX_FRAME_DELTA);
    this.lastFrame = now;

    if (this.state) {
      // Refresh auto time roughly once per minute instead of checking wall-clock every frame.
      if (this.options.getSelectedTime() === 'auto' && now - this.lastClockUpdateMs > 60_000) {
        this.options.onAutoTimeHour(this.options.getCurrentLocalHour());
        this.lastClockUpdateMs = now;
      }

      if (!this.options.getPaused()) {
        // Advance the deterministic simulation in fixed steps so its behavior is
        // independent of frame rate.
        this.accumulator += frameDelta;
        while (this.accumulator >= FIXED_STEP) {
          this.previousState = this.state;
          this.state = this.options.step(FIXED_STEP);
          this.accumulator -= FIXED_STEP;
        }
      }

      // Variable per-frame visual/render pass (runs even while paused so
      // camera/UI stay in sync).
      this.options.onState(this.interpolatedState(), Math.min(frameDelta, MAX_VISUAL_DELTA));

      if (this.state.tripComplete) {
        this.terminalReachedAt ??= now;

        if (now - this.terminalReachedAt >= this.options.turnaroundMs) {
          // Prevent repeated turnaround callbacks while route restart is being handled.
          this.terminalReachedAt = Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
          this.options.onTerminalTurnaround();
        }
      } else {
        this.terminalReachedAt = null;
      }
    }

    this.frameHandle = requestAnimationFrame(this.frame);
  };

  /** Smooths visual motion between deterministic simulation ticks. */
  private interpolatedState(): SimulatorState {
    const current = this.state!;
    const previous = this.previousState ?? current;
    const alpha = Math.min(1, this.accumulator / FIXED_STEP);
    const output = this.visualState ?? { ...current };
    Object.assign(output, current, {
      distanceM: previous.distanceM + (current.distanceM - previous.distanceM) * alpha,
      speedMps: previous.speedMps + (current.speedMps - previous.speedMps) * alpha
    });
    this.visualState = output;
    return output;
  }
}

--- app/InputController.ts ---
export type InputCallbacks = {
  onThrottle(held: boolean): void;
  onBrake(held: boolean): void;
  onLook(left: boolean, right: boolean): void;
  onZoom(delta: number): void;
  onPhotoToggle(): void;
  onPhotoExit(): void;
};

/** Owns keyboard, wheel, and touch gestures without coupling them to React or Three.js. */
export class InputController {
  private touchId: number | null = null;
  private startX = 0;
  private startY = 0;
  private pinchDistance: number | null = null;

  constructor(
    private readonly target: HTMLElement,
    private readonly callbacks: InputCallbacks
  ) {
    addEventListener('keydown', this.keyDown);
    addEventListener('keyup', this.keyUp);
    addEventListener('blur', this.releaseAll);
    target.addEventListener('wheel', this.wheel, { passive: false });
    target.addEventListener('touchstart', this.touchStart, { passive: true });
    target.addEventListener('touchmove', this.touchMove, { passive: false });
    target.addEventListener('touchend', this.touchEnd, { passive: true });
    target.addEventListener('touchcancel', this.touchEnd, { passive: true });
  }

  dispose(): void {
    // Release held controls so a disposed controller cannot leave input latched.
    this.releaseAll();
    removeEventListener('keydown', this.keyDown);
    removeEventListener('keyup', this.keyUp);
    removeEventListener('blur', this.releaseAll);
    this.target.removeEventListener('wheel', this.wheel);
    this.target.removeEventListener('touchstart', this.touchStart);
    this.target.removeEventListener('touchmove', this.touchMove);
    this.target.removeEventListener('touchend', this.touchEnd);
    this.target.removeEventListener('touchcancel', this.touchEnd);
  }

  /** Releases every held control; used on focus loss and disposal. */
  private releaseAll = (): void => {
    this.callbacks.onThrottle(false);
    this.callbacks.onBrake(false);
    this.callbacks.onLook(false, false);
  };

  private keyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent): void => {
    if (e.key === 'Escape') this.callbacks.onPhotoExit();
    if ((e.key === 'p' || e.key === 'P') && !e.repeat) {
      e.preventDefault();
      this.callbacks.onPhotoToggle();
    }
    if (e.repeat) return;
    if (e.key === 'w' || e.key === 'ArrowUp') this.callbacks.onThrottle(true);
    if (e.key === 's' || e.key === 'ArrowDown') this.callbacks.onBrake(true);
    if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft') this.callbacks.onLook(true, false);
    if (e.key === 'ArrowRight') this.callbacks.onLook(false, true);
  };

  private keyUp = (e: KeyboardEvent): void => {
    if (e.key === 'w' || e.key === 'ArrowUp') this.callbacks.onThrottle(false);
    if (e.key === 's' || e.key === 'ArrowDown') this.callbacks.onBrake(false);
    if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft' || e.key === 'ArrowRight') this.callbacks.onLook(false, false);
  };

  private wheel = (e: WheelEvent): void => {
    e.preventDefault();
    this.callbacks.onZoom(-e.deltaY * 0.0012);
  };

  private touchStart = (e: TouchEvent): void => {
    if (e.touches.length === 2) {
      this.pinchDistance = distance(e.touches[0], e.touches[1]);
      this.touchId = null;
      return;
    }
    const touch = e.touches[0];
    if (touch) {
      this.touchId = touch.identifier;
      this.startX = touch.clientX;
      this.startY = touch.clientY;
    }
  };

  private touchMove = (e: TouchEvent): void => {
    if (e.touches.length === 2) {
      const [a, b] = [e.touches[0], e.touches[1]];
      if (!a || !b) return;
      const next = distance(a, b);
      if (this.pinchDistance != null) this.callbacks.onZoom((next - this.pinchDistance) * 0.004);
      this.pinchDistance = next;
      e.preventDefault();
      return;
    }
    const touch = Array.from(e.touches).find((entry) => entry.identifier === this.touchId);
    if (!touch) return;
    const x = touch.clientX - this.startX;
    const y = touch.clientY - this.startY;
    if (Math.abs(y) > 64) return this.callbacks.onLook(false, false);
    this.callbacks.onLook(x > 24, x < -24);
  };

  private touchEnd = (): void => {
    this.touchId = null;
    this.pinchDistance = null;
    this.callbacks.onLook(false, false);
  };
}

const distance = (a: Touch, b: Touch): number => Math.hypot(b.clientX - a.clientX, b.clientY - a.clientY);

--- app/AppShell.tsx ---
import { lazy, Suspense, useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { routeMode, type DemoRoute } from '../route';
import { transportModeLabel } from '../domain/transport';
import { SimulatorCommandsContext } from '../features/simulation/useSimulatorRuntime';
import { SimulatorViewport } from '../features/simulation/SimulatorViewport';
import { SimulatorSnapshotStore, useSimulatorSnapshot } from '../features/simulation/SimulatorSnapshotStore';
import { SelectionPanel } from '../features/selection/SelectionPanel';
import { RealtimePanel } from '../features/realtime/RealtimePanel';
import { SimulatorHud } from '../features/simulation/SimulatorHud';
import { DriverControls } from '../features/simulation/DriverControls';
import { MiniMapPanel } from '../features/minimap/MiniMapPanel';
import { RouteDiagramPanel } from '../features/route-diagram/RouteDiagramPanel';
import { PhotoModeOverlay } from '../features/photo/PhotoModeOverlay';
import { LiveEntryButton } from '../features/live/LiveEntryButton';
import { LoadingOverlay } from './LoadingOverlay';
import { useAppBootstrap } from './useAppBootstrap';
import { useAppState } from './useAppState';
import { useAppUrlSync } from './useAppUrlSync';
import type { SimulatorRuntime, RouteSession } from '../simulator/SimulatorRuntime';
import { InputController } from './InputController';
import { RuntimeBridge } from './RuntimeBridge';
import type { DesiredRuntimeState } from '../engine/runtime/DesiredRuntimeState';
import { LIVE_MODE_ENABLED } from './liveModeFlag';

// The live-traffic workspace is heavy (Leaflet + realtime tools) and Stuttgart-only,
// so it is code-split and loaded only when the user enters Live (concept §2.1).
const LiveView = lazy(() => import('../features/live/LiveView').then((m) => ({ default: m.LiveView })));

/**
 * The React shell: owns selection/input state and drives the imperative
 * SimulatorRuntime through effects. It never touches the Three.js scene.
 */
export function AppShell() {
  const { state, dispatch } = useAppState();
  const catalog = useAppBootstrap();
  useAppUrlSync(state, catalog.ready);

  const store = useMemo(() => new SimulatorSnapshotStore(), []);
  const snapshot = useSimulatorSnapshot(store);
  const [runtime, setRuntime] = useState<SimulatorRuntime | null>(null);
  const [activeRealtimeRoute, setActiveRealtimeRoute] = useState<DemoRoute | null>(null);
  const [collapsed, setCollapsed] = useState(() => window.matchMedia('(max-width: 760px)').matches);
  const [photoLabel, setPhotoLabel] = useState('Platform fly-by');
  // Photo activation is owned by the runtime; the shell only mirrors it for the overlay.
  const [photoActive, setPhotoActive] = useState(false);

  const onRuntime = useCallback((next: SimulatorRuntime | null) => setRuntime(next), []);
  const catalogRoute = catalog.routes.find((entry) => entry.id === state.selectedRouteId);
  // Realtime routes are Stuttgart-referenced, so simulate them against the Stuttgart city context.
  const realtimeCity = state.realtimeRoute ? catalog.cities.find((c) => c.id === 'stuttgart') ?? catalog.currentCity : null;
  const session = useMemo<RouteSession | null>(() => {
    if (state.realtimeRoute && realtimeCity) {
      return {
        route: state.realtimeRoute,
        networkRoutes: [state.realtimeRoute],
        city: realtimeCity,
        mode: state.selectedMode,
        startStationIndex: 0,
        season: state.selectedSeason,
        time: state.selectedTime,
        cameraMode: state.selectedCameraMode
      };
    }
    return catalogRoute && catalog.currentCity
      ? {
          route: catalogRoute,
          networkRoutes: catalog.routes,
          city: catalog.currentCity,
          mode: state.selectedMode,
          startStationIndex: state.selectedStartStation,
          season: state.selectedSeason,
          time: state.selectedTime,
          cameraMode: state.selectedCameraMode
        }
      : null;
  }, [state.realtimeRoute, realtimeCity, catalogRoute, catalog.routes, catalog.currentCity, state.selectedMode, state.selectedStartStation, state.selectedSeason, state.selectedTime, state.selectedCameraMode]);

  // Realtime transfers change the world imperatively, so keep presentation
  // panels aligned with the currently active journey leg.
  const route = state.realtimeRoute ? activeRealtimeRoute ?? state.realtimeRoute : session?.route ?? catalogRoute;
  const realtimeLegs = useMemo(
    () => state.realtimeRoute ? [state.realtimeRoute, ...(state.realtimeRoute.journeyLegs ?? [])] : [],
    [state.realtimeRoute]
  );
  const journeyDiagramRoute = useMemo(
    () => realtimeLegs.length > 1 ? combinedJourneyRoute(realtimeLegs) : undefined,
    [state.realtimeRoute]
  );

  // One declarative desired-state object drives the runtime through RuntimeBridge,
  // replacing the former chain of one-effect-per-command. worldKey changes rebuild
  // the world; everything else updates the existing systems in place.
  const desired = useMemo<DesiredRuntimeState>(() => ({
    session,
    simulation: { paused: state.paused },
    presentation: { season: state.selectedSeason, time: state.selectedTime, cameraMode: state.selectedCameraMode }
  }), [session, state.paused, state.selectedSeason, state.selectedTime, state.selectedCameraMode]);

  // Low-frequency notifications (not commands) stay as one-shot subscriptions.
  useEffect(() => { runtime?.onPhotoLabel(setPhotoLabel); }, [runtime]);
  useEffect(() => { runtime?.onPhotoMode(setPhotoActive); }, [runtime]);
  useEffect(() => { runtime?.onActiveRoute(setActiveRealtimeRoute); }, [runtime]);
  useEffect(() => setActiveRealtimeRoute(state.realtimeRoute), [state.realtimeRoute]);

  // Physical input is translated to semantic commands written straight to the
  // runtime — held throttle/brake/look never touch React state.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!runtime) return;
    const target = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>('.simulator-viewport');
    if (!target) return;
    // Physical events become semantic EngineInputEvents routed through the
    // active workspace (simulation/photo), which decides what to do with them.
    const input = new InputController(target, {
      onThrottle: (held) => runtime.handleInput({ type: 'throttle', value: held ? 1 : 0 }),
      onBrake: (held) => runtime.handleInput({ type: 'brake', value: held ? 1 : 0 }),
      onLook: (left, right) => runtime.handleInput({ type: 'look', left, right }),
      onZoom: (delta) => runtime.handleInput({ type: 'zoom', delta }),
      onPhotoToggle: () => runtime.handleInput({ type: 'photo-toggle' }),
      onPhotoExit: () => runtime.handleInput({ type: 'photo-exit' })
    });
    return () => input.dispose();
  }, [runtime]);

  const notice = route && catalog.currentCity
    ? `${catalog.currentCity.shortLabel} · ${transportModeLabel(routeMode(route), catalog.currentCity.transportModes)} · ${route.agency ?? catalog.currentCity.noticeNetworkName} · ${route.description} ${catalog.currentCity.freeRideNotice} Source: ${route.source ?? 'demo route'}. ${catalog.attribution} Vector 3D scenery: OpenFreeMap, © OpenMapTiles, © OpenStreetMap contributors. Relief: Mapzen Terrain Tiles on AWS Open Data.`
    : '';

  return (
    <SimulatorCommandsContext.Provider value={runtime}>
      <SimulatorViewport store={store} onRuntime={onRuntime} />
      <RuntimeBridge runtime={runtime} desired={desired} />
      <LoadingOverlay message={catalog.loadingMessage} error={catalog.error} />
      <div className={`hud ${collapsed ? 'collapsed' : ''} ${state.selectedMode === 'driver' ? 'driver-mode' : ''}`}>
        <div className="hud-header">
          <div>
            <h1>{catalog.currentCity?.menuTitle ?? 'Transit Simulator'}</h1>
            <div className="label">{catalog.currentCity?.freeRideNotice ?? 'Free, non-commercial simulator'}</div>
          </div>
          <button
            className="menu-toggle"
            aria-expanded={!collapsed}
            aria-label={collapsed ? 'Expand menu' : 'Collapse menu'}
            onClick={() => setCollapsed((value) => !value)}
          >
            {collapsed ? '+' : '−'}
          </button>
        </div>
        {/*<div className="hud-tabs">*/}
          {/*<button*/}
          {/*  className={state.hudTab === 'simulator' ? 'active' : ''}*/}
          {/*  onClick={() => dispatch({ type: 'hud-tab-selected', tab: 'simulator' })}*/}
          {/*>*/}
          {/*  Simulator*/}
          {/*</button>*/}
          {/*<button*/}
          {/*  className={state.hudTab === 'realtime' ? 'active' : ''}*/}
          {/*  onClick={() => dispatch({ type: 'hud-tab-selected', tab: 'realtime' })}*/}
          {/*>*/}
          {/*  Realtime*/}
          {/*</button>*/}
        {/*</div>*/} {/* TODO: realtime mode */}
        <div className="hud-content">
          {state.hudTab === 'realtime' ? (
            <>
              <SimulatorHud snapshot={snapshot} />
              <RealtimePanel />
            </>
          ) : (
            <>
              <SelectionPanel catalog={catalog} />
              <SimulatorHud snapshot={snapshot} />
              <div className="controls">
                <button onClick={() => dispatch({ type: 'pause-changed', paused: !state.paused })}>{state.paused ? 'Resume' : 'Pause'}</button>
                <button onClick={() => { const mode = runtime?.cycleCamera(); if (mode) dispatch({ type: 'camera-selected', cameraMode: mode }); }}>Camera</button>
                <button onClick={() => runtime?.enterPhotoMode()}>Photo</button>
                <button onClick={() => session && runtime?.resetSimulation(session)}>Restart</button>
                <LiveEntryButton
                  eligible={LIVE_MODE_ENABLED && !!catalog.currentCity?.liveTraffic}
                  onEnter={() => dispatch({ type: 'view-changed', view: 'live' })}
                />
              </div>
              <DriverControls />
            </>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="right-rail">
        <RouteDiagramPanel route={journeyDiagramRoute ?? route} routes={realtimeLegs.length ? realtimeLegs : catalog.routes} activeRoute={route} journeyLegs={realtimeLegs.length > 1 ? realtimeLegs : undefined} snapshot={snapshot} />
      </div>
      <MiniMapPanel route={route} distanceM={snapshot.distanceM} />
      <div className="notice">{notice}</div>
      {runtime && (
        <PhotoModeOverlay
          active={photoActive}
          label={photoLabel}
          commands={runtime}
          onExit={() => runtime.exitPhotoMode()}
        />
      )}
      {state.view === 'live' && LIVE_MODE_ENABLED && catalog.currentCity?.liveTraffic && (
        <Suspense fallback={<div className="lv-root" />}>
          <LiveView onExit={() => dispatch({ type: 'view-changed', view: 'simulator' })} city={catalog.currentCity} />
        </Suspense>
      )}
    </SimulatorCommandsContext.Provider>
  );
}

/** A schematic-only route that keeps every transit leg visible in one panel. */
function combinedJourneyRoute(legs: DemoRoute[]): DemoRoute {
  let offsetM = 0;
  const stations = legs.flatMap((leg) => {
    const output = leg.stations.map((station) => ({ ...station, distanceM: station.distanceM + offsetM }));
    offsetM += leg.routeLengthM;
    return output;
  });
  return {
    ...legs[0],
    id: `${legs[0].id}-journey-diagram`,
    line: legs.map((leg) => leg.line).join(' → '),
    destination: legs.at(-1)?.destination ?? legs[0].destination,
    description: 'Realtime journey',
    routeLengthM: offsetM,
    stations
  };
}

--- app/AppProvider.tsx ---
import { createContext, useMemo, useReducer, type Dispatch, type PropsWithChildren } from 'react';
import type { AppAction } from './app-actions';
import { appReducer } from './app-reducer';
import { createInitialAppState, type AppState } from './AppState';

export const AppStateContext = createContext<AppState | null>(null);
export const AppDispatchContext = createContext<Dispatch<AppAction> | null>(null);

/** Provides the app selection/input state and dispatcher to the React tree. */
export function AppProvider({ children }: PropsWithChildren) {
  const [state, dispatch] = useReducer(appReducer, undefined, () => createInitialAppState());
  const dispatchValue = useMemo(() => dispatch, []);
  return (
    <AppStateContext.Provider value={state}>
      <AppDispatchContext.Provider value={dispatchValue}>{children}</AppDispatchContext.Provider>
    </AppStateContext.Provider>
  );
}

--- app/useAppUrlSync.ts ---
import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { writeUrlState } from './AppUrlState';
import type { AppState } from './AppState';

/** Mirrors persistable selection state into the URL once bootstrap is ready. */
export function useAppUrlSync(state: AppState, ready: boolean): void {
  const canWrite = useRef(false);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (ready) canWrite.current = true;
  }, [ready]);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!canWrite.current || !state.selectedRouteId) return;
    writeUrlState(state);
  }, [
    state.selectedCityId,
    state.selectedTransportMode,
    state.selectedRouteId,
    state.selectedStartStation,
    state.selectedMode,
    state.selectedSeason,
    state.selectedTime,
    state.selectedCameraMode,
    state.view,
    state.realtimeJourney
  ]);
}

--- app/machines/workspaceMachine.test.ts ---
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { workspaceInitialState, workspaceTransition } from './workspaceMachine';

describe('workspaceTransition', () => {
  it('starts in simulation', () => {
    expect(workspaceInitialState()).toEqual({ value: 'simulation' });
  });

  it('enters photo, preserving the interrupted camera + resume intent', () => {
    const s = workspaceTransition(workspaceInitialState(), {
      type: 'ENTER_PHOTO',
      previousCameraMode: 'chase',
      resumeAfterExit: true
    });
    expect(s).toEqual({ value: 'photo', previousCameraMode: 'chase', resumeAfterExit: true });
  });

  it('restores simulation on exit', () => {
    const photo = workspaceTransition(workspaceInitialState(), {
      type: 'ENTER_PHOTO',
      previousCameraMode: 'cab',
      resumeAfterExit: false
    });
    expect(workspaceTransition(photo, { type: 'EXIT_PHOTO' })).toEqual({ value: 'simulation' });
  });

  it('ignores ENTER_PHOTO while already in photo (keeps original snapshot)', () => {
    const photo = workspaceTransition(workspaceInitialState(), {
      type: 'ENTER_PHOTO',
      previousCameraMode: 'chase',
      resumeAfterExit: true
    });
    const again = workspaceTransition(photo, {
      type: 'ENTER_PHOTO',
      previousCameraMode: 'passenger',
      resumeAfterExit: false
    });
    expect(again).toBe(photo);
  });

  it('ignores EXIT_PHOTO while in simulation', () => {
    const sim = workspaceInitialState();
    expect(workspaceTransition(sim, { type: 'EXIT_PHOTO' })).toBe(sim);
  });
});

--- app/machines/bootstrapMachine.ts ---
import type { CityDefinition } from '../../cities';
import type { TransportMode } from '../../domain/transport';
import type { DemoRoute } from '../../route';

/** Everything the shell needs once the catalog + selected routes are loaded. */
export type LoadedCatalog = {
  cities: CityDefinition[];
  currentCity: CityDefinition;
  routes: DemoRoute[];
  availableModes: TransportMode[];
  attribution: string;
};

/**
 * Catalog bootstrap as an explicit state machine, replacing the loose
 * ready/loadingMessage/error/initialized flags the hook used to juggle. The
 * `operationId` carried through `loading-routes` lets stale async responses be
 * rejected so a slow request cannot overwrite a newer city selection.
 */
export type BootstrapState =
  | { value: 'loading-cities' }
  | { value: 'loading-manifest'; cityId: string }
  | { value: 'loading-routes'; cityId: string; mode: TransportMode; operationId: number }
  | { value: 'ready'; catalog: LoadedCatalog }
  | { value: 'failed'; error: Error };

export type BootstrapEvent =
  | { type: 'LOAD_MANIFEST'; cityId: string }
  | { type: 'LOAD_ROUTES'; cityId: string; mode: TransportMode; operationId: number }
  | { type: 'ROUTES_LOADED'; operationId: number; catalog: LoadedCatalog }
  | { type: 'FAIL'; operationId?: number; error: Error };

export function bootstrapInitialState(): BootstrapState {
  return { value: 'loading-cities' };
}

/**
 * Pure transition. A `LOAD_MANIFEST` (city selection) supersedes any in-flight
 * route load; late `ROUTES_LOADED`/`FAIL` events whose operationId no longer
 * matches the active load are ignored.
 */
export function bootstrapTransition(state: BootstrapState, event: BootstrapEvent): BootstrapState {
  switch (event.type) {
    case 'LOAD_MANIFEST':
      // A newer city selection always wins, even over a ready/failed state.
      return { value: 'loading-manifest', cityId: event.cityId };
    case 'LOAD_ROUTES':
      return state.value === 'loading-manifest' && state.cityId === event.cityId
        ? { value: 'loading-routes', cityId: event.cityId, mode: event.mode, operationId: event.operationId }
        : state;
    case 'ROUTES_LOADED':
      return state.value === 'loading-routes' && state.operationId === event.operationId
        ? { value: 'ready', catalog: event.catalog }
        : state;
    case 'FAIL':
      // Ignore failures from a superseded operation; unscoped failures always apply.
      if (event.operationId !== undefined && state.value === 'loading-routes' && state.operationId !== event.operationId) {
        return state;
      }
      return { value: 'failed', error: event.error };
    default:
      return state;
  }
}

--- app/machines/bootstrapMachine.test.ts ---
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import {
  bootstrapInitialState,
  bootstrapTransition,
  type LoadedCatalog
} from './bootstrapMachine';

const catalog = { cities: [], routes: [], availableModes: [], attribution: '' } as unknown as LoadedCatalog;

describe('bootstrapTransition', () => {
  it('walks cities → manifest → routes → ready', () => {
    let s = bootstrapInitialState();
    expect(s.value).toBe('loading-cities');
    s = bootstrapTransition(s, { type: 'LOAD_MANIFEST', cityId: 'stuttgart' });
    expect(s).toEqual({ value: 'loading-manifest', cityId: 'stuttgart' });
    s = bootstrapTransition(s, { type: 'LOAD_ROUTES', cityId: 'stuttgart', mode: 'stadtbahn', operationId: 1 });
    expect(s.value).toBe('loading-routes');
    s = bootstrapTransition(s, { type: 'ROUTES_LOADED', operationId: 1, catalog });
    expect(s.value).toBe('ready');
  });

  it('rejects a stale ROUTES_LOADED from a superseded operation', () => {
    let s = bootstrapTransition(
      bootstrapTransition(bootstrapInitialState(), { type: 'LOAD_MANIFEST', cityId: 'ulm' }),
      { type: 'LOAD_ROUTES', cityId: 'ulm', mode: 'stadtbahn', operationId: 2 }
    );
    // A response tagged with the old operation id must not flip to ready.
    const stale = bootstrapTransition(s, { type: 'ROUTES_LOADED', operationId: 1, catalog });
    expect(stale).toBe(s);
  });

  it('lets a newer city selection supersede an in-flight route load', () => {
    const routing = bootstrapTransition(
      bootstrapTransition(bootstrapInitialState(), { type: 'LOAD_MANIFEST', cityId: 'ulm' }),
      { type: 'LOAD_ROUTES', cityId: 'ulm', mode: 'stadtbahn', operationId: 1 }
    );
    const next = bootstrapTransition(routing, { type: 'LOAD_MANIFEST', cityId: 'odessa' });
    expect(next).toEqual({ value: 'loading-manifest', cityId: 'odessa' });
    // The stale ulm response can no longer overwrite the odessa selection.
    expect(bootstrapTransition(next, { type: 'ROUTES_LOADED', operationId: 1, catalog }).value).toBe('loading-manifest');
  });

  it('ignores a scoped failure from a superseded operation but applies unscoped failures', () => {
    const routing = bootstrapTransition(
      bootstrapTransition(bootstrapInitialState(), { type: 'LOAD_MANIFEST', cityId: 'ulm' }),
      { type: 'LOAD_ROUTES', cityId: 'ulm', mode: 'stadtbahn', operationId: 5 }
    );
    expect(bootstrapTransition(routing, { type: 'FAIL', operationId: 4, error: new Error('stale') })).toBe(routing);
    expect(bootstrapTransition(routing, { type: 'FAIL', error: new Error('fatal') }).value).toBe('failed');
  });
});

--- app/machines/workspaceMachine.ts ---
import type { CameraMode } from '../../world/CameraController';

/**
 * Which workspace is active. Only simulation and photo exist today; the union is
 * intentionally extendable (e.g. a future vehicle-constructor workspace) without
 * changing existing callers.
 *
 * Photo remembers the camera mode it interrupted and whether the simulation was
 * running so exit can restore both.
 */
export type WorkspaceState =
  | { value: 'simulation' }
  | { value: 'photo'; previousCameraMode: CameraMode; resumeAfterExit: boolean };

export type WorkspaceEvent =
  | { type: 'ENTER_PHOTO'; previousCameraMode: CameraMode; resumeAfterExit: boolean }
  | { type: 'EXIT_PHOTO' };

export function workspaceInitialState(): WorkspaceState {
  return { value: 'simulation' };
}

/** Pure transition; unknown transitions return the same reference (ignored). */
export function workspaceTransition(state: WorkspaceState, event: WorkspaceEvent): WorkspaceState {
  switch (event.type) {
    case 'ENTER_PHOTO':
      // Entering photo while already in photo keeps the original snapshot.
      return state.value === 'simulation'
        ? { value: 'photo', previousCameraMode: event.previousCameraMode, resumeAfterExit: event.resumeAfterExit }
        : state;
    case 'EXIT_PHOTO':
      return state.value === 'photo' ? { value: 'simulation' } : state;
    default:
      return state;
  }
}

--- app/LoadingOverlay.tsx ---
/** Full-screen loading/error overlay driven by the bootstrap state. */
export function LoadingOverlay({ message, error }: { message: string | null; error: Error | null }) {
  return <div className={`loading ${!message && !error ? 'hidden' : ''}`}>{error?.message ?? message}</div>;
}

--- app/RuntimeBridge.tsx ---
import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import type { SimulatorRuntime } from '../simulator/SimulatorRuntime';
import { RuntimeReconciler } from '../engine/runtime/RuntimeReconciler';
import type { DesiredRuntimeState } from '../engine/runtime/DesiredRuntimeState';

/**
 * Single bridge between React and the runtime. Replaces AppShell's chain of
 * per-command effects: React computes one {@link DesiredRuntimeState}, the
 * reconciler applies the minimal diff. Renders nothing.
 */
export function RuntimeBridge({
  runtime,
  desired
}: {
  runtime: SimulatorRuntime | null;
  desired: DesiredRuntimeState;
}) {
  const reconciler = useRef<RuntimeReconciler | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    reconciler.current = runtime ? new RuntimeReconciler(runtime) : null;
    // A fresh runtime starts with no prior desired state; apply immediately so
    // the newly mounted world reflects the current selection.
    reconciler.current?.apply(desired);
    // desired intentionally excluded: re-created only when the runtime changes.
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [runtime]);

  useEffect(() => {
    reconciler.current?.apply(desired);
  }, [desired]);

  return null;
}

--- app/AppState.ts ---
import type { TransportMode } from '../domain/transport';
import type { CameraMode } from '../world/CameraController';
import { demoRoute, type DemoRoute } from '../route';
import type { RealtimeJourneySimulation } from '../features/realtime/journeyRoute';
import type { HudTab, Mode, Season } from './types';

/**
 * Serializable selection state for the app. Routes are referenced by id and
 * resolved through RouteRepository so the state stays lightweight and easy to
 * persist (URL/localStorage) without carrying full DemoRoute objects.
 *
 * Transient held input (throttle/brake/look) and photo-mode activation are NOT
 * stored here — they are owned by the engine (see engine/runtime/InputState and
 * SimulatorRuntime) so holding a control never re-renders the shell.
 */
export type AppState = {
  hudTab: HudTab;
  // Top-level workspace: the route simulator vs. the live-traffic view. Live is
  // only reachable for cities with a liveTraffic config (currently Stuttgart).
  view: 'simulator' | 'live';
  // A synthetic route built from live realtime data, simulated in place of a
  // catalog route when set.
  realtimeRoute: DemoRoute | null;
  realtimeJourney: RealtimeJourneySimulation | null;
  selectedCityId: string;
  selectedRouteId: string;
  selectedTransportMode: TransportMode;
  selectedMode: Mode;
  selectedSeason: Season;
  selectedTime: 'auto' | number;
  selectedStartStation: number;
  selectedCameraMode: CameraMode;
  paused: boolean;
};

export function createInitialAppState(routeId: string = demoRoute.id): AppState {
  return {
    hudTab: 'simulator',
    view: 'simulator',
    realtimeRoute: null,
    realtimeJourney: null,
    selectedCityId: 'stuttgart',
    selectedRouteId: routeId,
    selectedTransportMode: 'stadtbahn',
    selectedMode: 'ride',
    selectedSeason: 'summer',
    selectedTime: 'auto',
    selectedStartStation: 0,
    selectedCameraMode: 'chase',
    paused: false
  };
}

--- app/useAppBootstrap.ts ---
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import type { CityDefinition } from '../cities';
import type { TransportMode } from '../domain/transport';
import type { DemoRoute } from '../route';
import { RouteRepository, stuttgartCity } from '../route-repository';
import { readUrlState } from './AppUrlState';
import { useAppState } from './useAppState';
import { searchJourneys } from '../features/realtime/api';
import { enrichRouteFromTerrain } from '../features/realtime/catalogTerrain';
import { buildJourneyRoute } from '../features/realtime/journeyRoute';
import { LIVE_MODE_ENABLED } from './liveModeFlag';

export type BootstrapResult = {
  ready: boolean;
  loadingMessage: string | null;
  error: Error | null;
  cities: CityDefinition[];
  currentCity: CityDefinition | null;
  routes: DemoRoute[];
  availableModes: TransportMode[];
  attribution: string;
};

/**
 * Loads the city catalog, then lazily loads the manifest + routes for the
 * selected city/mode, dispatching resolved selections back into app state.
 * All Three.js / rendering work stays out of here.
 */
export function useAppBootstrap(): BootstrapResult {
  const { state, dispatch } = useAppState();
  const repository = useRef<RouteRepository | null>(null);
  repository.current ??= new RouteRepository();
  const initialUrl = useRef(readUrlState());
  const initialized = useRef(false);
  const restoredRealtime = useRef(false);
  const operation = useRef(0);
  const [result, setResult] = useState<BootstrapResult>({
    ready: false,
    loadingMessage: 'Loading city catalog...',
    error: null,
    cities: [],
    currentCity: null,
    routes: [],
    availableModes: [],
    attribution: ''
  });

  // 1. Load the city catalog once and apply URL-derived environment state.
  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;
    void repository.current!.loadCities()
      .then((cities) => {
        if (!active) return;
        const requested = initialUrl.current.city;
        const city = cities.find((entry) => entry.id === requested) ?? cities[0] ?? stuttgartCity;
        const url = initialUrl.current;
        dispatch({
          type: 'initial-state-loaded',
          state: {
            selectedCityId: city.id,
            // Restore Live only for a live-eligible city with the feature enabled;
            // otherwise fall back to the simulator view (concept §3: a non-VVS or
            // disabled ?live=1 degrades gracefully).
            ...(url.liveView && city.liveTraffic && LIVE_MODE_ENABLED ? { view: 'live' as const } : {}),
            ...(url.rideMode && { selectedMode: url.rideMode }),
            ...(url.season && { selectedSeason: url.season }),
            ...(url.time !== undefined && { selectedTime: url.time }),
            ...(url.cameraMode && { selectedCameraMode: url.cameraMode })
          }
        });
        setResult((old) => ({ ...old, cities, currentCity: city, loadingMessage: `Loading ${city.name} routes...` }));
      })
      .catch((error: unknown) => setResult((old) => ({ ...old, error: asError(error), loadingMessage: null })));
    return () => {
      active = false;
    };
  }, [dispatch]);

  // 2. For the selected city/mode, load the manifest + routes and resolve a route.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!result.cities.length) return;
    const id = ++operation.current;
    const city = result.cities.find((entry) => entry.id === state.selectedCityId) ?? result.cities[0] ?? stuttgartCity;
    void repository.current!.loadManifest(city)
      .then(async (manifest) => {
        const availableModes = manifest.modes.map((entry) => entry.mode);
        const requested = !initialized.current ? initialUrl.current.transportMode : state.selectedTransportMode;
        const mode = requested && availableModes.includes(requested) ? requested : city.defaultTransportMode;
        const loaded = await repository.current!.loadRoutes(city, mode);
        if (id !== operation.current) return;
        const preferred = !initialized.current ? initialUrl.current.routeId : state.selectedRouteId;
        const route = loaded.routes.find((entry) => entry.id === preferred)
          ?? loaded.routes.find((entry) => entry.id === city.defaultRouteIdByMode?.[mode])
          ?? loaded.routes.find((entry) => entry.line === city.defaultLineByMode[mode])
          ?? loaded.routes[0];
        if (!route) throw new Error(`No ${mode} routes are available for ${city.name}`);
        const station = !initialized.current ? initialUrl.current.startStationIndex ?? 0 : 0;
        initialized.current = true;
        dispatch({
          type: 'initial-state-loaded',
          state: {
            selectedCityId: city.id,
            selectedTransportMode: mode,
            selectedRouteId: route.id,
            selectedStartStation: Math.min(station, Math.max(0, route.stations.length - 1))
          }
        });
        setResult({ ready: true, loadingMessage: null, error: null, cities: result.cities, currentCity: city, routes: loaded.routes, availableModes, attribution: loaded.attribution });
      })
      .catch((error: unknown) => {
        if (id === operation.current) setResult((old) => ({ ...old, error: asError(error), loadingMessage: null }));
      });
  }, [state.selectedCityId, state.selectedTransportMode, result.cities, dispatch]);

  // A realtime URL stores its endpoints and journey id, then refreshes the
  // live result on load rather than putting a potentially huge geometry blob in
  // the address bar.
  useEffect(() => {
    const saved = initialUrl.current.realtime;
    if (!saved || restoredRealtime.current || !result.ready) return;
    restoredRealtime.current = true;
    void searchJourneys({
      origin: saved.origin,
      originId: saved.origin.id,
      destination: saved.destination,
      destinationId: saved.destination.id
    }).then(async (journeys) => {
      const journey = journeys.find((candidate) => candidate.id === saved.journeyId);
      if (!journey) return;
      const route = buildJourneyRoute(journey, saved.origin, saved.destination);
      await Promise.all([route, ...(route.journeyLegs ?? [])].map((leg) => enrichRouteFromTerrain(leg).catch(() => false)));
      dispatch({ type: 'realtime-route-simulated', route, journey: { origin: saved.origin, destination: saved.destination, journey } });
    }).catch(() => undefined);
  }, [result.ready, dispatch]);

  return result;
}

const asError = (error: unknown) => (error instanceof Error ? error : new Error(String(error)));

--- app/liveModeFlag.ts ---
// Live traffic is gated behind a build-time flag everywhere, including local dev.
// Set VITE_ENABLE_LIVE=true (e.g. in .env.local, or the deployment's env vars) to
// turn it on.
export const LIVE_MODE_ENABLED = import.meta.env.VITE_ENABLE_LIVE === 'true';

--- app/app-reducer.ts ---
import type { AppAction } from './app-actions';
import type { AppState } from './AppState';

/** Pure reducer for all selection/input state. Side effects live in effects/runtime. */
export function appReducer(state: AppState, action: AppAction): AppState {
  switch (action.type) {
    case 'hud-tab-selected':
      return { ...state, hudTab: action.tab };
    case 'view-changed':
      return { ...state, view: action.view };
    case 'realtime-route-simulated':
      // Switch to the simulator view so the freshly built route is visible.
      return { ...state, realtimeRoute: action.route, realtimeJourney: action.journey, selectedMode: 'ride', hudTab: 'simulator' };
    case 'city-selected':
      // A manual city change leaves realtime mode and drops any simulated route.
      // Live only exists for Stuttgart, so switching city always exits Live first.
      return { ...state, selectedCityId: action.cityId, view: 'simulator', selectedStartStation: 0, realtimeRoute: null, realtimeJourney: null };
    case 'transport-mode-selected':
      return { ...state, selectedTransportMode: action.mode, selectedStartStation: 0 };
    case 'route-selected':
      // Selecting a catalog line supersedes any simulated realtime route.
      return { ...state, selectedRouteId: action.routeId, selectedStartStation: 0, realtimeRoute: null, realtimeJourney: null };
    case 'start-station-selected':
      return { ...state, selectedStartStation: action.stationIndex };
    case 'ride-mode-selected':
      return { ...state, selectedMode: action.mode };
    case 'season-selected':
      return { ...state, selectedSeason: action.season };
    case 'time-selected':
      return { ...state, selectedTime: action.time };
    case 'camera-selected':
      return { ...state, selectedCameraMode: action.cameraMode };
    case 'pause-changed':
      return { ...state, paused: action.paused };
    case 'initial-state-loaded':
      return { ...state, ...action.state };
  }
}

--- app/app-actions.ts ---
import type { TransportMode } from '../domain/transport';
import type { CameraMode } from '../world/CameraController';
import type { DemoRoute } from '../route';
import type { AppState } from './AppState';
import type { RealtimeJourneySimulation } from '../features/realtime/journeyRoute';
import type { HudTab, Mode, Season } from './types';

/** All state transitions the React shell can dispatch. */
export type AppAction =
  | { type: 'hud-tab-selected'; tab: HudTab }
  | { type: 'view-changed'; view: 'simulator' | 'live' }
  | { type: 'realtime-route-simulated'; route: DemoRoute; journey: RealtimeJourneySimulation }
  | { type: 'city-selected'; cityId: string }
  | { type: 'transport-mode-selected'; mode: TransportMode }
  | { type: 'route-selected'; routeId: string }
  | { type: 'start-station-selected'; stationIndex: number }
  | { type: 'ride-mode-selected'; mode: Mode }
  | { type: 'season-selected'; season: Season }
  | { type: 'time-selected'; time: 'auto' | number }
  | { type: 'camera-selected'; cameraMode: CameraMode }
  | { type: 'pause-changed'; paused: boolean }
  | { type: 'initial-state-loaded'; state: Partial<AppState> };

// @vitest-environment jsdom

import { cleanup, fireEvent, render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { DISCLAIMER_STORAGE_KEY, DisclaimerModal } from './DisclaimerModal';

describe('DisclaimerModal', () => {
  beforeEach(() => window.localStorage.clear());
  afterEach(cleanup);

  it('shows on first launch and remembers acknowledgement', () => {
    const { unmount } = render(<DisclaimerModal />);

    expect(screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Unofficial transit simulator' })).toBeTruthy();
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'I understand' }));

    expect(screen.queryByRole('dialog')).toBeNull();
    expect(window.localStorage.getItem(DISCLAIMER_STORAGE_KEY)).toBe('true');

    unmount();
    render(<DisclaimerModal />);
    expect(screen.queryByRole('dialog')).toBeNull();
  });
});

--- route-repository.ts ---
import { loadCityCatalog, resolveCities, stuttgartCity, type CityDefinition } from './cities';
import { loadRouteManifest, loadRoutesForMode, type DemoRoute, type RouteFeedManifest } from './route';
import type { TransportMode } from './domain/transport';

/**
 * Owns city catalog + lazy per-city/mode route loading and caching, independent
 * of React state so the React shell can await it during bootstrap.
 */
export class RouteRepository {
  private readonly routes = new Map<string, DemoRoute[]>();
  private readonly manifests = new Map<string, RouteFeedManifest>();

  /** Loads the resolved city catalog. */
  async loadCities(): Promise<CityDefinition[]> {
    return resolveCities(await loadCityCatalog());
  }

  /** Loads (and caches) one city's route manifest. */
  async loadManifest(city: CityDefinition): Promise<RouteFeedManifest> {
    const cached = this.manifests.get(city.id);
    if (cached) return cached;
    const manifest = await loadRouteManifest(city.manifestFile, city.routeManifestFallback);
    this.manifests.set(city.id, manifest);
    return manifest;
  }

  /** Loads (and caches) the routes for a city/mode, returning them with attribution. */
  async loadRoutes(city: CityDefinition, mode: TransportMode): Promise<{ routes: DemoRoute[]; attribution: string }> {
    const key = `${city.id}:${mode}`;
    const manifest = await this.loadManifest(city);
    const cached = this.routes.get(key);
    if (cached) return { routes: cached, attribution: manifest.attribution };
    const loaded = await loadRoutesForMode(mode, manifest, city.demoRoutesFallback, city.routeManifestFallback.attribution);
    this.routes.set(key, loaded.routes);
    return loaded;
  }
}

export { stuttgartCity };

--- route-diagram.ts ---
import type { DemoRoute } from './route';
import type { SimulatorState } from './simulator/simulator-types';

// The diagram only needs a few live fields; accepting a subset lets the React
// snapshot drive it without constructing a full SimulatorState.
export type RouteDiagramProgress = Pick<SimulatorState, 'distanceM' | 'doorsOpen' | 'stationIndex' | 'speedMps'>;

const SVG_NS = 'http://www.w3.org/2000/svg';
const STATION_GAP = 34;
const TOP_PADDING = 26;

export class TransitRouteDiagram {
  private route: DemoRoute | null = null;
  private stationTransfers = new Map<string, string[]>();
  private readonly title: HTMLElement;
  private readonly scroll: HTMLElement;
  private readonly svg: SVGSVGElement;
  private readonly stationNodes: SVGCircleElement[] = [];
  private readonly stationXs: number[] = [];
  private vehicleMarker: SVGGElement | null = null;
  private lastAutoScrollAt = 0;

  constructor(private readonly root: HTMLElement) {
    this.root.innerHTML = `
      <div class="route-diagram-header">
        <div class="label">Transit route schematic</div>
        <div class="route-diagram-title">-</div>
      </div>
      <div class="route-diagram-scroll"><svg aria-label="Selected transit line schematic"></svg></div>
    `;
    this.title = this.root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('.route-diagram-title')!;
    this.scroll = this.root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('.route-diagram-scroll')!;
    this.svg = this.root.querySelector<SVGSVGElement>('svg')!;
  }

  /** Clears the diagram DOM; used when the owning React panel unmounts. */
  dispose(): void {
    this.root.replaceChildren();
  }

  setRoute(route: DemoRoute, networkRoutes: DemoRoute[] = [route], journeyLegs?: DemoRoute[]): void {
    this.route = route;
    this.stationTransfers = this.computeTransfers(route, networkRoutes);
    this.stationNodes.length = 0;
    this.stationXs.length = 0;
    this.svg.replaceChildren();
    this.title.textContent = `${route.line}  ·  ${route.stations[0]?.name ?? ''} → ${route.destination}`;
    this.root.style.setProperty('--route-color', route.color ?? '#ffd900');

    const height = Math.max(130, TOP_PADDING * 2 + Math.max(1, route.stations.length - 1) * STATION_GAP);
    this.svg.setAttribute('viewBox', `0 0 390 ${height}`);
    this.svg.setAttribute('height', String(height));

    const legOffsets = journeyLegs?.reduce<number[]>((offsets, leg) => {
      offsets.push((offsets.at(-1) ?? 0) + leg.stations.length);
      return offsets;
    }, [0]);
    const stationX = (index: number): number => {
      if (!journeyLegs?.length || !legOffsets) return 30;
      const legIndex = Math.max(0, legOffsets.findIndex((offset, candidate) => index < offset && candidate > 0));
      return 30 + (legIndex % 3) * 104;
    };
    if (journeyLegs?.length && legOffsets) {
      const colors = ['#ffd900', '#62c9ff', '#f28b6b', '#9ee493'];
      journeyLegs.forEach((leg, legIndex) => {
        const start = legOffsets[legIndex];
        const end = legOffsets[legIndex + 1] - 1;
        const x = stationX(start);
        const segment = this.svgElement('line', 'route-diagram-line');
        segment.setAttribute('x1', String(x));
        segment.setAttribute('x2', String(x));
        segment.setAttribute('y1', String(this.stationY(start)));
        segment.setAttribute('y2', String(this.stationY(end)));
        segment.setAttribute('stroke', colors[legIndex % colors.length]);
        this.svg.appendChild(segment);

        const label = this.svgElement('text', 'route-station-transfer');
        label.setAttribute('x', String(x + 12));
        label.setAttribute('y', String(Math.max(15, this.stationY(start) - 12)));
        label.textContent = `${leg.line} → ${leg.destination}`;
        this.svg.appendChild(label);
        if (legIndex >= journeyLegs.length - 1) return;
        const nextStart = legOffsets[legIndex + 1];
        const connector = this.svgElement('line', 'route-diagram-line');
        connector.setAttribute('x1', String(x));
        connector.setAttribute('y1', String(this.stationY(end)));
        connector.setAttribute('x2', String(stationX(nextStart)));
        connector.setAttribute('y2', String(this.stationY(nextStart)));
        connector.setAttribute('stroke-dasharray', '4 4');
        this.svg.appendChild(connector);
        const transfer = this.svgElement('text', 'route-station-transfer');
        transfer.setAttribute('x', String(Math.min(x, stationX(nextStart)) + 12));
        transfer.setAttribute('y', String((this.stationY(end) + this.stationY(nextStart)) / 2 + 4));
        const seconds = leg.transferAfterS;
        transfer.textContent = seconds === undefined ? 'Transfer' : `Transfer · ${Math.round(seconds / 60)} min`;
        this.svg.appendChild(transfer);
      });
    } else {
      const line = this.svgElement('line', 'route-diagram-line');
      line.setAttribute('x1', '30');
      line.setAttribute('x2', '30');
      line.setAttribute('y1', String(TOP_PADDING));
      line.setAttribute('y2', String(height - TOP_PADDING));
      this.svg.appendChild(line);
    }

    route.stations.forEach((station, index) => {
      const y = this.stationY(index);
      const x = stationX(index);
      const group = this.svgElement('g', 'route-station');
      group.dataset.stationIndex = String(index);
      const node = this.svgElement('circle', 'route-station-node');
      node.setAttribute('cx', String(x));
      node.setAttribute('cy', String(y));
      node.setAttribute('r', index === 0 || index === route.stations.length - 1 ? '6.5' : '5');
      const label = this.svgElement('text', 'route-station-label');
      label.setAttribute('x', String(x + 18));
      label.setAttribute('y', String(y + 4));
      label.textContent = station.name;
      const transfers = this.stationTransfers.get(station.name) ?? [];
      if (transfers.length) {
        const transferText = this.svgElement('tspan', 'route-station-transfer');
        transferText.textContent = `  ·  ${transfers.join(' ')}`;
        label.appendChild(transferText);
      }
      group.append(node, label);
      this.svg.appendChild(group);
      this.stationNodes.push(node);
      this.stationXs.push(x);
    });

    this.vehicleMarker = this.svgElement('g', 'route-vehicle');
    const halo = this.svgElement('circle', 'route-vehicle-halo');
    halo.setAttribute('r', '10');
    const body = this.svgElement('rect', 'route-vehicle-body');
    body.setAttribute('x', '-5');
    body.setAttribute('y', '-8');
    body.setAttribute('width', '10');
    body.setAttribute('height', '16');
    body.setAttribute('rx', '3');
    this.vehicleMarker.append(halo, body);
    this.svg.appendChild(this.vehicleMarker);
    this.updateMarker(this.stationXs[0] ?? 30, 0);
  }

  update(state: RouteDiagramProgress): void {
    if (!this.route || !this.vehicleMarker) return;
    const distanceM = Math.max(0, Math.min(this.route.routeLengthM, state.distanceM));
    const { x: markerX, y: markerY } = this.distancePosition(distanceM);
    this.updateMarker(markerX, markerY);

    const stoppedIndex = state.doorsOpen ? this.nearestStationIndex(distanceM) : -1;
    const nextIndex = Math.min(this.route.stations.length - 1, Math.max(0, state.stationIndex + (state.doorsOpen ? 0 : 1)));
    const nextStation = this.route.stations[nextIndex];
    const remainingM = nextStation ? nextStation.distanceM - distanceM : Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
    const approaching = !state.doorsOpen && remainingM >= 0 && remainingM <= Math.max(450, state.speedMps * 28);

    this.stationNodes.forEach((node, index) => {
      node.classList.toggle('passed', this.route!.stations[index].distanceM < distanceM - 12);
      node.classList.toggle('current', index === stoppedIndex);
      node.classList.toggle('approaching', approaching && index === nextIndex);
    });

    const now = performance.now();
    if (now - this.lastAutoScrollAt > 350) {
      const desired = markerY - this.scroll.clientHeight / 2;
      this.scroll.scrollTop = Math.max(0, Math.min(this.scroll.scrollHeight - this.scroll.clientHeight, desired));
      this.lastAutoScrollAt = now;
    }
  }

  private distancePosition(distanceM: number): { x: number; y: number } {
    const stations = this.route?.stations ?? [];
    if (stations.length < 2) return { x: this.stationXs[0] ?? 30, y: TOP_PADDING };
    let next = stations.findIndex((station) => station.distanceM >= distanceM);
    if (next <= 0) return { x: this.stationXs[Math.max(0, next)] ?? 30, y: this.stationY(Math.max(0, next)) };
    if (next < 0) return { x: this.stationXs.at(-1) ?? 30, y: this.stationY(stations.length - 1) };
    const previous = next - 1;
    const span = stations[next].distanceM - stations[previous].distanceM || 1;
    const progress = (distanceM - stations[previous].distanceM) / span;
    return {
      x: (this.stationXs[previous] ?? 30) + ((this.stationXs[next] ?? 30) - (this.stationXs[previous] ?? 30)) * progress,
      y: this.stationY(previous) + progress * STATION_GAP
    };
  }

  private nearestStationIndex(distanceM: number): number {
    if (!this.route?.stations.length) return -1;
    let nearest = 0;
    let nearestDistance = Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
    this.route.stations.forEach((station, index) => {
      const distance = Math.abs(station.distanceM - distanceM);
      if (distance < nearestDistance) {
        nearest = index;
        nearestDistance = distance;
      }
    });
    return nearest;
  }

  private updateMarker(x: number, y: number): void {
    this.vehicleMarker?.setAttribute('transform', `translate(${x} ${y})`);
  }

  private computeTransfers(route: DemoRoute, networkRoutes: DemoRoute[]): Map<string, string[]> {
    const transfers = new Map<string, Set<string>>();
    const currentStations = new Set(route.stations.map((station) => station.name));
    for (const candidate of networkRoutes) {
      if (candidate.id === route.id || candidate.line === route.line) continue;
      for (const station of candidate.stations) {
        if (!currentStations.has(station.name)) continue;
        let stationTransfers = transfers.get(station.name);
        if (!stationTransfers) {
          stationTransfers = new Set<string>();
          transfers.set(station.name, stationTransfers);
        }
        stationTransfers.add(candidate.line);
      }
    }
    return new Map(
      [...transfers.entries()].map(([stationName, lines]) => [stationName, [...lines].sort((a, b) => a.localeCompare(b, 'de', { numeric: true }))])
    );
  }

  private stationY(index: number): number {
    return TOP_PADDING + index * STATION_GAP;
  }

  private svgElement<K extends keyof SVGElementTagNameMap>(tag: K, className: string): SVGElementTagNameMap[K] {
    const element = document.createElementNS(SVG_NS, tag);
    element.setAttribute('class', className);
    return element;
  }
}

/** Live line/speed/next-station/doors/driver stat cards. */
export function SimulatorHud({ snapshot }: { snapshot: SimulatorUiSnapshot }) {
  return (
    <div className="meta">
      <div className="card">
        <div className="label">Line</div>
        <div className="value">{snapshot.line ? `${snapshot.line} → ${snapshot.destination}` : '-'}</div>
      </div>
      <div className="card">
        <div className="label">Speed</div>

      </div>

        <div className="label">Doors</div>
        <div className="value">{snapshot.doorsOpen ? `Open ${snapshot.dwellRemainingS.toFixed(0)}s` : 'Closed'}</div>
      </div>

        <div className="value">{snapshot.mode === 'driver' ? snapshot.driverScore : '-'}</div>
      </div>
      <div className="card driver-stat">
        <div className="label">Schedule</div>
        <div className="value">
          {snapshot.mode === 'driver' ? `${snapshot.timetableDeltaS >= 0 ? '+' : ''}${snapshot.timetableDeltaS.toFixed(0)}s` : '-'}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export const SimulatorCommandsContext = createContext<SimulatorCommands | null>(null);

export function useSimulatorRuntime(): SimulatorCommands {

--- features/simulation/SimulatorViewport.tsx ---
import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { SimulatorRuntime } from '../../simulator/SimulatorRuntime';
import type { SimulatorSnapshotStore } from './SimulatorSnapshotStore';

/**
 * Mounts the imperative SimulatorRuntime (Three.js canvas) into a plain div.
 * React owns the element lifecycle; the runtime owns everything inside it.
 */
export function SimulatorViewport({
  store,
  onRuntime
}: {
  store: SimulatorSnapshotStore;
  onRuntime: (runtime: SimulatorRuntime | null) => void;
}) {
  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!containerRef.current) return;
    const runtime = new SimulatorRuntime(containerRef.current, store);
    onRuntime(runtime);
    void runtime.load().catch((error: unknown) => {
      console.error(error);
    });
    return () => {
      onRuntime(null);
      runtime.dispose();
    };
  }, [onRuntime, store]);

  return <div ref={containerRef} className="simulator-viewport" />;
}

--- features/simulation/SimulatorSnapshotStore.ts ---
import { useSyncExternalStore } from 'react';
import type { SimulatorState } from '../../simulator/simulator-types';

/** UI-facing projection of the simulator state, decoupled from render frames. */
export type SimulatorUiSnapshot = {
  line: string;
  destination: string;
  speedKph: number;
  speedMps: number;
  nextStation: string;
  doorsOpen: boolean;
  dwellRemainingS: number;
  tripComplete: boolean;
  mode: 'ride' | 'driver';
  driverScore: number;
  timetableDeltaS: number;
  distanceM: number;
  stationIndex: number;
};

const EMPTY: SimulatorUiSnapshot = {
  line: '',
  destination: '',
  speedKph: 0,
  speedMps: 0,
  nextStation: '',
  doorsOpen: false,
  dwellRemainingS: 0,
  tripComplete: false,
  mode: 'ride',
  driverScore: 0,
  timetableDeltaS: 0,
  distanceM: 0,
  stationIndex: 0
};

/**
 * External store the imperative SimulatorRuntime pushes into each frame, read by
 * React via useSyncExternalStore. Publishes are throttled and deduped so the 60fps
 * render loop does not thrash React re-renders.
 */
export class SimulatorSnapshotStore {
  private snapshot = EMPTY;
  private listeners = new Set<() => void>();
  private lastPublishedAt = 0;

  getSnapshot = (): SimulatorUiSnapshot => this.snapshot;

  subscribe = (listener: () => void): (() => void) => {
    this.listeners.add(listener);
    return () => {
      this.listeners.delete(listener);
    };
  };

  update(state: SimulatorState, force = false): void {
    const now = performance.now();
    if (!force && now - this.lastPublishedAt < 100) return;
    const next: SimulatorUiSnapshot = {
      line: state.line,
      destination: state.destination,
      speedKph: Math.round(state.speedMps * 3.6),
      speedMps: state.speedMps,
      nextStation: state.nextStation,
      doorsOpen: state.doorsOpen,
      dwellRemainingS: state.dwellRemainingS,
      tripComplete: state.tripComplete,
      mode: state.mode,
      driverScore: state.driverScore,
      timetableDeltaS: state.timetableDeltaS,
      distanceM: state.distanceM,
      stationIndex: state.stationIndex
    };
    if (equal(next, this.snapshot)) return;
    this.snapshot = next;
    this.lastPublishedAt = now;
    for (const listener of this.listeners) listener();
  }
}

function equal(a: SimulatorUiSnapshot, b: SimulatorUiSnapshot): boolean {
  return a.line === b.line
    && a.destination === b.destination
    && a.speedKph === b.speedKph
    && a.nextStation === b.nextStation
    && a.doorsOpen === b.doorsOpen
    && a.dwellRemainingS === b.dwellRemainingS
    && a.tripComplete === b.tripComplete
    && a.mode === b.mode
    && a.driverScore === b.driverScore
    && a.timetableDeltaS === b.timetableDeltaS
    && a.distanceM === b.distanceM
    && a.stationIndex === b.stationIndex;
}

export function useSimulatorSnapshot(store: SimulatorSnapshotStore): SimulatorUiSnapshot {
  return useSyncExternalStore(store.subscribe, store.getSnapshot, store.getSnapshot);
}

--- features/simulation/DriverControls.tsx ---
import { useContext } from 'react';
import { SimulatorCommandsContext } from './useSimulatorRuntime';

/** Pointer throttle/brake buttons (visible only in driver mode via CSS). */
export function DriverControls() {
  // The runtime mounts asynchronously; tolerate its absence during early renders.
  const commands = useContext(SimulatorCommandsContext);
  const bind = (control: 'throttle' | 'brake') => ({
    onPointerDown: () => set(control, 1),
    onPointerUp: () => set(control, 0),
    onPointerLeave: () => set(control, 0)
  });
  const set = (control: 'throttle' | 'brake', value: number) => {
    if (!commands) return;
    if (control === 'throttle') commands.input.setThrottle(value);
    else commands.input.setBrake(value);
  };
  return (
    <div className="driver-controls">
      <button {...bind('throttle')}>Throttle</button>
      <button {...bind('brake')}>Brake</button>
      <div className="hint">W/ArrowUp throttle · S/ArrowDown brake</div>
    </div>
  );
}

--- features/route-diagram/RouteDiagramPanel.tsx ---
import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { TransitRouteDiagram } from '../../route-diagram';
import type { DemoRoute } from '../../route';
import type { SimulatorUiSnapshot } from '../simulation/SimulatorSnapshotStore';

/** React wrapper that owns a TransitRouteDiagram (SVG schematic) imperatively. */
export function RouteDiagramPanel({
  route,
  routes,
  activeRoute,
  journeyLegs,
  snapshot
}: {
  route?: DemoRoute;
  routes: DemoRoute[];
  activeRoute?: DemoRoute;
  journeyLegs?: DemoRoute[];
  snapshot: SimulatorUiSnapshot;
}) {
  const el = useRef<HTMLElement>(null);
  const diagram = useRef<TransitRouteDiagram | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!el.current) return;
    diagram.current = new TransitRouteDiagram(el.current);
    return () => {
      diagram.current?.dispose();
      diagram.current = null;
    };
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (route) diagram.current?.setRoute(route, routes, journeyLegs);
  }, [route, routes, journeyLegs]);

  useEffect(() => {
    // Catalog routes are alternatives, not consecutive segments. Only a
    // transfer journey needs a cumulative offset in the combined schematic.
    const activeIndex = activeRoute && journeyLegs ? journeyLegs.findIndex((candidate) => candidate.id === activeRoute.id) : -1;
    const distanceOffset = activeIndex > 0
      ? journeyLegs!.slice(0, activeIndex).reduce((total, candidate) => total + candidate.routeLengthM, 0)
      : 0;
    const stationOffset = activeIndex > 0
      ? journeyLegs!.slice(0, activeIndex).reduce((total, candidate) => total + candidate.stations.length, 0)
      : 0;
    diagram.current?.update({
      distanceM: snapshot.distanceM + distanceOffset,
      doorsOpen: snapshot.doorsOpen,
      stationIndex: snapshot.stationIndex + stationOffset,
      speedMps: snapshot.speedMps
    });
  }, [snapshot, activeRoute, journeyLegs]);

  return <section ref={el} className="route-diagram" />;
}

--- features/minimap/MiniMapPanel.tsx ---
import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { VehicleMiniMap } from '../../minimap';
import type { DemoRoute } from '../../route';

/** React wrapper that owns a VehicleMiniMap (Leaflet) instance imperatively. */
export function MiniMapPanel({ route, distanceM }: { route?: DemoRoute; distanceM: number }) {
  const el = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const map = useRef<VehicleMiniMap | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!el.current) return;
    map.current = new VehicleMiniMap(el.current);
    return () => {
      map.current?.dispose();
      map.current = null;
    };
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!route) return;
    map.current?.setRoute(route);
    const handle = window.setTimeout(() => map.current?.invalidateSize(), 0);
    return () => window.clearTimeout(handle);
  }, [route]);

  useEffect(() => map.current?.update(distanceM), [distanceM]);

  return <div ref={el} className="minimap" />;
}

--- features/realtime/generated/api-types.ts ---
/* eslint-disable */
/* tslint:disable */
// @ts-nocheck
/*
 * ---------------------------------------------------------------
 * ## THIS FILE WAS GENERATED VIA SWAGGER-TYPESCRIPT-API        ##
 * ##                                                           ##
 * ## AUTHOR: acacode                                           ##
 * ## SOURCE: https://github.com/acacode/swagger-typescript-api ##
 * ---------------------------------------------------------------
 */

export interface Departure {
  cancelled?: boolean;
  countdownMinutes?: number;
  destination?: string;
  estimatedTime?: string;
  id?: string;
  line?: string;
  plannedTime?: string;
  platform?: string;
}

export interface DeparturesResponse {
  departures?: Departure[];
  generatedAt?: string;
}

export type DomainLegMode = "walk" | "stadtbahn" | "s-bahn" | "bus";

export type DomainLocationType = "stop" | "address" | "poi" | "coord";

export interface GeoPoint {
  lat?: number;
  lon?: number;
}

export interface Journey {
  arrivalTime?: string;
  departureTime?: string;
  distanceM?: number;
  durationMinutes?: number;
  id?: string;
  interchanges?: number;
  legs?: JourneyLeg[];
  messages?: string[];
  summary?: string;
}

export interface JourneyLeg {
  destination?: string;
  destinationName?: string;
  destinationTime?: string;
  durationMinutes?: number;
  geometry?: GeoPoint[];
  id?: string;
  line?: string;

--- features/realtime/DeparturesModal.tsx ---
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { Modal } from './Modal';
import { RealtimeMap, type MapPin } from './RealtimeMap';
import { LocationField, type EndpointValue } from './LocationField';
import { stationDepartures } from './api';
import { formatClock } from './time';
import type { Departure } from './types';

/** Live station departure board; the station can be preselected or searched. */
export function DeparturesModal({
  onClose,
  initialStation
}: {
  onClose: () => void;
  initialStation: EndpointValue | null;
}) {
  const [station, setStation] = useState<EndpointValue | null>(initialStation);
  const [departures, setDepartures] = useState<Departure[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Load the board whenever the chosen station changes.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!station) return;
    if (!station.id) {
      setError('Pick a stop from the suggestions to load departures.');
      setDepartures([]);
      return;
    }
    const controller = new AbortController();
    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);
    stationDepartures(station.id, { name: station.name, lat: station.lat, lon: station.lon }, controller.signal)
      .then((results) => {
        setDepartures(results);
        if (results.length === 0) setError('No upcoming departures.');
      })
      .catch((e: unknown) => {
        if (!controller.signal.aborted) setError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Departures request failed.');
      })
      .finally(() => {
        if (!controller.signal.aborted) setLoading(false);
      });
    return () => controller.abort();
  }, [station?.id, station?.name, station?.lat, station?.lon]);

  const pins = useMemo<MapPin[]>(
    () => (station ? [{ id: 'station', lat: station.lat, lon: station.lon, kind: 'active', title: station.name }] : []),
    [station]
  );

  return (
    <Modal title="Departures" subtitle={station?.name ?? 'Live station board'} onClose={onClose}>
      <div className="rt-split">
        <RealtimeMap pins={pins} hint="Live station board" />
        <div className="rt-panel">
          <div className="rt-fields">
            <LocationField placeholder="Search a stop" value={station} onSelect={setStation} />
          </div>
          {error && <div className="rt-error">{error}</div>}
          <div className="rt-list">
            {!station && <div className="rt-list-empty">Search for a stop to see live departures.</div>}
            {loading && <div className="rt-list-empty">Loading departures…</div>}
            {departures.map((departure) => (
              <div key={departure.id} className={`rt-item ${departure.cancelled ? 'cancelled' : ''}`}>
                <div className="rt-item-title">
                  <span>
                    <span className="rt-badge rt-line">{departure.line}</span> {departure.destination}
                  </span>
                  <span className="rt-badge">
                    {departure.cancelled
                      ? 'Cancelled'
                      : typeof departure.countdownMinutes === 'number'
                        ? departure.countdownMinutes <= 0
                          ? 'now'
                          : `${departure.countdownMinutes} min`
