                        : formatClock(departure.estimatedTime ?? departure.plannedTime)}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <div className="rt-item-sub">
                  {formatClock(departure.plannedTime)}
                  {departure.estimatedTime && departure.estimatedTime !== departure.plannedTime && (
                    <span className="rt-delay"> → {formatClock(departure.estimatedTime)}</span>
                  )}
                  {departure.platform && ` · Pl. ${departure.platform}`}
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </Modal>
  );
}

--- features/realtime/catalogTerrain.ts ---
import { RouteRepository, stuttgartCity } from '../../route-repository';
import type { DemoRoute } from '../../route';
import { pointDistance, type GeoPoint } from '../../minimap/route-position';

const EARTH_M_PER_DEG_LAT = 111_320;
const MAX_ENDPOINT_OFFSET_M = 140;
const MAX_SHAPE_OFFSET_M = 65;
const repository = new RouteRepository();

const round = (value: number) => Math.round(value * 100) / 100;

function closestIndex(point: GeoPoint, route: DemoRoute): { index: number; distanceM: number } | null {
  const geo = route.geoPoints;
  if (!geo?.length) return null;
  let index = 0;
  let distanceM = Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
  geo.forEach(([lat, lon], candidateIndex) => {
    const distance = pointDistance(point, { lat, lon });
    if (distance < distanceM) {
      index = candidateIndex;
      distanceM = distance;
    }
  });
  return { index, distanceM };
}

function normalizedLine(line: string | undefined): string {
  return (line ?? '').replaceAll(' ', '').toUpperCase();
}

function routeDistance(points: GeoPoint[]): number {
  return points.reduce((total, point, index) => index === 0 ? 0 : total + pointDistance(points[index - 1], point), 0);
}

function maskRanges(mask: boolean[], points: GeoPoint[]): { startM: number; endM: number }[] {
  const distanceAt = [0];
  for (let index = 1; index < points.length; index++) {
    distanceAt.push(distanceAt[index - 1] + pointDistance(points[index - 1], points[index]));
  }
  const ranges: { startM: number; endM: number }[] = [];
  for (let index = 0; index < mask.length; index++) {
    if (!mask[index]) continue;
    const start = index;
    while (index + 1 < mask.length && mask[index + 1]) index++;
    ranges.push({ startM: round(distanceAt[start]), endM: round(distanceAt[index]) });
  }
  return ranges;
}

type Match = { route: DemoRoute; start: number; end: number; score: number };

/**
 * Finds a single directed catalog trip for a realtime leg. This deliberately
 * never combines nearest samples from separate services: that was the cause of
 * the bumpy profile and false tunnel portals at crossings and parallel tracks.
 */
async function matchCatalogRoute(route: DemoRoute): Promise<Match | null> {
  const geometry = route.geoPoints?.map(([lat, lon]) => ({ lat, lon })) ?? [];
  if (geometry.length < 2 || !route.transportMode) return null;
  try {
    const { routes } = await repository.loadRoutes(stuttgartCity, route.transportMode);
    const sameLine = routes.filter((candidate) => normalizedLine(candidate.line) === normalizedLine(route.line));
    let best: Match | null = null;

    for (const candidate of sameLine) {
      if (!candidate.geoPoints?.length || candidate.elevationDatumM === undefined) continue;
      const start = closestIndex(geometry[0], candidate);
      const end = closestIndex(geometry.at(-1)!, candidate);
      if (!start || !end || start.index >= end.index) continue;
      if (start.distanceM > MAX_ENDPOINT_OFFSET_M || end.distanceM > MAX_ENDPOINT_OFFSET_M) continue;

      // Check a few points across the returned leg, while retaining one ordered
      // catalog slice instead of performing an independent per-point mapping.
      let shapeOffsetM = 0;
      const checks = Math.min(9, geometry.length);
      for (let sample = 0; sample < checks; sample++) {
        const point = geometry[Math.round(sample * (geometry.length - 1) / Math.max(1, checks - 1))];
        shapeOffsetM += closestIndex(point, candidate)?.distanceM ?? Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
      }
      shapeOffsetM /= checks;
      if (shapeOffsetM > MAX_SHAPE_OFFSET_M) continue;
      const score = start.distanceM + end.distanceM + shapeOffsetM * 2;
      if (!best || score < best.score) best = { route: candidate, start: start.index, end: end.index, score };
    }
    return best;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

/**
 * Replaces a realtime leg with the matching contiguous enriched catalog shape.
 * The catalog retains its own terrain, tunnel and bridge data, so tunnel
 * rendering follows the selected trip rather than a nearby unrelated line.
 */
export async function enrichRouteFromTerrain(route: DemoRoute): Promise<boolean> {
  const match = await matchCatalogRoute(route);
  if (!match) return false;

  const realtimeLengthM = Math.max(1, route.routeLengthM);
  const source = match.route;
  const selectedGeo = source.geoPoints!.slice(match.start, match.end + 1).map(([lat, lon]) => ({ lat, lon }));
  const datum = source.elevationDatumM!;
  const selectedControl = source.controlPoints.slice(match.start, match.end + 1);
  const selectedSurface = source.surfaceHeightsM?.slice(match.start, match.end + 1) ?? selectedControl.map((point) => point[1]);
  const selectedTunnel = source.tunnelMask?.slice(match.start, match.end + 1) ?? selectedControl.map(() => false);
  const selectedBridge = source.bridgeMask?.slice(match.start, match.end + 1) ?? selectedControl.map(() => false);
  if (selectedGeo.length < 2 || selectedControl.length !== selectedGeo.length) return false;

  // Rebase the catalog slice onto its own geographic origin, matching the
  // projection used by buildRealtimeRoute.
  const origin = selectedGeo[0];
  const longitudeScale = Math.cos(origin.lat * Math.PI / 180) * EARTH_M_PER_DEG_LAT;
  const absoluteTrack = selectedControl.map((point) => datum + point[1]);
  const outputDatum = absoluteTrack[0];
  route.geoPoints = selectedGeo.map((point): [number, number] => [point.lat, point.lon]);
  route.controlPoints = selectedGeo.map((point, index): [number, number, number] => [
    -(point.lon - origin.lon) * longitudeScale,
    round(absoluteTrack[index] - outputDatum),
    (point.lat - origin.lat) * EARTH_M_PER_DEG_LAT
  ]);
  route.elevationDatumM = round(outputDatum);
  route.surfaceHeightsM = selectedSurface.map((height) => round(datum + height - outputDatum));
  route.tunnelMask = selectedTunnel;
  route.bridgeMask = selectedBridge;
  route.tunnelRanges = maskRanges(selectedTunnel, selectedGeo);
  route.routeLengthM = routeDistance(selectedGeo);
  // The selected catalog trip is the same service corridor; retain the journey
  // timetable and its existing stop order, but scale distances to its precise
  // enriched shape length.
  route.stations = route.stations.map((station) => ({
    ...station,
    distanceM: round(station.distanceM / realtimeLengthM * route.routeLengthM)
  }));
  return true;
}

--- features/realtime/api.ts ---
import type {
  Departure,
  DeparturesResponse,
  Journey,
  JourneysResponse,
  LocationsResponse,
  RealtimeLocation,
  SearchJourneyRequest
} from './types';

// Base URL of the transit-simulator-backend service. In dev we always call the
// same origin so requests flow through the Vite proxy (the backend sends no CORS
// headers); production builds call VITE_REALTIME_API_BASE directly (deploy behind
// a reverse proxy or enable CORS on the backend).
const CONFIGURED_BASE = (import.meta.env.VITE_REALTIME_API_BASE as string | undefined)?.replace(/\/$/, '') ?? '';
const API_BASE = import.meta.env.DEV ? '' : CONFIGURED_BASE;
const API_ROOT = `${API_BASE}/api/v1`;

// Envelope the backend uses for 4xx errors.
type ErrorEnvelope = { error?: { code?: string; message?: string } };

async function request<T>(path: string, init?: RequestInit): Promise<T> {
  const response = await fetch(`${API_ROOT}${path}`, {
    ...init,
    headers: { Accept: 'application/json', ...(init?.body ? { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } : {}), ...init?.headers }
  });
  if (!response.ok) {
    let message = `Request failed (${response.status})`;
    try {
      const body = (await response.json()) as ErrorEnvelope;
      if (body.error?.message) message = body.error.message;
    } catch {
      // Non-JSON error body; keep the status-based message.
    }
    throw new Error(message);
  }
  return (await response.json()) as T;
}

// Keeps only fully-formed locations (id + coordinates) so callers get the
// tightened RealtimeLocation shape the UI relies on.
function usableLocations(response: LocationsResponse): RealtimeLocation[] {
  return (response.locations ?? []).filter(
    (loc): loc is RealtimeLocation => !!loc.id && !!loc.name && typeof loc.lat === 'number' && typeof loc.lon === 'number'
  );
}

/** Autocomplete stops/addresses/POIs by free-text query. */
export async function searchLocations(query: string, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<RealtimeLocation[]> {
  return usableLocations(await request<LocationsResponse>(`/locations?query=${encodeURIComponent(query)}`, { signal }));
}

/** Finds stops/places within radiusM meters of a coordinate. */
export async function nearbyLocations(lat: number, lon: number, radiusM = 700, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<RealtimeLocation[]> {
  const params = new URLSearchParams({ lat: String(lat), lon: String(lon), radiusM: String(radiusM) });
  return usableLocations(await request<LocationsResponse>(`/locations/nearby?${params}`, { signal }));
}

/** Searches journeys between two places (by id and/or coordinates). */
export async function searchJourneys(body: SearchJourneyRequest, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<Journey[]> {
  const response = await request<JourneysResponse>('/journeys', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify(body), signal });
  return (response.journeys ?? []).filter((j): j is Journey => !!j.id && !!j.legs && !!j.departureTime && !!j.arrivalTime);
}

/** Live departure board for a station; name/lat/lon help TRIAS resolve the stop. */
export async function stationDepartures(
  stationId: string,
  options: { name?: string; lat?: number; lon?: number; at?: string } = {},
  signal?: AbortSignal
): Promise<Departure[]> {
  const params = new URLSearchParams();
  if (options.name) params.set('name', options.name);
  if (options.lat !== undefined) params.set('lat', String(options.lat));
  if (options.lon !== undefined) params.set('lon', String(options.lon));
  if (options.at) params.set('at', options.at);
  const query = params.toString();
  const response = await request<DeparturesResponse>(
    `/stations/${encodeURIComponent(stationId)}/departures${query ? `?${query}` : ''}`,
    { signal }
  );
  return (response.departures ?? []).filter((d): d is Departure => !!d.id && !!d.line && !!d.destination);
}

--- features/realtime/buildRealtimeRoute.ts ---
import type { DemoRoute } from '../../route';
import type { TransportMode } from '../../domain/transport';
import { pointDistance, type GeoPoint } from '../../minimap/route-position';
import type { LegMode } from './types';

// Matches TransitWorld.projectGeo so a synthetic route's control points align
// with the OSM scenery it streams from geoPoints.
const EARTH_M_PER_DEG_LAT = 111_320;

// A station placed along the synthetic route with its position on the ground.
export type RealtimeStation = { name: string; lat: number; lon: number; scheduledS: number };

export type RealtimeRouteInput = {
  id: string;
  line: string;
  destination: string;
  description: string;
  transportMode: TransportMode;
  color?: string;
  // Ordered ground waypoints defining the ride path (origin first, destination last).
  waypoints: GeoPoint[];
  // Ordered stops along the path; distances are derived from their positions.
  stations: RealtimeStation[];
  speedLimitMps?: number;
};

// Maps a TRIAS leg mode to a renderable simulator transport mode.
export function legModeToTransportMode(mode: LegMode): TransportMode {
  return mode === 'walk' ? 'stadtbahn' : mode;
}

// Densifies a polyline so short 2-point paths still stream scenery and curve smoothly.
function densify(points: GeoPoint[], maxSegmentM: number): GeoPoint[] {
  if (points.length < 2) return points;
  const out: GeoPoint[] = [points[0]];
  for (let i = 1; i < points.length; i++) {
    const a = points[i - 1];
    const b = points[i];
    const segments = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(pointDistance(a, b) / maxSegmentM));
    for (let s = 1; s <= segments; s++) {
      const t = s / segments;
      out.push({ lat: a.lat + (b.lat - a.lat) * t, lon: a.lon + (b.lon - a.lon) * t });
    }
  }
  return out;
}

// Cumulative ground distance in meters along a polyline.
function cumulative(points: GeoPoint[]): number[] {
  const distances = [0];
  for (let i = 1; i < points.length; i++) {
    distances.push(distances[i - 1] + pointDistance(points[i - 1], points[i]));
  }
  return distances;
}

// Ground distance from the route start to the nearest waypoint of a stop.
function stationDistance(station: GeoPoint, points: GeoPoint[], distances: number[]): number {
  let best = 0;
  let bestScore = Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
  for (let i = 0; i < points.length; i++) {
    const score = pointDistance(station, points[i]);
    if (score < bestScore) {
      bestScore = score;
      best = distances[i];
    }
  }
  return best;
}

/**
 * Builds a self-contained DemoRoute from live realtime geometry so the existing
 * simulator/world can drive it. Control points use the same projection as
 * TransitWorld.projectGeo, keeping the vehicle aligned with streamed scenery.
 */
export function buildRealtimeRoute(input: RealtimeRouteInput): DemoRoute {
  const waypoints = densify(input.waypoints, input.transportMode === 'bus' ? 90 : 160);
  const distances = cumulative(waypoints);
  const routeLengthM = Math.max(1, distances[distances.length - 1]);

  const origin = waypoints[0];
  const lonScale = Math.cos((origin.lat * Math.PI) / 180) * EARTH_M_PER_DEG_LAT;
  const controlPoints = waypoints.map(
    (p): [number, number, number] => [-(p.lon - origin.lon) * lonScale, 0, (p.lat - origin.lat) * EARTH_M_PER_DEG_LAT]
  );

  // Place stops by nearest waypoint, then keep them monotonic and de-duplicated.
  const stations = input.stations
    .map((s) => ({ name: s.name, distanceM: stationDistance(s, waypoints, distances), scheduledS: s.scheduledS, lat: s.lat, lon: s.lon }))
    .sort((a, b) => a.distanceM - b.distanceM)
    .filter((s, i, arr) => i === 0 || (s.distanceM - arr[i - 1].distanceM > 5 && s.name !== arr[i - 1].name));

  return {
    id: input.id,
    line: input.line,
    destination: input.destination,
    description: input.description,
    transportMode: input.transportMode,
    freeRideLabel: `${input.line} live ride`,
    source: 'VVS/TRIAS realtime',
    color: input.color,
    speedLimitMps: input.speedLimitMps ?? (input.transportMode === 'bus' ? 13.8 : 19.4),
    routeLengthM,
    stations: stations.length >= 2
      ? stations
      : [
          { name: input.stations[0]?.name ?? 'Start', distanceM: 0, scheduledS: 0 },
          { name: input.destination, distanceM: routeLengthM, scheduledS: Math.round(routeLengthM / 12) }
        ],
    controlPoints,
    geoPoints: waypoints.map((p): [number, number] => [p.lat, p.lon])
  };
}

--- features/realtime/JourneySearchModal.tsx ---
import { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { useAppState } from '../../app/useAppState';
import { Modal } from './Modal';
import { RealtimeMap, type MapPin, type MapPoint } from './RealtimeMap';
import { LocationField, type EndpointValue } from './LocationField';
import { searchJourneys } from './api';
import { buildJourneyRoute, journeyPath, journeyStations } from './journeyRoute';
import { enrichRouteFromTerrain } from './catalogTerrain';
import { formatClock, secondsBetween } from './time';
import type { Journey } from './types';

type Endpoint = EndpointValue | null;

/** Journey search: pick origin/destination (typed or on the map), list trips, visualize, simulate. */
export function JourneySearchModal({ onClose }: { onClose: () => void }) {
  const { dispatch } = useAppState();
  const [origin, setOrigin] = useState<Endpoint>(null);
  const [destination, setDestination] = useState<Endpoint>(null);
  const [picking, setPicking] = useState<'origin' | 'destination' | null>(null);
  const [journeys, setJourneys] = useState<Journey[]>([]);
  const [selectedId, setSelectedId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [preparing, setPreparing] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const selected = journeys.find((j) => j.id === selectedId) ?? null;

  const stops = useMemo(
    () => (selected && origin && destination ? journeyStations(selected, origin, destination) : []),
    [selected, origin, destination]
  );

  const pins = useMemo<MapPin[]>(() => {
    const list: MapPin[] = [];
    if (origin) list.push({ id: 'origin', lat: origin.lat, lon: origin.lon, kind: 'origin', title: `From: ${origin.name}` });
    if (destination) list.push({ id: 'dest', lat: destination.lat, lon: destination.lon, kind: 'destination', title: `To: ${destination.name}` });
    for (const s of stops.slice(1, -1)) list.push({ id: `stop-${s.name}-${s.scheduledS}`, lat: s.lat, lon: s.lon, kind: 'stop', title: s.name });
    return list;
  }, [origin, destination, stops]);

  const path = useMemo<MapPoint[] | null>(
    () => (selected && origin && destination ? journeyPath(selected, origin, destination) : null),
    [selected, origin, destination]
  );

  const onMapClick = (point: MapPoint) => {
    if (!picking) return;
    const value: EndpointValue = { name: `Pin ${point.lat.toFixed(4)}, ${point.lon.toFixed(4)}`, lat: point.lat, lon: point.lon };
    if (picking === 'origin') setOrigin(value);
    else setDestination(value);
    setPicking(null);
  };

  const runSearch = () => {
    if (!origin || !destination) return;
    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);
    setSelectedId(null);
    searchJourneys({
      origin: { id: origin.id, name: origin.name, lat: origin.lat, lon: origin.lon },
      originId: origin.id,
      destination: { id: destination.id, name: destination.name, lat: destination.lat, lon: destination.lon },
      destinationId: destination.id
    })
      .then((results) => {
        setJourneys(results);
        if (results[0]) setSelectedId(results[0].id);
        if (results.length === 0) setError('No journeys found for these points.');
      })
      .catch((e: unknown) => setError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Journey search failed.'))
      .finally(() => setLoading(false));
  };

  const simulate = async () => {
    if (!selected || !origin || !destination) return;
    setPreparing(true);
    const route = buildJourneyRoute(selected, origin, destination);
    // Match every vehicle leg independently so transfers keep their own
    // geometry, terrain profile, tunnel data, and vehicle type.
    try {
      await Promise.all([route, ...(route.journeyLegs ?? [])].map((leg) => enrichRouteFromTerrain(leg)));
    } catch {
      // Keep the un-enriched route.
    }
    dispatch({ type: 'realtime-route-simulated', route, journey: { origin, destination, journey: selected } });
    onClose();
  };

  return (
    <Modal title="Journey search" subtitle="Live VVS / TRIAS routing" onClose={onClose}>
      <div className="rt-split">
        <RealtimeMap pins={pins} path={path} onMapClick={onMapClick} hint={picking ? `Click to set ${picking}` : 'Pin ⌖ to pick on map'} />
        <div className="rt-panel">
          <div className="rt-fields">
            <LocationField
              placeholder="From (stop or address)"
              value={origin}
              picking={picking === 'origin'}
              onSelect={setOrigin}
              onPickOnMap={() => setPicking((p) => (p === 'origin' ? null : 'origin'))}
            />
            <LocationField
              placeholder="To (stop or address)"
              value={destination}
              picking={picking === 'destination'}
              onSelect={setDestination}
              onPickOnMap={() => setPicking((p) => (p === 'destination' ? null : 'destination'))}
            />
            <button className="primary" onClick={runSearch} disabled={!origin || !destination || loading}>
              {loading ? 'Searching…' : 'Search journeys'}
            </button>
          </div>
          {error && <div className="rt-error">{error}</div>}
          <div className="rt-list">
            {journeys.length === 0 && !error && <div className="rt-list-empty">Pick a start and finish, then search.</div>}
            {journeys.map((journey) => {
              const transit = journey.legs.filter((leg) => leg.mode !== 'walk');
              const duration = journey.durationMinutes ?? Math.round(secondsBetween(journey.departureTime, journey.arrivalTime) / 60);
              return (
                <button
                  key={journey.id}
                  className={`rt-item ${journey.id === selectedId ? 'selected' : ''}`}
                  onClick={() => setSelectedId(journey.id)}
                >
                  <div className="rt-item-title">
                    <span>{formatClock(journey.departureTime)} → {formatClock(journey.arrivalTime)}</span>
                    <span className="rt-badge">{duration} min</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="rt-item-sub">
                    {journey.summary ?? (transit.map((l) => l.line).filter(Boolean).join(' · ') || 'Walk')}
                    {typeof journey.interchanges === 'number' && ` · ${journey.interchanges} change${journey.interchanges === 1 ? '' : 's'}`}
                  </div>
                </button>
              );
            })}
          </div>
          <div className="rt-actions">
            <button className="primary" onClick={() => void simulate()} disabled={!selected || preparing}>
              {preparing ? 'Preparing journey…' : 'Simulate journey'}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </Modal>
  );
}

--- features/realtime/types.ts ---
// App-facing vocabulary over the generated OpenAPI contracts.
//
// The wire types in ./generated/api-types.ts are the source of truth — never
// edit them by hand; regenerate with `npm run gen:api` after the backend spec
// changes. Because the Swagger 2.0 spec marks every field optional, this module
// renames a few types and tightens the fields the UI relies on always being
// present (the api client normalizes responses to guarantee them).

import type {
  Departure as WireDeparture,
  Journey as WireJourney,
  Location as WireLocation
} from './generated/api-types';

export type {
  DomainLegMode as LegMode,
  DomainLocationType as LocationType,
  GeoPoint,
  JourneyLeg,
  JourneyStop,
  Place,
  SearchJourneyRequest,
  LocationsResponse,
  JourneysResponse,
  DeparturesResponse
} from './generated/api-types';

// A resolved place always has coordinates and a name once the client keeps it.
export type RealtimeLocation = WireLocation & Required<Pick<WireLocation, 'id' | 'name' | 'lat' | 'lon' | 'type'>>;

// A journey the UI can key on and iterate over.
export type Journey = WireJourney & Required<Pick<WireJourney, 'id' | 'legs' | 'departureTime' | 'arrivalTime'>>;

// A departure row the UI can key on and label.
export type Departure = WireDeparture & Required<Pick<WireDeparture, 'id' | 'line' | 'destination'>>;

--- features/realtime/LocationField.tsx ---
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { searchLocations } from './api';
import type { RealtimeLocation } from './types';

// A chosen endpoint: either a resolved TRIAS location or a raw coordinate pin.
export type EndpointValue = { id?: string; name: string; lat: number; lon: number };

/**
 * Debounced location autocomplete input. Emits the picked location, and offers a
 * "pick on map" affordance the parent wires to its map's click handler.
 */
export function LocationField({
  placeholder,
  value,
  picking,
  onSelect,
  onPickOnMap
}: {
  placeholder: string;
  value: EndpointValue | null;
  picking?: boolean;
  onSelect: (value: EndpointValue) => void;
  onPickOnMap?: () => void;
}) {
  const [text, setText] = useState(value?.name ?? '');
  const [results, setResults] = useState<RealtimeLocation[]>([]);
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const skipNextSearch = useRef(false);

  // Reflect externally set values (e.g. a map pick) into the input text.
  useEffect(() => {
    skipNextSearch.current = true;
    setText(value?.name ?? '');
    setOpen(false);
  }, [value?.name, value?.lat, value?.lon]);

  // Debounced query; skips the fire that a programmatic text change triggers.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (skipNextSearch.current) {
      skipNextSearch.current = false;
      return;
    }
    const query = text.trim();
    if (query.length < 2) {
      setResults([]);
      return;
    }
    const controller = new AbortController();
    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
      void searchLocations(query, controller.signal)
        .then((locations) => {
          setResults(locations.slice(0, 8));
          setOpen(true);
        })
        .catch(() => undefined);
    }, 250);
    return () => {
      controller.abort();
      clearTimeout(timer);
    };
  }, [text]);

  return (
    <div className="rt-field">
      <input
        type="text"
        placeholder={picking ? 'Click the map to place a point…' : placeholder}
        value={text}
        onChange={(e) => setText(e.target.value)}
        onFocus={() => results.length && setOpen(true)}
        onBlur={() => setTimeout(() => setOpen(false), 150)}
      />
      {onPickOnMap && (
        <button
          type="button"
          className={`rt-pick-btn ${picking ? 'active' : ''}`}
          title="Pick on map"
          onClick={onPickOnMap}
        >
          ⌖
        </button>
      )}
      {open && results.length > 0 && (
        <div className="rt-suggestions">
          {results.map((loc) => (
            <button
              key={loc.id || `${loc.lat},${loc.lon}`}
              type="button"
              onMouseDown={(e) => e.preventDefault()}
              onClick={() => {
                onSelect({ id: loc.id, name: loc.name, lat: loc.lat, lon: loc.lon });
                setOpen(false);
              }}
            >
              {loc.name}
              <span className="rt-suggestion-type"> · {loc.type}</span>
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

--- features/realtime/RealtimePanel.tsx ---
import { useContext, useState } from 'react';
import { useAppState } from '../../app/useAppState';
import { SimulatorCommandsContext } from '../simulation/useSimulatorRuntime';
import { JourneySearchModal } from './JourneySearchModal';
import { StopSearchModal } from './StopSearchModal';
import { DeparturesModal } from './DeparturesModal';
import type { EndpointValue } from './LocationField';

type RealtimeModal = 'journey' | 'stop' | 'departures' | null;

/**
 * Realtime tab body: live TRIAS tools (journey search, stop search, departures)
 * plus the shared camera / photo / time-of-day controls. The heavy search UIs
 * open in modals so the 3D viewport stays visible behind them.
 */
export function RealtimePanel() {
  const { state, dispatch } = useAppState();
  const commands = useContext(SimulatorCommandsContext);
  const [modal, setModal] = useState<RealtimeModal>(null);
  // A station handed from stop search into the departures board.
  const [departuresStation, setDeparturesStation] = useState<EndpointValue | null>(null);

  const openDepartures = (station: EndpointValue | null) => {
    setDeparturesStation(station);
    setModal('departures');
  };

  return (
    <>
      <div className="toolbar realtime-tools">
        <button onClick={() => setModal('journey')}>Journey search</button>
        <button onClick={() => setModal('stop')}>Stop search</button>
        <button onClick={() => openDepartures(null)}>Departures</button>
      </div>

      <div className="toolbar">
        <select
          aria-label="Time of day"
          value={state.selectedTime}
          onChange={(e) => dispatch({ type: 'time-selected', time: e.target.value === 'auto' ? 'auto' : Number(e.target.value) })}
        >
          <option value="auto">Local time (auto)</option>
          <option value="6">06:00 · Dawn</option>
          <option value="9">09:00 · Morning</option>
          <option value="13">13:00 · Day</option>
          <option value="18">18:00 · Evening</option>
        </select>
      </div>

      <div className="controls">
        <button
          onClick={() => {
            const mode = commands?.cycleCamera();
            if (mode) dispatch({ type: 'camera-selected', cameraMode: mode });
          }}
        >
          Camera
        </button>
        <button onClick={() => commands?.enterPhotoMode()}>Photo</button>
      </div>

      {modal === 'journey' && <JourneySearchModal onClose={() => setModal(null)} />}
      {modal === 'stop' && <StopSearchModal onClose={() => setModal(null)} onOpenDepartures={openDepartures} />}
      {modal === 'departures' && <DeparturesModal onClose={() => setModal(null)} initialStation={departuresStation} />}
    </>
  );
}

--- features/realtime/StopSearchModal.tsx ---
import { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { Modal } from './Modal';
import { RealtimeMap, type MapPin, type MapPoint } from './RealtimeMap';
import { LocationField, type EndpointValue } from './LocationField';
import { nearbyLocations } from './api';
import type { RealtimeLocation } from './types';

const RADII = [500, 700, 1000, 1500];

/** Stop search: choose a point (typed or on the map), list nearby stops, visualize, open departures. */
export function StopSearchModal({
  onClose,
  onOpenDepartures
}: {
  onClose: () => void;
  onOpenDepartures: (station: EndpointValue) => void;
}) {
  const [center, setCenter] = useState<EndpointValue | null>(null);
  const [radiusM, setRadiusM] = useState(700);
  const [stops, setStops] = useState<RealtimeLocation[]>([]);
  const [selectedId, setSelectedId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const search = (point: EndpointValue, radius = radiusM) => {
    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);
    setSelectedId(null);
    nearbyLocations(point.lat, point.lon, radius)
      .then((locations) => {
        setStops(locations);
        if (locations.length === 0) setError('No stops found near this point.');
      })
      .catch((e: unknown) => setError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Nearby search failed.'))
      .finally(() => setLoading(false));
  };

  const setPoint = (value: EndpointValue) => {
    setCenter(value);
    search(value);
  };

  const onMapClick = (point: MapPoint) => {
    setPoint({ name: `Pin ${point.lat.toFixed(4)}, ${point.lon.toFixed(4)}`, lat: point.lat, lon: point.lon });
  };

  const pins = useMemo<MapPin[]>(() => {
    const list: MapPin[] = [];
    if (center) list.push({ id: 'center', lat: center.lat, lon: center.lon, kind: 'origin', title: center.name });
    for (const stop of stops) {
      list.push({
        id: stop.id || `${stop.lat},${stop.lon}`,
        lat: stop.lat,
        lon: stop.lon,
        kind: stop.id === selectedId ? 'active' : 'stop',
        title: stop.name,
        onClick: () => setSelectedId(stop.id)
      });
    }
    return list;
  }, [center, stops, selectedId]);

  return (
    <Modal title="Stop search" subtitle="Stops near a point" onClose={onClose}>
      <div className="rt-split">
        <RealtimeMap pins={pins} onMapClick={onMapClick} hint="Click the map to search nearby stops" />
        <div className="rt-panel">
          <div className="rt-fields">
            <LocationField placeholder="Address, POI, or stop" value={center} onSelect={setPoint} />
            <div className="rt-radius">
              <select aria-label="Search radius" value={radiusM} onChange={(e) => setRadiusM(Number(e.target.value))}>
                {RADII.map((r) => (
                  <option key={r} value={r}>Within {r} m</option>
                ))}
              </select>
              <button className="primary" onClick={() => center && search(center)} disabled={!center || loading}>
                {loading ? 'Searching…' : 'Search here'}
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
          {error && <div className="rt-error">{error}</div>}
          <div className="rt-list">
            {stops.length === 0 && !error && <div className="rt-list-empty">Pick a point to find stops nearby.</div>}
            {stops.map((stop) => (
              <div key={stop.id || `${stop.lat},${stop.lon}`} className={`rt-item ${stop.id === selectedId ? 'selected' : ''}`}>
                <div className="rt-item-title">
                  <span>{stop.name}</span>
                  <span className="rt-badge">{stop.type}</span>
                </div>
                <div className="rt-item-actions">
                  <button onClick={() => setSelectedId(stop.id)}>Show on map</button>
                  <button onClick={() => onOpenDepartures({ id: stop.id, name: stop.name, lat: stop.lat, lon: stop.lon })}>
                    Departures
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </Modal>
  );
}

--- features/realtime/RealtimeMap.tsx ---
import L from 'leaflet';
import 'leaflet/dist/leaflet.css';
import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';

export type MapPoint = { lat: number; lon: number };
export type PinKind = 'origin' | 'destination' | 'stop' | 'active';
export type MapPin = { id: string; lat: number; lon: number; kind: PinKind; title?: string; onClick?: () => void };

const STUTTGART: [number, number] = [48.7758, 9.1829];

const PIN_COLORS: Record<PinKind, string> = {
  origin: '#37d67a',
  destination: '#ff5c5c',
  stop: '#4aa8ff',
  active: '#ffd900'
};

/**
 * Interactive Leaflet map shared by the realtime modals: renders point pins and
 * an optional route path, and reports map clicks for pick-on-map workflows.
 */
export function RealtimeMap({
  pins,
  path,
  hint,
  onMapClick,
  fit = true
}: {
  pins: MapPin[];
  path?: MapPoint[] | null;
  hint?: string;
  onMapClick?: (point: MapPoint) => void;
  fit?: boolean;
}) {
  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const mapRef = useRef<L.Map | null>(null);
  const pinLayer = useRef<L.LayerGroup | null>(null);
  const pathLayer = useRef<L.LayerGroup | null>(null);
  const clickRef = useRef(onMapClick);
  clickRef.current = onMapClick;

  // Create the map once; keep it alive for the modal's lifetime.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!containerRef.current) return;
    const map = L.map(containerRef.current, { zoomControl: true, attributionControl: true }).setView(STUTTGART, 12);
    L.tileLayer('https://{s}.tile.openstreetmap.org/{z}/{x}/{y}.png', {
      maxZoom: 19,
      attribution: '&copy; OpenStreetMap contributors'
    }).addTo(map);
    map.on('click', (e: L.LeafletMouseEvent) => clickRef.current?.({ lat: e.latlng.lat, lon: e.latlng.lng }));
    pinLayer.current = L.layerGroup().addTo(map);
    pathLayer.current = L.layerGroup().addTo(map);
    mapRef.current = map;
    // Leaflet mis-measures a container that mounted this frame inside the modal.
    const raf = requestAnimationFrame(() => map.invalidateSize());
    return () => {
      cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
      map.remove();
      mapRef.current = null;
    };
  }, []);

  // Redraw pins and path, then fit the view to whatever geometry exists.
  useEffect(() => {
    const map = mapRef.current;
    if (!map || !pinLayer.current || !pathLayer.current) return;
    pinLayer.current.clearLayers();
    pathLayer.current.clearLayers();

    for (const pin of pins) {
      const marker = L.circleMarker([pin.lat, pin.lon], {
        radius: pin.kind === 'stop' ? 7 : 9,
        color: '#0b1017',
        weight: 2,
        fillColor: PIN_COLORS[pin.kind],
        fillOpacity: 0.95
      });
      if (pin.title) marker.bindTooltip(pin.title, { direction: 'top' });
      if (pin.onClick) marker.on('click', (e) => { L.DomEvent.stop(e); pin.onClick!(); });
      marker.addTo(pinLayer.current);
    }

    const latLngs: L.LatLngExpression[] = [];
    if (path && path.length >= 2) {
      const line = path.map((p): L.LatLngExpression => [p.lat, p.lon]);
      L.polyline(line, { color: '#ffd900', weight: 5, opacity: 0.9 }).addTo(pathLayer.current);
      latLngs.push(...line);
    }
    for (const pin of pins) latLngs.push([pin.lat, pin.lon]);

    if (fit && latLngs.length) {
      const bounds = L.latLngBounds(latLngs);
      if (latLngs.length === 1) map.setView(latLngs[0] as [number, number], 14);
      else map.fitBounds(bounds, { padding: [28, 28], maxZoom: 15 });
    }
  }, [pins, path, fit]);

  return (
    <div className="rt-map">
      <div ref={containerRef} style={{ width: '100%', height: '100%' }} />
      {hint && <div className="rt-map-hint">{hint}</div>}
    </div>
  );
}

--- features/realtime/Modal.tsx ---
import { useEffect, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import { createPortal } from 'react-dom';

/** Full-screen dimmed modal shell used by the realtime tools. Escape / backdrop close. */
export function Modal({
  title,
  subtitle,
  onClose,
  children
}: {
  title: string;
  subtitle?: string;
  onClose: () => void;
  children: ReactNode;
}) {
  useEffect(() => {
    const onKey = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (event.key === 'Escape') onClose();
    };
    window.addEventListener('keydown', onKey);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey);
  }, [onClose]);

  // Portal to <body> so the fixed backdrop escapes the HUD's backdrop-filter
  // containing block and covers the full viewport.
  return createPortal(
    <div className="rt-modal-backdrop" onClick={onClose}>
      <div className="rt-modal" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label={title} onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
        <div className="rt-modal-header">
          <div>
            <h2>{title}</h2>
            {subtitle && <div className="label">{subtitle}</div>}
          </div>
          <button className="rt-modal-close" aria-label="Close" onClick={onClose}>
            ×
          </button>
        </div>
        <div className="rt-modal-body">{children}</div>
      </div>
    </div>,
    document.body
  );
}

--- features/realtime/journeyRoute.ts ---
import type { DemoRoute } from '../../route';
import type { GeoPoint } from '../../minimap/route-position';
import { buildRealtimeRoute, legModeToTransportMode, type RealtimeStation } from './buildRealtimeRoute';
import { parseTimeMs } from './time';
import type { EndpointValue } from './LocationField';
import type { Journey } from './types';

/** Persisted realtime selection used to restore a journey from the URL. */
export type RealtimeJourneySimulation = { origin: EndpointValue; destination: EndpointValue; journey: Journey };

// Asserts a wire point has real coordinates while preserving its other fields.
function isCoord<T extends { lat?: number; lon?: number }>(p: T): p is T & GeoPoint {
  return typeof p.lat === 'number' && typeof p.lon === 'number';
}

/** Ground path of a journey: the real per-leg geometry concatenated in order. */
export function journeyGeometry(journey: Journey): GeoPoint[] {
  const points: GeoPoint[] = [];
  for (const leg of journey.legs) {
    for (const g of leg.geometry ?? []) if (isCoord(g)) points.push({ lat: g.lat, lon: g.lon });
  }
  return points;
}

/**
 * Stations along a journey. Prefers the real intermediate stops (with
 * coordinates and times); when the backend omits them, falls back to
 * distributing leg boundaries along the straight origin→destination line by
 * their share of the total travel time.
 */
export function journeyStations(journey: Journey, origin: EndpointValue, destination: EndpointValue): RealtimeStation[] {
  const depMs = parseTimeMs(journey.departureTime);

  const real: RealtimeStation[] = [];
  for (const leg of journey.legs) {
    for (const stop of leg.stops ?? []) {
      if (!isCoord(stop)) continue;
      const tMs = parseTimeMs(stop.departureTime ?? stop.arrivalTime);
      real.push({
        name: stop.name ?? '',
        lat: stop.lat,
        lon: stop.lon,
        scheduledS: depMs !== null && tMs !== null ? Math.max(0, Math.round((tMs - depMs) / 1000)) : 0
      });
    }
  }
  if (real.length >= 2) return real;

  // Fallback: no per-stop data, so place leg boundaries by time fraction.
  const arrMs = parseTimeMs(journey.arrivalTime);
  const total = depMs !== null && arrMs !== null ? Math.max(1, arrMs - depMs) : null;
  const boundaries: { name: string; ms: number | null }[] = [
    { name: journey.legs[0]?.originName ?? origin.name, ms: parseTimeMs(journey.legs[0]?.originTime) ?? depMs }
  ];
  for (const leg of journey.legs) boundaries.push({ name: leg.destinationName ?? destination.name, ms: parseTimeMs(leg.destinationTime) });

  return boundaries.map((b, i) => {
    const fraction = total && depMs !== null && b.ms !== null
      ? Math.min(1, Math.max(0, (b.ms - depMs) / total))
      : i / Math.max(1, boundaries.length - 1);
    return {
      name: b.name,
      lat: origin.lat + (destination.lat - origin.lat) * fraction,
      lon: origin.lon + (destination.lon - origin.lon) * fraction,
      scheduledS: total && depMs !== null && b.ms !== null ? Math.round((b.ms - depMs) / 1000) : Math.round(fraction * 600)
    };
  });
}

/** Polyline to draw for a journey: real geometry when present, else a straight line. */
export function journeyPath(journey: Journey, origin: EndpointValue, destination: EndpointValue): GeoPoint[] {
  const geometry = journeyGeometry(journey);
  return geometry.length >= 2 ? geometry : [{ lat: origin.lat, lon: origin.lon }, { lat: destination.lat, lon: destination.lon }];
}

/** Stops belonging to one vehicle run, with times relative to that run's departure. */
function transitLegStations(leg: Journey['legs'][number]): RealtimeStation[] {
  const departureMs = parseTimeMs(leg.originTime);
  return (leg.stops ?? []).flatMap((stop) => {
    if (!isCoord(stop)) return [];
    const timeMs = parseTimeMs(stop.departureTime ?? stop.arrivalTime);
    return [{
      name: stop.name ?? '',
      lat: stop.lat,
      lon: stop.lon,
      scheduledS: departureMs !== null && timeMs !== null ? Math.max(0, Math.round((timeMs - departureMs) / 1000)) : 0
    }];
  });
}

/** Builds the individual vehicle runs in a journey; walking legs are not simulated. */
export function buildJourneyRoutes(journey: Journey, origin: EndpointValue, destination: EndpointValue): DemoRoute[] {
  const transitLegs = journey.legs
    .filter((leg) => leg.mode !== 'walk')
    .map((transit, index) => ({ transit, index }));
  const routes = transitLegs.map(({ transit, index }) => {
  const geometry = transit?.geometry?.filter(isCoord).map((point) => ({ lat: point.lat, lon: point.lon })) ?? [];
  const waypoints = geometry.length >= 2 ? geometry : [{ lat: origin.lat, lon: origin.lon }, { lat: destination.lat, lon: destination.lon }];
  // A passenger's requested endpoint can be an address or a transfer stop.
  // Vehicle signs must show the service's advertised direction instead.
  const lineDestination = transit?.destination ?? transit?.destinationName ?? destination.name;

  return buildRealtimeRoute({
    id: `rt-journey-${journey.id}-${transit.id ?? index}`,
    line: transit?.line ?? 'Trip',
    destination: lineDestination,
    description: journey.summary ?? `${origin.name} → ${destination.name}`,
    transportMode: legModeToTransportMode(transit.mode ?? 'stadtbahn'),
    waypoints,
    stations: transitLegStations(transit)
  });
  });
  routes.forEach((route, index) => {
    const current = transitLegs[index].transit;
    const next = transitLegs[index + 1]?.transit;
    const arrival = parseTimeMs(current.destinationTime);
    const departure = next ? parseTimeMs(next.originTime) : null;
    if (arrival !== null && departure !== null) route.transferAfterS = Math.max(0, Math.round((departure - arrival) / 1000));
  });
  return routes;
}

/**
 * Builds a sequential realtime journey. The first route is rendered initially;
 * SimulatorRuntime swaps to the attached later vehicle runs at each transfer.
 */
export function buildJourneyRoute(journey: Journey, origin: EndpointValue, destination: EndpointValue): DemoRoute {
  const legs = buildJourneyRoutes(journey, origin, destination);
  if (legs[0]) {
    legs[0].journeyLegs = legs.slice(1);
    return legs[0];
  }
  return buildRealtimeRoute({
    id: `rt-journey-${journey.id}-walk`,
    line: 'Walk',
    destination: destination.name,
    description: journey.summary ?? `${origin.name} → ${destination.name}`,
    transportMode: 'stadtbahn',
    waypoints: [{ lat: origin.lat, lon: origin.lon }, { lat: destination.lat, lon: destination.lon }],
    stations: []
  });
}

--- features/realtime/time.ts ---
// Parsing/formatting helpers for the string timestamps the backend returns.
// Values may be ISO 8601 or a bare "HH:MM"; both are handled.

/** Parses a backend timestamp to epoch milliseconds, or null if unparseable. */
export function parseTimeMs(value: string | undefined): number | null {
  if (!value) return null;
  const iso = Date.parse(value);
  if (!Number.isNaN(iso)) return iso;
  const hm = /^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})/.exec(value);
  if (hm) {
    const d = new Date();
    d.setHours(Number(hm[1]), Number(hm[2]), 0, 0);
    return d.getTime();
  }
  return null;
}

/** Formats a backend timestamp as a local "HH:MM" clock, falling back to the raw value. */
export function formatClock(value: string | undefined): string {
  const ms = parseTimeMs(value);
  if (ms === null) return value ?? '--:--';
  return new Date(ms).toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' });
}

/** Whole seconds between two backend timestamps (b - a), or 0 when either is missing. */
export function secondsBetween(a: string | undefined, b: string | undefined): number {
  const ta = parseTimeMs(a);
  const tb = parseTimeMs(b);
  if (ta === null || tb === null) return 0;
  return Math.round((tb - ta) / 1000);
}

--- features/live/liveProjection.ts ---
import * as THREE from 'three';

// Equirectangular lat/lon <-> world projection around a fixed origin, matching
// TransitWorld.projectGeo (world.ts) so the reused OSM/terrain primitives place
// geometry consistently. The live 3D scene fixes its origin at the map center at
// hand-off and never moves it, so world coordinates stay stable while flying.

const EARTH_M_PER_DEG_LAT = 111_320;

export type LatLon = { lat: number; lon: number };

export type Projection = {
  origin: LatLon;
  /** lat/lon -> world metres (y=0; height comes from the terrain sampler). */
  project(lat: number, lon: number): THREE.Vector3;
  /** world metres -> lat/lon (inverse of {@link project}, ignoring y). */
  unproject(x: number, z: number): LatLon;
  /** Metres per degree of longitude at the origin latitude. */
  readonly lonScale: number;
};

export function makeProjection(origin: LatLon): Projection {
  const lonScale = Math.cos((origin.lat * Math.PI) / 180) * EARTH_M_PER_DEG_LAT;
  return {
    origin,
    lonScale,
    project(lat, lon) {
      return new THREE.Vector3(-(lon - origin.lon) * lonScale, 0, (lat - origin.lat) * EARTH_M_PER_DEG_LAT);
    },
    unproject(x, z) {
      return { lat: origin.lat + z / EARTH_M_PER_DEG_LAT, lon: origin.lon - x / lonScale };
    }
  };
}

--- features/live/liveDaylight.test.ts ---
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { daylightAt } from './liveDaylight';

describe('daylightAt', () => {
  it('puts the sun highest and brightest at midday', () => {
    const noon = daylightAt(12);
    const midnight = daylightAt(0);
    expect(noon.elevationDeg).toBeGreaterThan(midnight.elevationDeg);
    expect(noon.sunIntensity).toBeGreaterThan(midnight.sunIntensity);
  });

  it('keeps the sun below the horizon at night', () => {
    expect(daylightAt(0).elevationDeg).toBeLessThan(0);
    expect(daylightAt(23).elevationDeg).toBeLessThan(0);
  });

  it('is continuous across midnight (24:00 === 0:00)', () => {
    const a = daylightAt(0);
    const b = daylightAt(24);
    expect(b.skyColor).toBe(a.skyColor);
    expect(b.sunIntensity).toBeCloseTo(a.sunIntensity, 6);
    expect(b.elevationDeg).toBeCloseTo(a.elevationDeg, 6);
  });

  it('wraps out-of-range hours into the day', () => {
    expect(daylightAt(25).elevationDeg).toBeCloseTo(daylightAt(1).elevationDeg, 6);
    expect(daylightAt(-1).sunIntensity).toBeCloseTo(daylightAt(23).sunIntensity, 6);
  });

  it('interpolates sky color between keyframes rather than snapping', () => {
    // 10:00 sits between the 08:00 and 12:00 keyframes.
    const mid = daylightAt(10).skyColor;
    expect(mid).not.toBe(daylightAt(8).skyColor);
    expect(mid).not.toBe(daylightAt(12).skyColor);
  });
});

--- features/live/LiveModePanel.tsx ---
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { ALL_LIVE_MODES, modeColor, modeLabel, type LiveMode } from './liveTypes';
import type { LiveSummary } from './useLiveVehicles';

// Freshness thresholds (§2.5): amber past ~30 s, red when the snapshot is degraded.
const STALE_SECONDS = 30;

function freshnessAgeSeconds(summary: LiveSummary, nowMs: number): number | null {
  if (summary.snapshotAgeSeconds === null || summary.lastUpdatedMs === null) return null;
  // Server-relative age at fetch time, plus the client time elapsed since — this
  // keeps counting up between polls without trusting the client's absolute clock.
  return summary.snapshotAgeSeconds + Math.max(0, Math.round((nowMs - summary.lastUpdatedMs) / 1000));
}

/**
 * Slim Live HUD (concept §2.5): mode filter chips, data freshness, vehicle count,
 * a real-time clock, and the always-visible exit.
 */
export function LiveModePanel({
  summary,
  userModes,
  onToggleMode,
  onExit
}: {
  summary: LiveSummary;
  userModes: Set<LiveMode>;
  onToggleMode: (mode: LiveMode) => void;
  onExit: () => void;
}) {
  const [now, setNow] = useState(() => Date.now());
  useEffect(() => {
    const id = setInterval(() => setNow(Date.now()), 1000);
    return () => clearInterval(id);
  }, []);

  const age = freshnessAgeSeconds(summary, now);
  const requested = new Set(summary.modesRequested);
  const freshnessClass = summary.degraded ? 'degraded' : age !== null && age > STALE_SECONDS ? 'stale' : 'fresh';
  const freshnessText = summary.error
    ? 'reconnecting…'
    : summary.degraded
      ? `degraded · ${age ?? '?'}s`
      : age === null
        ? 'connecting…'
        : `live · updated ${age}s ago`;

  const clock = new Date(now).toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' });

  return (
    <div className="lv-hud">
      <div className="lv-hud-top">
        <span className="lv-hud-title">Live · VVS</span>
        <button className="lv-exit" onClick={onExit}>
          Exit Live
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="lv-chips">
        {ALL_LIVE_MODES.map((mode) => {
          const on = userModes.has(mode);
          // Enabled by the user but not currently requested because the zoom tier
          // suppresses it — hint that zooming in will bring it back.
          const tierHidden = on && !requested.has(mode);
          return (
            <button
              key={mode}
              className={`lv-chip ${on ? 'on' : 'off'} ${tierHidden ? 'tier-hidden' : ''}`}
              style={{ '--c': modeColor(mode) } as React.CSSProperties}
              title={tierHidden ? 'Zoom in to see this mode' : undefined}
              onClick={() => onToggleMode(mode)}
            >
              {modeLabel(mode)}
              {tierHidden && <span className="lv-chip-zoom">⤢</span>}
            </button>
          );
        })}
      </div>

      <div className="lv-hud-foot">
        <span className={`lv-fresh ${freshnessClass}`}>{freshnessText}</span>
        <span className="lv-count">{summary.count} in view</span>
        <span className="lv-clock">{clock}</span>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

--- features/live/liveDaylight.ts ---
// Time-of-day daylight curve for the live 3D view (concept §2.5: "the world's
// lighting keeps following real local time"). Pure and unit-tested; Live3DScene
// samples it from the real local hour and drives the sun, sky and fog.

export type Daylight = {
  skyColor: number; // hex; used for scene background + fog
  sunColor: number; // hex directional light color
  sunIntensity: number;
  hemiIntensity: number; // ambient sky/ground fill
  elevationDeg: number; // sun height above the horizon
  azimuthRad: number; // sun horizontal direction (east → west across the day)
};

type Keyframe = { h: number; sky: number; sun: number; sunI: number; hemiI: number; elev: number };

// Hour-sorted keyframes spanning a full day. The 0:00 and 24:00 frames match so
// the curve is continuous across midnight.
// Night keeps a lifted ambient floor and a lighter sky so the city stays legible
// after dark — it still reads as night, just not pitch black.
const KEYS: Keyframe[] = [
  { h: 0, sky: 0x1c2c44, sun: 0x6474a0, sunI: 0.45, hemiI: 0.62, elev: -10 },
  { h: 5, sky: 0x2a3a54, sun: 0x7482a6, sunI: 0.55, hemiI: 0.66, elev: -3 },
  { h: 6.5, sky: 0xd0966a, sun: 0xffc08a, sunI: 1.2, hemiI: 0.95, elev: 6 },
  { h: 8, sky: 0xa2c6e8, sun: 0xfff0dd, sunI: 2.0, hemiI: 1.2, elev: 22 },
  { h: 12, sky: 0xa7d3ff, sun: 0xffffff, sunI: 2.3, hemiI: 1.35, elev: 60 },
  { h: 16, sky: 0xa4cdec, sun: 0xfff2e0, sunI: 2.05, hemiI: 1.25, elev: 30 },
  { h: 18.5, sky: 0xdc8f5f, sun: 0xffb073, sunI: 1.25, hemiI: 0.92, elev: 6 },
  { h: 20.5, sky: 0x33475f, sun: 0x7d8ab4, sunI: 0.55, hemiI: 0.68, elev: -3 },
  { h: 24, sky: 0x1c2c44, sun: 0x6474a0, sunI: 0.45, hemiI: 0.62, elev: -10 }
];

function lerp(a: number, b: number, t: number): number {
  return a + (b - a) * t;
}

// Interpolates two packed 0xRRGGBB colors per channel.
function lerpHex(a: number, b: number, t: number): number {
  const ar = (a >> 16) & 0xff;
  const ag = (a >> 8) & 0xff;
  const ab = a & 0xff;
  const br = (b >> 16) & 0xff;
  const bg = (b >> 8) & 0xff;
  const bb = b & 0xff;
  const r = Math.round(lerp(ar, br, t));
  const g = Math.round(lerp(ag, bg, t));
  const bl = Math.round(lerp(ab, bb, t));
  return (r << 16) | (g << 8) | bl;
}

/** Daylight parameters at a local hour (0–24, fractional). */
export function daylightAt(hour: number): Daylight {
  const h = ((hour % 24) + 24) % 24;
  let lo = KEYS[0];
  let hi = KEYS[KEYS.length - 1];
  for (let i = 0; i < KEYS.length - 1; i++) {
    if (h >= KEYS[i].h && h <= KEYS[i + 1].h) {
      lo = KEYS[i];
      hi = KEYS[i + 1];
      break;
    }
  }
  const span = hi.h - lo.h;
  const t = span > 0 ? (h - lo.h) / span : 0;

  // Sun sweeps from the east before noon to the west after (a plausible arc, not
  // an astronomical ephemeris).
  const azimuthRad = ((h - 12) / 24) * 2 * Math.PI;

  return {
    skyColor: lerpHex(lo.sky, hi.sky, t),
    sunColor: lerpHex(lo.sun, hi.sun, t),
    sunIntensity: lerp(lo.sunI, hi.sunI, t),
    hemiIntensity: lerp(lo.hemiI, hi.hemiI, t),
    elevationDeg: lerp(lo.elev, hi.elev, t),
    azimuthRad
  };
}

--- features/live/LiveTerrain.ts ---
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { TerrainSampler } from '../../terrain';
import type { Projection } from './liveProjection';

// Terrain relief for the 3D view (concept §4.2). A deformed grid provides both
// the visible ground mesh and a fast synchronous height lookup for placing
// vehicles, labels, tracks, tunnels and the follow camera on Stuttgart's actual
// topography instead of a flat plane.
//
// The grid recenters on the camera focus (like the OSM streamer) so following a
// vehicle far from the entry origin keeps the ground under it — the elevation
// datum stays fixed at the origin, so a recentered patch still lines up with the
// buildings the streamer places via the shared `surfaceHeight` sampler.

const GRID = 160; // cells per side → (GRID+1)² sampled nodes
const SPAN_M = 12_000; // grid covers ±6 km around its center
const STEP_M = SPAN_M / GRID; // 75 m cells
// The OSM streamer places roads/land at the exact per-point terrain sample, while
// this mesh only interpolates between grid nodes. On a slope the interpolated
// surface can sit above those features and bury them, so the backdrop is sunk a
// little. Placement heights (heightAt) are NOT sunk — only the visible mesh.
const MESH_SINK_M = 1.5;
// Resample when the focus drifts more than this from the current grid center,
// leaving a comfortable margin inside the ±6 km patch.
const RECENTER_M = 3_000;

export class LiveTerrain {
  readonly mesh: THREE.Mesh;
  /** Resolves once the datum and first heightfield are ready. */
  readonly ready: Promise<void>;

  /** Bumps whenever the heightfield changes; 0 means "no real heights yet". */
  get revision(): number {
    return this.rev;
  }

  private sampler: TerrainSampler | null = null;
  private heights: Float32Array | null = null; // (GRID+1)² by [iz][ix], relative to datum
  // 0 until the first heightfield lands, then bumped on every resample. Layers
  // that bake ground height into geometry (tunnels, tracks) watch this so they
  // never build against the flat placeholder and rebuild after a recenter.
  private rev = 0;
  private readonly center = new THREE.Vector2(0, 0); // world x/z the grid is centered on
  private resampling = false;

  constructor(private readonly projection: Projection) {
    const geometry = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(SPAN_M, SPAN_M, GRID, GRID);
    geometry.rotateX(-Math.PI / 2); // lie flat in the XZ plane, +y up
    // Match Explore's ground: a saturated green PBR surface (RouteLayer uses the
    // same 0x52764b), which reads far greener under the sun than a flat Lambert.
    this.mesh = new THREE.Mesh(geometry, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x52764b, roughness: 1 }));
    this.mesh.name = 'Live terrain relief';
    this.ready = this.init().catch((error) => {
      console.warn('Live 3D: terrain relief unavailable; falling back to flat ground.', error);
    });
  }

  /**
   * Terrain height (world y) at a world x/z, bilinearly interpolated. Returns 0
   * until the heightfield loads and outside the current grid patch, so callers
   * degrade gracefully to flat ground.
   */
  heightAt(x: number, z: number): number {
    const h = this.heights;
    if (!h) return 0;
    const fx = (x - this.center.x + SPAN_M / 2) / STEP_M;
    const fz = (z - this.center.y + SPAN_M / 2) / STEP_M;
    if (fx < 0 || fx > GRID || fz < 0 || fz > GRID) return 0;
    const ix = Math.min(GRID - 1, Math.floor(fx));
    const iz = Math.min(GRID - 1, Math.floor(fz));
    const tx = fx - ix;
    const tz = fz - iz;
    const row = GRID + 1;
    const h00 = h[iz * row + ix];
    const h10 = h[iz * row + ix + 1];
    const h01 = h[(iz + 1) * row + ix];
    const h11 = h[(iz + 1) * row + ix + 1];
    return THREE.MathUtils.lerp(THREE.MathUtils.lerp(h00, h10, tx), THREE.MathUtils.lerp(h01, h11, tx), tz);
  }

  /** Surface height for the OSM streamer; awaits the datum so tiles land on relief. */
  surfaceHeight = async (lat: number, lon: number): Promise<number> => {
    await this.ready;
    return this.sampler ? this.sampler.sample(lat, lon) : 0;
  };

  /** Recenters the grid on a world focus if it has drifted too far (call per frame). */
  update(focusX: number, focusZ: number): void {
    if (!this.sampler || this.resampling) return;
    if (Math.hypot(focusX - this.center.x, focusZ - this.center.y) < RECENTER_M) return;
    void this.resample(focusX, focusZ);
  }

  private async init(): Promise<void> {
    // Datum = elevation at the origin, so the origin sits at y ≈ 0 and every
    // recentered patch shares the same vertical reference as the OSM streamer.
    const probe = new TerrainSampler(0);
    const originElevation = await probe.sample(this.projection.origin.lat, this.projection.origin.lon);
    this.sampler = new TerrainSampler(originElevation);
    await this.resample(0, 0);
  }

  private async resample(cx: number, cz: number): Promise<void> {
    if (!this.sampler) return;
    this.resampling = true;
    try {
      const position = this.mesh.geometry.attributes.position as THREE.BufferAttribute;
      const count = position.count;
      // Local x/z are fixed; sample real elevation at (center + local) per vertex.
      const samples: Promise<number>[] = [];
      for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        const { lat, lon } = this.projection.unproject(cx + position.getX(i), cz + position.getZ(i));
        samples.push(this.sampler.sample(lat, lon));
      }
      const elevations = await Promise.all(samples);

      const row = GRID + 1;
      const heights = new Float32Array(row * row);
      for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        const elevation = elevations[i];
        position.setY(i, elevation);
        const ix = Math.round((position.getX(i) + SPAN_M / 2) / STEP_M);
        const iz = Math.round((position.getZ(i) + SPAN_M / 2) / STEP_M);
        if (ix >= 0 && ix <= GRID && iz >= 0 && iz <= GRID) heights[iz * row + ix] = elevation;
      }
      position.needsUpdate = true;
      this.mesh.geometry.computeVertexNormals();
      this.mesh.position.set(cx, -MESH_SINK_M, cz);
      this.heights = heights;
      this.center.set(cx, cz);
      this.rev++;
    } finally {
      this.resampling = false;
    }
  }
}

--- features/live/LiveTrackLayer.ts ---
import * as THREE from 'three';
import type { Projection } from './liveProjection';
import type { TrackedVehicle } from './LiveVehicleStore';
import type { LiveTunnelIndex } from './LiveTunnelIndex';
import { resampleTrackXZ, smoothTrackXZ } from './liveTrackGeometry';

// Overground running rails for the live rail network (Stadtbahn / S-Bahn / rail),
// so vehicles don't float on bare terrain. Mirrors the Explore GuidewayLayer look
// — a ballast strip with two steel rails — but derived from the live route feeds
// (via LiveTunnelIndex) and drawn only along non-tunnel segments. Built lazily and
// cached per route, and only once the camera is low enough to actually see rails.

// Below this altitude rails are worth drawing; above it they are sub-pixel and we
// skip the geometry entirely.
const BUILD_ALTITUDE_M = 1600;
const GAUGE_M = 1.5;
const RAIL_RADIUS_M = 0.12;
const BALLAST_HALF_WIDTH_M = 1.5;

// Segment de-duplication: many routes share the same physical alignment, and
// drawing each one stacks near-identical rails. A segment is skipped when a
// previously drawn one already runs within DEDUP_RADIUS_M at a similar heading.
// The radius stays under real double-track spacing (~4 m) so genuinely parallel
// tracks still both get drawn — only true duplicates collapse.
const DEDUP_CELL_M = 12; // spatial hash bucket (must exceed the radius)
const DEDUP_RADIUS_M = 3;
const DEDUP_ANGLE_RAD = (25 * Math.PI) / 180;
const DEDUP_SAMPLE_M = 6; // sample spacing along a segment
const DEDUP_COVER_RATIO = 0.6; // fraction of samples already covered to skip

type Route = { id: string; geoPoints?: [number, number][]; tunnelMask?: boolean[] };
type Occupancy = { x: number; z: number; ang: number };

export class LiveTrackLayer {
  readonly group = new THREE.Group();
  private readonly built = new Set<string>();
  // Spatial hash of already-drawn track samples, keyed by DEDUP_CELL_M bucket.
  private readonly occupancy = new Map<string, Occupancy[]>();
  private terrainRevision = -1;
  private readonly railMaterial = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x8a9095, metalness: 0.7, roughness: 0.3 });
  private readonly ballastMaterial = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x5c5a54, roughness: 0.96 });

  constructor(
    private readonly projection: Projection,
    private readonly index: LiveTunnelIndex,
    private readonly groundHeightAt: (x: number, z: number) => number
  ) {
    this.group.name = 'Live overground rails';
  }

  update(vehicles: TrackedVehicle[], altitudeM: number, terrainRevision: number): void {
    if (altitudeM > BUILD_ALTITUDE_M) return;
    // Rails bake in ground height, so skip the flat placeholder and rebuild once
    // the terrain resolves or recenters.
    if (terrainRevision === 0) return;
    if (terrainRevision !== this.terrainRevision) {
      this.terrainRevision = terrainRevision;
      this.clear();
    }
    for (const vehicle of vehicles) {
      const route = this.index.routeFor(vehicle) as Route | null;
      if (!route?.geoPoints || route.geoPoints.length < 2 || this.built.has(route.id)) continue;
      this.buildRoute(route);
      this.built.add(route.id);
    }
  }

  dispose(): void {
    this.group.traverse((object) => {
      const mesh = object as THREE.Mesh;
      mesh.geometry?.dispose?.();
    });
    this.railMaterial.dispose();
    this.ballastMaterial.dispose();
    this.group.removeFromParent();
  }

  private buildRoute(route: Route): void {
    const geo = route.geoPoints!;
    const mask = route.tunnelMask ?? [];
    // Project + smooth the whole route once. Smoothing preserves the point count,
    // so the tunnel mask below stays index-aligned.
    const smoothed = smoothTrackXZ(geo.map(([lat, lon]) => this.projection.project(lat, lon)));
    for (const [start, end] of overgroundRanges(mask, geo.length)) {
      if (end - start < 1) continue;
      // Resample this overground stretch to a smooth, denser centerline matching the
      // motion path, then bake rail height from the terrain per resampled point.
      const centers = resampleTrackXZ(smoothed.slice(start, end + 1));
      if (centers.length < 2) continue;
      for (const c of centers) c.y = this.groundHeightAt(c.x, c.z) + 0.12;
      // Drop stretches another route already laid rails on, then draw what's left
      // as contiguous runs.
      for (const run of this.claimRuns(centers)) this.buildRun(run);
    }
  }

  /** Drops all built rails so they can be rebuilt at corrected heights. */
  private clear(): void {
    this.group.traverse((object) => {
      const mesh = object as THREE.Mesh;
      mesh.geometry?.dispose?.();
    });
    this.group.clear();
    this.built.clear();
    this.occupancy.clear();
  }

  /** Rails + ballast for one contiguous stretch of centerline points. */
  private buildRun(centers: THREE.Vector3[]): void {
    const laterals = horizontalNormals(centers);
    const segments = Math.max(8, centers.length * 2);
    for (const sign of [-1, 1]) {
      const railPoints = centers.map((c, i) => c.clone().addScaledVector(laterals[i], (sign * GAUGE_M) / 2));
      const rail = new THREE.Mesh(
        new THREE.TubeGeometry(new THREE.CatmullRomCurve3(railPoints, false, 'centripetal'), segments, RAIL_RADIUS_M, 6, false),
        this.railMaterial
      );
      rail.name = 'Live rail';
      this.group.add(rail);
    }
    this.group.add(new THREE.Mesh(ribbonGeometry(centers, laterals, BALLAST_HALF_WIDTH_M, -0.06), this.ballastMaterial));
  }

  /**
   * Splits a centerline into runs of segments that are not already covered by
   * previously drawn track, claiming the ones it keeps. Duplicated stretches
   * become gaps, which the route that got there first already fills.
   */
  private claimRuns(centers: THREE.Vector3[]): THREE.Vector3[][] {
    const runs: THREE.Vector3[][] = [];
    let current: THREE.Vector3[] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < centers.length - 1; i++) {
      const a = centers[i];
      const b = centers[i + 1];
      if (this.segmentCovered(a, b)) {
        if (current.length >= 2) runs.push(current);
        current = [];
        continue;
      }
      this.claimSegment(a, b);
      if (current.length === 0) current.push(a);
      current.push(b);
    }
    if (current.length >= 2) runs.push(current);
    return runs;
  }

  private segmentCovered(a: THREE.Vector3, b: THREE.Vector3): boolean {
    let total = 0;
    let covered = 0;
    for (const s of sampleSegment(a, b)) {
      total++;
      if (this.sampleCovered(s.x, s.z, s.ang)) covered++;
    }
    return total > 0 && covered / total >= DEDUP_COVER_RATIO;
  }

  private claimSegment(a: THREE.Vector3, b: THREE.Vector3): void {
    for (const s of sampleSegment(a, b)) {
      const key = cellKey(s.x, s.z);
      const list = this.occupancy.get(key);
      if (list) list.push({ x: s.x, z: s.z, ang: s.ang });
      else this.occupancy.set(key, [{ x: s.x, z: s.z, ang: s.ang }]);
    }
  }

  private sampleCovered(x: number, z: number, ang: number): boolean {
    const cx = Math.floor(x / DEDUP_CELL_M);
    const cz = Math.floor(z / DEDUP_CELL_M);
    for (let dx = -1; dx <= 1; dx++) {
      for (let dz = -1; dz <= 1; dz++) {
        const list = this.occupancy.get(`${cx + dx}/${cz + dz}`);
        if (!list) continue;
        for (const o of list) {
          if (Math.hypot(o.x - x, o.z - z) <= DEDUP_RADIUS_M && angleClose(o.ang, ang)) return true;
        }
      }
    }
    return false;
  }
}

function cellKey(x: number, z: number): string {
  return `${Math.floor(x / DEDUP_CELL_M)}/${Math.floor(z / DEDUP_CELL_M)}`;
}

// Heading folded to [0, π): direction-agnostic, so the two directions of one
// physical track compare as the same alignment.
function foldAngle(dx: number, dz: number): number {
  let ang = Math.atan2(dz, dx);
  if (ang < 0) ang += Math.PI;
  return ang % Math.PI;
}

function angleClose(a: number, b: number): boolean {
  let d = Math.abs(a - b);
  if (d > Math.PI / 2) d = Math.PI - d;
  return d <= DEDUP_ANGLE_RAD;
}

/** Points every ~DEDUP_SAMPLE_M along a segment, with its folded heading. */
function sampleSegment(a: THREE.Vector3, b: THREE.Vector3): Array<{ x: number; z: number; ang: number }> {
  const dx = b.x - a.x;
  const dz = b.z - a.z;
  const len = Math.hypot(dx, dz);
  const ang = foldAngle(dx, dz);
  const steps = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(len / DEDUP_SAMPLE_M));
  const out: Array<{ x: number; z: number; ang: number }> = [];
  for (let i = 0; i <= steps; i++) {
    const t = i / steps;
    out.push({ x: a.x + dx * t, z: a.z + dz * t, ang });
  }
  return out;
}

// Index ranges [start, end] where the route is NOT in a tunnel. With no mask the
// whole route is overground.
function overgroundRanges(mask: boolean[], length: number): Array<[number, number]> {
  const result: Array<[number, number]> = [];
  let start = -1;
  for (let i = 0; i < length; i++) {
    const inTunnel = Boolean(mask[i]);
    if (!inTunnel && start < 0) start = i;
    if (inTunnel && start >= 0) {
      result.push([start, i - 1]);
      start = -1;
    }
  }
  if (start >= 0) result.push([start, length - 1]);
  return result;
}

// Per-point horizontal unit vector perpendicular to the track direction.
function horizontalNormals(points: THREE.Vector3[]): THREE.Vector3[] {
  return points.map((_, i) => {
    const a = points[Math.max(0, i - 1)];
    const b = points[Math.min(points.length - 1, i + 1)];
    const dir = new THREE.Vector3(b.x - a.x, 0, b.z - a.z);
    if (dir.lengthSq() < 1e-6) return new THREE.Vector3(1, 0, 0);
    dir.normalize();
    return new THREE.Vector3(dir.z, 0, -dir.x); // rotate 90° in the ground plane
  });
}

// A flat ribbon strip following the centerline, `halfWidth` to each side.
function ribbonGeometry(centers: THREE.Vector3[], laterals: THREE.Vector3[], halfWidth: number, yOffset: number): THREE.BufferGeometry {
  const vertices: number[] = [];
  const indices: number[] = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < centers.length; i++) {
    const c = centers[i];
    const l = laterals[i];
    const left = c.clone().addScaledVector(l, halfWidth);
    const right = c.clone().addScaledVector(l, -halfWidth);
    vertices.push(left.x, left.y + yOffset, left.z, right.x, right.y + yOffset, right.z);
    if (i < centers.length - 1) {
      const base = i * 2;
      indices.push(base, base + 2, base + 1, base + 1, base + 2, base + 3);
    }
  }
  const geometry = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
  geometry.setAttribute('position', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(vertices, 3));
  geometry.setIndex(indices);
  geometry.computeVertexNormals();
  return geometry;
}

--- features/live/LiveVehicleStore.ts ---
// Rendering-agnostic client-side vehicle store (concept §6.1 merge + §7 motion).
//
// Snapshots merge in keyed by runId; between snapshots `tick()` advances each
// vehicle by dead reckoning. The store knows nothing about Leaflet or Three.js —
// LiveMap (or a future 3D LiveTrafficSystem) reads `list()` and draws it. That
// separation is deliberate: the polling/merge/interpolation core is reusable by
// whichever renderer the app grows.

import { bearingDeg, easeTowards, haversineM, predict, type LatLon } from './interpolation';
import type { LiveVehicle, RenderableVehicle } from './liveTypes';

export type TrackedVehicle = {
  runId: string;
  data: LiveVehicle; // latest server payload (line/direction/delay/status/stops)
  server: LatLon; // latest server-reported position
  render: LatLon; // eased/dead-reckoned display position
  bearing: number; // degrees; server-provided when present, else inferred
  speedMps: number; // inferred from consecutive server fixes
  lastFixMs: number; // wall-clock ms of the latest server fix
  misses: number; // consecutive polls this run was absent from
};

// A run absent from this many consecutive polls is despawned; one miss is
// tolerated because bbox edges flicker (§6.1).
const MAX_MISSES = 2;

// Inferred-speed bounds. The backend positions are schedule estimates, so the
// speed we derive from consecutive fixes is noisy — especially around stops,
// where a stop→depart pair produces a spurious burst. We cap it to a plausible
// Stadtbahn/regional display speed and rate-limit how fast it can change so a bad
// fix can't fling the marker forward and then jerk it back (concept §7).
const MAX_INFERRED_SPEED_MPS = 35; // ~126 km/h; display cap, below the rail cap
const MAX_ACCEL_MPS2 = 1.4; // realistic light-rail acceleration
const MAX_BRAKE_MPS2 = 2.2; // realistic service braking

function fixTime(v: LiveVehicle, fallbackMs: number): number {
  if (v.estimatedAt) {
    const t = Date.parse(v.estimatedAt);
    if (Number.isFinite(t)) return t;
  }
  return fallbackMs;
}

export class LiveVehicleStore {
  private vehicles = new Map<string, TrackedVehicle>();

  clear(): void {
    this.vehicles.clear();
  }

  get size(): number {
    return this.vehicles.size;
  }

  get(runId: string): TrackedVehicle | undefined {
    return this.vehicles.get(runId);
  }

  list(): TrackedVehicle[] {
    return [...this.vehicles.values()];
  }

  /**
   * Merges a *complete* snapshot: spawns new runs, updates seen ones, and
   * despawns runs that have been absent too long or completed. Only this method
   * ages/removes unseen runs — a single-run patch must never touch other runs
   * (that was a bug: the 5 s follow poll despawned the rest of the fleet, churning
   * model pools and letting the camera latch onto the wrong vehicle).
   */
  applySnapshot(incoming: RenderableVehicle[], nowMs: number = Date.now()): void {
    const seen = new Set<string>();

    for (const v of incoming) {
      seen.add(v.runId);
      this.upsert(v, nowMs);
    }

    for (const [runId, tracked] of this.vehicles) {
      if (seen.has(runId)) continue;
      tracked.misses += 1;
      if (tracked.misses >= MAX_MISSES || tracked.data.status === 'completed') {
        this.vehicles.delete(runId);
      }
    }
  }

  /**
   * Spawns or updates a single run, recomputing its inferred (smoothed) speed and
   * bearing. Never ages or removes other runs. Stale fixes — an older estimate
   * arriving after a newer one (e.g. a slow follow response overtaken by a fresh
   * snapshot) — are ignored for motion so they can't rewind the vehicle.
   */
  upsert(v: RenderableVehicle, nowMs: number = Date.now()): void {
    const server: LatLon = { lat: v.lat, lon: v.lon };
    const fixMs = fixTime(v, nowMs);
    const existing = this.vehicles.get(v.runId);

    if (!existing) {
      this.vehicles.set(v.runId, {
        runId: v.runId,
        data: v,
        server,
        render: server,
        bearing: v.bearing ?? 0,
        speedMps: 0,
        lastFixMs: fixMs,
        misses: 0
      });
      return;
    }

    // Older-or-equal estimate: refresh metadata (delay/next stop/status) but leave
    // position and inferred speed untouched so we never move backward on it.
    if (fixMs <= existing.lastFixMs) {
      existing.data = v;
      existing.misses = 0;
      return;
    }

    const dtSec = (fixMs - existing.lastFixMs) / 1000;
    const movedM = haversineM(existing.server, server);
    // Prefer the server bearing; fall back to the heading between fixes when the
    // vehicle actually moved.
    const bearing = v.bearing ?? (movedM > 2 ? bearingDeg(existing.server, server) : existing.bearing);
    existing.data = v;
    existing.server = server;
    existing.speedMps = this.inferSpeed(existing.speedMps, movedM, dtSec, v.status);
    existing.bearing = bearing;
    existing.lastFixMs = fixMs;
    existing.misses = 0;
    // A large jump means our render lagged badly — snap on the next tick.
  }

  /** Removes a single run (e.g. the follow endpoint reports it finished). */
  remove(runId: string): void {
    this.vehicles.delete(runId);
  }

  // Turns a raw fix-to-fix displacement into a stable display speed. At a stop the
  // speed is forced to zero; otherwise the measurement is capped and rate-limited
  // by plausible acceleration/braking so a noisy schedule estimate can't produce
  // the "stop → sudden lurch → snap back" the raw movedM/dt did.
  private inferSpeed(prevMps: number, movedM: number, dtSec: number, status: LiveVehicle['status']): number {
    if (status === 'at_stop' || status === 'not_started') return 0;
    if (dtSec <= 0) return prevMps;
    const measured = Math.max(0, Math.min(movedM / dtSec, MAX_INFERRED_SPEED_MPS));
    const lower = Math.max(0, prevMps - MAX_BRAKE_MPS2 * dtSec);
    const upper = prevMps + MAX_ACCEL_MPS2 * dtSec;
    return Math.max(lower, Math.min(measured, upper));
  }

  /** Patches a single run from the per-run endpoint while following (§6.2). */
  patchOne(v: LiveVehicle, nowMs: number = Date.now()): void {
    if (!v.runId || typeof v.lat !== 'number' || typeof v.lon !== 'number') return;
    this.upsert(v as RenderableVehicle, nowMs);
  }

  /** Advances every vehicle's render position by `dtSec` (called each frame). */
  tick(dtSec: number, nowMs: number = Date.now()): void {
    for (const tracked of this.vehicles.values()) {
      const status = tracked.data.status;
      if (status === 'at_stop' || status === 'not_started') {
        // Hold at the stop; still ease out any residual error.
        tracked.render = easeTowards(tracked.render, tracked.server, dtSec);
        continue;
      }
      const secondsSinceFix = (nowMs - tracked.lastFixMs) / 1000;
      const target = predict(tracked.server, tracked.bearing, tracked.speedMps, secondsSinceFix);
      tracked.render = easeTowards(tracked.render, target, dtSec);
    }
  }
}

--- features/live/viewport.ts ---
// Viewport → request math (concept §5). Pure, side-effect free, and unit-tested:
// these functions decide *what area* we ask for, *which modes*, and *how often*,
// which is the core of the "don't burn traffic" requirement.

import { ALL_LIVE_MODES, RAIL_MODES, type LiveMode } from './liveTypes';

export type BBox = { latMin: number; lonMin: number; latMax: number; lonMax: number };

// Three level-of-detail tiers, driven by map zoom (the Leaflet analogue of the
// concept's camera-altitude bands).
export type Tier = 'city' | 'district' | 'street';
