// Leaflet zoom thresholds. Below district → city overview; at/above street → street.
const DISTRICT_MIN_ZOOM = 12;
const STREET_MIN_ZOOM = 15;

/** Classifies a Leaflet zoom level into an LOD tier. */
export function tierForZoom(zoom: number): Tier {
  if (zoom >= STREET_MIN_ZOOM) return 'street';
  if (zoom >= DISTRICT_MIN_ZOOM) return 'district';
  return 'city';
}

/** Poll cadence per tier (§5.2): smaller viewport ⇒ fewer vehicles ⇒ poll more often. */
export function pollIntervalMs(tier: Tier): number {
  switch (tier) {
    case 'city':
      return 30_000;
    case 'district':
      return 15_000;
    case 'street':
      return 8_000;
  }
}

// Rough width of a bbox in km at its mid-latitude (1° lat ≈ 111 km).
export function bboxWidthKm(bbox: BBox): number {
  const midLat = (bbox.latMin + bbox.latMax) / 2;
  return Math.abs(bbox.lonMax - bbox.lonMin) * 111 * Math.cos((midLat * Math.PI) / 180);
}

export function bboxHeightKm(bbox: BBox): number {
  return Math.abs(bbox.latMax - bbox.latMin) * 111;
}

// Buses are only requested once the visible area is small enough that their
// count is bounded (§5.2).
const BUS_MAX_SPAN_KM = 6;

/**
 * The modes to request for a tier and area, intersected with the user's filter
 * chips (which can only ever *narrow* the automatic set).
 */
export function modesForTier(tier: Tier, bbox: BBox, userFilter?: Iterable<LiveMode>): LiveMode[] {
  let auto: LiveMode[];
  if (tier === 'street') {
    auto = [...ALL_LIVE_MODES];
  } else if (tier === 'district') {
    const compact = bboxWidthKm(bbox) < BUS_MAX_SPAN_KM && bboxHeightKm(bbox) < BUS_MAX_SPAN_KM;
    auto = compact ? [...ALL_LIVE_MODES] : [...RAIL_MODES];
  } else {
    auto = [...RAIL_MODES];
  }
  if (!userFilter) return auto;
  const allowed = new Set(userFilter);
  const narrowed = auto.filter((mode) => allowed.has(mode));
  // The API requires a non-empty modes list; if the user filtered everything out
  // of the auto set we still send the auto set (the chips gray out instead).
  return narrowed.length ? narrowed : auto;
}

/** Expands a bbox by `margin` on each side (fraction of its span). */
export function expandBBox(bbox: BBox, margin = 0.25): BBox {
  const latPad = (bbox.latMax - bbox.latMin) * margin;
  const lonPad = (bbox.lonMax - bbox.lonMin) * margin;
  return {
    latMin: bbox.latMin - latPad,
    lonMin: bbox.lonMin - lonPad,
    latMax: bbox.latMax + latPad,
    lonMax: bbox.lonMax + lonPad
  };
}

/**
 * Snaps a bbox out to a coordinate grid so small pans produce byte-identical
 * request URLs (§5.1) — cache-friendly and easy to dedupe.
 */
export function quantizeBBox(bbox: BBox, grid = 0.01): BBox {
  return {
    latMin: Math.floor(bbox.latMin / grid) * grid,
    lonMin: Math.floor(bbox.lonMin / grid) * grid,
    latMax: Math.ceil(bbox.latMax / grid) * grid,
    lonMax: Math.ceil(bbox.lonMax / grid) * grid
  };
}

/** The request bbox for a visible viewport: margin then quantize. */
export function requestBBox(visible: BBox, margin = 0.25, grid = 0.01): BBox {
  return quantizeBBox(expandBBox(visible, margin), grid);
}

/** True when `inner` lies fully inside `outer` (used for pan hysteresis §5.1). */
export function bboxContains(outer: BBox, inner: BBox): boolean {
  return (
    inner.latMin >= outer.latMin &&
    inner.lonMin >= outer.lonMin &&
    inner.latMax <= outer.latMax &&
    inner.lonMax <= outer.lonMax
  );
}

export function bboxEquals(a: BBox, b: BBox, epsilon = 1e-9): boolean {
  return (
    Math.abs(a.latMin - b.latMin) < epsilon &&
    Math.abs(a.lonMin - b.lonMin) < epsilon &&
    Math.abs(a.latMax - b.latMax) < epsilon &&
    Math.abs(a.lonMax - b.lonMax) < epsilon
  );
}

--- features/live/LiveTunnelIndex.ts ---
import type { DemoRoute, GtfsRouteFeed } from '../../route';
import { haversineM } from './interpolation';
import type { TrackedVehicle } from './LiveVehicleStore';

type TunnelRoute = Pick<DemoRoute, 'id' | 'line' | 'routeId' | 'shapeId' | 'geoPoints' | 'tunnelMask'>;

type CachedMatch = {
  lat: number;
  lon: number;
  checkedAt: number;
  route: TunnelRoute | null;
  inTunnel: boolean;
};

// Once a run is matched to an alignment we keep it: rematching every refresh lets
// the "closest" route flip between parallel/overlapping tracks (shared central
// tunnels, terminals, crossings), which teleports a followed vehicle across the
// map. We only re-evaluate when the shapeId changes, or the run has stayed farther
// than this from its locked route for several consecutive checks.
const ROUTE_LOCK_M = 80;
const ROUTE_UNLOCK_MISSES = 3;

type RouteLock = { route: TunnelRoute; shapeId?: string; consecutiveFar: number };

const RAIL_FILES = {
  'light-rail': '/data/routes-stadtbahn.json',
  tram: '/data/routes-stadtbahn.json',
  's-bahn': '/data/routes-s-bahn.json',
  rail: '/data/routes-s-bahn.json'
} as const;

/**
 * Lazily resolves live rail vehicles against the already-generated GTFS route
 * packages. It deliberately loads only the small rail feeds that are needed for
 * a visible vehicle; buses have no tunnel treatment in the Live view.
 */
export class LiveTunnelIndex {
  private readonly routes: TunnelRoute[] = [];
  private readonly loading = new Map<string, Promise<void>>();
  private readonly matches = new Map<string, CachedMatch>();
  private readonly locks = new Map<string, RouteLock>();
  private revision = 0;

  get version(): number {
    return this.revision;
  }

  isInTunnel(vehicle: TrackedVehicle): boolean {
    const file = RAIL_FILES[vehicle.data.mode as keyof typeof RAIL_FILES];
    if (!file) return false;
    void this.ensure(file);

    const now = performance.now();
    const cached = this.matches.get(vehicle.runId);
    if (
      cached &&
      now - cached.checkedAt < 500 &&
      haversineM(cached, vehicle.render) < 25
    ) {
      return cached.inTunnel;
    }

    const route = this.matchRoute(vehicle);
    const inTunnel = route ? this.pointIsInTunnel(route, vehicle.render) : false;
    this.matches.set(vehicle.runId, {
      lat: vehicle.render.lat,
      lon: vehicle.render.lon,
      checkedAt: now,
      route,
      inTunnel
    });
    return inTunnel;
  }

  routeFor(vehicle: TrackedVehicle): TunnelRoute | null {
    this.isInTunnel(vehicle); // starts the lazy load and refreshes the cache
    return this.matches.get(vehicle.runId)?.route ?? null;
  }

  private async ensure(file: string): Promise<void> {
    let load = this.loading.get(file);
    if (!load) {
      load = fetch(file)
        .then(async (response) => {
          if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`Route feed request failed: ${response.status}`);
          const feed = (await response.json()) as GtfsRouteFeed;
          // Keep every rail alignment with geometry (not only tunnelled ones): the
          // same index feeds the overground track layer and the tunnel layer.
          this.routes.push(...(feed.routes ?? []).filter((route) => route.geoPoints?.length));
          this.revision++;
        })
        .catch((error) => console.warn('Live 3D: could not load tunnel route data.', error));
      this.loading.set(file, load);
    }
    await load;
  }

  private matchRoute(vehicle: TrackedVehicle): TunnelRoute | null {
    const v = vehicle.data;

    // Keep a previously locked alignment unless the shapeId changed or the run has
    // drifted away from it for several consecutive checks — otherwise the closest
    // route can flip between parallel tracks and fling a followed vehicle away.
    const lock = this.locks.get(vehicle.runId);
    if (lock && lock.shapeId === v.shapeId) {
      if (this.routeDistanceM(lock.route, vehicle.render) <= ROUTE_LOCK_M) {
        lock.consecutiveFar = 0;
        return lock.route;
      }
      if (++lock.consecutiveFar < ROUTE_UNLOCK_MISSES) return lock.route;
    }

    // Cascade shapeId → routeId → line: the live TRIAS feed's shapeIds usually
    // don't match the GTFS route packages, so most vehicles fall through to a
    // line match. The closest-alignment pick below then resolves direction.
    let candidates: TunnelRoute[] = [];
    if (v.shapeId) candidates = this.routes.filter((route) => route.shapeId === v.shapeId);
    if (!candidates.length && v.routeId) candidates = this.routes.filter((route) => route.routeId === v.routeId);
    if (!candidates.length && v.line) candidates = this.routes.filter((route) => route.line === v.line);
    if (!candidates.length) {
      this.locks.delete(vehicle.runId);
      return null;
    }

    // A route id/line can have both directions. Pick the alignment closest to
    // the live fix, which also handles feeds whose shapeId is not exposed.
    const best = candidates.reduce((closest, route) =>
      this.routeDistanceM(route, vehicle.render) < this.routeDistanceM(closest, vehicle.render) ? route : closest
    );
    this.locks.set(vehicle.runId, { route: best, shapeId: v.shapeId, consecutiveFar: 0 });
    return best;
  }

  private pointIsInTunnel(route: TunnelRoute, point: { lat: number; lon: number }): boolean {
    const points = route.geoPoints;
    const mask = route.tunnelMask;
    if (!points?.length || !mask?.length) return false;
    let nearest = 0;
    let distance = Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
    for (let i = 0; i < points.length; i++) {
      const d = haversineM({ lat: points[i][0], lon: points[i][1] }, point);
      if (d < distance) {
        distance = d;
        nearest = i;
      }
    }
    return Boolean(mask[nearest] || mask[nearest - 1] || mask[nearest + 1]);
  }

  private routeDistanceM(route: TunnelRoute, point: { lat: number; lon: number }): number {
    const points = route.geoPoints ?? [];
    let nearest = Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
    for (const [lat, lon] of points) nearest = Math.min(nearest, haversineM({ lat, lon }, point));
    return nearest;
  }
}

--- features/live/LiveMap.tsx ---
import L from 'leaflet';
import 'leaflet/dist/leaflet.css';
import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import type { LiveVehicleStore, TrackedVehicle } from './LiveVehicleStore';
import { delayLevel, modeColor } from './liveTypes';
import type { BBox } from './viewport';

// Whole-VVS overview: the S-Bahn bounding box, city tier by default (§2.2).
const STUTTGART_CENTER: [number, number] = [48.7827, 9.18];
const INITIAL_ZOOM = 11;
// Line badges fade in below the mid-altitude band (§2.3); on the map that is
// district zoom and closer.
const BADGE_MIN_ZOOM = 13;

function boundsToBBox(b: L.LatLngBounds): BBox {
  return { latMin: b.getSouth(), lonMin: b.getWest(), latMax: b.getNorth(), lonMax: b.getEast() };
}

function markerHtml(v: TrackedVehicle): string {
  const color = modeColor(v.data.mode);
  const planned = v.data.source === 'schedule';
  const delay = delayLevel(v.data.delaySeconds);
  const line = v.data.line ?? '';
  return (
    `<span class="lv-arrow" style="--c:${color}"></span>` +
    `<span class="lv-badge lv-delay-${delay}">${escapeHtml(line)}</span>` +
    (planned ? '<span class="lv-planned" title="Scheduled position"></span>' : '')
  );
}

function escapeHtml(s: string): string {
  return s.replace(/[&<>"]/g, (c) => ({ '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;' })[c] as string);
}

/**
 * Full-screen Leaflet map that renders the live vehicle store. It reports its
 * viewport up (for tier/bbox polling) and animates markers from the store every
 * frame — the store owns interpolation, this component only paints it.
 */
export function LiveMap({
  store,
  onViewportChange,
  onSelect,
  selectedRunId,
  followRunId,
  initialView
}: {
  store: LiveVehicleStore;
  onViewportChange: (bbox: BBox, zoom: number) => void;
  onSelect: (runId: string | null) => void;
  selectedRunId: string | null;
  followRunId: string | null;
  /** Restores map center/zoom when returning from the 3D view. */
  initialView?: { lat: number; lon: number; zoom: number };
}) {
  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const mapRef = useRef<L.Map | null>(null);
  const markers = useRef(new Map<string, L.Marker>());
  const bearings = useRef(new Map<string, number>());
  const rafRef = useRef<number | null>(null);
  const lastTimeRef = useRef<number>(0);

  // Latest prop values read inside the imperative loop without re-subscribing.
  const onViewportRef = useRef(onViewportChange);
  onViewportRef.current = onViewportChange;
  const onSelectRef = useRef(onSelect);
  onSelectRef.current = onSelect;
  const selectedRef = useRef(selectedRunId);
  selectedRef.current = selectedRunId;
  const followRef = useRef(followRunId);
  followRef.current = followRunId;

  // Create the map once and drive the animation loop for its lifetime.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!containerRef.current) return;
    const startCenter: [number, number] = initialView ? [initialView.lat, initialView.lon] : STUTTGART_CENTER;
    const startZoom = initialView ? initialView.zoom : INITIAL_ZOOM;
    const map = L.map(containerRef.current, { zoomControl: true, attributionControl: true, preferCanvas: true }).setView(
      startCenter,
      startZoom
    );
    L.tileLayer('https://{s}.tile.openstreetmap.org/{z}/{x}/{y}.png', {
      maxZoom: 19,
      attribution: '&copy; OpenStreetMap contributors'
    }).addTo(map);
    mapRef.current = map;

    const report = () => onViewportRef.current(boundsToBBox(map.getBounds()), map.getZoom());
    map.on('moveend', report);
    map.on('zoomend', report);
    map.on('click', () => onSelectRef.current(null));
    // Leaflet mis-measures a container that just mounted; settle then report.
    const raf = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      map.invalidateSize();
      report();
    });

    const frame = (time: number) => {
      const dt = lastTimeRef.current ? Math.min(0.1, (time - lastTimeRef.current) / 1000) : 0.016;
      lastTimeRef.current = time;
      store.tick(dt);
      reconcile(map);
      // Follow mode keeps the camera locked to the vehicle (chase framing §2.4).
      const followed = followRef.current ? store.get(followRef.current) : undefined;
      if (followed) map.setView([followed.render.lat, followed.render.lon], map.getZoom(), { animate: false });
      rafRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(frame);
    };
    rafRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(frame);

    return () => {
      cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
      if (rafRef.current) cancelAnimationFrame(rafRef.current);
      rafRef.current = null;
      lastTimeRef.current = 0;
      markers.current.clear();
      bearings.current.clear();
      map.remove();
      mapRef.current = null;
    };
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [store]);

  // Restyle selection/follow highlight when it changes (loop reads the refs too,
  // but this makes the change immediate without waiting a frame).
  useEffect(() => {
    for (const [runId, marker] of markers.current) applyHighlight(marker, runId, selectedRunId, followRunId);
  }, [selectedRunId, followRunId]);

  function reconcile(map: L.Map) {
    const showBadges = map.getZoom() >= BADGE_MIN_ZOOM;
    const live = new Set<string>();

    for (const v of store.list()) {
      live.add(v.runId);
      let marker = markers.current.get(v.runId);
      if (!marker) {
        marker = L.marker([v.render.lat, v.render.lon], {
          icon: L.divIcon({ className: 'lv-icon', html: markerHtml(v), iconSize: [26, 26], iconAnchor: [13, 13] }),
          keyboard: false,
          riseOnHover: true
        });
        marker.on('click', (e) => {
          L.DomEvent.stop(e);
          onSelectRef.current(v.runId);
        });
        marker.addTo(map);
        markers.current.set(v.runId, marker);
        applyHighlight(marker, v.runId, selectedRef.current, followRef.current);
      } else {
        marker.setLatLng([v.render.lat, v.render.lon]);
      }
      updateMarkerElement(marker, v, showBadges);
    }

    // Despawn markers whose runs left the store.
    for (const [runId, marker] of markers.current) {
      if (live.has(runId)) continue;
      marker.remove();
      markers.current.delete(runId);
      bearings.current.delete(runId);
    }
  }

  function updateMarkerElement(marker: L.Marker, v: TrackedVehicle, showBadges: boolean) {
    const el = marker.getElement();
    if (!el) return;
    const arrow = el.querySelector<HTMLElement>('.lv-arrow');
    if (arrow) {
      const prev = bearings.current.get(v.runId);
      // Only touch the DOM when the heading actually changed.
      if (prev === undefined || Math.abs(prev - v.bearing) > 1) {
        arrow.style.transform = `rotate(${v.bearing}deg)`;
        bearings.current.set(v.runId, v.bearing);
      }
    }
    const badge = el.querySelector<HTMLElement>('.lv-badge');
    if (badge) badge.style.display = showBadges && v.data.line ? '' : 'none';
  }

  return (
    <div className="lv-map">
      <div ref={containerRef} style={{ width: '100%', height: '100%' }} />
    </div>
  );
}

function applyHighlight(marker: L.Marker, runId: string, selected: string | null, followed: string | null) {
  const el = marker.getElement();
  if (!el) return;
  el.classList.toggle('lv-selected', runId === selected);
  el.classList.toggle('lv-followed', runId === followed);
}

--- features/live/LiveTrackMotion.ts ---
import type { Projection } from './liveProjection';
import type { TrackedVehicle } from './LiveVehicleStore';
import type { LiveTunnelIndex } from './LiveTunnelIndex';
import { buildSmoothTrackXZ } from './liveTrackGeometry';

// Along-track motion for matched rail vehicles (concept §7 "along-track dead
// reckoning"). Instead of straight-line extrapolation — which drifts off curved
// track between polls and then snaps/jumps (sometimes backward) on the next
// response — each vehicle is projected onto its route polyline and advanced ALONG
// it at the inferred speed. Corrections on a new fix are forward-biased, so the
// vehicle stays on the rails and glides instead of teleporting.

const DEG = Math.PI / 180;
// Beyond this lateral distance the vehicle clearly isn't on this alignment, so we
// fall back to the raw server position rather than snapping to the wrong track.
const MAX_LATERAL_M = 45;
// Along-track error above which we hard-snap (e.g. a run that was off-screen, or a
// confirmed re-acquisition). Below it we correct with speed, never by moving the
// position — a followed vehicle makes even a small backward jump very visible.
const SNAP_ERR_M = 250;
const MAX_SPEED_MPS = 45;
// A behind-schedule vehicle is caught up by adding at most this much to its
// display speed, spread over the correction horizon; ahead-of-schedule it slows
// (down to a pause) but never reverses.
const MAX_CATCH_UP_MPS = 6;
const CORRECTION_HORIZON_S = 4;

type Geom = { xs: Float64Array; zs: Float64Array; cum: Float64Array; total: number };
// `corrMps` is the current along-track speed correction (+ catch up, − ease off),
// recomputed on each server fix and held until the next one.
type State = { s: number; lastFixMs: number; seg: number; dir: 1 | -1; serverS: number; corrMps: number };
type Resolved = { lat: number; lon: number; bearing: number };

export class LiveTrackMotion {
  private readonly geoms = new Map<string, Geom>(); // by route id
  private readonly states = new Map<string, State>(); // by runId
  private readonly out = new Map<string, Resolved>(); // by runId
  private readonly runGeom = new Map<string, Geom>(); // by runId — for offset queries

  constructor(
    private readonly projection: Projection,
    private readonly index: LiveTunnelIndex
  ) {}

  /** Advances every matched vehicle along its route; call once per frame. */
  tick(vehicles: TrackedVehicle[], dtSec: number): void {
    const seen = new Set<string>();
    for (const v of vehicles) {
      const route = this.index.routeFor(v);
      if (!route?.geoPoints || route.geoPoints.length < 2) continue;
      const geom = this.geomFor(route.id, route.geoPoints);
      const resolved = this.advance(v, geom, dtSec);
      if (resolved) {
        this.out.set(v.runId, resolved);
        this.runGeom.set(v.runId, geom);
        seen.add(v.runId);
      }
    }
    for (const id of [...this.out.keys()]) {
      if (!seen.has(id)) {
        this.out.delete(id);
        this.states.delete(id);
        this.runGeom.delete(id);
      }
    }
  }

  /** Snapped position + track-aligned bearing for a run, if it is on a route. */
  positionOf(runId: string): Resolved | undefined {
    return this.out.get(runId);
  }

  /**
   * World-space pose (projected x/z + bearing) at `offsetM` along the run's track
   * from its current position — positive ahead, negative behind travel. Used to
   * articulate a model's cars so the train bends through curves. Returns null when
   * the run isn't currently matched to a route.
   */
  offsetPoseOf(runId: string, offsetM: number): { x: number; z: number; bearing: number } | null {
    const st = this.states.get(runId);
    const geom = this.runGeom.get(runId);
    if (!st || !geom) return null;
    const s = Math.max(0, Math.min(st.s + st.dir * offsetM, geom.total));
    return this.pointAtWorld(geom, s, st.dir);
  }

  private advance(v: TrackedVehicle, geom: Geom, dtSec: number): Resolved | null {
    const server = this.projection.project(v.server.lat, v.server.lon);
    let st = this.states.get(v.runId);
    const near = this.project(geom, server.x, server.z, st?.seg ?? -1);
    if (near.lateral > MAX_LATERAL_M) {
      this.states.delete(v.runId);
      return null;
    }
    if (!st) {
      st = { s: near.s, lastFixMs: v.lastFixMs, seg: near.seg, dir: 1, serverS: near.s, corrMps: 0 };
      this.states.set(v.runId, st);
    }
    st.seg = near.seg;

    // On a fresh server fix: infer travel direction from how the projected
    // along-track distance progressed (the matched shape may run either way),
    // then decide how to reconcile with the server position. Large errors hard-snap
    // (off-screen run, re-acquisition); ordinary errors are corrected by nudging
    // the *display speed* toward closing the gap over CORRECTION_HORIZON_S — the
    // vehicle speeds up when behind and eases off when ahead, but never reverses.
    if (v.lastFixMs !== st.lastFixMs) {
      st.lastFixMs = v.lastFixMs;
      const progressed = near.s - st.serverS;
      if (Math.abs(progressed) > 1) st.dir = progressed > 0 ? 1 : -1;
      st.serverS = near.s;
      const err = near.s - st.s;
      if (Math.abs(err) > SNAP_ERR_M) {
        st.s = near.s;
        st.corrMps = 0;
      } else {
        // Signed along the travel direction: positive => client is behind.
        const behind = st.dir * err;
        const base = Math.max(0, Math.min(v.speedMps, MAX_SPEED_MPS));
        st.corrMps = Math.max(-base, Math.min(behind / CORRECTION_HORIZON_S, MAX_CATCH_UP_MPS));
      }
    }

    const base = Math.max(0, Math.min(v.speedMps, MAX_SPEED_MPS));
    const displayMps = Math.max(0, base + st.corrMps);
    st.s += st.dir * displayMps * dtSec;
    st.s = Math.max(0, Math.min(st.s, geom.total));
    return this.pointAt(geom, st.s, st.dir);
  }

  private geomFor(id: string, geoPoints: [number, number][]): Geom {
    const cached = this.geoms.get(id);
    if (cached) return cached;
    // Smooth + resample the raw GTFS shape to the same centerline the rails use, so
    // the vehicle glides along curves instead of stepping between straight segments.
    const projected = geoPoints.map(([lat, lon]) => this.projection.project(lat, lon));
    const points = buildSmoothTrackXZ(projected);
    const n = points.length;
    const xs = new Float64Array(n);
    const zs = new Float64Array(n);
    const cum = new Float64Array(n);
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      xs[i] = points[i].x;
      zs[i] = points[i].z;
      if (i > 0) cum[i] = cum[i - 1] + Math.hypot(xs[i] - xs[i - 1], zs[i] - zs[i - 1]);
    }
    const geom: Geom = { xs, zs, cum, total: cum[n - 1] };
    this.geoms.set(id, geom);
    return geom;
  }

  // Nearest point on the polyline to (x,z): along-track distance, lateral offset,
  // and segment index. Searches near the cached segment first for speed.
  private project(geom: Geom, x: number, z: number, hintSeg: number): { s: number; lateral: number; seg: number } {
    const { xs, zs, cum } = geom;
    const n = xs.length;
    let best = { s: 0, lateral: Infinity, seg: 0 };
    const consider = (i: number) => {
      const ax = xs[i];
      const az = zs[i];
      const vx = xs[i + 1] - ax;
      const vz = zs[i + 1] - az;
      const l2 = vx * vx + vz * vz || 1;
      const t = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, ((x - ax) * vx + (z - az) * vz) / l2));
      const lateral = Math.hypot(x - (ax + vx * t), z - (az + vz * t));
      if (lateral < best.lateral) best = { s: cum[i] + Math.sqrt(l2) * t, lateral, seg: i };
    };
    if (hintSeg >= 0) {
      for (let i = Math.max(0, hintSeg - 8); i <= Math.min(n - 2, hintSeg + 8); i++) consider(i);
      if (best.lateral <= MAX_LATERAL_M) return best;
    }
    best = { s: 0, lateral: Infinity, seg: 0 };
    for (let i = 0; i < n - 1; i++) consider(i);
    return best;
  }

  private pointAt(geom: Geom, s: number, dir: 1 | -1): Resolved {
    const { x, z, bearing } = this.pointAtWorld(geom, s, dir);
    const { lat, lon } = this.projection.unproject(x, z);
    return { lat, lon, bearing };
  }

  // Projected world x/z and track-aligned bearing at along-track distance `s`.
  private pointAtWorld(geom: Geom, s: number, dir: 1 | -1): { x: number; z: number; bearing: number } {
    const { xs, zs, cum } = geom;
    const n = xs.length;
    let lo = 0;
    let hi = n - 1;
    while (lo + 1 < hi) {
      const mid = (lo + hi) >> 1;
      if (cum[mid] <= s) lo = mid;
      else hi = mid;
    }
    const segLen = cum[lo + 1] - cum[lo] || 1;
    const t = (s - cum[lo]) / segLen;
    const dx = xs[lo + 1] - xs[lo];
    const dz = zs[lo + 1] - zs[lo];
    // Bearing that matches the model rotation convention (rotate.y = -bearing):
    // world forward (dx,dz) corresponds to (-sinβ, cosβ), so β = atan2(-dx, dz).
    // Flip 180° when the vehicle runs against the polyline's stored direction.
    const bearing = ((Math.atan2(-dx * dir, dz * dir) / DEG) + 360) % 360;
    return { x: xs[lo] + dx * t, z: zs[lo] + dz * t, bearing };
  }
}

--- features/live/liveTrackGeometry.ts ---
import * as THREE from 'three';

// Shared smoothing for live rail geometry. The GTFS-derived shape points the live
// feed matches against are sparse and slightly jagged, so raw polylines read as a
// chain of straight segments with visible kinks at each vertex. Explore avoids this
// by pre-smoothing its control points and fitting a centripetal Catmull-Rom curve
// (see createRouteCurve in route.ts). We mirror that here so the live rails, ballast
// and the vehicle motion path all follow the same smooth centerline.

/**
 * Two light averaging passes over the XZ of a polyline, endpoints fixed. Reduces
 * jaggedness without changing the point count (so a parallel tunnel mask stays
 * index-aligned) or the Y values (terrain height is applied separately).
 */
export function smoothTrackXZ(points: THREE.Vector3[], passes = 2): THREE.Vector3[] {
  let pts = points.map((p) => p.clone());
  for (let pass = 0; pass < passes; pass++) {
    pts = pts.map((p, i, src) => {
      if (i === 0 || i === src.length - 1) return p.clone();
      const x = src[i - 1].x * 0.2 + p.x * 0.6 + src[i + 1].x * 0.2;
      const z = src[i - 1].z * 0.2 + p.z * 0.6 + src[i + 1].z * 0.2;
      return new THREE.Vector3(x, p.y, z);
    });
  }
  return pts;
}

/**
 * Resamples a polyline along a centripetal Catmull-Rom curve at roughly `spacingM`
 * intervals, rounding off corners. Returns at least the original density. With
 * fewer than 3 points there is nothing to curve, so the input is returned as-is.
 */
export function resampleTrackXZ(points: THREE.Vector3[], spacingM = 5): THREE.Vector3[] {
  if (points.length < 3) return points.map((p) => p.clone());
  const curve = new THREE.CatmullRomCurve3(
    points.map((p) => p.clone()),
    false,
    'centripetal'
  );
  const length = curve.getLength();
  const count = Math.max(points.length - 1, Math.ceil(length / spacingM));
  return curve.getSpacedPoints(count);
}

/** Explore-style smoothing: light averaging then a resampled Catmull-Rom. */
export function buildSmoothTrackXZ(points: THREE.Vector3[], spacingM = 5): THREE.Vector3[] {
  return resampleTrackXZ(smoothTrackXZ(points), spacingM);
}

--- features/live/LiveTrackLayer.test.ts ---
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { LiveTrackLayer } from './LiveTrackLayer';
import { makeProjection } from './liveProjection';
import type { LiveTunnelIndex } from './LiveTunnelIndex';
import type { TrackedVehicle } from './LiveVehicleStore';

const ORIGIN = { lat: 48.78, lon: 9.18 };

type Route = { id: string; geoPoints: [number, number][]; tunnelMask?: boolean[] };

// A straight north-south alignment, optionally shifted east by `lonOffset`.
function line(id: string, lonOffset = 0): Route {
  const geoPoints: [number, number][] = [];
  for (let i = 0; i <= 10; i++) geoPoints.push([ORIGIN.lat + i * 0.0005, ORIGIN.lon + lonOffset]);
  return { id, geoPoints };
}

function makeLayer(routes: Record<string, Route>) {
  const index = { routeFor: (v: TrackedVehicle) => routes[v.runId] ?? null } as unknown as LiveTunnelIndex;
  return new LiveTrackLayer(makeProjection(ORIGIN), index, () => 0);
}

const vehicle = (runId: string) => ({ runId }) as unknown as TrackedVehicle;

describe('LiveTrackLayer de-duplication', () => {
  it('draws track for the first route on an alignment', () => {
    const layer = makeLayer({ a: line('a') });
    layer.update([vehicle('a')], 500, 1);
    expect(layer.group.children.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });

  it('does not redraw a second route sharing the same alignment', () => {
    const routes = { a: line('a'), b: line('b') };
    const layer = makeLayer(routes);
    layer.update([vehicle('a')], 500, 1);
    const afterFirst = layer.group.children.length;
    layer.update([vehicle('b')], 500, 1);
    expect(layer.group.children.length).toBe(afterFirst);
  });

  it('still draws a genuinely separate parallel track', () => {
    // ~73 m east — far beyond the de-dup radius, so it is real second infrastructure.
    const routes = { a: line('a'), far: line('far', 0.001) };
    const layer = makeLayer(routes);
    layer.update([vehicle('a')], 500, 1);
    const afterFirst = layer.group.children.length;
    layer.update([vehicle('far')], 500, 1);
    expect(layer.group.children.length).toBeGreaterThan(afterFirst);
  });

  it('skips building entirely above the altitude cutoff', () => {
    const layer = makeLayer({ a: line('a') });
    layer.update([vehicle('a')], 5000, 1);
    expect(layer.group.children.length).toBe(0);
  });

  it('does not build against the flat terrain placeholder (revision 0)', () => {
    // Ground height is baked into the rails, so building before the heightfield
    // lands would strand them at y=0 while the train rides real terrain.
    const layer = makeLayer({ a: line('a') });
    layer.update([vehicle('a')], 500, 0);
    expect(layer.group.children.length).toBe(0);
    layer.update([vehicle('a')], 500, 1);
    expect(layer.group.children.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });

  it('rebuilds at corrected heights when the terrain revision changes', () => {
    const layer = makeLayer({ a: line('a') });
    layer.update([vehicle('a')], 500, 1);
    const afterFirst = layer.group.children.length;
    // A recenter changes ground heights, so the route is rebuilt rather than
    // left stale — same geometry count, freshly placed.
    layer.update([vehicle('a')], 500, 2);
    expect(layer.group.children.length).toBe(afterFirst);
  });
});

--- features/live/liveApi.ts ---
import type { BBox } from './viewport';
import {
  isRenderable,
  type LiveMode,
  type LiveTrafficStatus,
  type LiveVehicle,
  type RenderableVehicle,
  type VehicleSnapshot
} from './liveTypes';

// Shares the realtime feature's base-URL strategy: same-origin (Vite proxy) in
// dev, VITE_REALTIME_API_BASE in production. Kept local to the live feature so
// it stays code-split and does not import the realtime module.
const CONFIGURED_BASE = (import.meta.env.VITE_REALTIME_API_BASE as string | undefined)?.replace(/\/$/, '') ?? '';
const API_BASE = import.meta.env.DEV ? '' : CONFIGURED_BASE;
const API_ROOT = `${API_BASE}/api/v1`;

type ErrorEnvelope = { error?: { code?: string; message?: string } };

async function request<T>(path: string, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<T> {
  const response = await fetch(`${API_ROOT}${path}`, { headers: { Accept: 'application/json' }, signal });
  if (!response.ok) {
    let message = `Request failed (${response.status})`;
    try {
      const body = (await response.json()) as ErrorEnvelope;
      if (body.error?.message) message = body.error.message;
    } catch {
      // Non-JSON error body; keep the status-based message.
    }
    const error = new Error(message) as Error & { status?: number };
    error.status = response.status;
    throw error;
  }
  return (await response.json()) as T;
}

/** Whether live traffic is available at all (double-gate with the city check). */
export async function fetchLiveStatus(signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<LiveTrafficStatus> {
  return request<LiveTrafficStatus>('/live/status', signal);
}

/** A tightened snapshot: only vehicles the UI can actually place on the map. */
export type LiveSnapshot = Omit<VehicleSnapshot, 'vehicles'> & { vehicles: RenderableVehicle[] };

/** Viewport-bounded vehicle snapshot. `modes` must be non-empty (API requires it). */
export async function fetchVehicles(
  bbox: BBox,
  modes: LiveMode[],
  options: { at?: string } = {},
  signal?: AbortSignal
): Promise<LiveSnapshot> {
  const params = new URLSearchParams({
    latMin: String(bbox.latMin),
    lonMin: String(bbox.lonMin),
    latMax: String(bbox.latMax),
    lonMax: String(bbox.lonMax),
    modes: modes.join(',')
  });
  if (options.at) params.set('at', options.at);
  const snapshot = await request<VehicleSnapshot>(`/live/vehicles?${params}`, signal);
  return { ...snapshot, vehicles: (snapshot.vehicles ?? []).filter(isRenderable) };
}

/** Per-run estimate used while following a single vehicle (concept §6.2). */
export async function fetchVehicle(runId: string, options: { at?: string } = {}, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<LiveVehicle> {
  const query = options.at ? `?at=${encodeURIComponent(options.at)}` : '';
  return request<LiveVehicle>(`/live/vehicles/${encodeURIComponent(runId)}${query}`, signal);
}

--- features/live/liveTrackGeometry.test.ts ---
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { smoothTrackXZ, resampleTrackXZ, buildSmoothTrackXZ } from './liveTrackGeometry';

const v = (x: number, z: number) => new THREE.Vector3(x, 0, z);

describe('smoothTrackXZ', () => {
  it('preserves point count and endpoints', () => {
    const pts = [v(0, 0), v(10, 4), v(20, -4), v(30, 0)];
    const out = smoothTrackXZ(pts, 2);
    expect(out).toHaveLength(pts.length);
    expect(out[0].equals(pts[0])).toBe(true);
    expect(out.at(-1)!.equals(pts.at(-1)!)).toBe(true);
  });

  it('pulls an interior zig-zag toward its neighbours', () => {
    const pts = [v(0, 0), v(10, 10), v(20, 0)];
    const out = smoothTrackXZ(pts, 1);
    // 0.2*0 + 0.6*10 + 0.2*0 = 6 < 10
    expect(out[1].z).toBeCloseTo(6, 5);
  });
});

describe('resampleTrackXZ', () => {
  it('returns short inputs unchanged', () => {
    const pts = [v(0, 0), v(10, 0)];
    expect(resampleTrackXZ(pts)).toHaveLength(2);
  });

  it('densifies a long polyline and stays near the original line', () => {
    const pts = [v(0, 0), v(50, 0), v(100, 0)];
    const out = resampleTrackXZ(pts, 5);
    expect(out.length).toBeGreaterThan(pts.length);
    for (const p of out) expect(Math.abs(p.z)).toBeLessThan(0.5); // straight line stays flat
  });
});

describe('buildSmoothTrackXZ', () => {
  it('smooths and resamples without drifting off a straight track', () => {
    const pts = [v(0, 0), v(30, 0), v(60, 0), v(90, 0)];
    const out = buildSmoothTrackXZ(pts, 5);
    expect(out.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(pts.length);
    expect(out[0].x).toBeCloseTo(0, 3);
    expect(out.at(-1)!.x).toBeCloseTo(90, 3);
  });
});

--- features/live/Live3DView.tsx ---
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Live3DScene } from './Live3DScene';
import { LiveModePanel } from './LiveModePanel';
import { LiveVehicleCard } from './LiveVehicleCard';
import type { LiveVehicleStore } from './LiveVehicleStore';
import type { LatLon } from './liveProjection';
import type { LiveMode, LiveVehicle } from './liveTypes';
import type { LiveSummary } from './useLiveVehicles';
import type { BBox } from './viewport';
import type { CameraMode } from '../../world/CameraController';
import type { CityDefinition } from '../../cities';

const CAMERA_MODE_LABEL: Record<CameraMode, string> = {
  chase: 'Chase',
  passenger: 'Passenger',
  cab: 'Driver'
};

/**
 * Full-screen 3D free-flight view (milestone 1). Mounts {@link Live3DScene},
 * reuses the slim HUD, and adds a "Map view" control. The scene reads the shared
 * {@link LiveVehicleStore}, so vehicles carry over from the map with no refetch.
 */
export function Live3DView({
  store,
  origin,
  summary,
  userModes,
  onToggleMode,
  onViewport,
  onBackToMap,
  onExitLive,
  selectedVehicle,
  following,
  onSelect,
  onToggleFollow,
  onStopFollow,
  onDepartures,
  onCloseSelection,
  city
}: {
  store: LiveVehicleStore;
  origin: LatLon;
  summary: LiveSummary;
  userModes: Set<LiveMode>;
  onToggleMode: (mode: LiveMode) => void;
  onViewport: (bbox: BBox, zoom: number) => void;
  onBackToMap: () => void;
  onExitLive: () => void;
  selectedVehicle: LiveVehicle | null;
  following: boolean;
  onSelect: (runId: string) => void;
  onToggleFollow: () => void;
  onStopFollow: () => void;
  onDepartures: () => void;
  onCloseSelection: () => void;
  city?: CityDefinition;
}) {
  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const onViewportRef = useRef(onViewport);
  onViewportRef.current = onViewport;
  const onBackRef = useRef(onBackToMap);
  onBackRef.current = onBackToMap;
  const onSelectRef = useRef(onSelect);
  onSelectRef.current = onSelect;
  const onStopFollowRef = useRef(onStopFollow);
  onStopFollowRef.current = onStopFollow;
  const sceneRef = useRef<Live3DScene | null>(null);
  const cityRef = useRef(city);
  cityRef.current = city;
  const [cameraMode, setCameraMode] = useState<CameraMode>('chase');
  const setCameraModeRef = useRef(setCameraMode);
  setCameraModeRef.current = setCameraMode;

  // Origin is fixed for the scene's lifetime; a new origin means a fresh mount.
  const originKey = `${origin.lat.toFixed(5)},${origin.lon.toFixed(5)}`;

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!containerRef.current) return;
    const scene = new Live3DScene(containerRef.current, {
      store,
      origin,
      onViewport: (bbox, zoom) => onViewportRef.current(bbox, zoom),
      onExitToMap: () => onBackRef.current(),
      onSelect: (runId) => onSelectRef.current(runId),
      onStopFollow: () => onStopFollowRef.current(),
      onCameraModeChange: (mode) => setCameraModeRef.current(mode),
      city: cityRef.current
    });
    sceneRef.current = scene;
    return () => {
      sceneRef.current = null;
      scene.dispose();
    };
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [store, originKey]);

  useEffect(() => {
    sceneRef.current?.setFollowRunId(following && selectedVehicle?.runId ? selectedVehicle.runId : null);
  }, [following, selectedVehicle?.runId]);

  return (
    <div className="lv-3d-root">
      <div ref={containerRef} className="lv-3d-canvas" />
      <LiveModePanel summary={summary} userModes={userModes} onToggleMode={onToggleMode} onExit={onExitLive} />
      <div className="lv-3d-bar">
        <button className="lv-3d-back" onClick={onBackToMap}>
          ↩ Map view
        </button>
        {following && (
          <button
            className="lv-3d-back"
            onClick={() => sceneRef.current?.cycleCameraMode()}
            title="Switch view (C)"
          >
            View: {CAMERA_MODE_LABEL[cameraMode]}
          </button>
        )}
        <span className="lv-3d-hint">
          {following ? 'C to switch view · drag to leave follow' : 'drag to pan · scroll to zoom · right-drag to rotate'}
        </span>
      </div>
      {selectedVehicle && (
        <LiveVehicleCard
          vehicle={selectedVehicle}
          following={following}
          onToggleFollow={onToggleFollow}
          onDepartures={onDepartures}
          onClose={onCloseSelection}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

--- features/live/LiveVehicleCard.tsx ---
import { delayLevel, modeColor, modeLabel, type LiveVehicle } from './liveTypes';

function delayText(delaySeconds: number | undefined): string {
  const s = delaySeconds ?? 0;
  if (Math.abs(s) < 60) return 'on time';
  const min = Math.round(s / 60);
  return min > 0 ? `+${min} min` : `${min} min`;
}

function statusText(status: LiveVehicle['status']): string {
  switch (status) {
    case 'at_stop':
      return 'at stop';
    case 'in_transit':
      return 'in transit';
    case 'not_started':
      return 'not started';
    case 'completed':
      return 'completed';
    default:
      return '—';
  }
}

function confidenceText(v: LiveVehicle): string {
  const conf = v.confidence ?? 'low';
  const source =
    v.source === 'measured' ? 'measured' : v.source === 'realtime_inferred' ? 'realtime' : 'planned';
  return `${source} · ${conf}`;
}

/**
 * Compact selection info card (concept §2.4). Fields map 1:1 to LiveVehicle;
 * Follow and Departures compose with existing tools.
 */
export function LiveVehicleCard({
  vehicle,
  following,
  onToggleFollow,
  onDepartures,
  onClose
}: {
  vehicle: LiveVehicle;
  following: boolean;
  onToggleFollow: () => void;
  onDepartures: () => void;
  onClose: () => void;
}) {
  const color = modeColor(vehicle.mode);
  const delay = delayLevel(vehicle.delaySeconds);
  const planned = vehicle.source === 'schedule';

  return (
    <div className={`lv-card ${planned ? 'planned' : ''}`}>
      <button className="lv-card-close" aria-label="Close" onClick={onClose}>
        ×
      </button>
      <div className="lv-card-head">
        <span className="lv-card-line" style={{ background: color }}>
          {vehicle.line ?? modeLabel(vehicle.mode)}
        </span>
        <span className="lv-card-dest">→ {vehicle.direction || 'Unknown'}</span>
      </div>
      <div className="lv-card-row">
        <span className={`lv-delay-tag lv-delay-${delay}`}>{delayText(vehicle.delaySeconds)}</span>
        <span className="lv-card-status">{statusText(vehicle.status)}</span>
        <span className="lv-card-conf" title="confidence · source">
          {planned ? '◔' : '●'} {confidenceText(vehicle)}
        </span>
      </div>
      <div className="lv-card-stops">
        <span>
          <em>Prev</em> {vehicle.previousStop || '—'}
        </span>
        <span>
          <em>Next</em> {vehicle.nextStop || '—'}
        </span>
      </div>
      <div className="lv-card-actions">
        <button className={following ? 'active' : ''} onClick={onToggleFollow}>
          {following ? 'Following' : 'Follow'}
        </button>
        <button onClick={onDepartures} disabled={!vehicle.nextStop}>
          Departures at next stop
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

--- features/live/Live3DScene.ts ---
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { OsmSceneStreamer } from '../../osm-scene';
import { makeProjection, type LatLon, type Projection } from './liveProjection';
import type { LiveVehicleStore, TrackedVehicle } from './LiveVehicleStore';
import { LiveVehicleModels } from './LiveVehicleModels';
import { LiveTerrain } from './LiveTerrain';
import { daylightAt } from './liveDaylight';
import { LiveTunnelIndex } from './LiveTunnelIndex';
import { LiveTunnelLayer } from './LiveTunnelLayer';
import { LiveTrackLayer } from './LiveTrackLayer';
import { LiveTrackMotion } from './LiveTrackMotion';
import { ALL_LIVE_MODES, modeColor, type LiveMode } from './liveTypes';
import type { BBox } from './viewport';
import { ChaseCameraRig } from '../../engine/cameras/ChaseCameraRig';
import { PassengerCameraRig } from '../../engine/cameras/PassengerCameraRig';
import { CabCameraRig } from '../../engine/cameras/CabCameraRig';
import type { CameraMode, CameraUpdateInput } from '../../world/CameraController';
import { LiveFollowVehicle } from './LiveFollowVehicle';
import { resolveLiveVehicleDefinition } from './liveVehicleDefinition';
import type { CityDefinition } from '../../cities';

// Body base height above terrain (matches the overview models); dropped into the
// bore when underground.
function followBaseOffset(underground: boolean): number {
  return underground ? -4.7 : 0.3;
}

// Free-flight 3D view over the live network (concept §2.2/§4): reused OSM scenery
// on Mapzen terrain relief (LiveTerrain), with near vehicles as full procedural
// models (LiveVehicleModels) and the rest as mode-colored instanced blocks, plus
// tunnel glows/linings for underground runs. Everything is placed at terrain
// height via LiveTerrain.heightAt so buildings, vehicles and follow camera share
// the same ground.

const DEG = Math.PI / 180;

// Camera altitude clamp (concept §2.2: ~80 m to ~12 km). Above the top we hand
// back to the map.
const MIN_DISTANCE_M = 120;
const MAX_DISTANCE_M = 12_000;
// Vehicle block dimensions (metres); size-exaggerated a touch so they read.
const CAR_LEN = 22;
const CAR_W = 3.0;
const CAR_H = 3.4;
const MAX_PER_MODE = 1024;
// Tunnel glows mark underground runs for someone looking down from above. Up
// close (and while riding a tunnel chase) they read as a big coloured blob, so
// they only draw from a distance.
const GLOW_MIN_ALTITUDE_M = 150;

type Live3DOptions = {
  store: LiveVehicleStore;
  origin: LatLon;
  /** Reports the camera ground footprint for viewport-bounded polling. */
  onViewport: (bbox: BBox, syntheticZoom: number) => void;
  /** Fired when the user zooms out past the top of the altitude band. */
  onExitToMap: () => void;
  /** Opens the shared live-vehicle info card for a picked vehicle. */
  onSelect: (runId: string) => void;
  /** Stops a locked follow when the user takes manual camera control. */
  onStopFollow: () => void;
  /** Reports the active follow camera view (chase / passenger / cab) to the UI. */
  onCameraModeChange?: (mode: CameraMode) => void;
  /** Current city, so followed vehicles resolve to the same design as the simulator. */
  city?: CityDefinition;
};

export class Live3DScene {
  private readonly renderer: THREE.WebGLRenderer;
  private readonly scene = new THREE.Scene();
  private readonly camera: THREE.PerspectiveCamera;
  private readonly projection: Projection;
  private readonly streamer: OsmSceneStreamer;

  // Orbit/fly rig state.
  private readonly target = new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, 0);
  private distance = 700;
  private azimuth = 0;
  private polar = 0.75; // radians from +Y; clamped so we never go underground

  private readonly meshes = new Map<LiveMode, THREE.InstancedMesh>();
  private readonly tunnelGlows = new Map<LiveMode, THREE.InstancedMesh>();
  private readonly instanceRunIds = new Map<LiveMode, string[]>();
  private readonly tunnelGlowRunIds = new Map<LiveMode, string[]>();
  private readonly dummy = new THREE.Object3D();
  // Near-field full vehicle models + line labels (LOD upgrade over the blocks).
  private readonly models: LiveVehicleModels;
  private readonly tunnelIndex = new LiveTunnelIndex();
  private readonly tunnels: LiveTunnelLayer;
  private readonly tracks: LiveTrackLayer;
  // Snaps matched rail vehicles onto their route and moves them along it.
  private readonly trackMotion: LiveTrackMotion;
  private readonly terrain: LiveTerrain;
  private readonly ground: THREE.Mesh;
  // Lighting follows real local time (concept §2.5).
  private readonly sun = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff, 1.6);
  private readonly hemi = new THREE.HemisphereLight(0xddecff, 0x586a70, 1.0);
  // A small constant ambient fill lifts shadowed faces, matching Explore's rig.
  private readonly fill = new THREE.AmbientLight(0x9bb0c8, 0.16);
  // Sky colour from the daylight curve, and whether we are currently inside a
  // tunnel — underground swaps the sky/fog for enclosing darkness so gaps in the
  // bore read as earth instead of bright daylight.
  private readonly skyColor = new THREE.Color(0x9fc6e8);
  private underground = false;
  private lastLightingMs = 0;
  private followRunId: string | null = null;
  // Follow camera reuses Explore's rigs so chase/passenger/driver match that view.
  private cameraMode: CameraMode = 'chase';
  private readonly rigs = {
    chase: new ChaseCameraRig(),
    passenger: new PassengerCameraRig(),
    cab: new CabCameraRig()
  } as const;
  // The fully-detailed model of the followed run (real destination display, doors,
  // interior board, per-view visibility). Built lazily on first follow frame.
  private followVehicle: LiveFollowVehicle | null = null;
  // 0 (night) → 1 (full day), for exterior lamp switching on the followed vehicle.
  private daylightFactor = 1;
  // Gated smoothing goal (world XZ) for the followed vehicle, so a single bad frame
  // (a route rematch flipping the snapped position) can't whip the camera.
  private readonly followTarget = new THREE.Vector3();
  private followTargetSet = false;
  private followFreezeFrames = 0;
  private followJustStarted = false;
  // Scratch reused by the follow camera each frame (no per-frame allocations).
  private readonly _followPos = new THREE.Vector3();
  private readonly _followTan = new THREE.Vector3();
  private readonly _followOff = new THREE.Vector3();

  private raf: number | null = null;
  private lastTime = 0;
  private disposed = false;

  // Viewport reporting throttle.
  private lastReport = 0;
  private cameraDirty = true;

  // Pointer drag state.
  private dragButton = -1;
  private lastPointer = { x: 0, y: 0 };
  private pointerStart = { x: 0, y: 0 };

  constructor(
    private readonly container: HTMLElement,
    private readonly opts: Live3DOptions
  ) {
    this.projection = makeProjection(opts.origin);

    this.renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ antialias: true });
    this.renderer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);
    this.renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio, 2));
    this.renderer.toneMapping = THREE.ACESFilmicToneMapping;
    this.renderer.toneMappingExposure = 1.08; // slightly brighter, matching Explore
    container.appendChild(this.renderer.domElement);

    this.scene.background = new THREE.Color(0x9fc6e8);
    this.scene.fog = new THREE.Fog(0x9fc6e8, 3000, 14000);

    this.camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(60, window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight, 1, 30_000);
    this.updateCamera();

    // Soft sky/ground hemisphere plus a directional sun, both driven by the
    // real local time of day (updated periodically in the frame loop).
    this.scene.add(this.hemi);
    this.scene.add(this.sun);
    this.scene.add(this.fill);
    this.applyDaylight();

    // Terrain relief around the fixed origin doubles as the base ground (so there
    // is no void before OSM tiles stream in) and the height source for everything
    // placed in the scene.
    this.terrain = new LiveTerrain(this.projection);
    this.ground = this.terrain.mesh;
    this.scene.add(this.ground);

    // Reused OSM scenery streamer, placed on the terrain via the shared sampler.
    this.streamer = new OsmSceneStreamer(
      this.scene,
      (lat, lon) => this.projection.project(lat, lon),
      (lat, lon) => this.terrain.surfaceHeight(lat, lon)
    );

    // One instanced block mesh per mode (≤5 draw calls for the whole fleet).
    for (const mode of ALL_LIVE_MODES) {
      const geom = new THREE.BoxGeometry(CAR_W, CAR_H, CAR_LEN);
      const mat = new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({ color: new THREE.Color(modeColor(mode)) });
      const mesh = new THREE.InstancedMesh(geom, mat, MAX_PER_MODE);
      mesh.instanceMatrix.setUsage(THREE.DynamicDrawUsage);
      mesh.count = 0;
      mesh.frustumCulled = false;
      this.scene.add(mesh);
      this.meshes.set(mode, mesh);

      const glow = new THREE.InstancedMesh(
        new THREE.CircleGeometry(8, 16).rotateX(-Math.PI / 2),
        new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: new THREE.Color(modeColor(mode)), transparent: true, opacity: 0.65 }),
        MAX_PER_MODE
      );
      glow.instanceMatrix.setUsage(THREE.DynamicDrawUsage);
      glow.count = 0;
      glow.frustumCulled = false;
      this.scene.add(glow);
      this.tunnelGlows.set(mode, glow);
    }

    this.models = new LiveVehicleModels(this.scene, this.projection, opts.city);
    this.tunnels = new LiveTunnelLayer(this.projection, this.tunnelIndex, (x, z) => this.terrain.heightAt(x, z));
    this.scene.add(this.tunnels.group);
    this.tracks = new LiveTrackLayer(this.projection, this.tunnelIndex, (x, z) => this.terrain.heightAt(x, z));
    this.scene.add(this.tracks.group);
    this.trackMotion = new LiveTrackMotion(this.projection, this.tunnelIndex);

    this.attachControls();
    window.addEventListener('resize', this.onResize);
    this.raf = requestAnimationFrame(this.frame);
  }

  dispose(): void {
    this.disposed = true;
    if (this.raf) cancelAnimationFrame(this.raf);
    window.removeEventListener('resize', this.onResize);
    this.detachControls();
    this.disposeFollowVehicle();
    this.models.dispose();
    this.tunnels.dispose();
    this.tracks.dispose();
    this.streamer.dispose();
    for (const mesh of this.meshes.values()) {
      mesh.geometry.dispose();
      (mesh.material as THREE.Material).dispose();
      this.scene.remove(mesh);
    }
    for (const mesh of this.tunnelGlows.values()) {
      mesh.geometry.dispose();
      (mesh.material as THREE.Material).dispose();
      this.scene.remove(mesh);
    }
    this.scene.traverse((o) => {
      const m = o as Partial<THREE.Mesh> & { material?: THREE.Material | THREE.Material[] };
      m.geometry?.dispose?.();
      const mat = m.material;
      if (Array.isArray(mat)) mat.forEach((x) => x.dispose());
      else mat?.dispose?.();
    });
    this.renderer.domElement.remove();
    this.renderer.dispose();
  }

  /** Camera ground altitude, used for tier mapping and the exit-to-map gate. */
  private get altitudeM(): number {
    return this.distance * Math.cos(this.polar);
  }

  /** Drives sun/sky/fog from the real local hour (concept §2.5). */
  private applyDaylight(): void {
    const now = new Date();
    const d = daylightAt(now.getHours() + now.getMinutes() / 60);
    // Lamps come on around dusk (sun below ~5°); full day well above the horizon.
    this.daylightFactor = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(d.elevationDeg / 12, 0, 1);
    this.skyColor.setHex(d.skyColor);
    this.applyEnvironment();
    this.sun.color.setHex(d.sunColor);
    this.sun.intensity = d.sunIntensity;
    this.hemi.intensity = d.hemiIntensity;
    const el = d.elevationDeg * DEG;
    const R = 4000;
    this.sun.position.set(
      Math.cos(el) * Math.sin(d.azimuthRad) * R,
      Math.max(Math.sin(el), 0.02) * R + 100,
      Math.cos(el) * Math.cos(d.azimuthRad) * R
    );
  }

  /**
   * Swaps between the daylight sky and enclosing tunnel darkness. Underground the
   * bore only covers the alignment, so without this the surrounding void renders
   * as bright sky instead of earth.
   */
  private applyEnvironment(): void {
    const background = this.scene.background as THREE.Color;
    const fog = this.scene.fog as THREE.Fog | null;
    if (this.underground) {
      background.setHex(0x0b0d10);
      if (fog) {
        fog.color.setHex(0x0b0d10);
        fog.near = 6;
        fog.far = 160;
      }
      return;
    }
    background.copy(this.skyColor);
    if (fog) {
      fog.color.copy(this.skyColor);
      fog.near = 3000;
      fog.far = 14_000;
    }
  }

  /** Applies underground presentation (hidden surface + dark enclosure). */
  private setUnderground(underground: boolean): void {
    this.ground.visible = !underground;
    this.streamer.group.visible = !underground;
    if (underground === this.underground) return;
    this.underground = underground;
    this.applyEnvironment();
  }

  private updateCamera(): void {
    const s = Math.sin(this.polar) * this.distance;
    this.camera.position.set(
      this.target.x + s * Math.sin(this.azimuth),
      this.target.y + this.distance * Math.cos(this.polar),
      this.target.z + s * Math.cos(this.azimuth)
    );
    this.camera.lookAt(this.target);
  }

  private readonly frame = (time: number) => {
    if (this.disposed) return;
    const dt = this.lastTime ? Math.min(0.1, (time - this.lastTime) / 1000) : 0.016;
    this.lastTime = time;

    // Refresh lighting occasionally; it changes slowly with wall-clock time.
    if (time - this.lastLightingMs > 15_000) {
      this.applyDaylight();
      this.lastLightingMs = time;
    }

    this.opts.store.tick(dt);
    const vehicles = this.opts.store.list();
    // Snap matched rail vehicles onto their route before anything reads positions.
    this.trackMotion.tick(vehicles, dt);
    this.tunnels.update(vehicles, this.terrain.revision);
    this.tracks.update(vehicles, this.altitudeM, this.terrain.revision);
    // Near vehicles get full models; the rest fall back to instanced blocks.
    const modeled = this.models.update(
      this.opts.store,
      this.target,
      this.altitudeM,
      (vehicle) => this.tunnelIndex.isInTunnel(vehicle),
      (x, z) => this.terrain.heightAt(x, z),
      (vehicle) => this.resolveWorld(vehicle),
      this.camera.position,
      (runId, offsetM) => this.trackMotion.offsetPoseOf(runId, offsetM),
      this.followRunId // the followed run renders via the dedicated LiveFollowVehicle
    );
    // Follow after models are placed so interior cameras read fresh car matrices.
    this.updateFollow(dt);
    this.syncVehicles(modeled);

    // Keep terrain relief + scenery centered on the camera focus (both no-op
    // unless the focus moved far enough, so calling every frame is cheap).
    this.terrain.update(this.target.x, this.target.z);
    const focus = this.projection.unproject(this.target.x, this.target.z);
    this.streamer.update(focus);

    this.reportViewport(time, focus);
    this.renderer.render(this.scene, this.camera);
    this.raf = requestAnimationFrame(this.frame);
  };

  private syncVehicles(modeled: Set<string>): void {
    const perMode = new Map<LiveMode, number>();
    const glowPerMode = new Map<LiveMode, number>();
    for (const mode of ALL_LIVE_MODES) perMode.set(mode, 0);
    for (const mode of ALL_LIVE_MODES) glowPerMode.set(mode, 0);

    for (const v of this.opts.store.list()) {
      if (v.runId === this.followRunId) continue; // rendered by the dedicated follow vehicle
      const mode = (v.data.mode ?? 'tram') as LiveMode;
      const mesh = this.meshes.get(mode);
      const glow = this.tunnelGlows.get(mode);
      if (!mesh || !glow) continue;
      const tunnel = this.tunnelIndex.isInTunnel(v);
      if (tunnel) {
        const i = glowPerMode.get(mode) ?? 0;
        if (i < MAX_PER_MODE && this.altitudeM >= GLOW_MIN_ALTITUDE_M) {
          this.placeTunnelGlow(glow, i, v);
          const ids = this.tunnelGlowRunIds.get(mode) ?? [];
          ids[i] = v.runId;
          this.tunnelGlowRunIds.set(mode, ids);
          glowPerMode.set(mode, i + 1);
        }
        continue;
      }
      // Vehicles shown as full models are omitted from the instanced blocks.
      if (modeled.has(v.runId)) continue;
      const i = perMode.get(mode) ?? 0;
      if (i >= MAX_PER_MODE) continue;
      this.placeInstance(mesh, i, v);
      const ids = this.instanceRunIds.get(mode) ?? [];
      ids[i] = v.runId;
      this.instanceRunIds.set(mode, ids);
      perMode.set(mode, i + 1);
    }

    for (const [mode, mesh] of this.meshes) {
      mesh.count = perMode.get(mode) ?? 0;
      mesh.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true;
      const glow = this.tunnelGlows.get(mode)!;
      glow.count = glowPerMode.get(mode) ?? 0;
      glow.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true;
    }
  }

  /** World position + bearing for a vehicle: route-snapped when matched, else raw. */
  private resolveWorld(v: TrackedVehicle): { x: number; z: number; bearing: number } {
    const snapped = this.trackMotion.positionOf(v.runId);
    const ll = snapped ?? v.render;
    const p = this.projection.project(ll.lat, ll.lon);
    return { x: p.x, z: p.z, bearing: snapped ? snapped.bearing : v.bearing };
  }

  private placeInstance(mesh: THREE.InstancedMesh, index: number, v: TrackedVehicle): void {
    const w = this.resolveWorld(v);
    this.dummy.position.set(w.x, this.terrain.heightAt(w.x, w.z) + CAR_H / 2 + 0.4, w.z);
    // Bearing is clockwise from north (+z); box forward is +z, so rotate by -bearing.
    this.dummy.rotation.set(0, -w.bearing * DEG, 0);
    this.dummy.updateMatrix();
    mesh.setMatrixAt(index, this.dummy.matrix);
  }

  private placeTunnelGlow(mesh: THREE.InstancedMesh, index: number, v: TrackedVehicle): void {
    const p = this.projection.project(v.render.lat, v.render.lon);
    this.dummy.position.set(p.x, this.terrain.heightAt(p.x, p.z) + 0.35, p.z);
    this.dummy.rotation.set(-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0);
    this.dummy.updateMatrix();
    mesh.setMatrixAt(index, this.dummy.matrix);
  }

  // Reports at most ~4×/s, and only when the camera moved since the last report.
  private reportViewport(time: number, focus: LatLon): void {
    if (!this.cameraDirty || time - this.lastReport < 250) return;
    this.cameraDirty = false;
    this.lastReport = time;

    const alt = this.altitudeM;
    if (this.distance >= MAX_DISTANCE_M * 0.95) {
      this.opts.onExitToMap();
      return;
    }
    // Visible ground radius grows with altitude; convert to a lat/lon box.
    const radiusM = Math.max(500, alt * 1.3);
    const dLat = radiusM / 111_320;
    const dLon = radiusM / this.projection.lonScale;
    const bbox: BBox = {
      latMin: focus.lat - dLat,
      lonMin: focus.lon - dLon,
      latMax: focus.lat + dLat,
      lonMax: focus.lon + dLon
    };
    // Synthetic Leaflet-style zoom so the polling hook picks a sensible tier.
    const zoom = alt < 1500 ? 16 : alt < 6000 ? 13 : 11;
    this.opts.onViewport(bbox, zoom);
  }

  // ---- Free-orbit controls ----

  private attachControls(): void {
    const el = this.renderer.domElement;
    el.addEventListener('pointerdown', this.onPointerDown);
    el.addEventListener('pointermove', this.onPointerMove);
    el.addEventListener('pointerup', this.onPointerUp);
    el.addEventListener('pointerleave', this.onPointerUp);
    el.addEventListener('wheel', this.onWheel, { passive: false });
    el.addEventListener('contextmenu', this.onContextMenu);
    window.addEventListener('keydown', this.onKeyDown);
  }

  private detachControls(): void {
    const el = this.renderer.domElement;
    el.removeEventListener('pointerdown', this.onPointerDown);
    el.removeEventListener('pointermove', this.onPointerMove);
    el.removeEventListener('pointerup', this.onPointerUp);
    el.removeEventListener('pointerleave', this.onPointerUp);
    el.removeEventListener('wheel', this.onWheel);
    el.removeEventListener('contextmenu', this.onContextMenu);
    window.removeEventListener('keydown', this.onKeyDown);
  }

  private readonly onContextMenu = (e: Event) => e.preventDefault();

  // 'c' cycles the follow view (chase / passenger / driver) while following.
  private readonly onKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
    if (e.key === 'c' || e.key === 'C') {
      if (this.followRunId) this.cycleCameraMode();
    }
  };

  private readonly onPointerDown = (e: PointerEvent) => {
    this.dragButton = e.button;
    this.lastPointer = { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY };
    this.pointerStart = this.lastPointer;
    (e.target as HTMLElement).setPointerCapture?.(e.pointerId);
  };

  private readonly onPointerUp = (e: PointerEvent) => {
    const moved = Math.hypot(e.clientX - this.pointerStart.x, e.clientY - this.pointerStart.y);
    if (this.dragButton === 0 && moved < 5) this.pick(e);
    this.dragButton = -1;
  };

  private readonly onPointerMove = (e: PointerEvent) => {
    if (this.dragButton < 0) return;
    const dx = e.clientX - this.lastPointer.x;
    const dy = e.clientY - this.lastPointer.y;
    this.lastPointer = { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY };

    const orbit = this.dragButton === 2 || e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey;
    if (Math.hypot(dx, dy) > 0 && this.followRunId) this.opts.onStopFollow();
    if (orbit) {
      // Grab-and-spin: dragging right/down swings the view that way.
      this.azimuth -= dx * 0.005;
      this.polar = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(this.polar - dy * 0.005, 0.15, this.followRunId ? 1.58 : 1.4);
    } else {
      // Grab-and-drag pan: the ground point under the cursor tracks the cursor,
      // so dragging right moves the world right (target moves the opposite way).
      const panScale = this.distance * 0.0018;
      const right = new THREE.Vector3().setFromMatrixColumn(this.camera.matrix, 0).setY(0).normalize();
      const forward = new THREE.Vector3().crossVectors(new THREE.Vector3(0, 1, 0), right).normalize();
      this.target.addScaledVector(right, -dx * panScale).addScaledVector(forward, -dy * panScale);
    }
    this.cameraDirty = true;
    this.updateCamera();
  };

  private readonly onWheel = (e: WheelEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (this.followRunId) this.opts.onStopFollow();
    const factor = e.deltaY > 0 ? 1.12 : 1 / 1.12;
    this.distance = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(this.distance * factor, MIN_DISTANCE_M, MAX_DISTANCE_M);
    this.cameraDirty = true;
    this.updateCamera();
  };

  setFollowRunId(runId: string | null): void {
    if (this.followRunId === runId) return;
    this.followRunId = runId;
    this.followTargetSet = false;
    this.followFreezeFrames = 0;
    this.followJustStarted = !!runId;
    this.disposeFollowVehicle(); // rebuilt for the new run on the next follow frame
    if (!runId) {
      this.setUnderground(false);
      this.camera.up.set(0, 1, 0); // clear any interior-camera up-vector for free orbit
      return;
    }
  }

  private ensureFollowVehicle(vehicle: TrackedVehicle): LiveFollowVehicle {
    if (this.followVehicle) return this.followVehicle;
    const definition = resolveLiveVehicleDefinition(vehicle.data, this.opts.city);
    this.followVehicle = new LiveFollowVehicle(this.scene, definition);
    return this.followVehicle;
  }

  private disposeFollowVehicle(): void {
    this.followVehicle?.dispose();
    this.followVehicle = null;
  }

  /** Cycles the follow view chase → passenger → cab, like Explore. */
  cycleCameraMode(): CameraMode {
    return this.setCameraMode(
      this.cameraMode === 'chase' ? 'passenger' : this.cameraMode === 'passenger' ? 'cab' : 'chase'
    );
  }

  setCameraMode(mode: CameraMode): CameraMode {
    this.cameraMode = mode;
    this.followJustStarted = true; // snap into the new view instead of sliding across
    this.opts.onCameraModeChange?.(mode);
    return mode;
  }

  // Positions the follow camera by reusing Explore's rigs, so chase shows the
  // vehicle's rear (with its route display), and passenger/cab match the ride view.
  // Orbit params are kept in sync so the hand-off to free flight (on stop-follow)
  // doesn't jump and viewport polling still gets a sane altitude.
  private updateFollow(dt: number): void {
    if (!this.followRunId) return;
    // Freeze at the last valid pose when the run vanishes from the store (e.g. a
    // brief bbox flicker); never latch onto another vehicle.
    const vehicle = this.opts.store.get(this.followRunId);
    if (!vehicle) return;

    const w = this.resolveWorld(vehicle);
    // Gate the horizontal goal: a plausible step is the vehicle's own motion plus a
    // margin. A larger jump means the snapped position teleported (route rematch, bad
    // fix); hold the previous goal, but accept it if the jump persists (~1 s).
    if (!this.followTargetSet) {
      this.followTarget.set(w.x, 0, w.z);
      this.followTargetSet = true;
      this.followFreezeFrames = 0;
    } else {
      const step = Math.hypot(w.x - this.followTarget.x, w.z - this.followTarget.z);
      const allowed = Math.max(40, 45 * dt * 3);
      if (step <= allowed || this.followFreezeFrames > 60) {
        this.followTarget.set(w.x, 0, w.z);
        this.followFreezeFrames = 0;
      } else {
        this.followFreezeFrames += 1;
      }
    }

    const px = this.followTarget.x;
    const pz = this.followTarget.z;
    const underground = this.tunnelIndex.isInTunnel(vehicle);
    const baseY = this.terrain.heightAt(px, pz) + followBaseOffset(underground);
    const position = this._followPos.set(px, baseY, pz);
    // Track-forward unit vector from bearing (model forward = (-sinβ, 0, cosβ)).
    const beta = w.bearing * DEG;
    const tangent = this._followTan.set(-Math.sin(beta), 0, Math.cos(beta));

    // Build the detailed followed vehicle on first frame (needs the run's data), then
    // drive its motion, doors, interior display, and per-view visibility.
    const follow = this.ensureFollowVehicle(vehicle);
    follow.update({
      x: px,
      z: pz,
      bearing: w.bearing,
      centerY: baseY,
      offsetPose: (offsetM) => this.trackMotion.offsetPoseOf(vehicle.runId, offsetM),
      mode: this.cameraMode,
      doorsOpen: vehicle.data.status === 'at_stop',
      nextStop: vehicle.data.nextStop ?? '',
      daylightFactor: this.daylightFactor,
      dt
    });

    const input: CameraUpdateInput = {
      position,
      tangent,
      underground,
      interiorCameraProfile: follow.profile,
      passengerOffsetM: 0,
      trainHalfLengthM: follow.trainHalfLengthM,
      train: follow.root,
      trainCars: follow.cars
    };
    const rig = this.rigs[this.cameraMode];
    const pose = rig.pose(input);

    if (this.followJustStarted) {
      this.camera.position.copy(pose.position);
      this.camera.up.copy(pose.up).normalize();
      this.followJustStarted = false;
    } else {
      this.camera.position.lerp(pose.position, rig.positionSmoothing);
      this.camera.up.lerp(pose.up, 0.32).normalize();
    }
    this.camera.lookAt(pose.target);
    this.setUnderground(underground);

    // Keep orbit state consistent with the rig camera for viewport + hand-off.
    this.target.set(px, baseY + 2, pz);
    const off = this._followOff.copy(this.camera.position).sub(this.target);
    this.distance = Math.max(1, off.length());
    this.polar = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(Math.acos(THREE.MathUtils.clamp(off.y / this.distance, -1, 1)), 0.05, 1.55);
    this.azimuth = Math.atan2(off.x, off.z);
    this.cameraDirty = true;
  }

  private pick(e: PointerEvent): void {
    const rect = this.renderer.domElement.getBoundingClientRect();
    const pointer = new THREE.Vector2(((e.clientX - rect.left) / rect.width) * 2 - 1, -((e.clientY - rect.top) / rect.height) * 2 + 1);
    const raycaster = new THREE.Raycaster();
    raycaster.setFromCamera(pointer, this.camera);
    const modelRunId = this.models.pick(raycaster);
    if (modelRunId) {
      this.opts.onSelect(modelRunId);
      return;
    }
    for (const [mode, mesh] of this.meshes) {
      const hit = raycaster.intersectObject(mesh)[0];
      const runId = hit?.instanceId === undefined ? null : this.instanceRunIds.get(mode)?.[hit.instanceId];
      if (runId) {
        this.opts.onSelect(runId);
        return;
      }
    }
    for (const [mode, mesh] of this.tunnelGlows) {
      const hit = raycaster.intersectObject(mesh)[0];
      const runId = hit?.instanceId === undefined ? null : this.tunnelGlowRunIds.get(mode)?.[hit.instanceId];
      if (runId) {
        this.opts.onSelect(runId);
        return;
      }
    }

    // Sprite and instanced-mesh raycasts can be flaky at a steep camera angle.
    // Keep the visual marker's generous click target by falling back to the
    // nearest projected vehicle, without ever picking scenery.
    let closest: { runId: string; distance: number } | null = null;
    for (const vehicle of this.opts.store.list()) {
      const world = this.projection.project(vehicle.render.lat, vehicle.render.lon);
      world.y = this.terrain.heightAt(world.x, world.z) + (this.tunnelIndex.isInTunnel(vehicle) ? 0.4 : CAR_H / 2);
      world.project(this.camera);
      if (world.z < -1 || world.z > 1) continue;
      const x = rect.left + ((world.x + 1) * rect.width) / 2;
      const y = rect.top + ((1 - world.y) * rect.height) / 2;
      const distance = Math.hypot(e.clientX - x, e.clientY - y);
      if (distance <= 34 && (!closest || distance < closest.distance)) closest = { runId: vehicle.runId, distance };
    }
    if (closest) this.opts.onSelect(closest.runId);
  }

  private readonly onResize = () => {
    this.camera.aspect = window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight;
    this.camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
    this.renderer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);
  };
}

--- features/live/LiveVehicleStore.test.ts ---
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { LiveVehicleStore } from './LiveVehicleStore';
import type { RenderableVehicle } from './liveTypes';

const BASE = { lat: 48.7758, lon: 9.1829 };

function veh(runId: string, over: Partial<RenderableVehicle> = {}): RenderableVehicle {
  return {
    runId,
    lat: BASE.lat,
    lon: BASE.lon,
    mode: 'light-rail',
    status: 'in_transit',
    ...over
  } as RenderableVehicle;
}

describe('LiveVehicleStore.patchOne', () => {
  it('updates one run without despawning the rest of the fleet', () => {
    const store = new LiveVehicleStore();
    const t0 = 1_000_000;
    store.applySnapshot([veh('a'), veh('b'), veh('c')], t0);
    expect(store.size).toBe(3);

    // The 5 s follow poll patches only the followed run, repeatedly. Before the
    // fix this ran a full-snapshot merge and aged out a, b, c after two misses.
    for (let i = 1; i <= 5; i++) {
      store.patchOne(veh('b', { estimatedAt: new Date(t0 + i * 1000).toISOString() }), t0 + i * 1000);
    }

    expect(store.size).toBe(3);
    expect(store.get('a')).toBeDefined();
    expect(store.get('c')).toBeDefined();
  });
});

describe('LiveVehicleStore snapshot despawn', () => {
  it('still ages out runs absent from a full snapshot', () => {
    const store = new LiveVehicleStore();
    store.applySnapshot([veh('a'), veh('b')], 0);
    store.applySnapshot([veh('a')], 1000); // b miss 1
    expect(store.size).toBe(2);
    store.applySnapshot([veh('a')], 2000); // b miss 2 -> despawn
    expect(store.get('b')).toBeUndefined();
  });
});

describe('LiveVehicleStore.upsert speed inference', () => {
  it('forces speed to zero at a stop', () => {
    const store = new LiveVehicleStore();
    store.upsert(veh('a'), 0);
    store.upsert(veh('a', { lat: BASE.lat + 0.001, status: 'at_stop', estimatedAt: new Date(2000).toISOString() }), 2000);
    expect(store.get('a')!.speedMps).toBe(0);
  });

  it('rate-limits inferred speed so a single fix cannot spike it', () => {
    const store = new LiveVehicleStore();
    store.upsert(veh('a'), 0);
    // ~111 m north over 2 s => raw ~55 m/s, but acceleration is capped.
    store.upsert(veh('a', { lat: BASE.lat + 0.001, estimatedAt: new Date(2000).toISOString() }), 2000);
    const speed = store.get('a')!.speedMps;
    expect(speed).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(speed).toBeLessThanOrEqual(1.4 * 2 + 1e-6); // MAX_ACCEL_MPS2 * dt
  });

  it('ignores a stale (older) fix instead of rewinding', () => {
    const store = new LiveVehicleStore();
    store.upsert(veh('a', { estimatedAt: new Date(5000).toISOString() }), 5000);
    const before = store.get('a')!.server;
    store.upsert(veh('a', { lat: BASE.lat + 0.01, estimatedAt: new Date(3000).toISOString() }), 3000);
    expect(store.get('a')!.server).toEqual(before); // position unchanged
  });
});

--- features/live/interpolation.test.ts ---
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { bearingDeg, destinationPoint, easeTowards, haversineM, predict, SNAP_THRESHOLD_M } from './interpolation';

const origin = { lat: 48.7758, lon: 9.1829 };

describe('haversineM', () => {
  it('is zero for identical points', () => {
    expect(haversineM(origin, origin)).toBeCloseTo(0, 5);
  });

  it('matches a known ~1.11 km per 0.01° latitude step', () => {
    const north = { lat: origin.lat + 0.01, lon: origin.lon };
    expect(haversineM(origin, north)).toBeGreaterThan(1100);
    expect(haversineM(origin, north)).toBeLessThan(1120);
  });
});

describe('bearingDeg', () => {
  it('reports due north and due east', () => {
    expect(bearingDeg(origin, { lat: origin.lat + 0.01, lon: origin.lon })).toBeCloseTo(0, 1);
    expect(bearingDeg(origin, { lat: origin.lat, lon: origin.lon + 0.01 })).toBeCloseTo(90, 0);
  });
});

describe('destinationPoint', () => {
  it('round-trips with haversine distance', () => {
    const dest = destinationPoint(origin, 45, 500);
    expect(haversineM(origin, dest)).toBeCloseTo(500, 0);
  });
});

describe('predict', () => {
  it('advances along the bearing at the given speed', () => {
    const p = predict(origin, 90, 10, 5); // 10 m/s east for 5 s = 50 m
    expect(haversineM(origin, p)).toBeCloseTo(50, 0);
  });

  it('holds position when barely moving', () => {
    expect(predict(origin, 90, 0, 5)).toEqual(origin);
  });

  it('caps extrapolation of a stale fix', () => {
    const far = predict(origin, 90, 40, 100_000); // absurd elapsed time
    // Capped at 45 s of travel, not 100000 s.
    expect(haversineM(origin, far)).toBeLessThan(40 * 46);
  });
});

describe('easeTowards', () => {
  it('moves partway toward a nearby target', () => {
    const target = { lat: origin.lat + 0.0005, lon: origin.lon };
    const eased = easeTowards(origin, target, 0.1);
    expect(eased.lat).toBeGreaterThan(origin.lat);
    expect(eased.lat).toBeLessThan(target.lat);
  });

  it('snaps when the error exceeds the threshold', () => {
    const target = destinationPoint(origin, 0, SNAP_THRESHOLD_M + 100);
    expect(easeTowards(origin, target, 0.016)).toEqual(target);
  });
});

--- features/live/vehiclePoolKey.test.ts ---
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { stuttgartCity } from '../../cities/stuttgart';
import { vehiclePoolKey } from './LiveVehicleModels';
import { resolveLiveVehicleDefinition } from './liveVehicleDefinition';
import type { LiveVehicle } from './liveTypes';

const live = (over: Partial<LiveVehicle>): LiveVehicle =>
  ({ runId: 'r', lat: 0, lon: 0, status: 'in_transit', ...over }) as LiveVehicle;

function keyFor(over: Partial<LiveVehicle>): string {
  return vehiclePoolKey(resolveLiveVehicleDefinition(live(over), stuttgartCity));
}

describe('vehiclePoolKey', () => {
  it('shares one pool across lines with the same design + formation', () => {
    // U12 and U6 are both coupled double Stadtbahn formations.
    expect(keyFor({ mode: 'light-rail', line: 'U12' })).toBe(keyFor({ mode: 'light-rail', line: 'U6' }));
  });

  it('separates single from double Stadtbahn formations', () => {
    expect(keyFor({ mode: 'light-rail', line: 'U12' })).not.toBe(keyFor({ mode: 'light-rail', line: 'U14' }));
  });

  it('separates rigid from articulated bus formations', () => {
    // '42' is an articulated line; '70' is rigid (see the VVS Citaro config).
    expect(keyFor({ mode: 'bus', line: '42' })).not.toBe(keyFor({ mode: 'bus', line: '70' }));
  });

  it('separates vehicle kinds', () => {
    expect(keyFor({ mode: 'light-rail', line: 'U5' })).not.toBe(keyFor({ mode: 's-bahn', line: 'S1' }));
  });
});

--- features/live/LiveFollowVehicle.ts ---
import * as THREE from 'three';
import type { InteriorCameraProfile } from '../../domain/transport';
