import type { CameraMode } from '../../world/CameraController';
import type { VehicleDefinition } from '../../vehicles/VehicleDefinition';
import type { VehicleVisualController } from '../../vehicles/VehicleVisualController';
import { vehicleVisualFactory } from '../../vehicles/VehicleVisualFactory';

// The fully-detailed model for the vehicle the user is following. It owns only
// live along-track placement; doors, the interior display, per-view visibility and
// lamps are delegated to the shared VehicleVisualController, so it behaves exactly
// like the simulator's active vehicle. Built once per followed run with the run's
// real line + destination.

const DEG = Math.PI / 180;

export type FollowOffsetPose = (offsetM: number) => { x: number; z: number; bearing: number } | null;

export type FollowUpdate = {
  x: number;
  z: number;
  bearing: number;
  centerY: number; // world Y the caller placed the body at (shared with the camera)
  offsetPose: FollowOffsetPose;
  mode: CameraMode;
  doorsOpen: boolean;
  nextStop: string;
  daylightFactor: number;
  dt: number;
};

export class LiveFollowVehicle {
  readonly root: THREE.Group;
  readonly cars: THREE.Group[];
  readonly trainHalfLengthM: number;
  readonly profile: InteriorCameraProfile;

  private readonly visual: VehicleVisualController;
  private readonly carDefaults: Array<{ pos: THREE.Vector3; quat: THREE.Quaternion }>;

  // Scratch reused each frame (no per-frame allocations).
  private readonly _v = new THREE.Vector3();
  private readonly _q = new THREE.Quaternion();
  private readonly _up = new THREE.Vector3(0, 1, 0);

  constructor(
    private readonly scene: THREE.Scene,
    definition: VehicleDefinition
  ) {
    this.profile = definition.interiorCameraProfile;
    // Build the complete presentation through the shared factory (same one the
    // simulator uses); only the along-track placement below is live-specific.
    this.visual = vehicleVisualFactory.create(definition);
    this.root = this.visual.root;
    this.cars = this.visual.cars;
    this.trainHalfLengthM = this.visual.trainHalfLengthM;
    this.carDefaults = this.cars.map((c) => ({ pos: c.position.clone(), quat: c.quaternion.clone() }));
    scene.add(this.root);
  }

  update(p: FollowUpdate): void {
    // ---- Live along-track placement (the only live-specific part) ----
    this.root.position.set(p.x, p.centerY, p.z);
    this.root.rotation.set(0, -p.bearing * DEG, 0); // yaw keeps root.quaternion in sync

    // Articulate the cars along the track (procedural Stadtbahn); blueprint sections
    // have no per-body offset and stay at their built local transform.
    const invRoot = this._q.copy(this.root.quaternion).invert();
    this.cars.forEach((car, i) => {
      const def = this.carDefaults[i];
      const offsetRaw = car.userData.centerOffsetM;
      const pose = offsetRaw === undefined ? null : p.offsetPose(Number(offsetRaw));
      if (!pose) {
        car.position.copy(def.pos);
        car.quaternion.copy(def.quat);
        return;
      }
      this._v.set(pose.x - p.x, 0, pose.z - p.z).applyQuaternion(invRoot);
      car.position.set(this._v.x, def.pos.y, this._v.z);
      car.quaternion.setFromAxisAngle(this._up, -pose.bearing * DEG).premultiply(invRoot);
    });

    // ---- Shared presentation ----
    this.visual.update({
      doorsOpen: p.doorsOpen,
      nextStop: p.nextStop,
      cameraMode: p.mode,
      cinematicActive: false,
      daylightFactor: p.daylightFactor,
      deltaSeconds: p.dt
    });
  }

  dispose(): void {
    this.scene.remove(this.root);
    this.visual.dispose(); // disposes the instance + interior display
    // Free this instance's own geometries/materials (freshly built, unshared).
    this.root.traverse((o) => {
      const m = o as Partial<THREE.Mesh> & { material?: THREE.Material | THREE.Material[] };
      m.geometry?.dispose?.();
      const mat = m.material;
      if (Array.isArray(mat)) mat.forEach((x) => x.dispose());
      else mat?.dispose?.();
    });
  }
}

--- features/live/useLiveVehicles.ts ---
import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { fetchVehicle, fetchVehicles } from './liveApi';
import { LiveVehicleStore } from './LiveVehicleStore';
import type { LiveMode } from './liveTypes';
import {
  bboxContains,
  modesForTier,
  pollIntervalMs,
  requestBBox,
  tierForZoom,
  type BBox,
  type Tier
} from './viewport';

export type LiveSummary = {
  count: number;
  tier: Tier;
  modesRequested: LiveMode[];
  snapshotAgeSeconds: number | null;
  generatedAt: string | null;
  degraded: boolean;
  positionModel: string | null;
  /** wall-clock ms of the last successful fetch, for the freshness clock. */
  lastUpdatedMs: number | null;
  error: string | null;
};

const INITIAL_SUMMARY: LiveSummary = {
  count: 0,
  tier: 'city',
  modesRequested: [],
  snapshotAgeSeconds: null,
  generatedAt: null,
  degraded: false,
  positionModel: null,
  lastUpdatedMs: null,
  error: null
};

const FOLLOW_INTERVAL_MS = 5_000;
const BACKOFF_BASE_MS = 2_000;
const BACKOFF_CAP_MS = 60_000;

type Options = {
  enabled: boolean;
  userModes: Set<LiveMode>;
  followRunId: string | null;
  /** RFC3339 replay timestamp; a debugging lever, normally undefined. */
  at?: string;
  onFollowEnded?: () => void;
};

/**
 * Polling state machine for live vehicles (concept §5–§6). Owns a
 * {@link LiveVehicleStore}, drives viewport-bounded snapshot polling with tier
 * cadence + pan hysteresis, follows a single run via the per-run endpoint, and
 * throttles on tab visibility. Positions animate in the renderer, not here, so
 * this hook only re-renders on snapshot boundaries (every 8–30 s).
 */
export function useLiveVehicles({ enabled, userModes, followRunId, at, onFollowEnded }: Options) {
  const store = useMemo(() => new LiveVehicleStore(), []);
  const [summary, setSummary] = useState<LiveSummary>(INITIAL_SUMMARY);

  const visibleRef = useRef<{ bbox: BBox; zoom: number } | null>(null);
  const fetchedBBoxRef = useRef<BBox | null>(null);
  const tierRef = useRef<Tier>('city');
  const userModesRef = useRef(userModes);
  userModesRef.current = userModes;
  const atRef = useRef(at);
  atRef.current = at;

  const pollTimer = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);
  const abortRef = useRef<AbortController | null>(null);
  const backoffRef = useRef(0);
  const onFollowEndedRef = useRef(onFollowEnded);
  onFollowEndedRef.current = onFollowEnded;

  const clearPoll = useCallback(() => {
    if (pollTimer.current) clearTimeout(pollTimer.current);
    pollTimer.current = null;
  }, []);

  const scheduleNext = useCallback(
    (delayMs: number, run: () => void) => {
      clearPoll();
      pollTimer.current = setTimeout(run, delayMs);
    },
    [clearPoll]
  );

  const doFetch = useCallback(
    async function fetchLoop() {
      const visible = visibleRef.current;
      if (!enabled || !visible) return;
      const tier = tierForZoom(visible.zoom);
      const reqBBox = requestBBox(visible.bbox);
      const modes = modesForTier(tier, visible.bbox, userModesRef.current);

      abortRef.current?.abort();
      const controller = new AbortController();
      abortRef.current = controller;

      try {
        const snapshot = await fetchVehicles(reqBBox, modes, { at: atRef.current }, controller.signal);
        if (controller.signal.aborted) return;
        store.applySnapshot(snapshot.vehicles);
        fetchedBBoxRef.current = reqBBox;
        tierRef.current = tier;
        backoffRef.current = 0;
        setSummary({
          count: store.size,
          tier,
          modesRequested: modes,
          snapshotAgeSeconds: snapshot.snapshotAgeSeconds ?? null,
          generatedAt: snapshot.generatedAt ?? null,
          degraded: snapshot.degraded ?? false,
          positionModel: snapshot.positionModel ?? null,
          lastUpdatedMs: Date.now(),
          error: null
        });
        scheduleNext(pollIntervalMs(tier), fetchLoop);
      } catch (err) {
        if (controller.signal.aborted || (err as Error)?.name === 'AbortError') return;
        backoffRef.current += 1;
        const delay = Math.min(BACKOFF_BASE_MS * 2 ** (backoffRef.current - 1), BACKOFF_CAP_MS);
        setSummary((prev) => ({ ...prev, error: (err as Error)?.message ?? 'Live traffic request failed' }));
        scheduleNext(delay, fetchLoop);
      }
    },
    [enabled, store, scheduleNext]
  );

  // Called by the map on move/zoom end. Applies pan hysteresis: only refetch when
  // the tier changes or the visible footprint escapes the last fetched bbox.
  const onViewportChange = useCallback(
    (bbox: BBox, zoom: number) => {
      visibleRef.current = { bbox, zoom };
      if (!enabled) return;
      const tier = tierForZoom(zoom);
      const escaped = !fetchedBBoxRef.current || !bboxContains(fetchedBBoxRef.current, bbox);
      if (tier !== tierRef.current || escaped) {
        tierRef.current = tier;
        void doFetch();
      }
    },
    [enabled, doFetch]
  );

  // Enable/disable lifecycle: reset everything on teardown.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!enabled) {
      clearPoll();
      abortRef.current?.abort();
      store.clear();
      setSummary(INITIAL_SUMMARY);
      return;
    }
    // Kick off immediately if the map already reported a viewport.
    if (visibleRef.current) void doFetch();
    return () => {
      clearPoll();
      abortRef.current?.abort();
    };
  }, [enabled, store, doFetch, clearPoll]);

  // User mode-filter changes narrow the request set: refetch now.
  const modeKey = useMemo(() => [...userModes].sort().join(','), [userModes]);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (enabled && visibleRef.current) void doFetch();
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [modeKey]);

  // Pause polling while the tab is hidden; refetch once on return (§5.4).
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!enabled) return;
    const onVisibility = () => {
      if (document.hidden) {
        clearPoll();
        abortRef.current?.abort();
      } else {
        void doFetch();
      }
    };
    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', onVisibility);
    return () => document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', onVisibility);
  }, [enabled, doFetch, clearPoll]);

  // Follow a single run via the per-run endpoint at a fixed 5 s cadence (§6.2).
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!enabled || !followRunId) return;
    let cancelled = false;
    let timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null;
    const controller = new AbortController();

    const refresh = async () => {
      try {
        const v = await fetchVehicle(followRunId, { at: atRef.current }, controller.signal);
        if (cancelled) return;
        store.patchOne(v);
        timer = setTimeout(refresh, FOLLOW_INTERVAL_MS);
      } catch (err) {
        if (cancelled || (err as Error)?.name === 'AbortError') return;
        if ((err as Error & { status?: number })?.status === 404) {
          onFollowEndedRef.current?.(); // run ended — exit follow gracefully
          return;
        }
        timer = setTimeout(refresh, FOLLOW_INTERVAL_MS);
      }
    };
    void refresh();

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
      controller.abort();
      if (timer) clearTimeout(timer);
    };
  }, [enabled, followRunId, store]);

  return { store, summary, onViewportChange };
}

--- features/live/liveTypes.ts ---
// App-facing vocabulary over the generated live-traffic OpenAPI contracts.
//
// The wire types in ../realtime/generated/api-types.ts are the source of truth —
// regenerate with `npm run gen:api` after the backend spec changes. The live
// feature mirrors the realtime feature's convention of re-exporting the wire
// types under nicer names and tightening the few fields the UI relies on.

import type {
  LiveVehicle as WireLiveVehicle,
  VehicleSnapshot as WireVehicleSnapshot,
  LiveTrafficStatus as WireLiveTrafficStatus,
  LivetrafficVehicleMode,
  LivetrafficVehicleStatus,
  LivetrafficPositionSource,
  LivetrafficConfidence
} from '../realtime/generated/api-types';

export type {
  LivetrafficVehicleMode as LiveMode,
  LivetrafficVehicleStatus as VehicleStatus,
  LivetrafficPositionSource as PositionSource,
  LivetrafficConfidence as Confidence
} from '../realtime/generated/api-types';

export type LiveTrafficStatus = WireLiveTrafficStatus;
export type VehicleSnapshot = WireVehicleSnapshot;
export type LiveVehicle = WireLiveVehicle;

/** Every live mode the backend can report, in overview-priority order. */
export const ALL_LIVE_MODES: LivetrafficVehicleMode[] = ['light-rail', 'tram', 's-bahn', 'rail', 'bus'];

/** Rail skeleton shown at overview zoom (buses omitted — invisible at that height). */
export const RAIL_MODES: LivetrafficVehicleMode[] = ['s-bahn', 'rail', 'light-rail', 'tram'];

/**
 * A vehicle the UI can actually render: it always carries an id and a position.
 * The client drops snapshot entries missing these before they reach components.
 */
export type RenderableVehicle = WireLiveVehicle &
  Required<Pick<WireLiveVehicle, 'runId' | 'lat' | 'lon'>>;

export function isRenderable(v: WireLiveVehicle): v is RenderableVehicle {
  return !!v.runId && typeof v.lat === 'number' && typeof v.lon === 'number';
}

// Mode → CSS color, aligned with the transport palette used elsewhere in the app
// (SSB yellow for Stadtbahn/tram, DB green for S-Bahn, red for bus).
const MODE_COLORS: Record<LivetrafficVehicleMode, string> = {
  'light-rail': '#00b8d4',
  tram: '#ffd800',
  's-bahn': '#37a935',
  rail: '#8a6cff',
  bus: '#e0202b'
};

export function modeColor(mode: LivetrafficVehicleMode | undefined): string {
  return (mode && MODE_COLORS[mode]) || '#9aa7b4';
}

export function modeLabel(mode: LivetrafficVehicleMode | undefined): string {
  switch (mode) {
    case 'light-rail':
      return 'Stadtbahn';
    case 'tram':
      return 'Tram';
    case 's-bahn':
      return 'S-Bahn';
    case 'rail':
      return 'Rail';
    case 'bus':
      return 'Bus';
    default:
      return 'Vehicle';
  }
}

/** Delay severity for badge coloring (on time / amber / red per concept §2.3). */
export type DelayLevel = 'on-time' | 'minor' | 'major';

export function delayLevel(delaySeconds: number | undefined): DelayLevel {
  const s = delaySeconds ?? 0;
  if (s > 300) return 'major';
  if (s >= 120) return 'minor';
  return 'on-time';
}

export type { LivetrafficVehicleStatus, LivetrafficPositionSource, LivetrafficConfidence };

--- features/live/interpolation.ts ---
// Smooth motion between snapshots (concept §7). Poll intervals of 8–30 s only
// look alive if vehicles keep moving between updates. This is presentation-only
// easing of server truth — it lives here and in LiveMap, never in Go/WASM.
//
// The map MVP uses bearing + inferred-speed straight-line dead reckoning (the
// concept's fallback for unmatched vehicles). Route-polyline matching is a later
// enhancement that can replace `predict()` without touching callers.

export type LatLon = { lat: number; lon: number };

const EARTH_RADIUS_M = 6_371_000;
const DEG = Math.PI / 180;

/** Great-circle distance in metres. */
export function haversineM(a: LatLon, b: LatLon): number {
  const dLat = (b.lat - a.lat) * DEG;
  const dLon = (b.lon - a.lon) * DEG;
  const lat1 = a.lat * DEG;
  const lat2 = b.lat * DEG;
  const h = Math.sin(dLat / 2) ** 2 + Math.cos(lat1) * Math.cos(lat2) * Math.sin(dLon / 2) ** 2;
  return 2 * EARTH_RADIUS_M * Math.asin(Math.min(1, Math.sqrt(h)));
}

/** Initial bearing from `a` to `b` in degrees (0 = north, clockwise). */
export function bearingDeg(a: LatLon, b: LatLon): number {
  const lat1 = a.lat * DEG;
  const lat2 = b.lat * DEG;
  const dLon = (b.lon - a.lon) * DEG;
  const y = Math.sin(dLon) * Math.cos(lat2);
  const x = Math.cos(lat1) * Math.sin(lat2) - Math.sin(lat1) * Math.cos(lat2) * Math.cos(dLon);
  return (Math.atan2(y, x) / DEG + 360) % 360;
}

/** Point `distM` metres from `origin` along `bearing` degrees. */
export function destinationPoint(origin: LatLon, bearing: number, distM: number): LatLon {
  const ang = distM / EARTH_RADIUS_M;
  const brg = bearing * DEG;
  const lat1 = origin.lat * DEG;
  const lon1 = origin.lon * DEG;
  const lat2 = Math.asin(Math.sin(lat1) * Math.cos(ang) + Math.cos(lat1) * Math.sin(ang) * Math.cos(brg));
  const lon2 =
    lon1 + Math.atan2(Math.sin(brg) * Math.sin(ang) * Math.cos(lat1), Math.cos(ang) - Math.sin(lat1) * Math.sin(lat2));
  return { lat: lat2 / DEG, lon: lon2 / DEG };
}

// Plausible ground speeds cap the inferred speed so a bad snapshot pair can't
// fling a marker across the map (§7).
const MAX_SPEED_MPS = 45; // ~160 km/h, covers S-Bahn/regional rail
const MIN_SPEED_MPS = 0;

export function clampSpeed(mps: number): number {
  if (!Number.isFinite(mps)) return 0;
  return Math.max(MIN_SPEED_MPS, Math.min(MAX_SPEED_MPS, mps));
}

/**
 * Where the vehicle should be *now*, extrapolating from its last server fix
 * along `bearing` at `speedMps` for `secondsSinceFix`. Extrapolation is capped
 * so a stale fix (e.g. a paused poll) doesn't run a marker off into nowhere.
 */
export function predict(serverPos: LatLon, bearing: number, speedMps: number, secondsSinceFix: number): LatLon {
  const capped = Math.max(0, Math.min(secondsSinceFix, 45));
  const dist = clampSpeed(speedMps) * capped;
  if (dist < 0.5) return serverPos;
  return destinationPoint(serverPos, bearing, dist);
}

// A rendered position past this error from the target snaps instead of easing
// (§7: "error above ~300 m snaps immediately").
export const SNAP_THRESHOLD_M = 300;

/**
 * Eases `current` toward `target` by a time-based factor (frame-rate independent).
 * `tauSeconds` is the smoothing time constant; larger = slower correction.
 * Snaps outright when the two are more than {@link SNAP_THRESHOLD_M} apart.
 */
export function easeTowards(current: LatLon, target: LatLon, dtSeconds: number, tauSeconds = 0.4): LatLon {
  if (haversineM(current, target) > SNAP_THRESHOLD_M) return target;
  const alpha = 1 - Math.exp(-dtSeconds / tauSeconds);
  return {
    lat: current.lat + (target.lat - current.lat) * alpha,
    lon: current.lon + (target.lon - current.lon) * alpha
  };
}

--- features/live/LiveView.tsx ---
import { lazy, Suspense, useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { DeparturesModal } from '../realtime/DeparturesModal';
import { searchLocations, nearbyLocations } from '../realtime/api';
import type { EndpointValue } from '../realtime/LocationField';
import { LiveMap } from './LiveMap';
import { LiveModePanel } from './LiveModePanel';
import { LiveVehicleCard } from './LiveVehicleCard';
import { ALL_LIVE_MODES, type LiveMode, type LiveVehicle } from './liveTypes';
import type { LatLon } from './liveProjection';
import type { BBox } from './viewport';
import { useLiveVehicles } from './useLiveVehicles';
import type { CityDefinition } from '../../cities';

// The 3D scene pulls in Three.js + the OSM streamer, so it is code-split and
// loaded only when the user actually zooms into the 3D view.
const Live3DView = lazy(() => import('./Live3DView').then((m) => ({ default: m.Live3DView })));

// Map zoom at/above which the map hands off to the 3D free-flight view, and the
// zoom the map restores to when the 3D view hands back (just below the trigger
// so returning doesn't immediately re-enter 3D).
const ENTER_3D_ZOOM = 16;
const RETURN_MAP_ZOOM = 15;

/** Resolves a stop *name* (all a LiveVehicle carries) to a full station via the
 * existing locations API, using the vehicle position to disambiguate. */
async function resolveStop(name: string, near: { lat: number; lon: number }): Promise<EndpointValue | null> {
  try {
    const byName = await searchLocations(name);
    const exact = byName.find((l) => l.name.toLowerCase() === name.toLowerCase()) ?? byName[0];
    if (exact) return { id: exact.id, name: exact.name, lat: exact.lat, lon: exact.lon };
  } catch {
    // fall through to a positional lookup
  }
  try {
    const near1 = await nearbyLocations(near.lat, near.lon, 900);
    const match = near1.find((l) => l.name.toLowerCase().includes(name.toLowerCase())) ?? near1[0];
    if (match) return { id: match.id, name: match.name, lat: match.lat, lon: match.lon };
  } catch {
    // ignore
  }
  return null;
}

/**
 * The full-screen Live workspace (map-based). Composes the viewport-tier polling
 * hook with the map renderer, the slim HUD, the selection card, and the existing
 * departures board. Rendered lazily and only for Stuttgart with live enabled.
 */
export function LiveView({ onExit, city }: { onExit: () => void; city?: CityDefinition }) {
  const [userModes, setUserModes] = useState<Set<LiveMode>>(() => new Set(ALL_LIVE_MODES));
  const [selectedRunId, setSelectedRunId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [followRunId, setFollowRunId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [selectedVehicle, setSelectedVehicle] = useState<LiveVehicle | null>(null);
  const [departuresStation, setDeparturesStation] = useState<EndpointValue | null>(null);
  const [departuresOpen, setDeparturesOpen] = useState(false);

  // Map ↔ 3D workspace. `origin` fixes the 3D projection at hand-off; `returnView`
  // restores the map camera when coming back.
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<'map' | '3d'>('map');
  const [origin, setOrigin] = useState<LatLon | null>(null);
  const [returnView, setReturnView] = useState<{ lat: number; lon: number; zoom: number } | undefined>(undefined);
  const focus3DRef = useRef<LatLon | null>(null);

  const onFollowEnded = useCallback(() => setFollowRunId(null), []);
  const { store, summary, onViewportChange } = useLiveVehicles({
    enabled: true,
    userModes,
    followRunId,
    onFollowEnded
  });

  // Map viewport: feed polling, and hand off to 3D once zoomed in far enough.
  const handleMapViewport = useCallback(
    (bbox: BBox, zoom: number) => {
      onViewportChange(bbox, zoom);
      if (zoom >= ENTER_3D_ZOOM) {
        setOrigin({ lat: (bbox.latMin + bbox.latMax) / 2, lon: (bbox.lonMin + bbox.lonMax) / 2 });
        setSelectedRunId(null);
        setMode('3d');
      }
    },
    [onViewportChange]
  );

  // 3D viewport: feed polling and remember the focus for the return trip.
  const handle3DViewport = useCallback(
    (bbox: BBox, zoom: number) => {
      onViewportChange(bbox, zoom);
      focus3DRef.current = { lat: (bbox.latMin + bbox.latMax) / 2, lon: (bbox.lonMin + bbox.lonMax) / 2 };
    },
    [onViewportChange]
  );

  const backToMap = useCallback(() => {
    const f = focus3DRef.current;
    if (f) setReturnView({ lat: f.lat, lon: f.lon, zoom: RETURN_MAP_ZOOM });
    setMode('map');
  }, []);

  // Keep the selection card in step with incoming snapshots / follow patches.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!selectedRunId) {
      setSelectedVehicle(null);
      return;
    }
    const read = () => {
      const tracked = store.get(selectedRunId);
      if (tracked) setSelectedVehicle(tracked.data);
    };
    read();
    const id = setInterval(read, 1000);
    return () => clearInterval(id);
  }, [selectedRunId, store, summary]);

  // A selected run that leaves the store closes the card and any follow.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (selectedRunId && !store.get(selectedRunId) && !selectedVehicle) {
      setSelectedRunId(null);
      setFollowRunId((f) => (f === selectedRunId ? null : f));
    }
  }, [summary, selectedRunId, selectedVehicle, store]);

  const toggleMode = useCallback((mode: LiveMode) => {
    setUserModes((prev) => {
      const next = new Set(prev);
      if (next.has(mode)) next.delete(mode);
      else next.add(mode);
      // Never allow an empty filter — the API requires at least one mode.
      return next.size ? next : prev;
    });
  }, []);

  const handleSelect = useCallback((runId: string | null) => {
    setSelectedRunId(runId);
  }, []);

  const toggleFollow = useCallback(() => {
    setFollowRunId((f) => (f === selectedRunId ? null : selectedRunId));
  }, [selectedRunId]);

  const openDepartures = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!selectedVehicle?.nextStop) return;
    const station = await resolveStop(selectedVehicle.nextStop, {
      lat: selectedVehicle.lat ?? 48.7827,
      lon: selectedVehicle.lon ?? 9.18
    });
    setDeparturesStation(station);
    setDeparturesOpen(true);
  }, [selectedVehicle]);

  const emptyHint = useMemo(
    () => mode === 'map' && summary.lastUpdatedMs !== null && summary.count === 0 && !summary.error,
    [mode, summary]
  );

  if (mode === '3d' && origin) {
    return (
      <Suspense fallback={<div className="lv-3d-root" />}>
        <Live3DView
          store={store}
          origin={origin}
          summary={summary}
          userModes={userModes}
          onToggleMode={toggleMode}
          onViewport={handle3DViewport}
          onBackToMap={backToMap}
          onExitLive={onExit}
          selectedVehicle={selectedVehicle}
          following={followRunId === selectedRunId}
          onSelect={handleSelect}
          onToggleFollow={toggleFollow}
          onStopFollow={() => setFollowRunId(null)}
          onDepartures={openDepartures}
          onCloseSelection={() => setSelectedRunId(null)}
          city={city}
        />
      </Suspense>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="lv-root">
      <LiveMap
        store={store}
        onViewportChange={handleMapViewport}
        onSelect={handleSelect}
        selectedRunId={selectedRunId}
        followRunId={followRunId}
        initialView={returnView}
      />
      <LiveModePanel summary={summary} userModes={userModes} onToggleMode={toggleMode} onExit={onExit} />
      {emptyHint && <div className="lv-empty">No vehicles in view — zoom out or check the mode filters.</div>}
      {selectedVehicle && (
        <LiveVehicleCard
          vehicle={selectedVehicle}
          following={followRunId === selectedRunId}
          onToggleFollow={toggleFollow}
          onDepartures={openDepartures}
          onClose={() => setSelectedRunId(null)}
        />
      )}
      {departuresOpen && (
        <DeparturesModal onClose={() => setDeparturesOpen(false)} initialStation={departuresStation} />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

--- features/live/LiveEntryButton.tsx ---
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { fetchLiveStatus } from './liveApi';

// Session-cached /live/status probe (concept §2.1): resolved once and shared, but
// re-attempted after a failure so a transient outage doesn't disable Live for good.
type Gate = 'loading' | 'enabled' | 'disabled' | 'error';
let cached: Promise<Gate> | null = null;

function probe(): Promise<Gate> {
  if (!cached) {
    cached = fetchLiveStatus()
      .then((s) => (s.enabled ? 'enabled' : 'disabled') as Gate)
      .catch(() => {
        cached = null; // allow a later retry
        return 'error' as Gate;
      });
  }
  return cached;
}

/**
 * The Live entry point. Rendered only for live-eligible cities (§1 gate); the
 * button itself is enabled only when the backend reports live traffic available.
 */
export function LiveEntryButton({ eligible, onEnter }: { eligible: boolean; onEnter: () => void }) {
  const [gate, setGate] = useState<Gate>('loading');

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!eligible) return;
    let active = true;
    void probe().then((g) => active && setGate(g));
    return () => {
      active = false;
    };
  }, [eligible]);

  if (!eligible) return null;

  const ready = gate === 'enabled';
  const title = ready
    ? 'Watch real VVS vehicles live'
    : gate === 'loading'
      ? 'Checking live traffic…'
      : 'Live traffic unavailable';

  return (
    <button className="lv-entry" title={title} disabled={!ready} onClick={onEnter}>
      Live
    </button>
  );
}

--- features/live/viewport.test.ts ---
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import {
  bboxContains,
  bboxWidthKm,
  expandBBox,
  modesForTier,
  pollIntervalMs,
  quantizeBBox,
  requestBBox,
  tierForZoom,
  type BBox
} from './viewport';

const stuttgart: BBox = { latMin: 48.74, lonMin: 9.14, latMax: 48.81, lonMax: 9.24 };

describe('tierForZoom', () => {
  it('maps zoom levels to LOD tiers', () => {
    expect(tierForZoom(10)).toBe('city');
    expect(tierForZoom(11)).toBe('city');
    expect(tierForZoom(12)).toBe('district');
    expect(tierForZoom(14)).toBe('district');
    expect(tierForZoom(15)).toBe('street');
    expect(tierForZoom(18)).toBe('street');
  });
});

describe('pollIntervalMs', () => {
  it('polls smaller viewports more often', () => {
    expect(pollIntervalMs('city')).toBeGreaterThan(pollIntervalMs('district'));
    expect(pollIntervalMs('district')).toBeGreaterThan(pollIntervalMs('street'));
  });
});

describe('modesForTier', () => {
  it('requests the rail skeleton only at city tier', () => {
    const modes = modesForTier('city', stuttgart);
    expect(modes).not.toContain('bus');
    expect(modes).toContain('s-bahn');
  });

  it('adds buses at district tier only for a small bbox', () => {
    const wide: BBox = { latMin: 48.5, lonMin: 9.0, latMax: 48.9, lonMax: 9.5 };
    const small: BBox = { latMin: 48.77, lonMin: 9.17, latMax: 48.79, lonMax: 9.2 };
    expect(modesForTier('district', wide)).not.toContain('bus');
    expect(modesForTier('district', small)).toContain('bus');
  });

  it('requests all modes at street tier', () => {
    expect(modesForTier('street', stuttgart)).toContain('bus');
  });

  it('lets the user filter only narrow the automatic set', () => {
    const modes = modesForTier('street', stuttgart, new Set(['tram']));
    expect(modes).toEqual(['tram']);
  });

  it('falls back to the auto set when the user filters everything out', () => {
    // City tier never includes bus, so a bus-only filter yields an empty
    // intersection; the API needs a non-empty list, so we send the auto set.
    const modes = modesForTier('city', stuttgart, new Set(['bus']));
    expect(modes.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(modes).not.toContain('bus');
  });
});

describe('bbox math', () => {
  it('expands symmetrically by the margin fraction', () => {
    const b: BBox = { latMin: 0, lonMin: 0, latMax: 10, lonMax: 20 };
    expect(expandBBox(b, 0.1)).toEqual({ latMin: -1, lonMin: -2, latMax: 11, lonMax: 22 });
  });

  it('quantizes outward to the grid', () => {
    const b: BBox = { latMin: 48.7449, lonMin: 9.1401, latMax: 48.8151, lonMax: 9.2399 };
    const q = quantizeBBox(b, 0.01);
    expect(q.latMin).toBeCloseTo(48.74, 6);
    expect(q.latMax).toBeCloseTo(48.82, 6);
    expect(q.lonMin).toBeCloseTo(9.14, 6);
    expect(q.lonMax).toBeCloseTo(9.24, 6);
  });

  it('returns a grid-aligned bbox that fully covers the visible viewport', () => {
    const visible: BBox = { latMin: 48.751, lonMin: 9.151, latMax: 48.759, lonMax: 9.159 };
    const req = requestBBox(visible);
    // Aligned to the 0.01° grid.
    for (const v of [req.latMin, req.lonMin, req.latMax, req.lonMax]) {
      expect(Math.round(v * 100) / 100).toBeCloseTo(v, 6);
    }
    // And it contains the visible viewport (so small pans stay covered).
    expect(bboxContains(req, visible)).toBe(true);
  });

  it('detects containment for hysteresis', () => {
    const outer: BBox = { latMin: 0, lonMin: 0, latMax: 10, lonMax: 10 };
    expect(bboxContains(outer, { latMin: 2, lonMin: 2, latMax: 8, lonMax: 8 })).toBe(true);
    expect(bboxContains(outer, { latMin: -1, lonMin: 2, latMax: 8, lonMax: 8 })).toBe(false);
  });

  it('estimates width in km', () => {
    expect(bboxWidthKm(stuttgart)).toBeGreaterThan(5);
    expect(bboxWidthKm(stuttgart)).toBeLessThan(12);
  });
});

--- features/live/LiveTunnelLayer.ts ---
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { smoothTrackXZ } from './liveTrackGeometry';
import type { Projection } from './liveProjection';
import type { TrackedVehicle } from './LiveVehicleStore';
import { LiveTunnelIndex } from './LiveTunnelIndex';

const TUNNEL_DEPTH_M = 5;
// Match Explore's tunnel bore (GuidewayLayer): a wide, smooth lining so the
// follow camera stays comfortably inside on curves instead of feeling cramped.
const BORE_RADIUS_M = 8.2;
const BORE_RADIAL_SEGMENTS = 18;

/**
 * Builds the same route-derived lining/portals used by Explore, but only for
 * rail alignments that currently have a visible live vehicle. The surface glow
 * remains visible from above; the lining is revealed when following a vehicle
 * underground.
 */
export class LiveTunnelLayer {
  readonly group = new THREE.Group();
  private readonly built = new Set<string>();
  private terrainRevision = -1;

  constructor(
    private readonly projection: Projection,
    private readonly index: LiveTunnelIndex,
    private readonly groundHeightAt: (x: number, z: number) => number = () => 0
  ) {
    this.group.name = 'Live route tunnel linings';
  }

  update(vehicles: TrackedVehicle[], terrainRevision: number): void {
    // Ground height is baked into the lining, so never build against the flat
    // placeholder (revision 0) and rebuild once the terrain changes — otherwise
    // the bore sits at y≈-5 while the train rides real terrain metres away.
    if (terrainRevision === 0) return;
    if (terrainRevision !== this.terrainRevision) {
      this.terrainRevision = terrainRevision;
      this.clear();
    }
    for (const vehicle of vehicles) {
      const route = this.index.routeFor(vehicle);
      if (!route || this.built.has(route.id)) continue;
      this.buildRoute(route);
      this.built.add(route.id);
    }
  }

  /** Drops all built linings so they can be rebuilt at corrected heights. */
  private clear(): void {
    this.group.traverse((object) => {
      const mesh = object as THREE.Mesh;
      mesh.geometry?.dispose?.();
    });
    this.group.clear();
    this.built.clear();
  }

  dispose(): void {
    this.group.traverse((object) => {
      const mesh = object as THREE.Mesh;
      mesh.geometry?.dispose?.();
      const materials = Array.isArray(mesh.material) ? mesh.material : [mesh.material];
      materials.filter(Boolean).forEach((material) => material.dispose());
    });
    this.group.removeFromParent();
  }

  private buildRoute(route: { geoPoints?: [number, number][]; tunnelMask?: boolean[] }): void {
    const points = route.geoPoints ?? [];
    const mask = route.tunnelMask ?? [];
    if (points.length < 2 || !mask.some(Boolean)) return;

    const liningMaterial = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x777a78, roughness: 0.92, side: THREE.BackSide });
    const portalMaterial = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x555957, roughness: 0.88 });
    const lightMaterial = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xffedbd });
    for (const [start, end] of ranges(mask)) {
      if (end - start < 1) continue;
      const routePoints = points.slice(Math.max(0, start - 1), Math.min(points.length, end + 2));
      // Smooth the bore centerline the same way as the overground rails and the
      // motion path, so a followed vehicle stays centered in the tunnel on curves.
      const curvePoints = smoothTrackXZ(
        routePoints.map(([lat, lon]) => this.projection.project(lat, lon))
      );
      for (const p of curvePoints) p.y = this.groundHeightAt(p.x, p.z) - TUNNEL_DEPTH_M;
      if (curvePoints.length < 2) continue;
      const curve = new THREE.CatmullRomCurve3(curvePoints, false, 'centripetal');
      const segments = Math.max(16, curvePoints.length * 3);
      const lining = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.TubeGeometry(curve, segments, BORE_RADIUS_M, BORE_RADIAL_SEGMENTS, false), liningMaterial);
      lining.name = 'Live tunnel lining';
      this.group.add(lining);

      const lights = curve.getSpacedPoints(Math.max(3, Math.floor(curve.getLength() / 40))).map((point) => point.add(new THREE.Vector3(0, 5.8, 0)));
      if (lights.length > 1) this.group.add(new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.TubeGeometry(new THREE.CatmullRomCurve3(lights), segments, 0.075, 5), lightMaterial));

      for (const t of [0, 1]) {
        const portal = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.TorusGeometry(BORE_RADIUS_M, 0.5, 8, 24), portalMaterial);
        portal.position.copy(curve.getPointAt(t));
        portal.quaternion.setFromUnitVectors(new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, 1), curve.getTangentAt(t).normalize());
        this.group.add(portal);
      }
    }
  }
}

function ranges(mask: boolean[]): Array<[number, number]> {
  const result: Array<[number, number]> = [];
  let start = -1;
  for (let i = 0; i <= mask.length; i++) {
    if (mask[i] && start < 0) start = i;
    if (!mask[i] && start >= 0) {
      result.push([start, i - 1]);
      start = -1;
    }
  }
  return result;
}

export { TUNNEL_DEPTH_M };

--- features/live/liveVehicleDefinition.test.ts ---
import { describe, it, expect, vi, beforeAll, afterAll } from 'vitest';
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { stuttgartCity } from '../../cities/stuttgart';
import { resolveRouteVehicleDefinition } from '../../vehicles/VehicleDefinition';
import { buildTransitVehicle } from '../../vehicles';
import type { DemoRoute } from '../../route';
import type { TransportModeDefinition } from '../../domain/transport';
import { resolveLiveVehicleDefinition } from './liveVehicleDefinition';
import type { LiveVehicle } from './liveTypes';

const stadtbahnMode = stuttgartCity.transportModes.find((m) => m.vehicleKind === 'stadtbahn') as TransportModeDefinition;
const u12Route = { line: 'U12', destination: 'Dürrlewang' } as unknown as DemoRoute;
const u12Live = { runId: 'r1', lat: 0, lon: 0, mode: 'light-rail', line: 'U12', direction: 'Dürrlewang', status: 'in_transit' } as LiveVehicle;

describe('vehicle definition parity', () => {
  it('resolves the same Stuttgart U12 design for simulator and live', () => {
    const routeDefinition = resolveRouteVehicleDefinition(u12Route, stuttgartCity, stadtbahnMode);
    const liveDefinition = resolveLiveVehicleDefinition(u12Live, stuttgartCity);

    expect(liveDefinition.vehicleDesignId).toBe(routeDefinition.vehicleDesignId);
    expect(liveDefinition.interiorCameraProfile).toBe(routeDefinition.interiorCameraProfile);
    expect(liveDefinition.vehicleKind).toBe(routeDefinition.vehicleKind);
    expect(liveDefinition.lineAccentColor).toBe(routeDefinition.lineAccentColor);
    // U12 is a coupled double formation in both paths.
    expect(routeDefinition.formation?.doubleFormation).toBe(true);
    expect(liveDefinition.formation?.doubleFormation).toBe(true);
  });
});

// Building a vehicle paints canvas labels; jsdom has no 2D context, so stub a
// permissive one for these assertions.
describe('followed-vehicle capabilities', () => {
  let getContext: unknown;
  beforeAll(() => {
    const ctx = new Proxy(
      {},
      {
        get(_t, prop) {
          if (prop === 'measureText') return () => ({ width: 10 });
          if (prop === 'createLinearGradient' || prop === 'createRadialGradient' || prop === 'createPattern') {
            return () => ({ addColorStop() {} });
          }
          return () => {};
        },
        set() {
          return true;
        }
      }
    );
    getContext = vi.spyOn(HTMLCanvasElement.prototype, 'getContext').mockReturnValue(ctx as CanvasRenderingContext2D);
  });
  afterAll(() => (getContext as { mockRestore(): void }).mockRestore());

  it('builds a Stuttgart U12 with an interior, doors and displays', () => {
    const definition = resolveLiveVehicleDefinition(u12Live, stuttgartCity);
    const instance = buildTransitVehicle({
      definition,
      makeInteriorDisplayMaterial: () => new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial()
    });
    // Interior/see-through capability: window bands + glass drive the passenger view.
    expect(instance.windowBands.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(instance.glassMaterial).toBeDefined();
    expect(instance.doors.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(instance.cabDisplays.length).toBeGreaterThan(0); // next-stop / route display meshes
    // Double formation => four Tango sections.
    expect(instance.cars.length).toBe(4);
  });
});

--- features/live/liveVehicleDefinition.ts ---
import type { CityDefinition } from '../../cities';
import type { VehicleKind } from '../../domain/transport';
import {
  defaultInteriorCameraProfile,
  defaultLineAccentColor,
  resolveFormation,
  type VehicleDefinition
} from '../../vehicles/VehicleDefinition';
import type { LiveMode, LiveVehicle } from './liveTypes';

// Maps a live traffic mode to a vehicle kind. Light-rail/tram share the Stadtbahn
// body; s-bahn/rail share the S-Bahn body.
function liveModeToKind(mode: LiveMode | undefined): VehicleKind {
  if (mode === 'bus') return 'bus';
  if (mode === 's-bahn' || mode === 'rail') return 's-bahn';
  return 'stadtbahn';
}

/**
 * Resolves a live vehicle into the same {@link VehicleDefinition} the simulator's
 * route resolver produces for the equivalent vehicle. It reads the vehicle's kind's
 * mode from the city so design id, interior-camera profile and accent match the
 * simulator; only when no city is available does it fall back to per-kind defaults.
 */
export function resolveLiveVehicleDefinition(vehicle: LiveVehicle, city: CityDefinition | undefined): VehicleDefinition {
  const kind = liveModeToKind(vehicle.mode);
  const mode = city?.transportModes.find((m) => m.vehicleKind === kind);
  const line = vehicle.line ?? '';
  return {
    cityId: city?.id,
    modeId: mode?.id ?? kind,
    vehicleKind: kind,
    vehicleDesignId: city?.vehicleDesignId ?? mode?.vehicleDesignId,
    interiorCameraProfile: mode?.interiorCameraProfile ?? defaultInteriorCameraProfile(kind),
    line,
    destination: vehicle.direction ?? '',
    lineAccentColor: mode?.lineAccentColor ?? defaultLineAccentColor(kind),
    formation: resolveFormation(kind, line, city?.id)
  };
}

--- features/live/LiveVehicleModels.ts ---
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { buildTransitVehicle } from '../../vehicles';
import type { VehicleDefinition } from '../../vehicles/VehicleDefinition';
import type { CityDefinition } from '../../cities';
import { makeLabelTexture } from '../../labels';
import { modeColor, type LiveMode } from './liveTypes';
import { resolveLiveVehicleDefinition } from './liveVehicleDefinition';
import type { LiveVehicleStore, TrackedVehicle } from './LiveVehicleStore';
import type { Projection } from './liveProjection';

/** World-space pose along a run's track at a metre offset ahead/behind its center. */
export type OffsetPose = (runId: string, offsetM: number) => { x: number; z: number; bearing: number } | null;

// Level-of-detail for the 3D view (concept §4.1): near vehicles get the full
// procedural models (the same kind-builders the ride view uses), everything else
// stays as the instanced blocks in Live3DScene. Line-label sprites fade in with
// altitude. Models and labels are pooled and reused — never built per frame.

const DEG = Math.PI / 180;
// Below this camera altitude, nearby vehicles upgrade to full models.
const MODEL_ALT_M = 900;
const MODEL_RADIUS_M = 1400;
const MODEL_CAP = 45;
// Labels fade in a bit higher than models and cover a wider ring.
const LABEL_ALT_M = 2400;
const LABEL_RADIUS_M = 2600;
const LABEL_CAP = 60;

// Overview models pool by resolved definition, so vehicles that build the same
// design + formation share one template (e.g. every double Stadtbahn line), while
// single/double Stadtbahn and rigid/articulated bus each get their own.
export function vehiclePoolKey(definition: VehicleDefinition): string {
  return [
    definition.vehicleDesignId ?? definition.vehicleKind,
    definition.formation?.doubleFormation ? 'double' : 'single',
    definition.formation?.articulated ? 'articulated' : 'rigid'
  ].join(':');
}

type NearHit = { v: TrackedVehicle; x: number; z: number; d2: number; bearing: number };

/** Owns the near-field vehicle models and floating line labels for Live3DScene. */
export class LiveVehicleModels {
  // Keyed by resolved-definition pool key (see vehiclePoolKey).
  private readonly templates = new Map<string, THREE.Group | null>();
  private readonly pools = new Map<string, THREE.Group[]>();
  private readonly labelPool: THREE.Sprite[] = [];
  private readonly labelTextures = new Map<string, THREE.Texture>();
  // Scratch objects reused across the per-car articulation loop (never per-frame allocs).
  private readonly _v = new THREE.Vector3();
  private readonly _q = new THREE.Quaternion();
  private readonly _up = new THREE.Vector3(0, 1, 0);

  constructor(
    private readonly scene: THREE.Scene,
    private readonly projection: Projection,
    private readonly city?: CityDefinition
  ) {}

  /**
   * Places models on the nearest vehicles and labels on a wider ring, both gated
   * by camera altitude. Returns the runIds that received a full model so the
   * caller can omit them from the instanced blocks.
   */
  update(
    store: LiveVehicleStore,
    target: THREE.Vector3,
    altitudeM: number,
    isInTunnel: (vehicle: TrackedVehicle) => boolean,
    groundHeightAt: (x: number, z: number) => number,
    resolveWorld: (vehicle: TrackedVehicle) => { x: number; z: number; bearing: number },
    cameraPos: THREE.Vector3,
    offsetPose: OffsetPose,
    excludeRunId: string | null = null
  ): Set<string> {
    const modeled = new Set<string>();

    // Project once (route-snapped) and reuse for both models and labels.
    const hits: NearHit[] = [];
    const wantModels = altitudeM < MODEL_ALT_M;
    const wantLabels = altitudeM < LABEL_ALT_M;
    if (wantModels || wantLabels) {
      const maxR = Math.max(wantModels ? MODEL_RADIUS_M : 0, wantLabels ? LABEL_RADIUS_M : 0);
      const maxR2 = maxR * maxR;
      for (const v of store.list()) {
        const w = resolveWorld(v);
        const dx = w.x - target.x;
        const dz = w.z - target.z;
        const d2 = dx * dx + dz * dz;
        if (d2 <= maxR2) hits.push({ v, x: w.x, z: w.z, d2, bearing: w.bearing });
      }
      hits.sort((a, b) => a.d2 - b.d2);
    }

    // ---- Models (nearest, closest ring) ----
    const usedByVariant = new Map<string, number>();
    if (wantModels) {
      const r2 = MODEL_RADIUS_M * MODEL_RADIUS_M;
      let placed = 0;
      for (const hit of hits) {
        if (placed >= MODEL_CAP) break;
        if (hit.d2 > r2) break;
        if (hit.v.runId === excludeRunId) continue; // dedicated follow vehicle renders this run
        const definition = resolveLiveVehicleDefinition(hit.v.data, this.city);
        const group = this.acquireModel(definition, vehiclePoolKey(definition), usedByVariant);
        if (!group) continue;
        const yOff = isInTunnel(hit.v) ? -4.7 : 0.3;
        this.placeVehicle(group, hit, groundHeightAt, offsetPose, yOff);
        group.userData.liveRunId = hit.v.runId;
        group.visible = true;
        modeled.add(hit.v.runId);
        placed++;
      }
    }
    this.hideUnusedModels(usedByVariant);

    // ---- Labels (wider ring) ----
    let labelCount = 0;
    if (wantLabels) {
      const r2 = LABEL_RADIUS_M * LABEL_RADIUS_M;
      for (const hit of hits) {
        if (labelCount >= LABEL_CAP) break;
        if (hit.d2 > r2) break;
        const line = hit.v.data.line;
        if (!line) continue;
        const sprite = this.acquireLabel(labelCount, line, hit.v.data.mode);
        const y = groundHeightAt(hit.x, hit.z) + 7;
        sprite.position.set(hit.x, y, hit.z);
        // Scale with distance to the camera so the badge stays a roughly constant,
        // compact on-screen size instead of ballooning when zoomed in on a vehicle.
        const dist = Math.hypot(hit.x - cameraPos.x, y - cameraPos.y, hit.z - cameraPos.z);
        const w = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(dist * 0.05, 3, 60);
        sprite.scale.set(w, w * 0.42, 1);
        sprite.userData.liveRunId = hit.v.runId;
        sprite.visible = true;
        labelCount++;
      }
    }
    for (let i = labelCount; i < this.labelPool.length; i++) this.labelPool[i].visible = false;

    return modeled;
  }

  dispose(): void {
    for (const group of this.pools.values()) for (const g of group) this.scene.remove(g);
    for (const sprite of this.labelPool) {
      this.scene.remove(sprite);
      (sprite.material as THREE.SpriteMaterial).dispose();
    }
    for (const tex of this.labelTextures.values()) tex.dispose();
    // Template geometries/materials are disposed by Live3DScene's scene sweep.
  }

  pick(raycaster: THREE.Raycaster): string | null {
    const labels = raycaster.intersectObjects(this.labelPool.filter((sprite) => sprite.visible), false);
    const label = labels[0]?.object.userData.liveRunId;
    if (typeof label === 'string') return label;

    const roots = [...this.pools.values()].flat().filter((group) => group.visible);
    const hit = raycaster.intersectObjects(roots, true)[0]?.object;
    let object: THREE.Object3D | null | undefined = hit;
    while (object) {
      const runId = object.userData.liveRunId;
      if (typeof runId === 'string') return runId;
      object = object.parent;
    }
    return null;
  }

  // Places a model. When the run is matched to a track, each car (a child group
  // carrying userData.centerOffsetM) is swung to its own along-track pose so the
  // train bends through curves — the same articulation the Explore view uses.
  // Otherwise cars are restored to their straight built-in layout.
  private placeVehicle(
    group: THREE.Group,
    hit: NearHit,
    groundHeightAt: (x: number, z: number) => number,
    offsetPose: OffsetPose,
    yOff: number
  ): void {
    const rootY = groundHeightAt(hit.x, hit.z) + yOff;
    group.position.set(hit.x, rootY, hit.z);
    group.rotation.set(0, -hit.bearing * DEG, 0); // yaw keeps group.quaternion in sync

    const cars = group.userData.liveCars as THREE.Group[] | undefined;
    if (!cars || cars.length < 2) return; // single-body vehicle: nothing to articulate

    const invRoot = this._q.copy(group.quaternion).invert();
    for (const car of cars) {
      const def = car.userData as { defaultPos: THREE.Vector3; defaultQuat: THREE.Quaternion };
      // Only cars with an explicit along-body offset articulate (procedural
      // Stadtbahn); blueprint sections bake their offset into meshes, so they stay
      // rigid at their built local transform.
      const offsetRaw = car.userData.centerOffsetM;
      const pose = offsetRaw === undefined ? null : offsetPose(hit.v.runId, Number(offsetRaw));
      if (!pose) {
        car.position.copy(def.defaultPos);
        car.quaternion.copy(def.defaultQuat);
        continue;
      }
      // Car center in the root's local frame (same height plane as the root).
      this._v.set(pose.x - hit.x, 0, pose.z - hit.z).applyQuaternion(invRoot);
      car.position.set(this._v.x, def.defaultPos.y, this._v.z);
      // Local yaw = inverse(root) * world-yaw(-pose.bearing).
      car.quaternion
        .setFromAxisAngle(this._up, -pose.bearing * DEG)
        .premultiply(invRoot);
    }
  }

  private acquireModel(definition: VehicleDefinition, key: string, used: Map<string, number>): THREE.Group | null {
    const pool = this.pools.get(key) ?? this.pools.set(key, []).get(key)!;
    const i = used.get(key) ?? 0;
    used.set(key, i + 1);
    let group = pool[i];
    if (!group) {
      const template = this.getTemplate(definition, key);
      if (!template) return null;
      group = template.clone(true); // shares geometry/materials with the template
      this.captureCars(group);
      pool.push(group);
      this.scene.add(group);
    }
    return group;
  }

  // Records a clone's cars (ordered by liveCarIndex) and each car's built-in
  // (straight) local transform, so placeVehicle can bend the articulable ones and
  // restore them, and the interior camera can anchor to a car.
  private captureCars(group: THREE.Group): void {
    const indexed: Array<{ index: number; car: THREE.Group }> = [];
    group.traverse((child) => {
      if (child instanceof THREE.Group && child.userData.liveCarIndex !== undefined) {
        child.userData.defaultPos = child.position.clone();
        child.userData.defaultQuat = child.quaternion.clone();
        indexed.push({ index: child.userData.liveCarIndex as number, car: child });
      }
    });
    indexed.sort((a, b) => a.index - b.index);
    group.userData.liveCars = indexed.map((e) => e.car);
  }

  private hideUnusedModels(used: Map<string, number>): void {
    for (const [key, pool] of this.pools) {
      for (let i = used.get(key) ?? 0; i < pool.length; i++) pool[i].visible = false;
    }
  }

  private getTemplate(definition: VehicleDefinition, key: string): THREE.Group | null {
    if (this.templates.has(key)) return this.templates.get(key) ?? null;
    let root: THREE.Group | null = null;
    try {
      // Build from the shared definition — the same design/formation the follow
      // vehicle and the simulator resolve. A dummy display material is fine; the
      // overview model has no live next-stop board.
      const instance = buildTransitVehicle({
        definition,
        makeInteriorDisplayMaterial: () => new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0x0b0f14 })
      });
      root = instance.root;
      root.visible = false; // template stays hidden; only clones are shown
      // Tag cars so clones can recover the ordered car list (used for articulation).
      instance.cars.forEach((car, index) => {
        car.userData.liveCarIndex = index;
      });
      this.scene.add(root); // keep it in the graph so dispose() sweeps its resources
    } catch (error) {
      console.warn(`Live 3D: could not build ${key} vehicle model; falling back to blocks.`, error);
    }
    this.templates.set(key, root);
    return root;
  }

  private acquireLabel(index: number, line: string, mode: LiveMode | undefined): THREE.Sprite {
    let sprite = this.labelPool[index];
    if (!sprite) {
      sprite = new THREE.Sprite(new THREE.SpriteMaterial({ depthTest: true }));
      sprite.scale.set(20, 9, 1);
      this.labelPool.push(sprite);
      this.scene.add(sprite);
    }
    const key = `${line}|${mode ?? ''}`;
    let texture = this.labelTextures.get(key);
    if (!texture) {
      texture = makeLabelTexture(line, { width: 220, height: 96, bg: modeColor(mode), fg: '#0b0f14' });
      this.labelTextures.set(key, texture);
    }
    const material = sprite.material as THREE.SpriteMaterial;
    if (material.map !== texture) {
      material.map = texture;
      material.needsUpdate = true;
    }
    return sprite;
  }
}

--- features/selection/SelectionPanel.tsx ---
import type { BootstrapResult } from '../../app/useAppBootstrap';
import { useAppState } from '../../app/useAppState';
import { TransportModeTabs } from './TransportModeTabs';

/** City / transport-mode / route / start-station / ride-mode / time selectors. */
export function SelectionPanel({ catalog }: { catalog: BootstrapResult }) {
  const { state, dispatch } = useAppState();
  const route = catalog.routes.find((entry) => entry.id === state.selectedRouteId);
  const stations = route?.stations.slice(0, Math.max(1, route.stations.length - 1)) ?? [];

  return (
    <>
      <div className="toolbar city-toolbar">
        <select
          aria-label="City"
          value={state.selectedCityId}
          onChange={(e) => dispatch({ type: 'city-selected', cityId: e.target.value })}
        >
          {catalog.cities.map((city) => (
            <option key={city.id} value={city.id}>{city.name}</option>
          ))}
        </select>
      </div>
      {catalog.currentCity && (
        <TransportModeTabs
          definitions={catalog.currentCity.transportModes}
          available={catalog.availableModes}
          selected={state.selectedTransportMode}
          onSelect={(mode) => dispatch({ type: 'transport-mode-selected', mode })}
        />
      )}
      <div className="toolbar">
        <select
          aria-label="Line"
          value={state.selectedRouteId}
          onChange={(e) => dispatch({ type: 'route-selected', routeId: e.target.value })}
        >
          {catalog.routes.map((item) => (
            <option key={item.id} value={item.id}>{item.line} to {item.destination}</option>
          ))}
        </select>
        <select
          aria-label="Start station"
          value={state.selectedStartStation}
          onChange={(e) => dispatch({ type: 'start-station-selected', stationIndex: Number(e.target.value) })}
        >
          {stations.map((station, index) => (
            <option key={`${station.name}-${index}`} value={index}>{station.name}</option>
          ))}
        </select>
        <select
          aria-label="Mode"
          value={state.selectedMode}
          onChange={(e) => dispatch({ type: 'ride-mode-selected', mode: e.target.value as 'ride' | 'driver' })}
        >
          <option value="ride">Ride</option>
          <option value="driver">Drive</option>
        </select>
        <select
          aria-label="Time of day"
          value={state.selectedTime}
          onChange={(e) => dispatch({ type: 'time-selected', time: e.target.value === 'auto' ? 'auto' : Number(e.target.value) })}
        >
          <option value="auto">Local time (auto)</option>
          <option value="6">06:00 · Dawn</option>
          <option value="9">09:00 · Morning</option>
          <option value="13">13:00 · Day</option>
        </select>
      </div>
    </>
  );
}

--- features/selection/TransportModeTabs.tsx ---
import type { TransportModeDefinition, TransportMode } from '../../domain/transport';

export function TransportModeTabs({
  definitions,
  available,
  selected,
  onSelect
}: {
  definitions: TransportModeDefinition[];
  available: TransportMode[];
  selected: TransportMode;
  onSelect: (mode: TransportMode) => void;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="transport-tabs" role="tablist" aria-label="Means of transport">
      {definitions.map((definition) => {
        const enabled = available.includes(definition.id);
        return (
          <button
            role="tab"
            key={definition.id}
            disabled={!enabled}
            className={selected === definition.id ? 'active' : ''}
            aria-selected={selected === definition.id}
            onClick={() => onSelect(definition.id)}
          >
            {definition.label}
          </button>
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );
}
