--- minimap.ts ---
import L from 'leaflet';
import 'leaflet/dist/leaflet.css';
import type { DemoRoute } from './route';
import { bearing, geoPoints, interpolate, pointDistance, type GeoPoint } from './minimap/route-position';

export class VehicleMiniMap {
  private map: L.Map;
  private routeLine?: L.Polyline;
  private marker: L.Marker;
  private routePoints: GeoPoint[] = [];
  private cumulativeDistances: number[] = [];
  private readonly markerEl = document.createElement('div');

  constructor(target: HTMLElement) {
    this.map = L.map(target, {
      zoomControl: false,
      attributionControl: true,
      dragging: false,
      scrollWheelZoom: false,
      doubleClickZoom: false,
      boxZoom: false,
      keyboard: false
    });

    L.tileLayer('https://{s}.tile.openstreetmap.org/{z}/{x}/{y}.png', {
      maxZoom: 19,
      attribution: '&copy; OpenStreetMap contributors'
    }).addTo(this.map);

    this.markerEl.className = 'vehicle-marker';
    this.markerEl.innerHTML = '<span></span>';
    this.marker = L.marker([48.7758, 9.1829], {
      icon: L.divIcon({
        className: 'vehicle-marker-wrap',
        html: this.markerEl,
        iconSize: [24, 24],
        iconAnchor: [12, 12]
      }),
      interactive: false
    }).addTo(this.map);
  }

  setRoute(route: DemoRoute): void {
    this.routePoints = geoPoints(route);
    this.cumulativeDistances = [0];
    for (let i = 1; i < this.routePoints.length; i++) {
      this.cumulativeDistances.push(this.cumulativeDistances[i - 1] + pointDistance(this.routePoints[i - 1], this.routePoints[i]));
    }

    this.routeLine?.remove();
    if (this.routePoints.length < 2) return;

    const latLngs = this.routePoints.map((p) => L.latLng(p.lat, p.lon));
    this.routeLine = L.polyline(latLngs, {
      color: route.color ?? '#ffd900',
      weight: 5,
      opacity: 0.9
    }).addTo(this.map);
    this.map.fitBounds(this.routeLine.getBounds(), { padding: [12, 12], animate: false });
    this.update(0);
  }

  update(distanceM: number): void {
    if (this.routePoints.length < 2) return;
    const total = this.cumulativeDistances[this.cumulativeDistances.length - 1] || 1;
    const target = Math.max(0, Math.min(total, distanceM));
    let index = 1;
    while (index < this.cumulativeDistances.length - 1 && this.cumulativeDistances[index] < target) index++;

    const prevDistance = this.cumulativeDistances[index - 1];
    const nextDistance = this.cumulativeDistances[index];
    const t = (target - prevDistance) / Math.max(1, nextDistance - prevDistance);
    const pos = interpolate(this.routePoints[index - 1], this.routePoints[index], t);
    const heading = bearing(this.routePoints[index - 1], this.routePoints[index]);

    this.marker.setLatLng([pos.lat, pos.lon]);
    this.markerEl.style.transform = `rotate(${heading}deg)`;
    this.map.panTo([pos.lat, pos.lon], { animate: false });
  }

  invalidateSize(): void {
    this.map.invalidateSize();
  }

  /** Tears down the Leaflet map and its DOM/event listeners. */
  dispose(): void {
    this.routeLine?.remove();
    this.marker.remove();
    this.map.remove();
  }
}

--- world/stations/StationStyleRegistry.ts ---
import type { StationStyleProvider } from './types';

/**
 * Keyed lookup of station style providers by `stationStyleId`. Cities/modes
 * register their provider once; StationLayer resolves by id at build time.
 * New styles register without editing existing dispatch logic, preserving
 * multi-city support (e.g. `vvs-stadtbahn`, `db-sbahn`, `odessa-tram`).
 */
export class StationStyleRegistry {
  private readonly providers = new Map<string, StationStyleProvider>();

  register(provider: StationStyleProvider): this {
    this.providers.set(provider.id, provider);
    return this;
  }

  has(id: string): boolean {
    return this.providers.has(id);
  }

  resolveOptional(id: string | undefined): StationStyleProvider | undefined {
    return id ? this.providers.get(id) : undefined;
  }
}

--- world/stations/stationStyle.test.ts ---
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { createStationStyleRegistry } from './index';
import { VvsStadtbahnStationProvider } from './styles/vvs/VvsStadtbahnStationProvider';
import { VvsStationCatalog } from './styles/vvs/VvsStationCatalog';
import { createSeededRandom, hashString } from './variation';
import type { StationPlacement, RouteStation } from './types';

function placementAt(station: RouteStation, underground: boolean): StationPlacement {
  return {
    station,
    index: 0,
    t: 0,
    center: new THREE.Vector3(),
    tangent: new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, 1),
    yaw: 0,
    underground
  };
}

describe('station style registry', () => {
  it('resolves the vvs-stadtbahn provider and no others by default', () => {
    const registry = createStationStyleRegistry();
    expect(registry.has('vvs-stadtbahn')).toBe(true);
    expect(registry.resolveOptional('vvs-stadtbahn')?.id).toBe('vvs-stadtbahn');
    expect(registry.resolveOptional('db-sbahn')).toBeUndefined();
    expect(registry.resolveOptional(undefined)).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it('classifies surface vs underground stops by archetype', () => {
    const provider = createStationStyleRegistry().resolveOptional('vvs-stadtbahn')!;
    // A name deliberately absent from the bundled catalog, so it uses defaults.
    const station: RouteStation = { name: 'Unclassified Test Stop', distanceM: 500 };

    const surface = provider.resolve(station, { layer: {} as never, placement: placementAt(station, false) });
    expect(surface.archetype).toBe('surface-side');
    expect(surface.platform.layout).toBe('side');

    const underground = provider.resolve(station, { layer: {} as never, placement: placementAt(station, true) });
    expect(underground.archetype).toBe('underground-side');
  });

  it('resolves the same appearance for a station across sessions (deterministic seed)', () => {
    const provider = new VvsStadtbahnStationProvider();
    const station: RouteStation = { name: 'Bopser', distanceM: 900 };
    const a = provider.resolve(station, { layer: {} as never, placement: placementAt(station, false) });
    const b = provider.resolve(station, { layer: {} as never, placement: placementAt(station, false) });
    expect(a.seed).toBe(b.seed);
    expect(a.furniture).toEqual(b.furniture);
  });

  it('lets catalog overrides win over seeded defaults', () => {
    const catalog = new VvsStationCatalog({
      schemaVersion: 1,
      style: 'vvs-stadtbahn',
      stations: {
        Bopser: { archetype: 'surface-interchange', furniture: { benches: 3, ticketMachines: 2 } }
      }
    });
    const provider = new VvsStadtbahnStationProvider(catalog);
    const station: RouteStation = { name: 'Bopser', distanceM: 900 };
    const resolved = provider.resolve(station, { layer: {} as never, placement: placementAt(station, false) });
    expect(resolved.archetype).toBe('surface-interchange');
    expect(resolved.furniture?.benches).toBe(3);
    expect(resolved.furniture?.ticketMachines).toBe(2);
  });

  it('applies bundled catalog classifications (Degerloch interchange)', () => {
    const provider = new VvsStadtbahnStationProvider();
    const station: RouteStation = { name: 'Degerloch', distanceM: 100 };
    const resolved = provider.resolve(station, { layer: {} as never, placement: placementAt(station, false) });
    expect(resolved.archetype).toBe('surface-interchange');
    expect(resolved.furniture?.dfiDisplays).toBe(2);
    expect(resolved.environment?.plaza).toBe(true);
  });
});

describe('seeded variation', () => {
  it('hashes strings to stable non-zero seeds', () => {
    expect(hashString('vvs:Bopser')).toBe(hashString('vvs:Bopser'));
    expect(hashString('vvs:Bopser')).not.toBe(hashString('vvs:Degerloch'));
    expect(hashString('')).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });

  it('produces a repeatable sequence for a given seed', () => {
    const a = createSeededRandom(1234);
    const b = createSeededRandom(1234);
    expect([a.next(), a.next(), a.next()]).toEqual([b.next(), b.next(), b.next()]);
    expect(createSeededRandom(42).int(1, 2)).toBe(createSeededRandom(42).int(1, 2));
  });
});

--- world/stations/placement.ts ---
import * as THREE from 'three';
import type { WorldLayerContext } from '../layers/WorldLayerContext';
import type { RouteStation, StationPlacement } from './types';

/**
 * Resolves a route stop's position, orientation, and undergrounding from shared
 * route geometry. Mirrors the sampling the legacy per-guideway builders did
 * inline, so a stop lands at exactly the same place regardless of style.
 */
export function resolveStationPlacement(
  station: RouteStation,
  index: number,
  layer: WorldLayerContext
): StationPlacement {
  const t = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(station.distanceM / layer.route.routeLengthM, 0, 1);
  const center = layer.curve.getPointAt(t);
  const tangent = layer.curve.getTangentAt(t).normalize();
  const yaw = Math.atan2(tangent.x, tangent.z);
  const underground = layer.stationIsUnderground(t);
  return { station, index, t, center, tangent, yaw, underground };
}

/** Along-route parameter range covering a platform of `lengthM` centered on the stop. */
export function platformSpanT(
  distanceM: number,
  lengthM: number,
  routeLengthM: number
): { startT: number; endT: number } {
  const halfM = lengthM / 2;
  return {
    startT: THREE.MathUtils.clamp((distanceM - halfM) / routeLengthM, 0, 1),
    endT: THREE.MathUtils.clamp((distanceM + halfM) / routeLengthM, 0, 1)
  };
}

--- world/stations/variation.ts ---
/**
 * Deterministic per-station variation. A stable string (e.g. `vvs:<stationId>`)
 * hashes to a seed, and the seeded generator drives *appearance-only* details
 * (canopy length, furniture counts, poster content). Topology, accessibility,
 * track geometry, and platform height must never be randomized — those come
 * from catalog data. Determinism keeps a station identical across sessions.
 */

// 32-bit FNV-1a string hash → non-zero seed.
export function hashString(value: string): number {
  let hash = 0x811c9dc5;
  for (let i = 0; i < value.length; i += 1) {
    hash ^= value.charCodeAt(i);
    hash = Math.imul(hash, 0x01000193);
  }
  return (hash >>> 0) || 1;
}

export type SeededRandom = {
  next(): number; // [0, 1)
  int(minInclusive: number, maxInclusive: number): number;
  pick<T>(items: readonly T[]): T;
};

// mulberry32 PRNG — small, fast, well-distributed for cosmetic variation.
export function createSeededRandom(seed: number): SeededRandom {
  let state = seed >>> 0;
  const next = (): number => {
    state = (state + 0x6d2b79f5) | 0;
    let t = Math.imul(state ^ (state >>> 15), 1 | state);
    t = (t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), 61 | t)) ^ t;
    return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
  };
  return {
    next,
    int: (min, max) => min + Math.floor(next() * (max - min + 1)),
    pick: (items) => items[Math.floor(next() * items.length)]
  };
}

--- world/stations/types.ts ---
import type * as THREE from 'three';
import type { StationConfig } from '../../simulator/simulator-types';
import type { WorldLayerContext } from '../layers/WorldLayerContext';

/**
 * Data-driven station kit types.
 *
 * The station visual system separates station *topology* (where platforms and
 * tracks go) from station *appearance* (canopies, walls, colors, furniture).
 * A city/mode selects a {@link StationStyleProvider} by `stationStyleId`; the
 * provider resolves each route stop into a {@link StationVisualDefinition} and
 * builds it from shared, parameterized components.
 *
 * These types are intentionally rendering-agnostic data: no Three.js geometry
 * lives here, only the description a provider needs to build a stop.
 */

// One route stop as seen by the station system. Route packages currently only
// carry name/distance/schedule; providers invent the rest from archetypes.
export type RouteStation = StationConfig;

// Coarse station family. Drives which archetype builder a provider selects and
// how much furniture/architecture a stop gets. Not every value is implemented
// by every provider; unknown archetypes fall back to the provider default.
export type StationArchetype =
  | 'surface-side'
  | 'surface-island'
  | 'surface-staggered'
  | 'surface-interchange'
  | 'cutting'
  | 'underground-side'
  | 'underground-island'
  | 'underground-interchange'
  | 'terminus'
  | 'landmark';

// Platform topology: layout and dimensions. Must not be randomized — this is
// real infrastructure, not decoration.
export type StationPlatformDefinition = {
  layout: 'side' | 'island' | 'mixed';
  lengthM: number;
  widthM: number;
  trackOffsetsM?: number[];
  platformOffsetsM?: number[];
  heightM: number;
  curved?: boolean;
  staggerM?: number;
};

// Appearance-only descriptors. Colors, finishes, and roof kind never determine
// where a platform goes.
export type StationArchitectureDefinition = {
  family?: string;
  roof?: 'none' | 'short-canopy' | 'full-canopy' | 'hall' | 'custom';
  wallFinish?: string;
  columnStyle?: string;
  accentColor?: string;
  customAsset?: string;
};

// Accessibility is topology, not decoration: recorded explicitly per stop.
export type StationAccessDefinition = {
  type: 'stairs' | 'ramp' | 'lift' | 'escalator' | 'crossing';
  side: 'left' | 'right' | 'center';
  offsetM: number;
  direction?: string;
};

export type StationFurnitureDefinition = {
  shelters?: number;
  benches?: number;
  ticketMachines?: number;
  dfiDisplays?: number;
  bins?: number;
  bicycleParking?: boolean;
};

export type StationEnvironmentDefinition = {
  retainingWall?: 'left' | 'right' | 'both';
  roadCrossing?: boolean;
  plaza?: boolean;
  vegetation?: 'none' | 'urban' | 'suburban' | 'wooded';
};

// The full resolved description of a single stop's appearance and topology.
export type StationVisualDefinition = {
  stationId: string;
  displayName: string;
  archetype: StationArchetype;
  platform: StationPlatformDefinition;
  architecture?: StationArchitectureDefinition;
  access?: StationAccessDefinition[];
  furniture?: StationFurnitureDefinition;
  environment?: StationEnvironmentDefinition;
  // Deterministic seed for controlled procedural variation of appearance-only
  // details (canopy length, bench count, poster content, ...). Never used to
  // vary topology, accessibility, or track geometry.
  seed?: number;
};

// Where a stop sits along the route curve, resolved once from route geometry
// and shared by every component that builds the stop. Underground/tangent/yaw
// come from the same helpers the legacy StationLayer used, so placement is
// identical to the previous per-guideway builders.
export type StationPlacement = {
  station: RouteStation;
  index: number;
  t: number;
  center: THREE.Vector3;
  tangent: THREE.Vector3;
  yaw: number;
  underground: boolean;
};

// Inputs available while turning a route stop into a visual definition.
export type StationResolveContext = {
  layer: WorldLayerContext;
  placement: StationPlacement;
};

// Shared build state passed to every station build. Materials and the scene
// root are created once per route so stops reuse GPU resources, matching the
// legacy builders which allocated materials once outside the station loop.
export type StationBuildContext = {
  layer: WorldLayerContext;
  // StationLayer.root; provider adds each stop's group here.
  root: THREE.Group;
};

// A built stop with a single owning group and disposal, so StationLayer can
// treat every style uniformly.
export type StationInstance = {
  root: THREE.Group;
  dispose: () => void;
};

/**
 * Resolves route stops into visual definitions and builds them. One provider
 * per city/mode station style (e.g. `vvs-stadtbahn`, `db-sbahn`). Providers own
 * their component kit; StationLayer only coordinates.
 */
export interface StationStyleProvider {
  readonly id: string;
  resolve(station: RouteStation, context: StationResolveContext): StationVisualDefinition;
  build(
    definition: StationVisualDefinition,
    placement: StationPlacement,
    context: StationBuildContext
  ): StationInstance;
}

--- world/stations/styles/vvs/VvsStadtbahnStationProvider.ts ---
import { createStationScaffold, disposeStation } from '../../StationFactory';
import type {
  RouteStation,
  StationBuildContext,
  StationInstance,
  StationPlacement,
  StationResolveContext,
  StationStyleProvider,
  StationVisualDefinition
} from '../../types';
import { createSeededRandom, hashString } from '../../variation';
import { VvsStationCatalog } from './VvsStationCatalog';
import { VvsStationMaterials } from './VvsStationMaterials';
import { buildVvsSidePlatforms } from './components/platform';
import { buildVvsUndergroundHall, buildVvsUndergroundSideLighting } from './components/undergroundHall';
import { buildVvsCanopy } from './components/canopy';
import { buildVvsFurniture } from './components/furniture';
import { buildVvsIslandStation } from './components/island';
import { buildVvsDfiBoards, buildVvsRetainingWall } from './components/environment';
import { buildVvsStationSigns, buildVvsWayfinding } from './components/signage';

// Legacy single-item platform layout, used as the default so unlisted stops
// keep close to the original look (seeded counts still allow 1–2 benches).
const DEFAULT_SURFACE_PLATFORM = { layout: 'side', lengthM: 76, widthM: 8, heightM: 0.22, curved: true } as const;

/**
 * SSB/VVS Stadtbahn station style. Resolves each stop into a semantic
 * definition (archetype + topology from the catalog, appearance filled from
 * seeded variation) and assembles it from shared, parameterized components.
 *
 * The palette is created lazily and reused across every stop in a route.
 */
export class VvsStadtbahnStationProvider implements StationStyleProvider {
  readonly id = 'vvs-stadtbahn';
  private materials: VvsStationMaterials | undefined;

  constructor(private readonly catalog = new VvsStationCatalog()) {}

  resolve(station: RouteStation, context: StationResolveContext): StationVisualDefinition {
    const underground = context.placement.underground;
    const override = this.catalog.get(station.name);
    const seed = override?.seed ?? hashString(`vvs:${station.name}`);
    const rng = createSeededRandom(seed);

    const archetype = override?.archetype ?? (underground ? 'underground-side' : 'surface-side');
    const platform = { ...DEFAULT_SURFACE_PLATFORM, ...override?.platform };

    // Appearance-only counts: catalog wins, else a small deterministic value.
    const furniture = {
      shelters: override?.furniture?.shelters ?? 1,
      benches: override?.furniture?.benches ?? rng.int(1, 2),
      ticketMachines: override?.furniture?.ticketMachines ?? 1,
      dfiDisplays: override?.furniture?.dfiDisplays ?? 0,
      bins: override?.furniture?.bins ?? rng.int(1, 2),
      bicycleParking: override?.furniture?.bicycleParking
    };

    return {
      stationId: station.name,
      displayName: override?.displayName ?? station.name,
      archetype,
      platform,
      architecture: override?.architecture,
      environment: override?.environment,
      furniture,
      seed
    };
  }

  build(
    definition: StationVisualDefinition,
    placement: StationPlacement,
    context: StationBuildContext
  ): StationInstance {
    const materials = (this.materials ??= new VvsStationMaterials());
    const { station, underground } = placement;
    const rng = createSeededRandom(definition.seed ?? hashString(`vvs:${station.name}`));
    // Canopy length varies within a tight band so surface stops read as related
    // but not identical. Never affects platform length or track geometry.
    const canopyLengthM = 22 + rng.next() * 5;
    const furniture = definition.furniture ?? {};
    const counts = {
      benches: furniture.benches ?? 1,
      ticketMachines: furniture.ticketMachines ?? 1,
      bins: furniture.bins ?? 1
    };

    const scaffold = createStationScaffold(
      `${underground ? 'Underground' : 'Surface'} station ${station.name}`,
      placement
    );
    context.root.add(scaffold.root);

    // Surface island platforms are a distinct topology; everything else uses the
    // paired side-platform layout (surface or underground).
    if (!underground && definition.archetype === 'surface-island') {
      buildVvsIslandStation(
        scaffold.root,
        scaffold.local,
        context.layer,
        materials,
        station,
        definition.platform,
        canopyLengthM,
        counts
      );
      return {
        root: scaffold.root,
        dispose: () => disposeStation(scaffold.root, materials.shared)
      };
    }

    if (underground) buildVvsUndergroundHall(scaffold.local, materials);

    // Curved platforms follow the route in world space → untransformed root.
    buildVvsSidePlatforms(scaffold.root, context.layer, materials, station, definition.platform);

    const retainingWall = definition.environment?.retainingWall;
    const dfiDisplays = definition.furniture?.dfiDisplays ?? 0;

    for (const side of [-1, 1]) {
      if (underground) buildVvsUndergroundSideLighting(scaffold.local, materials, side);
      else buildVvsCanopy(scaffold.local, materials, side, canopyLengthM);
      buildVvsFurniture(scaffold.local, materials, side, underground, counts);
      buildVvsStationSigns(scaffold.local, context.layer, side, station, underground);

      // Hillside/cutting stops carry a retaining wall behind the platform.
      if (!underground && (retainingWall === 'both' || (retainingWall === 'left' && side === -1) ||
        (retainingWall === 'right' && side === 1))) {
        buildVvsRetainingWall(scaffold.local, materials, side);
      }
      // Interchanges add track-facing departure boards (side 1 only to avoid
      // doubling; count spreads them along the platform).
      if (!underground && dfiDisplays > 0 && side === 1) {
        buildVvsDfiBoards(scaffold.local, context.layer, materials, side, station, dfiDisplays);
      }
    }

    if (underground) buildVvsWayfinding(scaffold.local, station);

    return {
      root: scaffold.root,
      dispose: () => disposeStation(scaffold.root, materials.shared)
    };
  }
}

--- world/stations/styles/vvs/VvsStationMaterials.ts ---
import * as THREE from 'three';

/**
 * SSB/VVS Stadtbahn material palette, created once per route and reused across
 * every stop so stations share GPU resources. Values are lifted verbatim from
 * the legacy StationLayer.buildRailStations so the modular components render an
 * identical result.
 */
export class VvsStationMaterials {
  readonly platform = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xb9b7b0, roughness: 0.9 });
  readonly platformEdge = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x696b6c, roughness: 0.82 });
  readonly tactile = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xeee7cf, roughness: 0.92 });
  readonly ssbBlue = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x174a73, roughness: 0.45, metalness: 0.28 });
  readonly glass = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
    color: 0xa9d0dd,
    roughness: 0.12,
    metalness: 0.08,
    transparent: true,
    opacity: 0.38,
    side: THREE.DoubleSide
  });
  readonly canopy = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xd7dde0, roughness: 0.38, metalness: 0.34 });
  readonly bench = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x234e68, roughness: 0.68 });
  readonly machine = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xffd800, roughness: 0.48 });
  readonly light = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xffffff, emissive: 0xfff4cf, emissiveIntensity: 2.1 });
  readonly undergroundWall = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xcacbc8, roughness: 0.88 });
  // Weathered concrete for hillside/cutting retaining walls.
  readonly concrete = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x9a978d, roughness: 0.96 });
  // Dark DFI departure-board face; amber text is faked with the label texture.
  readonly dfi = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0x111820 });

  // Materials owned by this palette (reused across stops); per-stop disposal
  // must skip these so a removed station doesn't free shared resources.
  readonly shared = new Set<THREE.Material>([
    this.platform,
    this.platformEdge,
    this.tactile,
    this.ssbBlue,
    this.glass,
    this.canopy,
    this.bench,
    this.machine,
    this.light,
    this.undergroundWall,
    this.concrete,
    this.dfi
  ]);

  dispose(): void {
    for (const material of this.shared) material.dispose();
  }
}

--- world/stations/styles/vvs/components/furniture.ts ---
import * as THREE from 'three';
import type { VvsStationMaterials } from '../VvsStationMaterials';

export type VvsFurnitureCounts = {
  benches: number;
  ticketMachines: number;
  bins: number;
};

// Anchor positions reproduce the legacy single-item layout; extra items are
// distributed to additional deterministic slots so a count of 1 is unchanged.
const BENCH_SLOTS = [8, -6, 20];
const MACHINE_SLOTS = [-8, -2];
const BIN_SLOTS = [14, 2];

function slots(anchors: number[], count: number): number[] {
  return anchors.slice(0, Math.max(1, Math.min(count, anchors.length)));
}

/**
 * Per-side platform furniture: benches, ticket machines, a timetable case, and
 * bins. Counts come from resolved appearance data; a count of 1 matches the
 * legacy rail builder exactly. Benches shift slightly along underground
 * platforms. Screen/timetable faces are per-stop MeshBasicMaterials.
 */
export function buildVvsFurniture(
  local: THREE.Group,
  materials: VvsStationMaterials,
  side: number,
  underground: boolean,
  counts: VvsFurnitureCounts
): void {
  const benchShift = underground ? 1 : 0;
  for (const z of slots(BENCH_SLOTS, counts.benches)) {
    const bench = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(1.15, 0.48, 4.8), materials.bench);
    bench.position.set(side * 8.8, 0.78, z + benchShift);
    bench.castShadow = true;
    local.add(bench);

    const backrest = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.22, 1.15, 4.8), materials.bench);
    backrest.position.set(side * 9.32, 1.25, z + benchShift);
    local.add(backrest);
  }

  for (const z of slots(MACHINE_SLOTS, counts.ticketMachines)) {
    const ticketMachine = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.72, 1.9, 1.05), materials.machine);
    ticketMachine.position.set(side * 9.25, 1.18, z);
    ticketMachine.castShadow = true;
    local.add(ticketMachine);

    const ticketScreen = new THREE.Mesh(
      new THREE.PlaneGeometry(0.48, 0.42),
      new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0x82c9ef })
    );
    ticketScreen.position.set(side * 9.63, 1.52, z);
    ticketScreen.rotation.y = (side * Math.PI) / 2;
    local.add(ticketScreen);
  }

  const timetableCase = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.16, 1.85, 1.35), materials.ssbBlue);
  timetableCase.position.set(side * 9.45, 1.46, -13);
  local.add(timetableCase);

  const timetable = new THREE.Mesh(
    new THREE.PlaneGeometry(1.08, 1.52),
    new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xf6f3e9 })
  );
  timetable.position.set(side * 9.55, 1.46, -13);
  timetable.rotation.y = (side * Math.PI) / 2;
  local.add(timetable);

  for (const z of slots(BIN_SLOTS, counts.bins)) {
    const bin = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.32, 0.36, 0.95, 12), materials.ssbBlue);
    bin.position.set(side * 9.35, 0.7, z);
    local.add(bin);
  }
}

--- world/stations/styles/vvs/components/island.ts ---
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { makeLabelTexture } from '../../../../../labels';
import type { WorldLayerContext } from '../../../../layers/WorldLayerContext';
import type { RouteStation, StationPlatformDefinition } from '../../../types';
import { platformSpanT } from '../../../placement';
import type { VvsStationMaterials } from '../VvsStationMaterials';
import type { VvsFurnitureCounts } from './furniture';

// A central island platform sits between the two fixed tracks (±2.1m), so it is
// narrow with edges facing outward on both sides. Track geometry is owned by
// the guideway layer and is never moved here.
const ISLAND_HALF_WIDTH_M = 1.7;
const DEFAULT_LENGTH_M = 76;
const BENCH_SLOTS = [6, -8, 18];
const BIN_SLOTS = [12, 0];

/**
 * Surface island-platform station: one central platform, a central canopy, and
 * track-facing signage on both edges. Platform strips follow the route in world
 * space (attach to `root`); canopy, furniture, and signs attach to `local`.
 */
export function buildVvsIslandStation(
  root: THREE.Group,
  local: THREE.Group,
  layer: WorldLayerContext,
  materials: VvsStationMaterials,
  station: RouteStation,
  platform: StationPlatformDefinition | undefined,
  canopyLengthM: number,
  counts: VvsFurnitureCounts
): void {
  const lengthM = platform?.lengthM ?? DEFAULT_LENGTH_M;
  const { startT, endT } = platformSpanT(station.distanceM, lengthM, layer.route.routeLengthM);

  const deck = new THREE.Mesh(
    layer.makeRouteStrip(0, ISLAND_HALF_WIDTH_M * 2, startT, endT, 24, 0.22),
    materials.platform
  );
  deck.receiveShadow = true;
  deck.name = `Island platform ${station.name}`;
  root.add(deck);

  for (const side of [-1, 1]) {
    const edge = new THREE.Mesh(
      layer.makeRouteStrip(side * (ISLAND_HALF_WIDTH_M - 0.1), 0.35, startT, endT, 28, 0.245),
      materials.platformEdge
    );
    root.add(edge);
    const tactile = new THREE.Mesh(
      layer.makeRouteStrip(side * (ISLAND_HALF_WIDTH_M - 0.45), 0.5, startT, endT, 28, 0.25),
      materials.tactile
    );
    tactile.name = `Island tactile edge ${station.name}`;
    root.add(tactile);
  }

  // Central canopy: single ridge roof over the platform with a row of posts.
  const center = 8;
  const half = canopyLengthM / 2;
  const roof = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(3.6, 0.24, canopyLengthM), materials.canopy);
  roof.position.set(0, 4.35, center);
  roof.castShadow = true;
  local.add(roof);
  const ridge = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.5, 0.34, canopyLengthM + 0.2), materials.ssbBlue);
  ridge.position.set(0, 4.5, center);
  local.add(ridge);
  for (const z of [center - half + 2, center, center + half - 2]) {
    const post = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.18, 4.2, 0.18), materials.ssbBlue);
    post.position.set(0, 2.1, z);
    post.castShadow = true;
    local.add(post);
    const lamp = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(2.6, 0.12, 0.22), materials.light);
    lamp.position.set(0, 4.15, z);
    local.add(lamp);
  }

  // Back-to-back central benches and bins.
  for (const z of BENCH_SLOTS.slice(0, Math.max(1, Math.min(counts.benches, BENCH_SLOTS.length)))) {
    const bench = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(1.6, 0.48, 3.6), materials.bench);
    bench.position.set(0, 0.78, z);
    bench.castShadow = true;
    local.add(bench);
    const backrest = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.22, 1.0, 3.6), materials.bench);
    backrest.position.set(0, 1.2, z);
    local.add(backrest);
  }
  for (const z of BIN_SLOTS.slice(0, Math.max(1, Math.min(counts.bins, BIN_SLOTS.length)))) {
    const bin = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.32, 0.36, 0.95, 12), materials.ssbBlue);
    bin.position.set(0, 0.7, z);
    local.add(bin);
  }

  // Central ticket machine facing along the platform.
  const machine = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(1.05, 1.9, 0.72), materials.machine);
  machine.position.set(0, 1.18, -13);
  machine.castShadow = true;
  local.add(machine);

  // Track-facing name signs on a central mast (both directions).
  const signTexture = makeLabelTexture(`${layer.route.line}  ${station.name}`, {
    width: 1024,
    height: 180,
    bg: '#174a73',
    fg: '#ffffff'
  });
  for (const z of [-21, 21]) {
    for (const side of [-1, 1]) {
      const sign = new THREE.Mesh(
        new THREE.PlaneGeometry(7.4, 1.3),
        new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map: signTexture, side: THREE.FrontSide })
      );
      sign.position.set(side * 1.75, 3.2, z);
      sign.rotation.y = (side * Math.PI) / 2;
      local.add(sign);
      layer.stationMarkers.push(sign);
    }
  }
}

--- world/stations/styles/vvs/components/environment.ts ---
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { makeLabelTexture } from '../../../../../labels';
import type { WorldLayerContext } from '../../../../layers/WorldLayerContext';
import type { RouteStation } from '../../../types';
import type { VvsStationMaterials } from '../VvsStationMaterials';

/**
 * Hillside/cutting retaining wall running behind a platform. Attaches to the
 * positioned local group, so it is a straight approximation of the wall over
 * the station's immediate length — enough to give sloped stops a recognizable
 * edge without re-deriving route curvature.
 */
export function buildVvsRetainingWall(local: THREE.Group, materials: VvsStationMaterials, side: number): void {
  const wall = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.6, 5.2, 82), materials.concrete);
  wall.position.set(side * 13, 2.0, 0);
  wall.receiveShadow = true;
  wall.castShadow = true;
  local.add(wall);

  // Coping along the top edge of the wall.
  const coping = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.9, 0.3, 82), materials.ssbBlue);
  coping.position.set(side * 13, 4.65, 0);
  local.add(coping);
}

/**
 * Interchange departure-info (DFI) boards on posts, facing the platform. One
 * per requested display, spread along the platform. `side` selects which
 * platform edge they sit against.
 */
export function buildVvsDfiBoards(
  local: THREE.Group,
  layer: WorldLayerContext,
  materials: VvsStationMaterials,
  side: number,
  station: RouteStation,
  count: number
): void {
  const slots = [-4, 12, -18].slice(0, Math.max(0, Math.min(count, 3)));
  const face = makeLabelTexture(`${layer.route.line}  ${layer.route.destination}`, {
    width: 768,
    height: 140,
    bg: '#111820',
    fg: '#ffb300'
  });
  for (const z of slots) {
    const post = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.12, 3.2, 0.12), materials.ssbBlue);
    post.position.set(side * 6.4, 1.6, z);
    local.add(post);

    const board = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.16, 0.9, 3.4), materials.dfi);
    board.position.set(side * 6.6, 3.05, z);
    local.add(board);

    const screen = new THREE.Mesh(
      new THREE.PlaneGeometry(3.2, 0.72),
      new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map: face, side: THREE.FrontSide })
    );
    screen.position.set(side * (6.6 - 0.09), 3.05, z);
    screen.rotation.y = (side * Math.PI) / 2;
    local.add(screen);
  }
}

--- world/stations/styles/vvs/components/undergroundHall.ts ---
import * as THREE from 'three';
import type { VvsStationMaterials } from '../VvsStationMaterials';

/**
 * Underground hall shell (ceiling + side walls). Attaches to the positioned,
 * rotated local group. Geometry matches the legacy 27x82m rail hall.
 */
export function buildVvsUndergroundHall(local: THREE.Group, materials: VvsStationMaterials): void {
  const ceiling = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(27, 0.48, 82), materials.undergroundWall);
  ceiling.position.y = 5.7;
  ceiling.receiveShadow = true;
  local.add(ceiling);

  for (const side of [-1, 1]) {
    const wall = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.45, 5.3, 82), materials.undergroundWall);
    wall.position.set(side * 12.2, 2.75, 0);
    wall.receiveShadow = true;
    local.add(wall);
  }
}

/** Per-side underground pillars and ceiling light strips. */
export function buildVvsUndergroundSideLighting(
  local: THREE.Group,
  materials: VvsStationMaterials,
  side: number
): void {
  for (const z of [-24, 0, 24]) {
    const pillar = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.58, 5.1, 0.58), materials.ssbBlue);
    pillar.position.set(side * 9.7, 2.72, z);
    pillar.castShadow = true;
    local.add(pillar);
  }
  for (const z of [-24, -8, 8, 24]) {
    const light = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(1.25, 0.12, 8.5), materials.light);
    light.position.set(side * 7.1, 5.4, z);
    local.add(light);
  }
}

--- world/stations/styles/vvs/components/signage.ts ---
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { makeLabelTexture } from '../../../../../labels';
import type { WorldLayerContext } from '../../../../layers/WorldLayerContext';
import type { RouteStation } from '../../../types';

/**
 * Per-side station name signs (front + back face), registered as station
 * markers so they can be batch-managed. Texture and placement match the legacy
 * rail builder, which built one sign texture per platform side.
 */
export function buildVvsStationSigns(
  local: THREE.Group,
  layer: WorldLayerContext,
  side: number,
  station: RouteStation,
  underground: boolean
): void {
  const signTexture = makeLabelTexture(`${layer.route.line}  ${station.name}`, {
    width: 1024,
    height: 180,
    bg: '#174a73',
    fg: '#ffffff'
  });
  for (const z of [-21, 21]) {
    const sign = new THREE.Mesh(
      new THREE.PlaneGeometry(8.6, 1.5),
      new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map: signTexture, side: THREE.FrontSide })
    );
    sign.position.set(side * 10.75, underground ? 3.55 : 3.05, z);
    sign.rotation.y = (side * Math.PI) / 2;
    local.add(sign);
    const signBack = sign.clone();
    signBack.rotation.y += Math.PI;
    local.add(signBack);
    layer.stationMarkers.push(sign, signBack);
  }
}

/** Underground exit wayfinding sign (front + back), centered above the tracks. */
export function buildVvsWayfinding(local: THREE.Group, station: RouteStation): void {
  const wayfindingTexture = makeLabelTexture(`Ausgang   ${station.name}   →`, {
    width: 1024,
    height: 160,
    bg: '#173f62',
    fg: '#ffffff'
  });
  const wayfinding = new THREE.Mesh(
    new THREE.PlaneGeometry(10, 1.55),
    new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map: wayfindingTexture, side: THREE.FrontSide })
  );
  wayfinding.position.set(0, 4.75, -18);
  local.add(wayfinding);
  const wayfindingBack = wayfinding.clone();
  wayfindingBack.rotation.y = Math.PI;
  local.add(wayfindingBack);
}

--- world/stations/styles/vvs/components/canopy.ts ---
import * as THREE from 'three';
import type { VvsStationMaterials } from '../VvsStationMaterials';

/**
 * Surface side-platform canopy: flat roof, blue fascia edges, a glass back
 * wall, and posts with light strips. One call per platform side. Geometry
 * matches the legacy rail surface canopy.
 */
export function buildVvsCanopy(
  local: THREE.Group,
  materials: VvsStationMaterials,
  side: number,
  lengthM = 25
): void {
  // Canopy centered at z=8; posts sit 2m inside each end so lengthM=25 exactly
  // reproduces the legacy z positions [-2.5, 8, 18.5].
  const center = 8;
  const half = lengthM / 2;
  const postZs = [center - half + 2, center, center + half - 2];

  const roof = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(6.4, 0.24, lengthM), materials.canopy);
  roof.position.set(side * 7.8, 4.25, center);
  roof.rotation.z = side * -0.035;
  roof.castShadow = true;
  local.add(roof);

  for (const roofEdge of [-1, 1]) {
    const fascia = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.16, 0.42, lengthM + 0.2), materials.ssbBlue);
    fascia.position.set(side * 7.8 + roofEdge * 3.05, 4.12, center);
    local.add(fascia);
  }

  const glassBack = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.1, 2.55, lengthM - 3), materials.glass);
  glassBack.position.set(side * 10.55, 2.55, center);
  local.add(glassBack);

  for (const z of postZs) {
    const post = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.16, 3.55, 0.16), materials.ssbBlue);
    post.position.set(side * 10.5, 2.25, z);
    post.castShadow = true;
    local.add(post);

    const lamp = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.22, 0.12, 5.5), materials.light);
    lamp.position.set(side * 7.8, 4.08, z);
    local.add(lamp);
  }
}

--- world/stations/styles/vvs/components/platform.ts ---
import * as THREE from 'three';
import type { WorldLayerContext } from '../../../../layers/WorldLayerContext';
import type { RouteStation, StationPlatformDefinition } from '../../../types';
import { platformSpanT } from '../../../placement';
import type { VvsStationMaterials } from '../VvsStationMaterials';

// Curved side platforms follow the route via world-space strips, so they attach
// to the untransformed station root. Defaults match the legacy rail builder;
// platform length comes from topology data (never seeded).
const PLATFORM_OFFSET_M = 7.4;
const DEFAULT_LENGTH_M = 76; // legacy halfLengthM = 38 → 76m platform

export function buildVvsSidePlatforms(
  root: THREE.Group,
  layer: WorldLayerContext,
  materials: VvsStationMaterials,
  station: RouteStation,
  platform?: StationPlatformDefinition
): void {
  const lengthM = platform?.lengthM ?? DEFAULT_LENGTH_M;
  const { startT, endT } = platformSpanT(station.distanceM, lengthM, layer.route.routeLengthM);

  for (const side of [-1, 1]) {
    const platformEdge = new THREE.Mesh(
      layer.makeRouteStrip(side * PLATFORM_OFFSET_M, 8.25, startT, endT, 28, 0.12),
      materials.platformEdge
    );
    platformEdge.receiveShadow = true;
    root.add(platformEdge);

    const platform = new THREE.Mesh(
      layer.makeRouteStrip(side * PLATFORM_OFFSET_M, 8, startT, endT, 24, 0.22),
      materials.platform
    );
    platform.receiveShadow = true;
    platform.name = `Curved platform ${station.name}`;
    root.add(platform);

    const tactile = new THREE.Mesh(
      layer.makeRouteStrip(side * 3.72, 0.72, startT, endT, 28, 0.245),
      materials.tactile
    );
    tactile.name = `Tactile platform edge ${station.name}`;
    root.add(tactile);
  }
}

--- world/stations/styles/vvs/VvsStationCatalog.ts ---
import type {
  StationArchetype,
  StationArchitectureDefinition,
  StationEnvironmentDefinition,
  StationFurnitureDefinition,
  StationPlatformDefinition
} from '../../types';
import catalogData from './configs/vvs-stadtbahn-stations.json';

/**
 * Optional per-station overrides, keyed by a stable station id (currently the
 * GTFS stop name the route feed carries). Only the fields a station actually
 * customizes need to be present; everything else falls back to archetype
 * defaults and seeded variation. Topology fields here are authoritative and are
 * never overridden by seeded variation.
 */
export type VvsStationOverride = {
  displayName?: string;
  archetype?: StationArchetype;
  platform?: Partial<StationPlatformDefinition>;
  architecture?: StationArchitectureDefinition;
  furniture?: StationFurnitureDefinition;
  environment?: StationEnvironmentDefinition;
  seed?: number;
};

type VvsStationCatalogFile = {
  schemaVersion: number;
  style: string;
  stations: Record<string, VvsStationOverride>;
};

/** Read-only lookup over the bundled VVS station catalog. */
export class VvsStationCatalog {
  private readonly stations: Record<string, VvsStationOverride>;

  constructor(file: VvsStationCatalogFile = catalogData as VvsStationCatalogFile) {
    this.stations = file.stations ?? {};
  }

  get(stationId: string): VvsStationOverride | undefined {
    return this.stations[stationId];
  }
}

--- world/stations/index.ts ---
import { StationStyleRegistry } from './StationStyleRegistry';
import { VvsStadtbahnStationProvider } from './styles/vvs/VvsStadtbahnStationProvider';

export * from './types';
export { StationStyleRegistry } from './StationStyleRegistry';
export { resolveStationPlacement } from './placement';

/**
 * Registry of the station styles known to the app. Cities/modes select one via
 * `stationStyleId`; unmatched styles fall back to the legacy per-guideway
 * builders in StationLayer.
 */
export function createStationStyleRegistry(): StationStyleRegistry {
  return new StationStyleRegistry().register(new VvsStadtbahnStationProvider());
}

--- world/stations/StationFactory.ts ---
import * as THREE from 'three';
import type { StationPlacement } from './types';

/**
 * Scene scaffold shared by every station build.
 *
 * `root` is an untransformed group added to the layer root. Route-following
 * strips built with `makeRouteStrip` are already in world space, so they attach
 * to `root` directly. `local` is translated to the stop and rotated to its
 * heading; furniture, canopies, and signs that were authored around the origin
 * attach to `local`. This reproduces the legacy StationLayer split where
 * platforms were added to the layer root and everything else to a positioned,
 * rotated group — but under a single owning `root` for clean disposal.
 */
export type StationScaffold = {
  root: THREE.Group;
  local: THREE.Group;
};

export function createStationScaffold(name: string, placement: StationPlacement): StationScaffold {
  const root = new THREE.Group();
  root.name = name;
  const local = new THREE.Group();
  local.position.copy(placement.center);
  local.rotation.y = placement.yaw;
  root.add(local);
  return { root, local };
}

/** Disposes geometries/materials under a station group. Materials shared across
 *  stops are owned by the provider and must be excluded from per-stop disposal. */
export function disposeStation(root: THREE.Object3D, sharedMaterials: Set<THREE.Material>): void {
  root.traverse((object) => {
    const mesh = object as Partial<THREE.Mesh> & Partial<THREE.Sprite>;
    if (mesh.geometry) mesh.geometry.dispose();
    const material = (mesh as THREE.Mesh).material;
    if (!material) return;
    const materials = Array.isArray(material) ? material : [material];
    for (const entry of materials) {
      if (!sharedMaterials.has(entry)) entry.dispose();
    }
  });
}

--- world/CameraController.ts ---
import * as THREE from 'three';
import type { InteriorCameraProfile } from '../domain/transport';
import type { CameraRig } from '../engine/cameras/CameraRig';
import { ChaseCameraRig } from '../engine/cameras/ChaseCameraRig';
import { PassengerCameraRig } from '../engine/cameras/PassengerCameraRig';
import { CabCameraRig } from '../engine/cameras/CabCameraRig';

export type CameraMode = 'passenger' | 'cab' | 'chase';

export type CameraUpdateInput = {
  position: THREE.Vector3;
  tangent: THREE.Vector3;
  underground: boolean;
  interiorCameraProfile: InteriorCameraProfile;
  passengerOffsetM: number;
  trainHalfLengthM: number;
  train: THREE.Group;
  trainCars: THREE.Group[];
};

/** Controls chase, passenger, and cab camera placement for the active vehicle. */
export class CameraController {
  private mode: CameraMode = 'chase';
  private zoomLevel = 1;
  private projectionDirty = true;

  // -1/1 are side-look inputs; 0 means looking forward.
  private lookDirection: -1 | 0 | 1 = 0;

  // Smoothed yaw offset for passenger/cab look-left/look-right controls.
  private currentLookYaw = 0;

  // Registered camera rigs keyed by mode; new rigs plug in without touching
  // existing ones or the shared smoothing below.
  private readonly rigs = new Map<CameraMode, CameraRig>();

  constructor(private readonly camera: THREE.PerspectiveCamera) {
    for (const rig of [new ChaseCameraRig(), new PassengerCameraRig(), new CabCameraRig()]) {
      this.registerRig(rig);
    }
  }

  /** Registers (or replaces) a camera rig for its mode. */
  registerRig(rig: CameraRig): void {
    this.rigs.set(rig.id, rig);
  }

  /** Cycles through exterior chase, passenger interior, and cab views. */
  cycle(): CameraMode {
    this.mode = this.mode === 'chase' ? 'passenger' : this.mode === 'passenger' ? 'cab' : 'chase';
    return this.mode;
  }

  /** Returns the active camera mode for HUD and vehicle-visibility logic. */
  currentMode(): CameraMode {
    return this.mode;
  }

  /** Restores a specific camera mode when rebuilding the world from selection state. */
  setMode(mode: CameraMode): CameraMode {
    this.mode = mode;
    return this.mode;
  }

  /** Stores held look direction from keyboard/touch input. */
  setLookInput(leftHeld: boolean, rightHeld: boolean): void {
    this.lookDirection = leftHeld === rightHeld ? 0 : leftHeld ? 1 : -1;
  }

  /** Adjusts the perspective zoom used by all camera modes. */
  adjustZoom(delta: number): void {
    const nextZoom = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(this.zoomLevel + delta, 0.7, 2.4);
    if (Math.abs(nextZoom - this.zoomLevel) < 0.0001) return;
    this.zoomLevel = nextZoom;
    this.projectionDirty = true;
  }

  /** Updates camera position, target, up-vector, and side-look yaw each frame. */
  update(input: CameraUpdateInput): void {
    if (this.projectionDirty) {
      this.camera.zoom = this.zoomLevel;
      this.camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
      this.projectionDirty = false;
    }

    const rig = this.rigs.get(this.mode) ?? this.rigs.get('chase')!;
    const pose = rig.pose(input);
    const camPos = pose.position;
    let lookAt = pose.target;
    const cameraUp = pose.up;

    const lookYawTarget = this.lookDirection * rig.lookYawAmount;
    this.currentLookYaw = THREE.MathUtils.lerp(this.currentLookYaw, lookYawTarget, rig.lookYawFactor);

    if (Math.abs(this.currentLookYaw) > 0.001) {
      const viewDirection = lookAt.clone().sub(camPos);

      if (viewDirection.lengthSq() > 1e-6) {
        viewDirection.applyAxisAngle(cameraUp, this.currentLookYaw);
        lookAt = camPos.clone().add(viewDirection);
      }
    }

    this.camera.position.lerp(camPos, rig.positionSmoothing);
    this.camera.up.lerp(cameraUp, 0.32).normalize(); // blend up-vector to avoid abrupt banking changes.
    this.camera.lookAt(lookAt);
  }
}

--- world/layers/StationLayer.ts ---
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { makeLabelTexture } from '../../labels';
import type { GuidewayKind } from '../../domain/transport';
import { BuilderRegistry } from '../../engine/builders/BuilderRegistry';
import { createStationStyleRegistry, resolveStationPlacement } from '../stations';
import type { StationStyleProvider } from '../stations';
import type { WorldLayerContext } from './WorldLayerContext';

/** Builds platforms, canopies, signage, and stops for every guideway type. */
export class StationLayer {
  /** All meshes go under this group; the world adds it to the scene. */
  readonly root = new THREE.Group();
  // Data-driven station styles selected by the active mode's stationStyleId.
  // Modes without a registered style fall back to the per-guideway builders.
  private readonly styleRegistry = createStationStyleRegistry();
  // Per-guideway station builders; unmapped kinds fall back to rail stations.
  private readonly builders = new BuilderRegistry<GuidewayKind, () => void>()
    .register('bus', () => this.buildBusStops())
    .register('ferry', () => this.buildFerryStops())
    .register('schwebebahn', () => this.buildSchwebebahnStations())
    .register('s-bahn', () => this.buildSbahnStations())
    .register('zacke', () => this.buildMountainRailwayStops())
    .register('seilbahn', () => this.buildMountainRailwayStops());

  constructor(private readonly ctx: WorldLayerContext) {}

  build(): void {
    const style = this.resolveStationStyle();
    if (style) {
      this.buildStyledStations(style);
      return;
    }
    this.builders.resolve(this.ctx.guidewayKind)();
  }

  // Prefer the mode's explicit stationStyleId; otherwise every stadtbahn-guideway
  // city (Odessa/Ulm/… trams, not just Stuttgart) falls back to the vvs-stadtbahn
  // provider, which is the generic light-rail design the legacy buildRailStations
  // produced. Non-rail guideways use their dedicated builders.
  private resolveStationStyle(): StationStyleProvider | undefined {
    const explicit = this.styleRegistry.resolveOptional(this.ctx.stationStyleId);
    if (explicit) return explicit;
    if (this.ctx.guidewayKind === 'stadtbahn') return this.styleRegistry.resolveOptional('vvs-stadtbahn');
    return undefined;
  }

  // Coordinator path for data-driven station styles: resolve placement + a
  // visual definition per stop, then let the provider build it.
  private buildStyledStations(style: StationStyleProvider): void {
    const buildContext = { layer: this.ctx, root: this.root };
    this.ctx.route.stations.forEach((station, index) => {
      const placement = resolveStationPlacement(station, index, this.ctx);
      const definition = style.resolve(station, { layer: this.ctx, placement });
      style.build(definition, placement, buildContext);
    });
  }

  private buildSbahnStations(): void {
    const platformMaterial = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xb6b3aa, roughness: 0.96 });
    const edgeMaterial = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x777a7b, roughness: 0.9 });
    const tactileMaterial = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xe9e4d3, roughness: 0.94 });
    const dbBlue = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x173f73, roughness: 0.5 });
    const steel = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x747b7f, roughness: 0.45, metalness: 0.55 });
    const glass = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xaacbd4, transparent: true, opacity: 0.34, roughness: 0.1, side: THREE.DoubleSide });
    const displayMaterial = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0x101417 });
    const undergroundMaterial = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xc8c8c3, roughness: 0.9 });

    for (const station of this.ctx.route.stations) {
      const t = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(station.distanceM / this.ctx.route.routeLengthM, 0, 1);
      const halfLengthM = 78;
      const startT = THREE.MathUtils.clamp((station.distanceM - halfLengthM) / this.ctx.route.routeLengthM, 0, 1);
      const endT = THREE.MathUtils.clamp((station.distanceM + halfLengthM) / this.ctx.route.routeLengthM, 0, 1);
      const center = this.ctx.curve.getPointAt(t);
      const tangent = this.ctx.curve.getTangentAt(t).normalize();
      const yaw = Math.atan2(tangent.x, tangent.z);
      const underground = this.ctx.stationIsUnderground(t);
      const stationGroup = new THREE.Group();
      stationGroup.name = `DB S-Bahn station ${station.name}`;
      stationGroup.position.copy(center);
      stationGroup.rotation.y = yaw;
      this.root.add(stationGroup);

      for (const side of [-1, 1]) {
        const platform = new THREE.Mesh(this.ctx.makeRouteStrip(side * 6.25, 5.7, startT, endT, 72, 0.28), platformMaterial);
        platform.receiveShadow = true;
        platform.name = `S-Bahn 96cm platform ${station.name}`;
        this.root.add(platform);
        const edge = new THREE.Mesh(this.ctx.makeRouteStrip(side * 3.55, 0.35, startT, endT, 76, 0.31), edgeMaterial);
        this.root.add(edge);
        const tactile = new THREE.Mesh(this.ctx.makeRouteStrip(side * 3.95, 0.55, startT, endT, 76, 0.335), tactileMaterial);
        this.root.add(tactile);

        for (const z of [-48, 0, 48]) {
          const sign = new THREE.Mesh(
            new THREE.PlaneGeometry(5.8, 0.88),
            new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map: makeLabelTexture(station.name, { width: 768, height: 128, bg: '#164279', fg: '#fff' }), side: THREE.DoubleSide })
          );
          sign.position.set(side * 7.55, 2.65, z);
          sign.rotation.y = side > 0 ? -Math.PI / 2 : Math.PI / 2;
          stationGroup.add(sign);
          this.ctx.stationMarkers.push(sign);
        }

        const canopy = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(4.8, 0.2, 28), steel);
        canopy.position.set(side * 6.55, 4.15, 5);
        stationGroup.add(canopy);
        const glassBack = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.08, 2.45, 18), glass);
        glassBack.position.set(side * 8.3, 2.35, 5);
        stationGroup.add(glassBack);
        for (const z of [-7, 5, 17]) {
          const post = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.14, 3.4, 0.14), steel);
          post.position.set(side * 6.55, 2.25, z);
          stationGroup.add(post);
        }

        const dfi = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.16, 0.82, 3.6), displayMaterial);
        dfi.position.set(side * 6.5, 3.45, -15);
        stationGroup.add(dfi);
        const clock = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.48, 0.48, 0.08, 24), new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xf7f7f2 }));
        clock.rotation.z = Math.PI / 2;
        clock.position.set(side * 6.5, 3.45, 22);
        stationGroup.add(clock);
        const sBadge = new THREE.Sprite(new THREE.SpriteMaterial({ map: makeLabelTexture('S', { width: 256, height: 256, bg: '#07864b', fg: '#fff' }) }));
        sBadge.position.set(side * 7.1, 4.7, -24);
        sBadge.scale.set(2.2, 2.2, 1);
        stationGroup.add(sBadge);
      }

      if (underground) {
        const ceiling = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(24, 0.42, 155), undergroundMaterial);
        ceiling.position.y = 5.7;
        stationGroup.add(ceiling);
        for (const side of [-1, 1]) {
          const wall = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.38, 5.2, 155), undergroundMaterial);
          wall.position.set(side * 11.7, 2.75, 0);
          stationGroup.add(wall);
        }
      }
    }
  }

  private buildMountainRailwayStops(): void {
    const platformMaterial = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xb4b0a5, roughness: 0.95 });
    const postMaterial = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x174a73, roughness: 0.55 });
    const wood = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x70472c, roughness: 0.82 });
    for (const station of this.ctx.route.stations) {
      const t = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(station.distanceM / this.ctx.route.routeLengthM, 0, 1);
      const group = new THREE.Group();
      group.position.copy(this.ctx.curve.getPointAt(t));
      group.rotation.y = Math.atan2(this.ctx.curve.getTangentAt(t).x, this.ctx.curve.getTangentAt(t).z);
      const length = this.ctx.guidewayKind === 'seilbahn' ? 14 : 28;
      const platform = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(3.2, 0.22, length), platformMaterial);
      platform.position.set(2.15, 0.08, 0);
      group.add(platform);
      const shelter = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(2.5, 0.18, 5.5), this.ctx.guidewayKind === 'seilbahn' ? wood : postMaterial);
      shelter.position.set(2.2, 3.05, 0);
      group.add(shelter);
      for (const z of [-2.4, 2.4]) {
        const post = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.12, 2.8, 0.12), postMaterial);
        post.position.set(2.2, 1.55, z);
        group.add(post);
      }
      const sign = new THREE.Sprite(new THREE.SpriteMaterial({ map: makeLabelTexture(`${this.ctx.route.line}  ${station.name}`, { width: 768, height: 160, bg: '#174a73', fg: '#fff' }) }));
      sign.position.set(2.2, 3.75, 0);
      sign.scale.set(7, 1.45, 1);
      group.add(sign);
      this.ctx.stationMarkers.push(sign);
      this.root.add(group);
    }
  }

  private buildSchwebebahnStations(): void {
    const platformMaterial = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xc8c5bb, roughness: 0.94 });
    const edgeMaterial = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x777c7e, roughness: 0.88 });
    const steel = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x5f6b73, roughness: 0.44, metalness: 0.58 });
    const blue = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x245d85, roughness: 0.5, metalness: 0.24 });
    const glass = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
      color: 0xb6d8e4,
      transparent: true,
      opacity: 0.32,
      roughness: 0.08,
      side: THREE.DoubleSide
    });

    for (const station of this.ctx.route.stations) {
      const t = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(station.distanceM / this.ctx.route.routeLengthM, 0, 1);
      const halfLengthM = 18;
      const startT = THREE.MathUtils.clamp((station.distanceM - halfLengthM) / this.ctx.route.routeLengthM, 0, 1);
      const endT = THREE.MathUtils.clamp((station.distanceM + halfLengthM) / this.ctx.route.routeLengthM, 0, 1);
      const center = this.ctx.curve.getPointAt(t);
      const tangent = this.ctx.curve.getTangentAt(t).normalize();
      const yaw = Math.atan2(tangent.x, tangent.z);

      const platform = new THREE.Mesh(this.ctx.makeRouteStrip(0, 4.9, startT, endT, 42, -1.38), platformMaterial);
      platform.name = `Schwebebahn island platform ${station.name}`;
      platform.receiveShadow = true;
      this.root.add(platform);

      for (const edgeOffset of [-1.48, 1.48]) {
        const edge = new THREE.Mesh(this.ctx.makeRouteStrip(edgeOffset, 0.18, startT, endT, 44, -1.31), edgeMaterial);
        edge.name = `Schwebebahn platform edge ${station.name}`;
        this.root.add(edge);
      }

      const stationGroup = new THREE.Group();
      stationGroup.name = `Schwebebahn station hall ${station.name}`;
      stationGroup.position.copy(center);
      stationGroup.rotation.y = yaw;
      this.root.add(stationGroup);

      const roof = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(6.4, 0.22, 19.5), steel);
      roof.position.set(0, 0.95, 0);
      roof.castShadow = true;
      stationGroup.add(roof);
      const ridge = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(1.1, 0.24, 19.7), blue);
      ridge.position.set(0, 1.12, 0);
      stationGroup.add(ridge);

      for (const x of [-2.45, 2.45]) {
        const wall = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.1, 2.55, 17.6), glass);
        wall.position.set(x, -0.22, 0);
        stationGroup.add(wall);
      }
      for (const z of [-7.8, 0, 7.8]) {
        for (const x of [-2.5, 2.5]) {
          const post = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.16, 2.8, 0.16), steel);
          post.position.set(x, -0.2, z);
          post.castShadow = true;
          stationGroup.add(post);
        }
      }

      const overheadBeam = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(4.2, 0.2, 0.55), steel);
      overheadBeam.position.set(0, 2.15, 0);
      stationGroup.add(overheadBeam);

      const signTexture = makeLabelTexture(`60  ${station.name}`, {
        width: 1024,
        height: 180,
        bg: '#174a73',
        fg: '#ffffff'
      });
      for (const z of [-6.4, 6.4]) {
        const sign = new THREE.Mesh(
          new THREE.PlaneGeometry(4.8, 0.9),
          new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map: signTexture, side: THREE.DoubleSide })
        );
        sign.position.set(0, 0.2, z);
        sign.rotation.y = Math.PI / 2;
        stationGroup.add(sign);
        this.ctx.stationMarkers.push(sign);
      }

      const dfi = new THREE.Mesh(
        new THREE.PlaneGeometry(3.6, 0.62),
        new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map: makeLabelTexture(`60  ${this.ctx.route.destination}`, { width: 768, height: 140, bg: '#111820', fg: '#ffb300' }), side: THREE.DoubleSide })
      );
      dfi.position.set(0, 0.02, -8.3);
      dfi.rotation.y = Math.PI;
      stationGroup.add(dfi);
    }
  }

  private buildBusStops(): void {
    const poleMaterial = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x4c555a, roughness: 0.58, metalness: 0.55 });
    const yellowMaterial = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xffdf00, roughness: 0.48 });
    const stopGreenMaterial = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x16723a, roughness: 0.5 });
    const shelterMaterial = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
      color: 0xb9dce7,
      transparent: true,
      opacity: 0.32,
      roughness: 0.1,
      side: THREE.DoubleSide
    });
    const seatMaterial = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x234e68, roughness: 0.75 });

    for (const station of this.ctx.route.stations) {
      const t = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(station.distanceM / this.ctx.route.routeLengthM, 0, 1);
      const tangent = this.ctx.curve.getTangentAt(t).normalize();
      const normal = this.ctx.normalAt(t);
      const group = new THREE.Group();
      group.name = `Bus stop ${station.name}`;
      group.position.copy(this.ctx.curve.getPointAt(t)).add(normal.multiplyScalar(-7.2));
      group.rotation.y = Math.atan2(tangent.x, tangent.z);

      const pole = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.055, 0.065, 3.3, 10), poleMaterial);
      pole.position.y = 1.65;
      group.add(pole);
      const discBorder = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.4, 0.4, 0.075, 24), stopGreenMaterial);
      discBorder.rotation.z = Math.PI / 2;
      discBorder.position.set(0, 2.85, 0);
      group.add(discBorder);
      const hBadge = new THREE.Sprite(new THREE.SpriteMaterial({ map: makeLabelTexture('H', { width: 256, height: 256, bg: '#ffdf00', fg: '#176f39' }) }));
      hBadge.position.set(0.06, 2.85, 0);
      hBadge.scale.set(0.65, 0.65, 1);
      group.add(hBadge);

      const label = new THREE.Sprite(new THREE.SpriteMaterial({
        map: makeLabelTexture(`${this.ctx.route.line}  ${station.name}`, { width: 768, height: 160, bg: '#174a73', fg: '#fff' })
      }));
      label.position.set(0, 3.65, 0);
      label.scale.set(7.2, 1.5, 1);
      group.add(label);

      const dfi = new THREE.Mesh(
        new THREE.PlaneGeometry(4.6, 0.82),
        new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map: makeLabelTexture(`${this.ctx.route.line}  ${this.ctx.route.destination}`, { width: 768, height: 140, bg: '#111820', fg: '#ffb300' }), side: THREE.DoubleSide })
      );
      dfi.position.set(-0.65, 3.18, 1.25);
      group.add(dfi);

      const back = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.1, 2.25, 5.6), shelterMaterial);
      back.position.set(-1.7, 1.42, 2.8);
      group.add(back);
      const roof = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(2.1, 0.12, 5.8), poleMaterial);
      roof.position.set(-0.7, 2.58, 2.8);
      group.add(roof);
      const fascia = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(2.18, 0.18, 5.95), yellowMaterial);
      fascia.position.set(-0.7, 2.68, 2.8);
      group.add(fascia);
      const seat = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.7, 0.18, 3.2), seatMaterial);
      seat.position.set(-1.25, 0.72, 2.8);
      group.add(seat);
      this.root.add(group);
      this.ctx.stationMarkers.push(label);
    }
  }

  private buildFerryStops(): void {
    const dockMaterial = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xb8b3a6, roughness: 0.96 });
    const postMaterial = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x1f5e88, roughness: 0.56 });
    const roofMaterial = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xe6ecee, roughness: 0.48 });
    const glassMaterial = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
      color: 0xb6dbe4,
      transparent: true,
      opacity: 0.3,
      roughness: 0.12,
      side: THREE.DoubleSide
    });
    for (const station of this.ctx.route.stations) {
      const t = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(station.distanceM / this.ctx.route.routeLengthM, 0, 1);
      const tangent = this.ctx.curve.getTangentAt(t).normalize();
      const normal = this.ctx.normalAt(t);
      const group = new THREE.Group();
      group.name = `Ferry dock ${station.name}`;
      group.position.copy(this.ctx.curve.getPointAt(t)).add(normal.multiplyScalar(6));
      group.rotation.y = Math.atan2(tangent.x, tangent.z);

      const pier = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(4.8, 0.28, 18), dockMaterial);
      pier.position.y = 0.08;
      group.add(pier);
      const pontoon = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(3.6, 0.4, 8.5), dockMaterial);
      pontoon.position.set(0, -0.12, -4.2);
      group.add(pontoon);
      const shelterRoof = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(3.2, 0.14, 5.4), roofMaterial);
      shelterRoof.position.set(0, 2.55, 3.1);
      group.add(shelterRoof);
      for (const x of [-1.2, 1.2]) {
        const post = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.12, 2.4, 0.12), postMaterial);
        post.position.set(x, 1.26, 3.1);
        group.add(post);
      }
      const glassBack = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(2.85, 1.8, 0.08), glassMaterial);
      glassBack.position.set(0, 1.55, 4.55);
      group.add(glassBack);
      const sign = new THREE.Sprite(new THREE.SpriteMaterial({
        map: makeLabelTexture(`${this.ctx.route.line}  ${station.name}`, { width: 768, height: 160, bg: '#1f5e88', fg: '#ffffff' })
      }));
      sign.position.set(0, 3.3, 3.1);
      sign.scale.set(7, 1.45, 1);
      group.add(sign);
      this.ctx.stationMarkers.push(sign);
      this.root.add(group);
    }
  }
}

--- world/layers/LandmarkLayer.ts ---
import * as THREE from 'three';
import type { WorldLayerContext } from './WorldLayerContext';

/** Fallback procedural city blocks lining the route when OSM scenery is absent. */
export class LandmarkLayer {
  /** All meshes go under this group; the world adds it to the scene. */
  readonly root = new THREE.Group();
  constructor(private readonly ctx: WorldLayerContext) {}

  build(): void {
    const mats = [
      new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xb6a38f, roughness: 0.82 }),
      new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xd1c8b7, roughness: 0.76 }),
      new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x8e9aa5, roughness: 0.8 }),
      new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xc0b3a2, roughness: 0.84 })
    ];

    const blockCount = Math.max(50, Math.min(180, Math.round(this.ctx.route.routeLengthM / 150)));
    for (let i = 0; i < blockCount; i++) {
      const t = i / Math.max(1, blockCount - 1);
      const p = this.ctx.curve.getPointAt(t);
      const tangent = this.ctx.curve.getTangentAt(t).normalize();
      const normal = new THREE.Vector3(tangent.z, 0, -tangent.x).normalize();
      for (const side of [-1, 1]) {
        const width = 18 + Math.random() * 16;
        const depth = 28 + Math.random() * 18;
        const height = 8 + Math.random() * 34;
        const b = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(width, height, depth), mats[Math.floor(Math.random() * mats.length)]);
        b.position.copy(p).add(normal.multiplyScalar(side * (72 + Math.random() * 140)));
        b.position.y = height / 2;
        b.position.add(tangent.multiplyScalar((Math.random() - 0.5) * 70));
        b.castShadow = true;
        b.receiveShadow = true;
        this.root.add(b);
      }
    }
  }
}

--- world/layers/WorldLayerContext.ts ---
import type * as THREE from 'three';
import type { DemoRoute } from '../../route';
import type { GuidewayKind } from '../../domain/transport';
import type { GeoPoint } from '../../landmarks';

/**
 * Shared route geometry, sampling, and scene access that the scene layers need.
 * TransitWorld owns the underlying curve/terrain helpers and exposes them here
 * so each layer can build its meshes without re-deriving route geometry.
 */
export type WorldLayerContext = {
  // The raw scene is intentionally not exposed; each layer owns a root group.
  curve: THREE.CatmullRomCurve3;
  route: DemoRoute;
  guidewayKind: GuidewayKind;
  // Station style provider id for the active mode; undefined uses the legacy
  // per-guideway station builders.
  stationStyleId?: string;
  activeLaneOffsetM: number;
  oppositeLaneOffsetM: number;
  railGaugeM: number;
  // Route-following billboards/signs collected so they can be batch-managed.
  stationMarkers: THREE.Object3D[];

  stationIsUnderground(t: number): boolean;
  normalAt(t: number): THREE.Vector3;
  offsetCurvePoints(offsetM: number, segments: number, heightM: number): THREE.Vector3[];
  surfaceHeightAt(t: number): number;
  makeRouteStrip(offsetM: number, widthM: number, startT: number, endT: number, segments: number, heightM: number): THREE.BufferGeometry;
  makeTrackStrip(offsetM: number, widthM: number, segments: number): THREE.BufferGeometry;
  maskRanges(mask: boolean[]): [number, number][];
  routeBounds(): { min: THREE.Vector3; max: THREE.Vector3; center: THREE.Vector3; size: THREE.Vector3 };
  routeGeoPoints(): GeoPoint[];
  updateArticulatedVehicle(root: THREE.Group, cars: THREE.Group[], distanceM: number, direction: 1 | -1, trackOffsetM: number): void;
  wrapDistance(distanceM: number): number;
};

--- world/layers/RouteLayer.ts ---
import * as THREE from 'three';
import type { WorldLayerContext } from './WorldLayerContext';

/** Builds the ground/terrain relief that follows the route corridor. */
export class RouteLayer {
  /** All meshes go under this group; the world adds it to the scene. */
  readonly root = new THREE.Group();
  constructor(private readonly ctx: WorldLayerContext) {}

  /** Adds the ground plane to the scene and returns it (for visibility toggling). */
  build(): THREE.Mesh {
    const bounds = this.ctx.routeBounds();
    const width = Math.max(900, bounds.size.x + 700);
    const depth = Math.max(900, bounds.size.z + 700);
    const groundGeometry = this.ctx.route.surfaceHeightsM?.length
      ? this.makeGroundTerrain(bounds.center, width, depth)
      : new THREE.PlaneGeometry(width, depth);
    const ground = new THREE.Mesh(groundGeometry, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
      color: 0x52764b,
      roughness: 1,
      depthWrite: false,
      polygonOffset: true,
      polygonOffsetFactor: 1,
      polygonOffsetUnits: 1
    }));
    if (!this.ctx.route.surfaceHeightsM?.length) {
      ground.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;
      ground.position.set(bounds.center.x, -0.04, bounds.center.z);
    }
    ground.receiveShadow = true;
    ground.renderOrder = -100;
    ground.name = 'Untextured terrain relief';
    this.root.add(ground);

    if (this.ctx.routeGeoPoints().length >= 2) return ground;

    const roadMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x394047, roughness: 0.9 });
    const roadCount = Math.max(3, Math.min(26, Math.round(this.ctx.route.routeLengthM / 850)));
    for (let i = 1; i < roadCount; i++) {
      const t = i / roadCount;
      const p = this.ctx.curve.getPointAt(t);
      const tangent = this.ctx.curve.getTangentAt(t).normalize();
      const yaw = Math.atan2(tangent.x, tangent.z);
      const road = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(520, 22), roadMat);
      road.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;
      road.rotation.z = yaw + Math.PI / 2;
      road.position.set(p.x, 0.01, p.z);
      this.root.add(road);
    }
    return ground;
  }

  private makeGroundTerrain(center: THREE.Vector3, widthM: number, depthM: number): THREE.PlaneGeometry {
    const xSegments = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(Math.ceil(widthM / 140), 40, 140);
    const zSegments = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(Math.ceil(depthM / 140), 40, 140);
    const geometry = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(widthM, depthM, xSegments, zSegments);
    geometry.rotateX(-Math.PI / 2);
    geometry.translate(center.x, 0, center.z);

    const routeSamples = Array.from({ length: 321 }, (_, index) => {
      const t = index / 320;
      const point = this.ctx.curve.getPointAt(t);
      return { x: point.x, y: point.y, z: point.z, t };
    });
    const positions = geometry.getAttribute('position') as THREE.BufferAttribute;
    for (let vertex = 0; vertex < positions.count; vertex++) {
      const x = positions.getX(vertex);
      const z = positions.getZ(vertex);
      let nearestDistanceSq = Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
      let nearestY = 0;
      let nearestT = 0;
      for (let index = 1; index < routeSamples.length; index++) {
        const a = routeSamples[index - 1];
        const b = routeSamples[index];
        const dx = b.x - a.x;
        const dz = b.z - a.z;
        const lengthSq = dx * dx + dz * dz || 1;
        const segmentT = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(((x - a.x) * dx + (z - a.z) * dz) / lengthSq, 0, 1);
        const closestX = a.x + dx * segmentT;
        const closestZ = a.z + dz * segmentT;
        const distanceSq = (x - closestX) ** 2 + (z - closestZ) ** 2;
        if (distanceSq < nearestDistanceSq) {
          nearestDistanceSq = distanceSq;
          nearestY = THREE.MathUtils.lerp(a.y, b.y, segmentT);
          nearestT = THREE.MathUtils.lerp(a.t, b.t, segmentT);
        }
      }
      // Use the actual curve Y so terrain tracks the spline exactly. For tunnel
      // segments the curve dips underground, so take max with surface height to
      // keep the terrain mesh at ground level above tunnels.
      const surfaceY = this.ctx.surfaceHeightAt(nearestT);
      positions.setY(vertex, Math.max(nearestY, surfaceY) - 0.55);
    }
    positions.needsUpdate = true;
    geometry.computeVertexNormals();
    return geometry;
  }
}

--- world/layers/OpposingTrafficLayer.ts ---
import * as THREE from 'three';
import type { WorldLayerContext } from './WorldLayerContext';

/** Oncoming services cloned from the player vehicle and run on the opposite lane. */
export class OpposingTrafficLayer {
  private readonly trains: { root: THREE.Group; cars: THREE.Group[]; phaseM: number }[] = [];

  /** All meshes go under this group; the world adds it to the scene. */
  readonly root = new THREE.Group();
  constructor(private readonly ctx: WorldLayerContext) {}

  /** Clones the player train into evenly-spaced oncoming services. */
  build(playerTrain: THREE.Group): void {
    const trainCount = this.ctx.guidewayKind === 'seilbahn' ? 1 : this.ctx.route.routeLengthM > 5000 ? 3 : 2;
    for (let index = 0; index < trainCount; index++) {
      const root = playerTrain.clone(true);
      root.name = `Opposing service ${index + 1}`;
      // Beam lights stay exclusive to the player train: cloned spotlights would
      // keep aiming at the original train's target and add per-fragment cost.
      const clonedLights: THREE.Object3D[] = [];
      root.traverse((object) => {
        if ((object as THREE.Light).isLight || object.name.startsWith('Exterior lamp')) clonedLights.push(object);
      });
      for (const light of clonedLights) light.parent?.remove(light);
      // A funicular's counterbalance car runs in reverse but its stepped body must
      // still point its tall end uphill, so flip the cloned shell 180° about its
      // vertical axis (relative to the player car's already uphill-oriented shell).
      if (this.ctx.guidewayKind === 'seilbahn') {
        const shell = root.getObjectByName('Funicular step body');
        if (shell) shell.rotation.y += Math.PI;
      }
      const cars = root.children.filter((child): child is THREE.Group => child instanceof THREE.Group && child.name.startsWith('Articulated car'));
      this.trains.push({
        root,
        cars,
        phaseM: ((index + 1) / (trainCount + 1)) * this.ctx.route.routeLengthM
      });
      this.root.add(root);
    }
  }

  /** Advances the oncoming services relative to the player distance along the route. */
  update(distanceM: number): void {
    for (const traffic of this.trains) {
      const opposingDistance = this.ctx.guidewayKind === 'seilbahn'
        ? this.ctx.route.routeLengthM - distanceM
        : this.ctx.wrapDistance(this.ctx.route.routeLengthM - distanceM + traffic.phaseM);
      this.ctx.updateArticulatedVehicle(traffic.root, traffic.cars, opposingDistance, -1, this.ctx.oppositeLaneOffsetM);
    }
  }
}

--- world/layers/GuidewayLayer.ts ---
import * as THREE from 'three';
import type { GuidewayKind } from '../../domain/transport';
import { BuilderRegistry } from '../../engine/builders/BuilderRegistry';
import type { WorldLayerContext } from './WorldLayerContext';

/** Builds the running way (rails, bus street, ferry channel, guideway) and tunnels. */
export class GuidewayLayer {
  /** All meshes go under this group; the world adds it to the scene. */
  readonly root = new THREE.Group();
  // Per-guideway track builders; unmapped kinds fall back to rail track.
  private readonly trackBuilders = new BuilderRegistry<GuidewayKind, () => void>()
    .register('bus', () => this.buildBusRouteStreet())
    .register('ferry', () => this.buildFerryRoutePath())
    .register('schwebebahn', () => this.buildSchwebebahnGuideway())
    .register('zacke', () => this.buildMountainRailwayTrack())
    .register('seilbahn', () => this.buildMountainRailwayTrack());

  constructor(private readonly ctx: WorldLayerContext) {}

  build(): void {
    this.buildTrack();
    this.buildTunnels();
  }

  private buildTrack(): void {
    (this.trackBuilders.resolveOptional(this.ctx.guidewayKind) ?? (() => this.buildRailTrack()))();
  }

  private buildRailTrack(): void {
    const railMaterial = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x7f858a, metalness: 0.7, roughness: 0.28 });
    const sleeperMaterial = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x453429, roughness: 0.85 });
    const ballastMaterial = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x6d6a63, roughness: 0.95 });
    const trackSegments = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(Math.ceil(this.ctx.route.routeLengthM / 10), 480, 5000);

    for (const trackOffset of [this.ctx.activeLaneOffsetM, this.ctx.oppositeLaneOffsetM]) {
      const ballast = new THREE.Mesh(this.ctx.makeTrackStrip(trackOffset, 4.5, trackSegments), ballastMaterial);
      ballast.receiveShadow = true;
      this.root.add(ballast);

      for (const railOffset of [-this.ctx.railGaugeM / 2, this.ctx.railGaugeM / 2]) {
