--- simulator/SimulatorRuntime.ts ---
import { createRouteCurve, routeMode, type DemoRoute } from '../route';
import type { CityDefinition } from '../cities';
import { resolveTransportModeDefinition } from '../domain/transport';
import type { CameraMode } from '../world/CameraController';
import { TransitWorld } from '../world';
import { SimulatorClient } from './SimulatorClient';
import { SimulatorLoop } from '../app/SimulatorLoop';
import type { Mode, Season } from '../app/types';
import type { SimulatorSnapshotStore } from '../features/simulation/SimulatorSnapshotStore';
import { InputState } from '../engine/runtime/InputState';
import { RuntimeMachine } from '../engine/runtime/RuntimeMachine';
import { worldKey } from '../engine/runtime/worldKey';
import { TransitEngine } from '../engine/core/TransitEngine';
import { LegacyWorldSystem } from '../engine/core/LegacyWorldSystem';
import { WorkspaceManager } from '../engine/workspace/WorkspaceManager';
import { SimulationWorkspace } from '../engine/workspace/SimulationWorkspace';
import { PhotoWorkspace } from '../engine/workspace/PhotoWorkspace';
import type { EngineInputEvent } from '../engine/workspace/EngineInputEvent';

/** Everything needed to (re)build the world and initialize the simulator. */
export type RouteSession = {
  route: DemoRoute;
  networkRoutes: DemoRoute[];
  city: CityDefinition;
  mode: Mode;
  startStationIndex: number;
  season: Season;
  time: 'auto' | number;
  cameraMode: CameraMode;
};

/** Imperative command surface the React shell drives via effects. */
export type SimulatorCommands = {
  /** Transient held input (throttle/brake/look); written to directly, never via React. */
  readonly input: InputState;
  /** Routes a semantic input event through the active workspace. */
  handleInput(event: EngineInputEvent): boolean;
  loadRoute(session: RouteSession): void;
  setStartStation(session: RouteSession): void;
  setRideMode(session: RouteSession): void;
  resetSimulation(session: RouteSession): void;
  setPaused(paused: boolean): void;
  cycleCamera(): CameraMode | undefined;
  setCameraMode(mode: CameraMode): void;
  adjustZoom(delta: number): void;
  setSeason(season: Season): void;
  setTime(time: 'auto' | number): void;
  togglePhotoMode(): void;
  enterPhotoMode(): void;
  exitPhotoMode(): void;
  onPhotoMode(handler: (active: boolean) => void): void;
  cyclePhotoPreset(): void;
  exportScreenshot(): Promise<void>;
};

/**
 * Owns the Three.js world, the Go/WASM simulator client, and the render loop.
 * React never touches these — it only issues commands and reads snapshots. The
 * world is rebuilt only on loadRoute; other changes re-init the simulator.
 */
export class SimulatorRuntime implements SimulatorCommands {
  readonly input = new InputState();
  // Runtime lifecycle (idle → loading-engine → ready → running/paused → …) lives
  // in an explicit machine instead of loaded/paused/disposed booleans.
  private readonly machine = new RuntimeMachine();
  private readonly simulator = new SimulatorClient();
  private world: TransitWorld | null = null;
  // Owns the render + system loop; wraps the current world via LegacyWorldSystem.
  private engine: TransitEngine | null = null;
  private session: RouteSession | null = null;
  private journeyLegs: DemoRoute[] = [];
  private journeyLegIndex = 0;
  // Input is routed through the active workspace (simulation ↔ photo).
  private readonly workspaces = new WorkspaceManager();
  private readonly simulationWorkspace = new SimulationWorkspace(this);
  private readonly photoWorkspace = new PhotoWorkspace(this);
  private photoActive = false;
  private photoLabelHandler: ((label: string) => void) | null = null;
  private photoModeHandler: ((active: boolean) => void) | null = null;
  private activeRouteHandler: ((route: DemoRoute) => void) | null = null;
  private readonly loop: SimulatorLoop;

  constructor(
    private readonly container: HTMLElement,
    private readonly snapshots: SimulatorSnapshotStore
  ) {
    // Held input goes straight to the simulator/world, never through React.
    this.input.onChange(() => this.applyInput());
    // Start in the driving workspace; photo mode swaps in the photo workspace.
    void this.workspaces.activate(this.simulationWorkspace);
    this.loop = new SimulatorLoop({
      turnaroundMs: 4000,
      getPaused: () => this.machine.matches('paused'),
      getSelectedTime: () => this.session?.time ?? 'auto',
      getCurrentLocalHour: currentLocalHour,
      step: (dt) => this.simulator.step(dt),
      onAutoTimeHour: (hour) => this.world?.setTimeOfDay(hour),
      onState: (state, dt) => {
        this.world?.updateTrain(state.distanceM, state.doorsOpen, dt, state.nextStation);
        this.engine?.update(dt, 1);
        this.snapshots.update(state);
      },
      onTerminalTurnaround: () => this.advanceJourneyLeg()
    });
  }

  /** Loads the WASM simulator; starts the loop and inits the pending session. */
  async load(): Promise<void> {
    this.machine.send({ type: 'LOAD_ENGINE' });
    try {
      await this.simulator.load();
    } catch (error) {
      this.machine.send({ type: 'FAIL', error: error as Error });
      throw error;
    }
    // Guard against React StrictMode's mount→unmount→mount: if this instance was
    // disposed while the WASM load was in flight, do not start its loop.
    if (this.machine.matches('disposed')) return;
    this.machine.send({ type: 'ENGINE_READY' });
    if (this.session) this.resetSimulation(this.session);
    this.loop.start();
  }

  /** Current runtime lifecycle state value (for debugging/telemetry). */
  get lifecycle(): string {
    return this.machine.current.value;
  }

  /** Registers a callback for cinematic-preset label changes (photo overlay). */
  onPhotoLabel(handler: (label: string) => void): void {
    this.photoLabelHandler = handler;
  }

  /** Registers the route currently shown in the world, including transfers. */
  onActiveRoute(handler: (route: DemoRoute) => void): void {
    this.activeRouteHandler = handler;
    if (this.session) handler(this.session.route);
  }

  loadRoute(session: RouteSession): void {
    this.journeyLegs = [session.route, ...(session.route.journeyLegs ?? [])];
    this.journeyLegIndex = 0;
    this.buildWorld({ ...session, route: this.journeyLegs[0], startStationIndex: 0 });
    if (this.machine.engineReady) this.initializeSimulation(this.session!);
  }

  private buildWorld(session: RouteSession): void {
    this.session = session;
    this.activeRouteHandler?.(session.route);
    // Disposing the engine disposes the previous world (via LegacyWorldSystem).
    this.engine?.dispose();
    this.container.replaceChildren();
    this.world = new TransitWorld(
      createRouteCurve(session.route),
      session.route,
      session.networkRoutes,
      session.city,
      resolveTransportModeDefinition(routeMode(session.route), session.city.transportModes)
    );
    this.world.mount(this.container);
    this.engine = new TransitEngine(this.world.scene, this.world.camera, this.world.renderer);
    this.engine.register(new LegacyWorldSystem(this.world));
    this.applyWorldSettings(session);
  }

  setStartStation(session: RouteSession): void {
    this.session = session;
    if (this.machine.engineReady) this.resetSimulation(session);
  }

  setRideMode(session: RouteSession): void {
    this.session = session;
    if (this.machine.engineReady) this.resetSimulation(session);
  }

  resetSimulation(session: RouteSession): void {
    const journeyLegs = [session.route, ...(session.route.journeyLegs ?? [])];
    if (journeyLegs.length > 1) {
      this.journeyLegs = journeyLegs;
      this.journeyLegIndex = 0;
      const firstSession = { ...session, route: journeyLegs[0], startStationIndex: 0 };
      if (this.session?.route !== journeyLegs[0]) this.buildWorld(firstSession);
      else this.session = firstSession;
    } else {
      this.journeyLegs = [session.route];
      this.journeyLegIndex = 0;
      this.session = session;
    }
    if (this.session) this.initializeSimulation(this.session);
  }

  private initializeSimulation(session: RouteSession): void {
    // Reaching a live world+sim is the machine's running state. LOAD_WORLD is
    // valid from ready/running/paused/terminal-dwell, so this also clears a
    // prior pause and completes a terminal turnaround.
    this.machine.send({ type: 'LOAD_WORLD', sessionKey: worldKey(session) });
    this.machine.send({ type: 'WORLD_READY' });
    const state = this.simulator.init({
      line: session.route.line,
      destination: session.route.destination,
      speedLimitMps: session.route.speedLimitMps,
      routeLengthM: session.route.routeLengthM,
      stations: session.route.stations,
      mode: session.mode,
      startStationIndex: session.startStationIndex
    });
    this.loop.setState(state);
    this.snapshots.update(state, true);
  }

  /** Rebuilds the world with the next vehicle type after a journey transfer. */
  private advanceJourneyLeg(): void {
    if (!this.session || this.journeyLegIndex >= this.journeyLegs.length - 1) return;
    // Mark the terminal dwell in the machine; ignored if it already fired, which
    // is the guard against a turnaround running twice.
    this.machine.send({ type: 'TERMINAL_REACHED', at: performance.now() });
    this.journeyLegIndex++;
    const nextSession: RouteSession = {
      ...this.session,
      route: this.journeyLegs[this.journeyLegIndex],
      networkRoutes: [this.journeyLegs[this.journeyLegIndex]],
      startStationIndex: 0
    };
    this.buildWorld(nextSession);
    this.initializeSimulation(nextSession);
  }

  setPaused(paused: boolean): void {
    this.machine.send(paused ? { type: 'PAUSE' } : { type: 'RESUME' });
    if (this.machine.engineReady) this.snapshots.update(this.simulator.setPaused(paused), true);
  }

  /** Forwards the current held input to the simulator (driver mode) and world (look). */
  private applyInput(): void {
    this.world?.setInteriorLookInput(this.input.lookX < 0, this.input.lookX > 0);
    if (this.machine.engineReady && this.session?.mode === 'driver') {
      this.snapshots.update(this.simulator.setDriverInput(this.input.throttle, this.input.brake), true);
    }
  }

  /** Routes a semantic input event through the active workspace (simulation/photo). */
  handleInput(event: EngineInputEvent): boolean {
    return this.workspaces.handleInput(event);
  }

  cycleCamera(): CameraMode | undefined {
    return this.world?.cycleCamera();
  }

  setCameraMode(mode: CameraMode): void {
    if (this.session) this.session.cameraMode = mode;
    this.world?.setCameraMode(mode);
  }

  adjustZoom(delta: number): void {
    this.world?.adjustCameraZoom(delta);
  }

  setSeason(season: Season): void {
    if (this.session) this.session.season = season;
    this.world?.setSeason(season);
  }

  setTime(time: 'auto' | number): void {
    if (this.session) this.session.time = time;
    this.world?.setTimeOfDay(time === 'auto' ? currentLocalHour() : time);
  }

  /** Registers a callback for photo-mode activation changes (overlay visibility). */
  onPhotoMode(handler: (active: boolean) => void): void {
    this.photoModeHandler = handler;
    handler(this.photoActive);
  }

  togglePhotoMode(): void {
    if (this.photoActive) this.exitPhotoMode();
    else this.enterPhotoMode();
  }

  enterPhotoMode(): void {
    // Switching workspace releases any held driver/look input.
    this.input.reset();
    const result = this.world?.setCinematicMode(true);
    this.photoActive = !!result?.active;
    document.body.classList.toggle('photo-mode', this.photoActive);
    if (result) this.photoLabelHandler?.(result.label);
    this.photoModeHandler?.(this.photoActive);
    void this.workspaces.activate(this.photoWorkspace);
    this.renderNow();
  }

  exitPhotoMode(): void {
    const result = this.world?.setCinematicMode(false);
    this.photoActive = false;
    document.body.classList.remove('photo-mode');
    if (result) this.photoLabelHandler?.(result.label);
    this.photoModeHandler?.(this.photoActive);
    void this.workspaces.activate(this.simulationWorkspace);
    this.renderNow();
  }

  cyclePhotoPreset(): void {
    const result = this.world?.cycleCinematicPreset();
    if (result) this.photoLabelHandler?.(result.label);
    this.renderNow();
  }

  async exportScreenshot(): Promise<void> {
    if (!this.world || !this.session) return;
    this.renderNow();
    document.body.classList.add('photo-capture');
    await new Promise<void>((resolve) => requestAnimationFrame(() => resolve()));
    const blob = await new Promise<Blob | null>((resolve) => this.world!.renderer.domElement.toBlob(resolve, 'image/png'));
    document.body.classList.remove('photo-capture');
    if (!blob) return;
    const link = document.createElement('a');
    link.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    link.download = `${this.session.city.id}_${this.session.route.line.replace(/[^\w-]+/g, '_')}_${this.world.currentCinematicPreset().replaceAll('-', '_')}.png`;
    link.click();
    setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(link.href), 1000);
  }

  /** Stops the loop and disposes the world; called on viewport unmount. */
  dispose(): void {
    this.machine.send({ type: 'DISPOSE' });
    this.input.onChange(null);
    this.input.reset();
    this.photoActive = false;
    this.loop.stop();
    this.loop.setState(null);
    this.engine?.dispose();
    this.engine = null;
    this.world = null;
    this.container.replaceChildren();
    document.body.classList.remove('photo-mode', 'photo-capture');
  }

  private applyWorldSettings(session: RouteSession): void {
    this.world?.setCameraMode(session.cameraMode);
    this.world?.setSeason(session.season);
    this.world?.setTimeOfDay(session.time === 'auto' ? currentLocalHour() : session.time);
  }

  private renderNow(): void {
    const state = this.loop.currentState();
    if (!state || !this.world) return;
    this.world.updateTrain(state.distanceM, state.doorsOpen, 1 / 60, state.nextStation);
    this.engine?.update(1 / 60, 1);
  }
}

const currentLocalHour = (): number => {
  const now = new Date();
  return now.getHours() + now.getMinutes() / 60;
};

--- simulator/simulator-types.ts ---
// Station data passed into the Go/WASM simulator.
export type StationConfig = {
  name: string;
  distanceM: number; // distance from route start in meters
  scheduledS?: number; // optional scheduled time from trip start in seconds
};

// Config used to initialize or restart one simulator session.
export type SimulatorConfig = {
  line: string;
  destination: string;
  routeLengthM: number; // total route length in meters
  speedLimitMps: number; // speed limit in meters per second
  mode?: 'ride' | 'driver';
  startStationIndex?: number; // optional selected departure station
  stations: StationConfig[];
};

// Authoritative simulator state returned from Go/WASM after each tick.
export type SimulatorState = {
  line: string;
  destination: string;
  distanceM: number; // current distance along the route in meters
  speedMps: number; // current speed in meters per second
  nextStation: string;
  stationIndex: number; // current/last reached station index
  doorsOpen: boolean;
  dwelling: boolean; // true while stopped for passenger dwell time
  dwellRemainingS: number; // remaining dwell time in seconds
  tripComplete: boolean;
  simTimeS: number; // elapsed simulation time in seconds
  mode: 'ride' | 'driver';
  driverScore: number;
  lastStopErrorM: number; // driver stop accuracy error in meters
  timetableDeltaS: number; // positive means late, negative means early
  safetyEvents: number;
  speeding: boolean;
};

--- simulator/SimulatorClient.ts ---
import type { SimulatorConfig, SimulatorState } from './simulator-types';

declare global {
  interface Window {
    // Go runtime constructor is provided by wasm_exec.js.
    Go: new () => { importObject: WebAssembly.Imports; run(instance: WebAssembly.Instance): Promise<void> };

    // Simulator bridge registered by the Go/WASM module.
    ssim?: SimulatorWasmBridge;
  }
}

// String-based Go/WASM bridge; JSON keeps the JS/WASM boundary simple.
type SimulatorWasmBridge = {
  initSim(configJson: string): string;
  stepSim(dtSeconds: number): string;
  resetSim(): string;
  setPaused(paused: boolean): string;
  setDriverInput(throttle: number, brake: number): string;
};

/** Loads one browser script tag and resolves when it is ready. */
function loadScript(src: string): Promise<void> {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const script = document.createElement('script');
    script.src = src;
    script.async = true;
    script.onload = () => resolve();
    script.onerror = () => reject(new Error(`Failed to load ${src}`));
    document.head.appendChild(script);
  });
}

/** Thin TypeScript client around the Go/WASM simulator bridge. */
export class SimulatorClient {
  // Shared promise prevents duplicate wasm_exec.js / sim.wasm initialization.
  private wasmReady: Promise<void> | null = null;

  /** Loads wasm_exec.js, instantiates sim.wasm, and waits for window.ssim. */
  async load(): Promise<void> {
    if (window.ssim) return;
    if (this.wasmReady) return this.wasmReady;

    this.wasmReady = (async () => {
      await loadScript('/wasm_exec.js'); // Go-provided JS runtime shim.

      const go = new window.Go();
      const response = await fetch('/sim.wasm'); // compiled Go simulator module.

      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(`Could not fetch sim.wasm: ${response.status}`);
      }

      const bytes = await response.arrayBuffer();
      const result = await WebAssembly.instantiate(bytes, go.importObject);

      // Go registers window.ssim before blocking inside its runtime loop.
      void go.run(result.instance);

      if (!window.ssim) {
        throw new Error('Go WASM loaded, but window.ssim was not registered.');
      }
    })();

    return this.wasmReady;
  }

  /** Initializes a new simulator session from route/config data. */
  init(config: SimulatorConfig): SimulatorState {
    return this.parse(window.ssim?.initSim(JSON.stringify(config)));
  }

  /** Advances the simulator by one frame delta in seconds. */
  step(dtSeconds: number): SimulatorState {
    return this.parse(window.ssim?.stepSim(dtSeconds));
  }

  /** Resets the current simulator session using its existing config. */
  reset(): SimulatorState {
    return this.parse(window.ssim?.resetSim());
  }

  /** Pauses or resumes simulator stepping without changing the active route. */
  setPaused(paused: boolean): SimulatorState {
    return this.parse(window.ssim?.setPaused(paused));
  }

  /** Sends normalized driver input: 0 means released, 1 means fully held. */
  setDriverInput(throttle: number, brake: number): SimulatorState {
    return this.parse(window.ssim?.setDriverInput(throttle, brake));
  }

  /** Parses JSON returned from Go/WASM and reports missing bridge errors consistently. */
  private parse(json: string | undefined): SimulatorState {
    if (!json) throw new Error('Simulator WASM bridge is not available.');
    return JSON.parse(json) as SimulatorState;
  }
}

--- route.ts ---
import * as THREE from 'three';
import type { SimulatorConfig } from './simulator/simulator-types';
import type { TransportMode } from './domain/transport';
import { routeTransportMode } from './domain/transport';

// Runtime route package used by both the renderer and Go/WASM simulator.
export type DemoRoute = SimulatorConfig & {
  id: string;
  transportMode?: TransportMode;
  agency?: string;
  description: string;
  freeRideLabel: string;
  source?: string;
  routeId?: string;
  tripId?: string;
  shapeId?: string;
  color?: string;
  textColor?: string;
  controlPoints: [number, number, number][]; // local Three.js route control points in meters
  geoPoints?: [number, number][]; // source GTFS/geo points as [lat, lon]
  elevationDatumM?: number; // terrain baseline used to normalize DEM heights
  surfaceHeightsM?: number[]; // sampled height profile along the route
  tunnelMask?: boolean[]; // per-shape/segment tunnel classification
  bridgeMask?: boolean[]; // per-shape/segment bridge classification
  tunnelRanges?: { startM: number; endM: number }[]; // route-distance tunnel ranges in meters
  vehicleDesignId?: string; // optional explicit vehicle registry id, overrides mode/family/kind resolution
  // Realtime-only vehicle runs after this route. The simulator swaps worlds
  // between them at transfers; generated catalog route files never use this.
  journeyLegs?: DemoRoute[];
  transferAfterS?: number; // realtime journey gap before the next vehicle run
};

// Complete route feed generated from GTFS or fallback/demo data.
export type GtfsRouteFeed = {
  generatedAt: string;
  sourceUrl: string;
  license: string;
  attribution: string;
  routes: DemoRoute[];
};

// Lightweight manifest so the app can lazy-load only the selected transport mode.
export type RouteFeedManifest = {
  generatedAt: string;
  sourceUrl: string;
  license: string;
  attribution: string;
  modes: Array<{
    mode: TransportMode;
    file: string;
    routeCount: number;
    lines: string[];
  }>;
};

// Default route used before external GTFS-derived route packages are loaded.
export const demoRoute = routePackages[0];

// Sorts bus-like numbered lines ahead of nonstandard labels while keeping natural German ordering.
function routeSortKey(route: DemoRoute): [number, string, string, string] {
  const line = route.line.trim();

  // Plain city bus line numbers, optionally with a one-letter suffix.
  const plainNumeric = /^\d{1,2}[A-Z]?$/i.test(line);

  const stuttgartBusPriority = routeMode(route) === 'bus'
    ? plainNumeric
      ? 0 // normal numeric bus lines first
      : /^\d/.test(line)
        ? 1 // other digit-starting labels next
        : 2 // named/special labels last
    : 0;

  return [stuttgartBusPriority, line, route.destination, route.id];
}

// Returns a sorted copy so callers do not mutate cached route arrays.
function sortRoutes(routes: DemoRoute[]): DemoRoute[] {
  return [...routes].sort((a, b) => {
    const [aPriority, aLine, aDestination, aId] = routeSortKey(a);
    const [bPriority, bLine, bDestination, bId] = routeSortKey(b);

    return aPriority - bPriority ||
      aLine.localeCompare(bLine, 'de', { numeric: true }) ||
      aDestination.localeCompare(bDestination, 'de') ||
      aId.localeCompare(bId, 'de');
  });
}

// Loads the city route manifest, falling back to built-in/demo metadata if unavailable.
export async function loadRouteManifest(
  file: string,
  fallbackManifest: RouteFeedManifest
): Promise<RouteFeedManifest> {
  try {
    const response = await fetch(file);
    if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`Route manifest request failed: ${response.status}`);

    const manifest = (await response.json()) as RouteFeedManifest;
    if (!Array.isArray(manifest.modes) || manifest.modes.length === 0) {
      throw new Error('Route manifest did not include any transport modes.');
    }

    return manifest;
  } catch (error) {
    console.warn('Falling back to demo route manifest.', error);
    return fallbackManifest;
  }
}

// Loads only the selected mode's route feed from the manifest.
export async function loadRoutesForMode(
  mode: TransportMode,
  manifest: RouteFeedManifest,
  fallbackRoutes: DemoRoute[],
  fallbackAttribution: string
): Promise<{ routes: DemoRoute[]; attribution: string }> {
  const matchingFallbackRoutes = sortRoutes(fallbackRoutes.filter((route) => routeMode(route) === mode));
  const descriptor = manifest.modes.find((entry) => entry.mode === mode);

  if (!descriptor) {
    if (matchingFallbackRoutes.length) return { routes: matchingFallbackRoutes, attribution: fallbackAttribution };
    return { routes: [], attribution: manifest.attribution };
  }

  try {
    const response = await fetch(descriptor.file);
    if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`Route feed request failed: ${response.status}`);

    const feed = (await response.json()) as GtfsRouteFeed;
    if (!Array.isArray(feed.routes) || feed.routes.length === 0) {
      throw new Error(`Route feed ${descriptor.file} did not include routes.`);
    }

    return { routes: sortRoutes(feed.routes), attribution: feed.attribution };
  } catch (error) {
    console.warn(`Falling back for ${mode} route feed.`, error);

    return matchingFallbackRoutes.length
      ? { routes: matchingFallbackRoutes, attribution: fallbackAttribution }
      : { routes: [], attribution: manifest.attribution };
  }
}

// Detects when generated local X coordinates need mirroring to match lon direction.
function inferredMirrorX(route: DemoRoute): boolean {
  const firstControl = route.controlPoints[0];
  const lastControl = route.controlPoints.at(-1);
  const firstGeo = route.geoPoints?.[0];
  const lastGeo = route.geoPoints?.at(-1);

  if (!firstControl || !lastControl || !firstGeo || !lastGeo) return false;

  const controlDx = lastControl[0] - firstControl[0];
  const geoDx = lastGeo[1] - firstGeo[1];

  // Ignore nearly vertical/noisy deltas that would make the sign check unstable.
  if (Math.abs(controlDx) < 1e-6 || Math.abs(geoDx) < 1e-9) return false;

  return Math.sign(controlDx) === Math.sign(geoDx);
}

// Builds the smoothed Three.js curve used by vehicles, tracks, stations, and route geometry.
export function createRouteCurve(route: DemoRoute, options?: { mirrorX?: boolean }): THREE.CatmullRomCurve3 {
  const mirrorX = (options?.mirrorX ?? inferredMirrorX(route)) ? -1 : 1;
  let points = route.controlPoints.map(([x, y, z]) => new THREE.Vector3(x * mirrorX, y, z));

  // Two light smoothing passes reduce jagged GTFS-derived XZ shapes without changing endpoints.
  // Y is excluded: elevation is already correctly set by terrain enrichment and must match surfaceHeightsM.
  for (let pass = 0; pass < 2; pass++) {
    points = points.map((point, index, source) => {
      if (index === 0 || index === source.length - 1) return point.clone();
      const blendedX = source[index - 1].x * 0.2 + point.x * 0.6 + source[index + 1].x * 0.2;
      const blendedZ = source[index - 1].z * 0.2 + point.z * 0.6 + source[index + 1].z * 0.2;
      return new THREE.Vector3(blendedX, point.y, blendedZ);
    });
  }

  // Centripetal curves reduce loops/overshoot on tight route bends.
  const curve = new THREE.CatmullRomCurve3(points, false, 'centripetal');

  // Minimum 1200 divisions keeps distance mapping smooth on short routes; 12 per point scales with long routes.
  curve.arcLengthDivisions = Math.max(1200, points.length * 12);
  curve.updateArcLengths();

  return curve;
}

// Converts route distance in meters to normalized curve progress.
export function distanceToT(distanceM: number, routeLengthM: number): number {
  return THREE.MathUtils.clamp(distanceM / routeLengthM, 0, 1);
}

// Normalizes missing/legacy route transport modes through the domain helper.
export function routeMode(route: DemoRoute): TransportMode {
  return routeTransportMode(route.transportMode);
}

--- landmarks.ts ---
import * as THREE from 'three';

export type GeoPoint = { lat: number; lon: number };
export type LandmarkZone = { lat: number; lon: number; radiusM: number };

type LandmarkKind = 'new-palace' | 'old-castle' | 'stiftskirche' | 'johanneskirche' | 'bonatzbau' | 'weissenhof' | 'tv-tower' | 'chapel';
type LandmarkDefinition = LandmarkZone & { name: string; kind: LandmarkKind; yawDeg: number; heightM: number };

// Curated Stuttgart landmarks used as simple recognizable silhouettes near relevant routes.
const LANDMARKS: LandmarkDefinition[] = [
  { name: 'Neues Schloss', kind: 'new-palace', lat: 48.7781573, lon: 9.1814868, radiusM: 55, yawDeg: 5, heightM: 23 },
  { name: 'Altes Schloss', kind: 'old-castle', lat: 48.7770198, lon: 9.1797704, radiusM: 48, yawDeg: -18, heightM: 24 },
  { name: 'Stiftskirche', kind: 'stiftskirche', lat: 48.776685, lon: 9.1779188, radiusM: 35, yawDeg: 72, heightM: 42 },
  { name: 'Johanneskirche am Feuersee', kind: 'johanneskirche', lat: 48.7733299, lon: 9.1645932, radiusM: 36, yawDeg: -14, heightM: 38 },
  { name: 'Hauptbahnhof Bonatzbau', kind: 'bonatzbau', lat: 48.7836167, lon: 9.1816624, radiusM: 70, yawDeg: 88, heightM: 48 },
  { name: 'Weißenhofsiedlung', kind: 'weissenhof', lat: 48.8005532, lon: 9.1772977, radiusM: 72, yawDeg: 15, heightM: 15 },
  { name: 'Fernsehturm Stuttgart', kind: 'tv-tower', lat: 48.7557623, lon: 9.1901682, radiusM: 28, yawDeg: 0, heightM: 217 },
  { name: 'Grabkapelle auf dem Württemberg', kind: 'chapel', lat: 48.7820408, lon: 9.2687406, radiusM: 24, yawDeg: 0, heightM: 24 }
];

type LandmarkMaterials = {
  sandstone: THREE.MeshStandardMaterial;
  redSandstone: THREE.MeshStandardMaterial;
  roof: THREE.MeshStandardMaterial;
  darkRoof: THREE.MeshStandardMaterial;
  white: THREE.MeshStandardMaterial;
  glass: THREE.MeshStandardMaterial;
  concrete: THREE.MeshStandardMaterial;
  metal: THREE.MeshStandardMaterial;
};

/** Builds route-proximity landmark silhouettes and exclusion zones for streamed OSM buildings. */
export function createStuttgartLandmarks(
  route: GeoPoint[],
  projectGeo: (lat: number, lon: number) => THREE.Vector3,
  surfaceHeight: (lat: number, lon: number) => Promise<number>
): { group: THREE.Group; zones: LandmarkZone[] } {
  const group = new THREE.Group();
  group.name = 'Curated Stuttgart landmark silhouettes';

  // Only render landmarks within 1.4 km of the selected route.
  const nearby = LANDMARKS.filter((landmark) => distanceToRouteM(landmark, route) <= 1400);
  const materials = makeMaterials();

  for (const landmark of nearby) {
    const model = buildLandmark(landmark, materials);
    model.name = landmark.name;
    model.position.copy(projectGeo(landmark.lat, landmark.lon));
    model.rotation.y = THREE.MathUtils.degToRad(landmark.yawDeg);

    model.traverse((object) => {
      if (!(object instanceof THREE.Mesh)) return;

      // The TV tower is thin/tall, so skipping shadows avoids noisy shadow artifacts.
      object.castShadow = landmark.kind !== 'tv-tower';
      object.receiveShadow = true;
    });

    group.add(model);

    // Height loads async so landmark creation does not block the world rebuild.
    void surfaceHeight(landmark.lat, landmark.lon)
      .then((height) => { model.position.y = height + 0.08; }) // tiny lift prevents z-fighting with terrain.
      .catch(() => undefined);
  }

  // Exclusion zones prevent streamed OSM buildings from overlapping curated landmarks.
  return { group, zones: nearby.map(({ lat, lon, radiusM }) => ({ lat, lon, radiusM })) };
}

/** Shared materials for all low-poly landmark silhouettes. */
function makeMaterials(): LandmarkMaterials {
  return {
    sandstone: new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xb7a48b, roughness: 0.9 }), // Stuttgart sandstone beige.
    redSandstone: new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x916f5d, roughness: 0.92 }), // church red sandstone.
    roof: new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x744b3c, roughness: 0.94 }), // warm tile roof.
    darkRoof: new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x3f484c, roughness: 0.82, metalness: 0.08 }), // dark metal/slate roof.
    white: new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xe8e5dc, roughness: 0.82 }), // light plaster/concrete.
    glass: new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x31434c, roughness: 0.35, metalness: 0.15 }), // dark reflective window bands.
    concrete: new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xaaa9a2, roughness: 0.88 }), // neutral concrete.
    metal: new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x7f898c, roughness: 0.38, metalness: 0.68 }) // grey metal.
  };
}

/** Dispatches each landmark kind to its procedural silhouette builder. */
function buildLandmark(definition: LandmarkDefinition, materials: LandmarkMaterials): THREE.Group {
  switch (definition.kind) {
    case 'new-palace': return buildNewPalace(materials);
    case 'old-castle': return buildOldCastle(materials);
    case 'stiftskirche': return buildStiftskirche(materials);
    case 'johanneskirche': return buildJohanneskirche(materials);
    case 'bonatzbau': return buildBonatzbau(materials);
    case 'weissenhof': return buildWeissenhof(materials);
    case 'tv-tower': return buildTvTower(materials);
    case 'chapel': return buildChapel(materials);
  }
}

/** Simplified Neues Schloss massing: central block, wings, hipped roofs, and dome. */
function buildNewPalace(m: LandmarkMaterials): THREE.Group {
  const group = new THREE.Group();

  addBox(group, [38, 13, 16], [0, 6.5, 0], m.sandstone); // central palace block.
  addBox(group, [13, 10, 43], [-24, 5, 13], m.sandstone); // left wing.
  addBox(group, [13, 10, 43], [24, 5, 13], m.sandstone); // right wing.

  addHippedRoof(group, 40, 18, 5, [0, 13, 0], m.darkRoof);
  addHippedRoof(group, 15, 45, 4, [-24, 10, 13], m.darkRoof);
  addHippedRoof(group, 15, 45, 4, [24, 10, 13], m.darkRoof);

  const dome = new THREE.Mesh(
    new THREE.SphereGeometry(5.2, 14, 7, 0, Math.PI * 2, 0, Math.PI / 2), // half-sphere dome.
    m.darkRoof
  );
  dome.position.set(0, 18, 0);
  group.add(dome);

  addBox(group, [2.2, 3, 2.2], [0, 20.2, 0], m.sandstone); // small dome lantern.

  return group;
}

/** Simplified Altes Schloss massing: courtyard block with four corner towers. */
function buildOldCastle(m: LandmarkMaterials): THREE.Group {
  const group = new THREE.Group();

  addBox(group, [38, 13, 31], [0, 6.5, 0], m.sandstone);
  addHippedRoof(group, 40, 33, 7, [0, 13, 0], m.roof);

  for (const x of [-18, 18]) {
    for (const z of [-14, 14]) {
      addCylinder(group, 4.3, 16, [x, 8, z], m.sandstone, 12); // corner tower body.
      addCone(group, 5, 6, [x, 19, z], m.roof, 12); // pointed tower roof.
    }
  }

  return group;
}

/** Simplified Stiftskirche with nave and two asymmetric towers. */
function buildStiftskirche(m: LandmarkMaterials): THREE.Group {
  const group = new THREE.Group();

  addBox(group, [17, 13, 40], [0, 6.5, 3], m.redSandstone); // church nave.
  addGableRoof(group, 18, 42, 6, [0, 13, 3], m.roof);

  addBox(group, [9, 28, 9], [-5, 14, -20], m.redSandstone); // taller tower.
  addHippedRoof(group, 10, 10, 6, [-5, 28, -20], m.darkRoof);

  addBox(group, [6.5, 20, 6.5], [5.5, 10, -19], m.redSandstone); // smaller tower.
  addCone(group, 5, 12, [5.5, 26, -19], m.darkRoof, 8);

  return group;
}

/** Simplified Johanneskirche silhouette with tall tower and nave. */
function buildJohanneskirche(m: LandmarkMaterials): THREE.Group {
  const group = new THREE.Group();

  addBox(group, [16, 14, 37], [0, 7, 2], m.redSandstone); // nave.
  addGableRoof(group, 17, 39, 7, [0, 14, 2], m.roof);

  addBox(group, [10, 31, 10], [0, 15.5, -22], m.redSandstone); // main tower.
  addBox(group, [10.8, 2, 10.8], [0, 32, -22], m.darkRoof); // flat tower cap.

  for (const x of [-8.7, 8.7]) {
    for (const z of [-10, 0, 10]) {
      addBox(group, [1.2, 8, 1.8], [x, 4, z], m.redSandstone); // side buttresses/window rhythm.
    }
  }

  return group;
}

/** Simplified Hauptbahnhof Bonatzbau with long hall, tower, glass strip, and clock. */
function buildBonatzbau(m: LandmarkMaterials): THREE.Group {
  const group = new THREE.Group();

  addBox(group, [62, 17, 20], [0, 8.5, 0], m.sandstone); // station hall block.
  addBox(group, [15, 47, 15], [-22, 23.5, 0], m.sandstone); // clock tower.
  addBox(group, [16, 1.2, 16], [-22, 47.5, 0], m.darkRoof); // tower cap.
  addBox(group, [44, 3.2, 0.5], [8, 11, -10.2], m.glass); // front glazing strip.

  const clock = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CircleGeometry(3, 20), m.white); // simple clock face.
  clock.position.set(-22, 36, -7.56);
  clock.rotation.y = Math.PI;
  group.add(clock);

  return group;
}

/** Simplified Weißenhofsiedlung: white modernist blocks with horizontal windows. */
function buildWeissenhof(m: LandmarkMaterials): THREE.Group {
  const group = new THREE.Group();

  const blocks: [number[], number[]][] = [
    [[28, 11, 12], [0, 5.5, 0]],
    [[16, 8, 13], [-24, 4, 8]],
    [[18, 9, 11], [23, 4.5, -7]],
    [[13, 7, 10], [4, 3.5, 18]]
  ];

  for (const [size, position] of blocks) {
    addBox(group, size as [number, number, number], position as [number, number, number], m.white);

    // Horizontal glazing band gives the white boxes a modernist silhouette.
    addBox(
      group,
      [size[0] * 0.82, 1.4, 0.3],
      [position[0], position[1] + 1, position[2] - size[2] / 2 - 0.16],
      m.glass
    );
  }

  return group;
}

/** Simplified Fernsehturm: tapered shaft, observation deck, and antenna. */
function buildTvTower(m: LandmarkMaterials): THREE.Group {
  const group = new THREE.Group();

  addCylinder(group, 1.8, 126, [0, 63, 0], m.concrete, 12, 1.1); // tapered concrete shaft.
  addCylinder(group, 8.2, 8, [0, 127, 0], m.concrete, 16); // observation deck base.
  addCylinder(group, 6.8, 6, [0, 134, 0], m.glass, 16); // glazed observation band.
  addCylinder(group, 7.5, 1.2, [0, 137.6, 0], m.metal, 16); // deck cap.
  addCylinder(group, 0.55, 79, [0, 177.5, 0], m.metal, 8, 0.18); // antenna mast.

  return group;
}

/** Simplified Grabkapelle: circular base, columns, dome, and lantern. */
function buildChapel(m: LandmarkMaterials): THREE.Group {
  const group = new THREE.Group();

  addCylinder(group, 11, 2.4, [0, 1.2, 0], m.sandstone, 18); // circular plinth.
  addCylinder(group, 7.2, 8, [0, 6.4, 0], m.white, 18); // round chapel body.

  for (let index = 0; index < 12; index++) {
    const angle = (index / 12) * Math.PI * 2;
    addCylinder(
      group,
      0.35,
      7,
      [Math.cos(angle) * 8.2, 5.9, Math.sin(angle) * 8.2],
      m.sandstone,
      8
    ); // ring of exterior columns.
  }

  const dome = new THREE.Mesh(
    new THREE.SphereGeometry(7.4, 16, 8, 0, Math.PI * 2, 0, Math.PI / 2), // half-sphere dome.
    m.darkRoof
  );
  dome.position.y = 10.4;
  group.add(dome);

  addCylinder(group, 1.2, 3.5, [0, 16, 0], m.white, 10); // small dome lantern.

  return group;
}

/** Adds a simple box mesh to a landmark group. */
function addBox(group: THREE.Group, size: [number, number, number], position: [number, number, number], material: THREE.Material): void {
  const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(...size), material);
  mesh.position.set(...position);
  group.add(mesh);
}

/** Adds a cylinder or tapered cylinder mesh to a landmark group. */
function addCylinder(
  group: THREE.Group,
  radius: number,
  height: number,
  position: [number, number, number],
  material: THREE.Material,
  segments: number,
  topRadius = radius
): void {
  const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(topRadius, radius, height, segments), material);
  mesh.position.set(...position);
  group.add(mesh);
}

/** Adds a cone mesh, usually used for towers or pointed roofs. */
function addCone(group: THREE.Group, radius: number, height: number, position: [number, number, number], material: THREE.Material, segments: number): void {
  const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.ConeGeometry(radius, height, segments), material);
  mesh.position.set(...position);
  group.add(mesh);
}

/** Adds a low-poly hipped roof using a scaled four-sided cone. */
function addHippedRoof(group: THREE.Group, width: number, depth: number, height: number, position: [number, number, number], material: THREE.Material): void {
  const roof = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.ConeGeometry(1, height, 4), material);
  roof.position.set(position[0], position[1] + height / 2, position[2]);
  roof.rotation.y = Math.PI / 4; // rotate square cone so roof ridges align diagonally.
  roof.scale.set(width / Math.SQRT2, 1, depth / Math.SQRT2); // compensate for cone's diagonal footprint.
  group.add(roof);
}

/** Adds a triangular prism gable roof with manually indexed faces. */
function addGableRoof(group: THREE.Group, width: number, depth: number, height: number, position: [number, number, number], material: THREE.Material): void {
  const halfW = width / 2;
  const halfD = depth / 2;

  // Two triangular ends plus rectangular roof slopes.
  const vertices = new Float32Array([
    -halfW, 0, -halfD, halfW, 0, -halfD, 0, height, -halfD,
    -halfW, 0, halfD, halfW, 0, halfD, 0, height, halfD
  ]);

  const geometry = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
  geometry.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(vertices, 3));

  // Face index order for end triangles and side rectangles.
  geometry.setIndex([0, 1, 2, 3, 5, 4, 0, 3, 1, 1, 3, 4, 0, 2, 3, 2, 5, 3, 1, 4, 2, 2, 4, 5]);
  geometry.computeVertexNormals();

  const roof = new THREE.Mesh(geometry, material);
  roof.position.set(...position);
  group.add(roof);
}

/** Returns the nearest approximate distance from a landmark point to sampled route points. */
function distanceToRouteM(point: GeoPoint, route: GeoPoint[]): number {
  if (!route.length) return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;

  const lonScale = Math.cos((point.lat * Math.PI) / 180) * 111_320; // meters per lon degree at this latitude.
  let nearest = Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;

  for (const sample of route) {
    nearest = Math.min(
      nearest,
      Math.hypot(
        (sample.lat - point.lat) * 111_320, // approximate meters per latitude degree.
        (sample.lon - point.lon) * lonScale
      )
    );
  }

  return nearest;
}

--- vehicles/VehicleInteriorDisplay.test.ts ---
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { VehicleInteriorDisplay } from './VehicleInteriorDisplay';

describe('VehicleInteriorDisplay', () => {
  it('exposes a reusable MeshBasicMaterial backed by a canvas texture', () => {
    const display = new VehicleInteriorDisplay();
    expect(display.material).toBeInstanceOf(THREE.MeshBasicMaterial);
    expect(display.material.map).toBeInstanceOf(THREE.CanvasTexture);
    display.dispose();
  });

  it('updates without throwing and skips redundant repaints', () => {
    const display = new VehicleInteriorDisplay();
    const input = { line: 'U12', destination: 'Dürrlewang', nextStop: 'Aldingen Mühle', doorsOpen: true, accentColor: '#ffd800' };
    expect(() => display.update(input)).not.toThrow();
    expect(() => display.update(input)).not.toThrow(); // same key → early return
    expect(() => display.update({ ...input, nextStop: 'End of line' })).not.toThrow();
    display.dispose();
  });
});

--- vehicles/VehicleVisualController.test.ts ---
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { VehicleVisualController } from './VehicleVisualController';
import type { VehicleInstance } from './types';

function makeInstance() {
  const doorLeaf = { group: new THREE.Group(), closedZ: 0, direction: 1 as const };
  const glass = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ transparent: true });
  const band = new THREE.Mesh();
  const cabShell = new THREE.Mesh();
  const passengerShell = new THREE.Mesh();
  const cabDisplay = new THREE.Mesh();
  const lights: boolean[] = [];
  const instance: VehicleInstance = {
    root: new THREE.Group(),
    cars: [],
    doors: [doorLeaf],
    windowBands: [band],
    cabShells: [cabShell],
    passengerShells: [passengerShell],
    cabDisplays: [cabDisplay],
    glassMaterial: glass,
    trainHalfLengthM: 20.15,
    setExteriorLightsOn: (on) => lights.push(on)
  };
  return { instance, doorLeaf, glass, band, cabShell, passengerShell, cabDisplay, lights };
}

function make() {
  const m = makeInstance();
  const controller = new VehicleVisualController(m.instance, null, {
    line: 'U12',
    destination: 'Dürrlewang',
    lineAccentColor: '#ffd800',
    interiorCameraProfile: 'tram'
  });
  return { ...m, controller };
}

describe('VehicleVisualController.setPresentation', () => {
  it('hides body shells and clears the glass in passenger view', () => {
    const { controller, glass, band, passengerShell, cabShell } = make();
    controller.setPresentation('passenger', false);
    expect(passengerShell.visible).toBe(false);
    expect(band.visible).toBe(false);
    expect(cabShell.visible).toBe(true); // cab shell only hides in cab view
    expect(glass.opacity).toBeCloseTo(0.1, 5);
  });

  it('hides the cab shell and display in cab view', () => {
    const { controller, cabShell, cabDisplay } = make();
    controller.setPresentation('cab', false);
    expect(cabShell.visible).toBe(false);
    expect(cabDisplay.visible).toBe(false);
  });

  it('restores the exterior in chase view', () => {
    const { controller, glass, passengerShell, cabShell } = make();
    controller.setPresentation('passenger', false);
    controller.setPresentation('chase', false);
    expect(passengerShell.visible).toBe(true);
    expect(cabShell.visible).toBe(true);
    expect(glass.opacity).toBeCloseTo(0.58, 5);
  });

  it('keeps the exterior solid while a cinematic (photo) is active', () => {
    const { controller, passengerShell } = make();
    controller.setPresentation('passenger', true);
    expect(passengerShell.visible).toBe(true);
  });
});

describe('VehicleVisualController.updateDoors', () => {
  it('slides doors open toward the full offset and back closed', () => {
    const { controller, doorLeaf } = make();
    for (let i = 0; i < 80; i++) controller.updateDoors(true, 0.1);
    expect(doorLeaf.group.position.z).toBeGreaterThan(0.6);
    for (let i = 0; i < 80; i++) controller.updateDoors(false, 0.1);
    expect(doorLeaf.group.position.z).toBeLessThan(0.05);
  });
});

describe('VehicleVisualController.updateExteriorLights', () => {
  it('switches lamps on below the daylight threshold', () => {
    const { controller, lights } = make();
    controller.updateExteriorLights(0.2);
    controller.updateExteriorLights(0.9);
    expect(lights).toEqual([true, false]);
  });
});

--- vehicles/parityCheck.ts ---
import * as THREE from 'three';
import type { CityDefinition } from '../cities';
import type { TransportModeDefinition } from '../domain/transport';
import type { DemoRoute } from '../route';
import { buildTransitVehicle } from './buildTransitVehicle';
import type { VehicleBuildContext } from './types';

// Dev-only parity harness: fingerprints a vehicle's full scene graph so a
// refactor of a builder can be proven visually identical (mesh-for-mesh) to
// the previous implementation. Not imported by app code; use from the browser
// console on the vite dev server:
//
//   const m = await import('/src/vehicles/parityCheck.ts');
//   m.captureBaseline();          // before the refactor
//   m.compareWithBaseline();      // after the refactor -> [] means identical
//
// Baselines persist in localStorage across reloads/HMR.

type ParityTarget = { line: string; vehicleKind: string; modeId: string };

const PARITY_TARGETS: Record<string, ParityTarget> = {
  'bus-rigid': { line: '35', vehicleKind: 'bus', modeId: 'bus' },
  'bus-articulated': { line: '40', vehicleKind: 'bus', modeId: 'bus' },
  'sbahn': { line: 'S1', vehicleKind: 's-bahn', modeId: 's-bahn' }
};

function makeContext(target: ParityTarget): VehicleBuildContext {
  const route = {
    id: `parity-${target.line}`,
    line: target.line,
    destination: 'Parity Test'
  } as unknown as DemoRoute;
  const city = { id: 'stuttgart' } as unknown as CityDefinition;
  const modeDefinition = {
    id: target.modeId,
    vehicleFamily: `Parity ${target.vehicleKind}`,
    vehicleKind: target.vehicleKind
  } as unknown as TransportModeDefinition;
  return {
    route,
    city,
    modeDefinition,
    makeInteriorDisplayMaterial: () => new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ side: THREE.DoubleSide })
  };
}

function round(value: number): number {
  return Math.round(value * 1e6) / 1e6;
}

// Keeps only JSON-safe primitive entries of THREE geometry parameter bags.
function sanitizeParams(params: unknown): Record<string, unknown> {
  const out: Record<string, unknown> = {};
  if (!params || typeof params !== 'object') return out;
  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(params as Record<string, unknown>)) {
    if (typeof value === 'number') out[key] = round(value);
    else if (typeof value === 'string' || typeof value === 'boolean') out[key] = value;
  }
  return out;
}

function fingerprintMaterial(material: THREE.Material | THREE.Material[]): unknown {
  if (Array.isArray(material)) return material.map((m) => fingerprintMaterial(m));
  const record: Record<string, unknown> = { type: material.type };
  const std = material as THREE.MeshStandardMaterial;
  if (std.color) record.color = std.color.getHexString();
  if (std.emissive) record.emissive = std.emissive.getHexString();
  if (typeof std.emissiveIntensity === 'number') record.emissiveIntensity = round(std.emissiveIntensity);
  if (typeof std.roughness === 'number') record.roughness = round(std.roughness);
  if (typeof std.metalness === 'number') record.metalness = round(std.metalness);
  record.opacity = round(material.opacity);
  record.transparent = material.transparent;
  record.side = material.side;
  record.depthWrite = material.depthWrite;
  record.hasMap = Boolean((std as unknown as { map?: unknown }).map);
  return record;
}

type Fingerprint = Record<string, unknown>;

function hashVertexPositions(geometry: THREE.BufferGeometry): string {
  const attribute = geometry.getAttribute('position');
  if (!attribute) return 'none';
  const array = attribute.array as ArrayLike<number>;
  let hash = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < array.length; i++) {
    hash = (hash * 31 + Math.round(array[i] * 10000)) | 0;
  }
  return `${array.length}:${hash}`;
}

function fingerprintObject(object: THREE.Object3D): Fingerprint {
  const record: Fingerprint = {
    type: object.type,
    name: object.name,
    position: object.position.toArray().map(round),
    rotation: [object.rotation.x, object.rotation.y, object.rotation.z].map(round),
    scale: object.scale.toArray().map(round),
    castShadow: object.castShadow,
    renderOrder: object.renderOrder
  };
  const mesh = object as THREE.Mesh;
  if (mesh.isMesh) {
    record.geometry = {
      type: mesh.geometry.type,
      params: sanitizeParams((mesh.geometry as unknown as { parameters?: unknown }).parameters),
      // Vertex-level hash so non-parametric geometries (Extrude/Tube shapes)
      // are covered too, not just constructor arguments.
      vertexHash: hashVertexPositions(mesh.geometry)
    };
    record.material = fingerprintMaterial(mesh.material);
  }
  const light = object as THREE.Light & { distance?: number; decay?: number; angle?: number; penumbra?: number };
  if ((object as THREE.Light).isLight) {
    record.light = {
      color: (object as THREE.Light).color.getHexString(),
      intensity: round((object as THREE.Light).intensity),
      distance: light.distance !== undefined ? round(light.distance) : undefined,
      decay: light.decay !== undefined ? round(light.decay) : undefined,
      angle: light.angle !== undefined ? round(light.angle) : undefined,
      penumbra: light.penumbra !== undefined ? round(light.penumbra) : undefined
    };
  }
  record.children = object.children.map((child) => fingerprintObject(child));
  return record;
}

function fingerprintVehicle(target: ParityTarget): Fingerprint {
  const vehicle = buildTransitVehicle(makeContext(target));
  const record: Fingerprint = {
    scene: fingerprintObject(vehicle.root),
    doors: vehicle.doors.map((door) => ({ closedZ: round(door.closedZ), direction: door.direction, leaves: door.group.children.length })),
    windowBands: vehicle.windowBands.length,
    cabShells: vehicle.cabShells.length,
    passengerShells: vehicle.passengerShells.length,
    cabDisplays: vehicle.cabDisplays.length,
    cars: vehicle.cars.map((car) => ({ name: car.name, centerOffsetM: car.userData.centerOffsetM })),
    glassMaterial: vehicle.glassMaterial ? fingerprintMaterial(vehicle.glassMaterial) : null,
    trainHalfLengthM: round(vehicle.trainHalfLengthM),
    hasExteriorLights: Boolean(vehicle.setExteriorLightsOn)
  };
  vehicle.root.traverse((object) => {
    const mesh = object as THREE.Mesh;
    if (mesh.isMesh) mesh.geometry.dispose();
  });
  return record;
}

function storageKey(variant: string): string {
  return `vehicle-parity-baseline-${variant}`;
}

export function captureBaseline(): Record<string, number> {
  const sizes: Record<string, number> = {};
  for (const [variant, target] of Object.entries(PARITY_TARGETS)) {
    const json = JSON.stringify(fingerprintVehicle(target));
    localStorage.setItem(storageKey(variant), json);
    sizes[variant] = json.length;
  }
  return sizes;
}

function diffValues(path: string, before: unknown, after: unknown, out: string[]): void {
  if (out.length >= 40) return;
  if (before === null || after === null || typeof before !== 'object' || typeof after !== 'object') {
    if (before !== after) out.push(`${path}: ${JSON.stringify(before)} -> ${JSON.stringify(after)}`);
    return;
  }
  const beforeRec = before as Record<string, unknown>;
  const afterRec = after as Record<string, unknown>;
  const keys = new Set([...Object.keys(beforeRec), ...Object.keys(afterRec)]);
  for (const key of keys) {
    diffValues(`${path}.${key}`, beforeRec[key], afterRec[key], out);
    if (out.length >= 40) return;
  }
}

export function compareWithBaseline(): Record<string, string[]> {
  const result: Record<string, string[]> = {};
  for (const [variant, target] of Object.entries(PARITY_TARGETS)) {
    const baselineJson = localStorage.getItem(storageKey(variant));
    if (!baselineJson) {
      result[variant] = ['NO BASELINE CAPTURED'];
      continue;
    }
    const diffs: string[] = [];
    diffValues(variant, JSON.parse(baselineJson), fingerprintVehicle(target), diffs);
    result[variant] = diffs;
  }
  return result;
}

--- vehicles/formation.test.ts ---
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { isStadtbahnDoubleFormation, isBusLineArticulated, defaultBusBlueprint } from './index';

describe('isStadtbahnDoubleFormation', () => {
  it('flags the coupled double-traction Stadtbahn lines', () => {
    for (const line of ['U1', 'U6', 'U7', 'U11', 'U12', 'U19']) {
      expect(isStadtbahnDoubleFormation(line)).toBe(true);
    }
  });

  it('treats other lines as single formation', () => {
    for (const line of ['U5', 'U14', 'U2', '']) {
      expect(isStadtbahnDoubleFormation(line)).toBe(false);
    }
    expect(isStadtbahnDoubleFormation(undefined)).toBe(false);
  });

  it('normalizes case and whitespace', () => {
    expect(isStadtbahnDoubleFormation(' u12 ')).toBe(true);
  });
});

describe('isBusLineArticulated (city unknown, as in Live)', () => {
  it('flags listed lines and X-prefixed express lines', () => {
    expect(isBusLineArticulated(defaultBusBlueprint, '42', undefined)).toBe(true);
    expect(isBusLineArticulated(defaultBusBlueprint, 'X1', undefined)).toBe(true);
  });

  it('treats ordinary lines as rigid', () => {
    expect(isBusLineArticulated(defaultBusBlueprint, '70', undefined)).toBe(false);
    expect(isBusLineArticulated(defaultBusBlueprint, undefined, undefined)).toBe(false);
  });

  it('respects the city allow-list when a city is known', () => {
    expect(isBusLineArticulated(defaultBusBlueprint, '42', 'stuttgart')).toBe(true);
    expect(isBusLineArticulated(defaultBusBlueprint, '42', 'ulm')).toBe(false);
  });
});

--- vehicles/parts.ts ---
import * as THREE from 'three';
import type { VehicleInstance } from './types';

// Shared vehicle part builders used by both the procedural factory and the
// blueprint renderers. Geometry and constants are unchanged extractions from
// the procedural builders; keep them byte-identical unless visuals should change.

export type ExteriorLightRig = {
  headLampMaterials: THREE.MeshStandardMaterial[];
  tailLampMaterials: THREE.MeshStandardMaterial[];
  headlightBeams: THREE.SpotLight[];
  tailGlows: THREE.PointLight[];
  cabinLights: THREE.PointLight[];
};

export function makeExteriorLightRig(): ExteriorLightRig {
  return { headLampMaterials: [], tailLampMaterials: [], headlightBeams: [], tailGlows: [], cabinLights: [] };
}

// Night-time light throw: a forward headlight beam and a red tail glow, both
// switched off in daylight via the setExteriorLightsOn handler.
export function addExteriorLightThrow(root: THREE.Object3D, rig: ExteriorLightRig, halfLengthM: number, beamHeightM: number): void {
  const headBeam = new THREE.SpotLight(0xfff3d0, 0, 80, 0.4, 0.6, 1.8);
  headBeam.name = 'Exterior lamp headlight beam';
  headBeam.position.set(0, beamHeightM, halfLengthM - 0.1);
  headBeam.target.name = 'Exterior lamp headlight target';
  headBeam.target.position.set(0, 0.15, halfLengthM + 26);
  root.add(headBeam, headBeam.target);
  rig.headlightBeams.push(headBeam);

  const tailGlow = new THREE.PointLight(0xd42020, 0, 16, 2);
  tailGlow.name = 'Exterior lamp tail glow';
  tailGlow.position.set(0, 0.9, -(halfLengthM + 1.4));
  root.add(tailGlow);
  rig.tailGlows.push(tailGlow);
}

// Warm cabin light so the interior stays readable in night/dawn views;
// switched together with the exterior lamps and off in daylight.
export function addCabinLight(rig: ExteriorLightRig, parent: THREE.Object3D, y: number, z = 0): void {
  const light = new THREE.PointLight(0xfff4dc, 0, 11, 1.8);
  light.name = 'Cabin light';
  light.position.set(0, y, z);
  parent.add(light);
  rig.cabinLights.push(light);
}

// Builds the day/night switch for every lamp material and light in the rig.
// Returns undefined when the vehicle has no switchable lights at all.
export function makeSetExteriorLightsOn(rig: ExteriorLightRig): ((on: boolean) => void) | undefined {
  if (rig.headLampMaterials.length + rig.tailLampMaterials.length + rig.cabinLights.length === 0) return undefined;
  return (on: boolean) => {
    for (const lamp of rig.headLampMaterials) lamp.emissiveIntensity = on ? 3.4 : 0.55;
    for (const lamp of rig.tailLampMaterials) lamp.emissiveIntensity = on ? 1.8 : 0.35;
    for (const beam of rig.headlightBeams) beam.intensity = on ? 260 : 0;
    for (const glow of rig.tailGlows) glow.intensity = on ? 8 : 0;
    for (const cabin of rig.cabinLights) cabin.intensity = on ? 6 : 0;
  };
}

// Scissor pantograph: four angled arms rising from a roof base to the
// contact shoe. Added to the vehicle root at an absolute roof position.
export function addPantograph(root: THREE.Object3D, position: THREE.Vector3, material: THREE.Material): void {
  const base = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(1.45, 0.14, 0.72), material);
  base.position.copy(position);
  root.add(base);
  const points = [
    [new THREE.Vector3(-0.55, 0, -0.2), new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.78, 0)],
    [new THREE.Vector3(0.55, 0, -0.2), new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.78, 0)],
    [new THREE.Vector3(-0.55, 0, 0.2), new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.78, 0)],
    [new THREE.Vector3(0.55, 0, 0.2), new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.78, 0)]
  ] as const;
  for (const [a, b] of points) {
    const curve = new THREE.LineCurve3(a.clone().add(position), b.clone().add(position));
    const arm = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.TubeGeometry(curve, 1, 0.025, 6), material);
    root.add(arm);
  }
  const shoe = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(1.85, 0.055, 0.16), material);
  shoe.position.copy(position).add(new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.84, 0));
  root.add(shoe);
}

export function addSlidingVehicleDoor(
  doors: VehicleInstance['doors'],
  car: THREE.Group,
  x: number,
  z: number,
  doorMaterial: THREE.Material,
  glassMaterial: THREE.Material,
  openingMaterial: THREE.Material,
  width: number,
  height: number
): void {
  const side = Math.sign(x) || 1;
  const opening = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(width, height), openingMaterial);
  opening.position.set(x + side * 0.055, 1.75, z);
  opening.rotation.y = side > 0 ? Math.PI / 2 : -Math.PI / 2;
  car.add(opening);
  for (const direction of [-1, 1] as const) {
    const closedZ = z + direction * width * 0.24;
    const leaf = new THREE.Group();
    leaf.position.z = closedZ;
    for (const edge of [-1, 1] as const) {
      const stile = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.125, height - 0.08, width * 0.075), doorMaterial);
      stile.position.set(x + side * 0.03, 1.75, edge * width * 0.205);
      leaf.add(stile);
    }
    const crystal = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.082, height * 0.8, width * 0.37), glassMaterial);
    crystal.position.set(x + side * 0.018, 1.9, 0);
    leaf.add(crystal);
    const frameTop = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.13, 0.16, width * 0.48), doorMaterial);
    frameTop.position.set(x + side * 0.032, 2.64, 0);
    leaf.add(frameTop);
    const frameBottom = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.13, 0.18, width * 0.48), doorMaterial);
    frameBottom.position.set(x + side * 0.032, 0.88, 0);
    leaf.add(frameBottom);
    car.add(leaf);
    doors.push({ group: leaf, closedZ, direction });
  }
}

export function addOpaqueTransitInterior(
  car: THREE.Group,
  length: number,
  halfWidth: number,
  material: THREE.Material,
  doorZones: (side: -1 | 1) => number[]
): void {
  for (const side of [-1, 1] as const) {
    const exclusions = doorZones(side).map((center) => ({ start: center - 0.92, end: center + 0.92 })).sort((a, b) => a.start - b.start);
    let cursor = -length / 2;
    for (const exclusion of [...exclusions, { start: length / 2, end: length / 2 }]) {
      const end = Math.min(length / 2, exclusion.start);
      if (end - cursor > 0.18) {
        const lowerWall = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.1, 0.68, end - cursor), material);
        lowerWall.position.set(side * halfWidth, 1.42, (cursor + end) / 2);
        car.add(lowerWall);
      }
      cursor = Math.max(cursor, exclusion.end);
    }
    const header = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.11, 0.38, length), material);
    header.position.set(side * (halfWidth - 0.03), 3.02, 0);
    car.add(header);
    for (let z = -length / 2 + 1.2; z < length / 2 - 0.6; z += 2.05) {
      if (exclusions.some((zone) => z >= zone.start && z <= zone.end)) continue;
      const mullion = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.12, 1.0, 0.1), material);
      mullion.position.set(side * halfWidth, 2.38, z);
      car.add(mullion);
    }
  }
}

--- vehicles/VehicleVisualFactory.ts ---
import type { CityDefinition } from '../cities';
import type { TransportModeDefinition } from '../domain/transport';
import type { DemoRoute } from '../route';
import { buildTransitVehicle } from './buildTransitVehicle';
import type { VehicleDefinition } from './VehicleDefinition';
import { VehicleInteriorDisplay } from './VehicleInteriorDisplay';
import { VehicleVisualController } from './VehicleVisualController';

// Legacy route/city/mode still forwarded to the builders during migration (e.g. a
// funicular reads route.controlPoints for its uphill orientation). Live callers
// pass none of these.
export type VehicleBuildCompat = {
  route?: DemoRoute;
  city?: CityDefinition;
  modeDefinition?: TransportModeDefinition;
};

// Builds the complete presentation for a vehicle from its definition: the mesh
// instance, its shared interior display, and the VehicleVisualController that owns
// them. The simulator and the live follow both go through this one factory so a
// given definition always yields the same interactive model.
export class VehicleVisualFactory {
  create(definition: VehicleDefinition, compat: VehicleBuildCompat = {}): VehicleVisualController {
    const display = new VehicleInteriorDisplay();
    const instance = buildTransitVehicle({
      definition,
      route: compat.route,
      city: compat.city,
      modeDefinition: compat.modeDefinition,
      makeInteriorDisplayMaterial: () => display.material
    });
    const controller = new VehicleVisualController(instance, display, {
      line: definition.line,
      destination: definition.destination,
      lineAccentColor: definition.lineAccentColor,
      interiorCameraProfile: definition.interiorCameraProfile
    });
    controller.updateInteriorDisplay(definition.destination, false); // initial board, not blank
    return controller;
  }
}

export const vehicleVisualFactory = new VehicleVisualFactory();

--- vehicles/VehicleVisualController.ts ---
import * as THREE from 'three';
import type { InteriorCameraProfile } from '../domain/transport';
import type { CameraMode } from '../world/CameraController';
import type { VehicleInstance } from './types';
import type { VehicleInteriorDisplay } from './VehicleInteriorDisplay';

// Everything that "presents" a built vehicle, independent of how it is placed:
// animated doors, the next-stop interior display, per-view window transparency and
// body/cab shell visibility, and exterior lamps. Shared by the simulator's active
// vehicle and the live follow vehicle so both behave identically; only placement
// (route distance vs live along-track) differs and lives outside this class.
//
// It owns presentation + shared resource disposal. It does NOT own route
// positioning, projection, terrain, cameras, live traffic, or schedule state.

/** Per-frame presentation inputs. */
export type VehiclePresentationState = {
  doorsOpen: boolean;
  nextStop: string;
  cameraMode: CameraMode;
  cinematicActive: boolean;
  daylightFactor: number;
  deltaSeconds: number;
};

// The presentation metadata the controller needs. In a later step this is replaced
// by the shared VehicleDefinition; kept minimal here so PR ordering stays clean.
export type VehiclePresentationConfig = {
  line: string;
  destination: string;
  lineAccentColor: string;
  interiorCameraProfile: InteriorCameraProfile;
};

export class VehicleVisualController {
  readonly root: THREE.Group;
  readonly cars: THREE.Group[];
  readonly trainHalfLengthM: number;
  readonly cameraProfile: InteriorCameraProfile;

  private doorOpenAmount = 0;

  constructor(
    private readonly instance: VehicleInstance,
    private readonly display: VehicleInteriorDisplay | null,
    private readonly config: VehiclePresentationConfig
  ) {
    this.root = instance.root;
    this.cars = instance.cars;
    this.trainHalfLengthM = instance.trainHalfLengthM;
    this.cameraProfile = config.interiorCameraProfile;
  }

  /** Applies every presentation aspect for the frame. */
  update(state: VehiclePresentationState): void {
    this.updateDoors(state.doorsOpen, state.deltaSeconds);
    this.updateInteriorDisplay(state.nextStop, state.doorsOpen);
    this.setPresentation(state.cameraMode, state.cinematicActive);
    this.updateExteriorLights(state.daylightFactor);
  }

  updateDoors(open: boolean, deltaSeconds: number): void {
    this.doorOpenAmount = THREE.MathUtils.damp(
      this.doorOpenAmount,
      open ? 1 : 0,
      open ? 5.2 : 6.5,
      THREE.MathUtils.clamp(deltaSeconds, 0, 0.1)
    );
    const eased = this.doorOpenAmount * this.doorOpenAmount * (3 - 2 * this.doorOpenAmount);
    for (const leaf of this.instance.doors) {
      leaf.group.position.z = leaf.closedZ + leaf.direction * 0.72 * eased;
    }
  }

  updateInteriorDisplay(nextStop: string, doorsOpen: boolean): void {
    this.display?.update({
      line: this.config.line,
      destination: this.config.destination,
      nextStop,
      doorsOpen,
      accentColor: this.config.lineAccentColor
    });
  }

  // In passenger/cab views the opaque body shells hide so the outside shows through
  // the glass; in cab view the cab shell/display hide so the line ahead is visible.
  setPresentation(mode: CameraMode, cinematicActive: boolean): void {
    const interiorView = !cinematicActive && (mode === 'passenger' || mode === 'cab');
    const cabView = mode === 'cab';
    const glass = this.instance.glassMaterial;
    if (glass) {
      glass.opacity = interiorView ? 0.1 : 0.58;
      glass.depthWrite = !interiorView;
      glass.color.setHex(interiorView ? 0xd9f2f8 : 0x91c7dd);
      glass.needsUpdate = true;
    }
    for (const band of this.instance.windowBands) band.visible = !interiorView;
    for (const shell of this.instance.cabShells) shell.visible = !cabView;
    for (const shell of this.instance.passengerShells) shell.visible = !interiorView;
    for (const display of this.instance.cabDisplays) display.visible = !cabView;
  }

  /** Head/tail lamps come on in low light (night, dawn, dusk). */
  updateExteriorLights(daylightFactor: number): void {
    this.instance.setExteriorLightsOn?.(daylightFactor < 0.45);
  }

  dispose(): void {
    this.instance.dispose?.();
    this.display?.dispose();
  }
}

--- vehicles/types.ts ---
import * as THREE from 'three';
import type { CityDefinition } from '../cities';
import type { TransportModeDefinition } from '../domain/transport';
import type { DemoRoute } from '../route';
import type { VehicleDefinition } from './VehicleDefinition';

export type VehicleDoorLeaf = {
  group: THREE.Group;
  closedZ: number;
  direction: -1 | 1;
};

export type VehicleTexturePaths = {
  exterior?: string[];
  interior?: string[];
};

export type VehicleVisualConfig = {
  vehicleFamily: string;
  destinationText: string;
  lineAccentColor: string;
  texturePaths?: VehicleTexturePaths;
  interiorCameraAnchors?: {
    passenger?: [number, number, number];
    cab?: [number, number, number];
  };
};

export type VehicleInstance = {
  root: THREE.Group;
  cars: THREE.Group[];
  doors: VehicleDoorLeaf[];
  windowBands: THREE.Mesh[];
  cabShells: THREE.Mesh[];
  passengerShells: THREE.Mesh[];
  cabDisplays: THREE.Mesh[];
  glassMaterial?: THREE.MeshStandardMaterial;
  trainHalfLengthM: number;
  /** Brightens head/tail lamps at night and dawn; dims them in daylight. */
  setExteriorLightsOn?: (on: boolean) => void;
  updateInteriorDisplay?: (nextStation: string, doorsOpen: boolean) => void;
  dispose?: () => void;
};

// What callers pass to buildTransitVehicle. The definition is the intended input;
// route/city/modeDefinition are temporary compatibility fields still accepted from
// existing call sites. buildTransitVehicle normalizes either form into a
// ResolvedVehicleBuildContext before the registry and builders run.
export type VehicleBuildContext = {
  definition?: VehicleDefinition;

  // Temporary compatibility fields.
  route?: DemoRoute;
  city?: CityDefinition;
  modeDefinition?: TransportModeDefinition;

  makeInteriorDisplayMaterial: () => THREE.MeshBasicMaterial;
};

// The normalized context the registry and builders actually consume: a definition
// is always present, and route/modeDefinition are always populated (synthesized
// from the definition when a caller only supplied one).
export type ResolvedVehicleBuildContext = {
  definition: VehicleDefinition;
  route: DemoRoute;
  city?: CityDefinition;
  modeDefinition: TransportModeDefinition;
  makeInteriorDisplayMaterial: () => THREE.MeshBasicMaterial;
};

--- vehicles/buildTransitVehicle.ts ---
import { buildBusVehicle } from './blueprint/bus/buildBusFromBlueprint';
import { defaultBusBlueprint } from './blueprint/bus/defaultBusBlueprint';
import { buildSBahnVehicle } from './blueprint/sbahn/buildSBahnFromBlueprint';
import { defaultSBahnBlueprint } from './blueprint/sbahn/defaultSBahnBlueprint';
import { buildProceduralTransitVehicle, createStadtbahnVehicle } from './procedural';
import { VehicleRegistry } from './registry';
import type { ResolvedVehicleBuildContext, VehicleBuildContext, VehicleInstance } from './types';
import { resolveRouteVehicleDefinition, type VehicleDefinition } from './VehicleDefinition';
import { fallbackTransportModeDefinition, type TransportModeDefinition } from '../domain/transport';
import type { DemoRoute } from '../route';
import genericModernTram from './configs/generic-modern-tram.json';
import genericBus from './configs/generic-bus.json';
import stuttgartDt8Tango from './configs/stuttgart-dt8-tango.json';

// Default app-wide vehicle registry. Built-in kind builders delegate to the
// existing procedural factory (unchanged behavior); blueprint configs are
// registered by id so routes/modes can opt in via vehicleDesignId without
// touching the procedural code path.
export const vehicleRegistry = new VehicleRegistry();

for (const kind of ['stadtbahn', 'bus', 's-bahn', 'zacke', 'seilbahn', 'ferry', 'schwebebahn'] as const) {
  vehicleRegistry.registerKindBuilder(kind, buildProceduralTransitVehicle);
}
vehicleRegistry.setFinalFallback(createStadtbahnVehicle);

// The bus kind renders from a declarative bus blueprint (see blueprint/bus/).
// Registering the default config by id also lets routes/cities opt into it
// explicitly via vehicleDesignId, and gives custom bus configs a template.
vehicleRegistry.registerBuilder(defaultBusBlueprint.id, (context) => buildBusVehicle(defaultBusBlueprint, context));
vehicleRegistry.registerBuilder(defaultSBahnBlueprint.id, (context) => buildSBahnVehicle(defaultSBahnBlueprint, context));

for (const rawConfig of [genericModernTram, genericBus, stuttgartDt8Tango]) {
  const result = vehicleRegistry.registerBlueprintFromJson(rawConfig);
  if (!result.ok) {
    // A broken built-in config should never crash the app at import time; surface it loudly instead.
    console.error(`Built-in vehicle blueprint "${(rawConfig as { id?: string }).id ?? 'unknown'}" failed validation:`, result.errors);
  }
}

// Minimal route/mode synthesized from a definition so the registry and existing
// builders (which still read route/modeDefinition) keep working when a caller
// supplies only a definition — e.g. the live follow vehicle, which no longer
// invents a fake DemoRoute of its own.
function synthesizeRoute(def: VehicleDefinition): DemoRoute {
  return {
    line: def.line,
    destination: def.destination,
    controlPoints: [],
    stations: [],
    vehicleDesignId: def.vehicleDesignId
  } as unknown as DemoRoute;
}

function synthesizeMode(def: VehicleDefinition): TransportModeDefinition {
  return {
    ...fallbackTransportModeDefinition,
    id: def.modeId,
    vehicleFamily: '',
    vehicleKind: def.vehicleKind,
    interiorCameraProfile: def.interiorCameraProfile,
    lineAccentColor: def.lineAccentColor,
    vehicleDesignId: def.vehicleDesignId
  };
}

// Normalizes either input form (new `definition`, or legacy route/city/mode) into
// one ResolvedVehicleBuildContext with a definition and populated route/mode.
export function resolveBuildContext(context: VehicleBuildContext): ResolvedVehicleBuildContext {
  const modeDefinition =
    context.modeDefinition ?? (context.definition ? synthesizeMode(context.definition) : { ...fallbackTransportModeDefinition });
  if (!context.route && !context.definition) {
    throw new Error('buildTransitVehicle requires a definition or a route.');
  }
  const route = context.route ?? synthesizeRoute(context.definition!);
  const definition = context.definition ?? resolveRouteVehicleDefinition(route, context.city, modeDefinition);
  return { definition, route, city: context.city, modeDefinition, makeInteriorDisplayMaterial: context.makeInteriorDisplayMaterial };
}

export function buildTransitVehicle(context: VehicleBuildContext): VehicleInstance {
  return vehicleRegistry.build(resolveBuildContext(context));
}

--- vehicles/registry.ts ---
import type { VehicleKind } from '../domain/transport';
import type { VehicleBlueprintConfig } from './blueprint/VehicleBlueprintConfig';
import { buildVehicleFromBlueprint } from './blueprint/buildVehicleFromBlueprint';
import { validateVehicleBlueprint } from './blueprint/validateVehicleBlueprint';
import type { ResolvedVehicleBuildContext, VehicleInstance } from './types';

export type VehicleBuilder = (context: ResolvedVehicleBuildContext) => VehicleInstance;

export type VehicleBlueprintRegistrationResult =
  | { ok: true; id: string; warnings: string[] }
  | { ok: false; errors: string[] };

// Resolution order (highest priority first):
//   1. Explicit vehicleDesignId from route/city/mode config.
//   2. modeDefinition.vehicleFamily, matched against a registered id.
//   3. modeDefinition.vehicleKind, matched against a built-in kind builder.
//   4. A registered generic blueprint fallback for that kind.
//   5. The final fallback builder (defaults to the Stuttgart Stadtbahn builder).
export class VehicleRegistry {
  private readonly buildersById = new Map<string, VehicleBuilder>();
  private readonly buildersByKind = new Map<VehicleKind, VehicleBuilder>();
  private readonly genericBuildersByKind = new Map<string, VehicleBuilder>();
  private finalFallbackBuilder: VehicleBuilder | null = null;

  registerBuilder(id: string, builder: VehicleBuilder): void {
    this.buildersById.set(id, builder);
  }

  registerKindBuilder(kind: VehicleKind, builder: VehicleBuilder): void {
    this.buildersByKind.set(kind, builder);
  }

  registerGenericKindBuilder(kind: string, builder: VehicleBuilder): void {
    this.genericBuildersByKind.set(kind, builder);
  }

  setFinalFallback(builder: VehicleBuilder): void {
    this.finalFallbackBuilder = builder;
  }

  registerBlueprint(config: VehicleBlueprintConfig): void {
    this.registerBuilder(config.id, (context) => buildVehicleFromBlueprint(config, context));
  }

  // Parses and validates an untrusted (e.g. user-uploaded) config before registering it.
  // Never throws: a malformed config is reported back as errors instead of crashing the app.
  registerBlueprintFromJson(raw: unknown): VehicleBlueprintRegistrationResult {
    try {
      const result = validateVehicleBlueprint(raw);
      if (!result.valid) return { ok: false, errors: result.errors };
      this.registerBlueprint(result.config);
      return { ok: true, id: result.config.id, warnings: result.warnings };
    } catch (error) {
      return { ok: false, errors: [`Unexpected error while loading vehicle blueprint: ${String(error)}`] };
    }
  }

  resolve(context: ResolvedVehicleBuildContext): VehicleBuilder {
    const explicitId = context.route.vehicleDesignId ?? context.city?.vehicleDesignId ?? context.modeDefinition?.vehicleDesignId;
    if (explicitId) {
      const builder = this.buildersById.get(explicitId);
      if (builder) return builder;
    }

    const family = context.modeDefinition?.vehicleFamily;
    if (family) {
      const builder = this.buildersById.get(family);
      if (builder) return builder;
    }

    const kind = context.modeDefinition?.vehicleKind ?? 'stadtbahn';
    const kindBuilder = this.buildersByKind.get(kind);
    if (kindBuilder) return kindBuilder;

    const genericBuilder = this.genericBuildersByKind.get(kind);
    if (genericBuilder) return genericBuilder;

    if (this.finalFallbackBuilder) return this.finalFallbackBuilder;
    throw new Error('VehicleRegistry has no final fallback builder registered.');
  }

  build(context: ResolvedVehicleBuildContext): VehicleInstance {
    return this.resolve(context)(context);
  }
}

--- vehicles/VehicleInteriorDisplay.ts ---
import * as THREE from 'three';

// The next-stop interior board painted onto a canvas texture (line badge, "NÄCHSTER
// HALT" / "TÜREN GEÖFFNET · DANACH", the station, and "Richtung <destination>").
// Shared so the simulator's active vehicle and the live follow vehicle render an
// identical display instead of each keeping its own copy (a recurring parity bug).
export class VehicleInteriorDisplay {
  readonly material: THREE.MeshBasicMaterial;
  private readonly canvas: HTMLCanvasElement;
  private readonly texture: THREE.CanvasTexture;
  private lastKey = '';

  constructor() {
    const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
    canvas.width = 1024;
    canvas.height = 256;
    this.canvas = canvas;
    const texture = new THREE.CanvasTexture(canvas);
    texture.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
    texture.minFilter = THREE.LinearFilter;
    texture.magFilter = THREE.LinearFilter;
    this.texture = texture;
    this.material = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map: texture, side: THREE.DoubleSide, toneMapped: false });
  }

  /** Repaints only when the visible content changes. */
  update(input: { line: string; destination: string; nextStop: string; doorsOpen: boolean; accentColor: string }): void {
    const station = input.nextStop === 'End of line' ? 'Endstation' : input.nextStop || input.destination;
