  engineHatch.position.set(0, 1.2, rearZ - 0.02);
  car.add(engineHatch);
  const rearWindow = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(1.9, 0.7, 0.06), m.glass);
  rearWindow.position.set(0, 2.5, rearZ - 0.04);
  car.add(rearWindow);
  const bumper = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(2.5, 0.46, 0.22), m.skirt);
  bumper.position.set(0, 0.72, rearZ - 0.02);
  car.add(bumper);
  for (const side of [-1, 1] as const) {
    const tail = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.24, 0.5, 0.07), m.tailLamp);
    tail.position.set(side * 1.0, 1.02, rearZ - 0.06);
    car.add(tail);
  }
}

// Flat connection ring at an articulation joint; the bellows spans the gap.
function addBusGangwayRing(car: THREE.Group, m: BusMaterials, jointZ: number, sign: 1 | -1): void {
  const ring = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(2.4, 2.5, 0.1), m.rubber);
  ring.position.set(0, 1.86, jointZ + sign * 0.02);
  car.add(ring);
  const aperture = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(1.4, 2.0, 0.14), m.black);
  aperture.position.set(0, 1.7, jointZ + sign * 0.03);
  car.add(aperture);
}

// Rubber accordion bellows bridging the two articulated sections at the centre
// turntable (z=0). Added to the root so it stays over the bend point.
function addBusBellows(state: BusBuildState, m: BusMaterials): void {
  const bellows = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(2.42, 2.54, 0.72), m.rubber);
  bellows.position.set(0, 1.86, 0);
  bellows.castShadow = true;
  state.root.add(bellows);
  state.passengerShells.push(bellows);
  // Accordion ribs for the folded-rubber look.
  for (const z of [-0.24, 0, 0.24]) {
    const rib = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(2.5, 2.62, 0.06), m.black);
    rib.position.set(0, 1.86, z);
    state.root.add(rib);
  }
  // Low turntable plate at floor level between the sections.
  const plate = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(1.1, 1.1, 0.1, 24), m.metal);
  plate.position.set(0, 0.62, 0);
  state.root.add(plate);
}

--- vehicles/blueprint/roof.ts ---
import * as THREE from 'three';
import type { VehiclePantographConfig, VehicleRoofEquipmentConfig } from './VehicleBlueprintConfig';
import { resolveMaterial } from './materials';

export function buildBlueprintPantograph(
  root: THREE.Group,
  config: VehiclePantographConfig,
  materials: Record<string, THREE.MeshStandardMaterial>
): void {
  const material = resolveMaterial(materials, config.material, 'metal');
  const position = new THREE.Vector3(config.x, config.y, config.z);
  const base = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(1.2, 0.12, 0.6), material);
  base.position.copy(position);
  root.add(base);

  const arms: [THREE.Vector3, THREE.Vector3][] = [
    [new THREE.Vector3(-0.45, 0, -0.16), new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.65, 0)],
    [new THREE.Vector3(0.45, 0, -0.16), new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.65, 0)],
    [new THREE.Vector3(-0.45, 0, 0.16), new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.65, 0)],
    [new THREE.Vector3(0.45, 0, 0.16), new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.65, 0)]
  ];
  for (const [a, b] of arms) {
    const curve = new THREE.LineCurve3(a.clone().add(position), b.clone().add(position));
    root.add(new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.TubeGeometry(curve, 1, 0.02, 6), material));
  }
  const shoe = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(1.55, 0.045, 0.14), material);
  shoe.position.copy(position).add(new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.7, 0));
  root.add(shoe);
}

export function buildBlueprintRoofEquipment(
  root: THREE.Group,
  config: VehicleRoofEquipmentConfig,
  materials: Record<string, THREE.MeshStandardMaterial>
): void {
  const material = resolveMaterial(materials, config.material, 'roof');
  const box = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(config.widthM, config.heightM, config.lengthM), material);
  box.position.set(config.x, config.y, config.z);
  root.add(box);
  if (config.type === 'hvac') {
    const slatCount = Math.max(1, Math.round(config.lengthM / 0.28));
    for (let index = 0; index < slatCount; index++) {
      const slat = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(config.widthM * 0.82, 0.02, 0.06), material);
      slat.position.set(config.x, config.y + config.heightM / 2 + 0.02, config.z - config.lengthM / 2 + index * 0.28 + 0.14);
      root.add(slat);
    }
  }
}

--- vehicles/blueprint/buildVehicleFromBlueprint.ts ---
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { makeLabelTexture } from '../../labels';
import type { ResolvedVehicleBuildContext, VehicleInstance } from '../types';
import type { VehicleBlueprintConfig, VehicleDoorConfig, VehicleSectionConfig } from './VehicleBlueprintConfig';
import { buildBlueprintMaterials, resolveMaterial } from './materials';
import { buildBlueprintSection } from './sections';
import { buildBlueprintDoors } from './doors';
import { buildBlueprintPantograph, buildBlueprintRoofEquipment } from './roof';
import { buildBlueprintInterior } from './interior';

// Builds a runtime VehicleInstance from a declarative VehicleBlueprintConfig.
// This is the generic counterpart to the hand-written procedural builders in
// ../procedural.ts: instead of a bespoke method per vehicle, any JSON config
// matching VehicleBlueprintConfig produces a working vehicle.
export function buildVehicleFromBlueprint(config: VehicleBlueprintConfig, context: ResolvedVehicleBuildContext): VehicleInstance {
  const root = new THREE.Group();
  root.name = `Vehicle blueprint ${config.id}`;
  const materials = buildBlueprintMaterials(config.materials);

  const cars: THREE.Group[] = [];
  const doors: VehicleInstance['doors'] = [];
  const windowBands: THREE.Mesh[] = [];
  const cabShells: THREE.Mesh[] = [];
  const passengerShells: THREE.Mesh[] = [];
  const cabDisplays: THREE.Mesh[] = [];

  const doubleFormation = Boolean(
    config.formation?.doubleFormationLines?.includes(context.route.line) && config.formation?.duplicateSetOffsetM
  );
  const offsets = doubleFormation ? [-(config.formation!.duplicateSetOffsetM ?? 0) / 2, (config.formation!.duplicateSetOffsetM ?? 0) / 2] : [0];

  for (const offset of offsets) {
    buildFormation(offset);
  }

  function buildFormation(centerOffsetM: number): void {
    config.sections.forEach((section, sectionIndex) => {
      const shiftedSection: VehicleSectionConfig = { ...section, center: section.center + centerOffsetM };
      const { group, passengerShells: shellMeshes, cabShells: cabMeshes, windowBands: bandMeshes } = buildBlueprintSection(
        shiftedSection,
        sectionIndex,
        config,
        materials,
        config.windows ?? []
      );
      passengerShells.push(...shellMeshes);
      cabShells.push(...cabMeshes);
      windowBands.push(...bandMeshes);

      const sectionDoors = (config.doors ?? []).filter((door) => door.section === sectionIndex);
      for (const door of sectionDoors) {
        doors.push(...buildBlueprintDoors(group, shiftedSection, door, materials));
      }

      buildBlueprintInterior(group, shiftedSection, sectionIndex, sectionDoors, materials, config.interior);

      if (section.cab) {
        const display = buildDestinationDisplayForCab(config, sectionIndex, section, materials, context);
        if (display) {
          group.add(display);
          cabDisplays.push(display);
        }
      }

      cars.push(group);
      root.add(group);
    });
  }

  for (const explicitDisplay of config.destinationDisplays ?? []) {
    const section = config.sections[explicitDisplay.section];
    if (!section) continue;
    const text = resolveDisplayText(explicitDisplay.text, context);
    const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(
      new THREE.PlaneGeometry(explicitDisplay.widthM ?? 1.5, explicitDisplay.heightM ?? 0.32),
      new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({
        map: makeLabelTexture(text, { width: 700, height: 128, bg: explicitDisplay.background ?? '#101216', fg: explicitDisplay.foreground ?? '#ffb300' }),
        side: THREE.DoubleSide
      })
    );
    mesh.position.set(...explicitDisplay.position);
    if (explicitDisplay.rotationY) mesh.rotation.y = explicitDisplay.rotationY;
    cars[explicitDisplay.section]?.add(mesh);
    cabDisplays.push(mesh);
  }

  for (const gangway of config.gangways ?? []) {
    const material = resolveMaterial(materials, gangway.material, 'dark');
    const bellows = new THREE.Mesh(
      new THREE.BoxGeometry(gangway.widthM ?? config.dimensions.widthM * 0.9, gangway.heightM ?? config.dimensions.heightM * 0.7, gangway.lengthM ?? 0.7),
      material
    );
    bellows.position.set(0, (gangway.heightM ?? config.dimensions.heightM * 0.7) / 2, gangway.z);
    root.add(bellows);
    passengerShells.push(bellows);
  }

  for (const bogie of config.bogies ?? []) {
    const material = resolveMaterial(materials, bogie.material, 'dark');
    const bogieMesh = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(bogie.widthM ?? 2.0, 0.36, bogie.lengthM ?? 1.5), material);
    bogieMesh.position.set(0, 0.42, bogie.z);
    root.add(bogieMesh);
  }

  for (const pantograph of config.pantographs ?? []) {
    buildBlueprintPantograph(root, pantograph, materials);
  }

  for (const equipment of config.roofEquipment ?? []) {
    buildBlueprintRoofEquipment(root, equipment, materials);
  }

  const trainHalfLengthM = config.sections.reduce((max, section) => {
    const extents = offsets.map((offset) => Math.abs(section.center + offset) + section.length / 2);
    return Math.max(max, ...extents);
  }, 0);

  return {
    root,
    cars,
    doors,
    windowBands,
    cabShells,
    passengerShells,
    cabDisplays,
    glassMaterial: materials.glass,
    trainHalfLengthM
  };
}

function resolveDisplayText(text: string | undefined, context: ResolvedVehicleBuildContext): string {
  if (text === 'route-line') return context.route.line;
  if (!text || text === 'route-destination') return `${context.route.line} ${context.route.destination}`;
  return text;
}

function buildDestinationDisplayForCab(
  config: VehicleBlueprintConfig,
  sectionIndex: number,
  section: VehicleSectionConfig,
  materials: Record<string, THREE.MeshStandardMaterial>,
  context: ResolvedVehicleBuildContext
): THREE.Mesh | null {
  // Skip if an explicit destinationDisplays entry already targets this section.
  if (config.destinationDisplays?.some((display) => display.section === sectionIndex)) return null;
  const cabDirection = section.cab === 'front' ? -1 : 1;
  const text = `${context.route.line} ${context.route.destination}`;
  const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(
    new THREE.PlaneGeometry(1.5, 0.32),
    new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({
      map: makeLabelTexture(text, { width: 700, height: 128, bg: '#101216', fg: '#ffb300' }),
      side: THREE.DoubleSide
    })
  );
  const heightM = section.heightM ?? config.dimensions.heightM;
  mesh.position.set(0, heightM * 0.86, cabDirection * (section.length / 2 + 0.06));
  if (cabDirection < 0) mesh.rotation.y = Math.PI;
  return mesh;
}

--- vehicles/blueprint/materials.ts ---
import * as THREE from 'three';
import type { VehicleMaterialConfig } from './VehicleBlueprintConfig';

export function buildBlueprintMaterials(
  materialConfigs: Record<string, VehicleMaterialConfig>
): Record<string, THREE.MeshStandardMaterial> {
  const materials: Record<string, THREE.MeshStandardMaterial> = {};
  for (const [name, materialConfig] of Object.entries(materialConfigs)) {
    materials[name] = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
      color: materialConfig.color,
      roughness: materialConfig.roughness ?? 0.5,
      metalness: materialConfig.metalness ?? 0.1,
      opacity: materialConfig.opacity ?? 1,
      transparent: materialConfig.transparent ?? false,
      emissive: materialConfig.emissive,
      emissiveIntensity: materialConfig.emissiveIntensity ?? 1,
      side: materialConfig.transparent ? THREE.DoubleSide : THREE.FrontSide
    });
  }
  return materials;
}

// Resolves a named material reference, falling back to a neutral grey so a bad
// or missing reference never crashes the builder.
export function resolveMaterial(
  materials: Record<string, THREE.MeshStandardMaterial>,
  name: string | undefined,
  fallback: string
): THREE.MeshStandardMaterial {
  if (name && materials[name]) return materials[name];
  if (materials[fallback]) return materials[fallback];
  return new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x888888, roughness: 0.6 });
}

--- vehicles/blueprint/interior.ts ---
import * as THREE from 'three';
import type { VehicleDoorConfig, VehicleInteriorConfig, VehicleSectionConfig } from './VehicleBlueprintConfig';
import { resolveMaterial } from './materials';

// Builds a simple opaque interior shell (floor, ceiling, wall segments that
// avoid door openings) plus optional auto-placed bench seats and grab poles.
export function buildBlueprintInterior(
  group: THREE.Group,
  section: VehicleSectionConfig,
  sectionIndex: number,
  sectionDoors: VehicleDoorConfig[],
  materials: Record<string, THREE.MeshStandardMaterial>,
  interior: VehicleInteriorConfig | undefined
): void {
  if (!interior?.enabled) return;

  const widthM = section.widthM ?? 2.4;
  const heightM = section.heightM ?? 3.2;
  const floorHeightM = heightM * 0.28;
  const bodyHeight = heightM - floorHeightM * 0.35;
  const bodyCenterY = floorHeightM * 0.35 + bodyHeight / 2;
  const floorMaterial = resolveMaterial(materials, interior.floorMaterial, 'floor');
  const wallMaterial = resolveMaterial(materials, interior.wallMaterial, 'interior');
  const poleMaterial = resolveMaterial(materials, interior.poleMaterial, 'metal');
  const seatMaterial = resolveMaterial(materials, interior.seatMaterial, 'seat');

  const floor = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(widthM * 0.86, 0.06, section.length - 0.14), floorMaterial);
  floor.position.y = floorHeightM * 0.35 + 0.03;
  group.add(floor);
  const ceiling = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(widthM * 0.82, 0.06, section.length - 0.2), wallMaterial);
  ceiling.position.y = bodyCenterY + bodyHeight / 2 - 0.05;
  group.add(ceiling);

  if (interior.autoPassengerShell !== false) {
    const doorZones = sectionDoors.map((door) => ({ start: door.z - (door.widthM ?? 1.3) * 0.6, end: door.z + (door.widthM ?? 1.3) * 0.6 })).sort((a, b) => a.start - b.start);
    for (const side of [-1, 1] as const) {
      let cursor = -section.length / 2;
      for (const zone of [...doorZones, { start: section.length / 2, end: section.length / 2 }]) {
        const end = Math.min(section.length / 2, zone.start);
        if (end - cursor > 0.2) {
          const wall = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.08, bodyHeight * 0.42, end - cursor), wallMaterial);
          wall.position.set(side * widthM * 0.46, bodyCenterY - bodyHeight * 0.05, (cursor + end) / 2);
          group.add(wall);
        }
        cursor = Math.max(cursor, zone.end);
      }
    }
  }

  if (interior.autoSeats !== false) {
    const usableLength = section.length - 1.6;
    if (usableLength > 1) {
      const rowCount = Math.max(1, Math.round(usableLength / 1.1));
      const step = usableLength / rowCount;
      const start = -usableLength / 2 + step / 2;
      for (let row = 0; row < rowCount; row++) {
        const z = start + row * step;
        for (const x of [-widthM * 0.24, widthM * 0.24]) {
          const cushion = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.4, 0.12, 0.5), seatMaterial);
          cushion.position.set(x, floorHeightM * 0.35 + 0.36, z);
          group.add(cushion);
          const back = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.4, 0.62, 0.1), seatMaterial);
          back.position.set(x, floorHeightM * 0.35 + 0.68, z - 0.24);
          group.add(back);
        }
      }
    }
  }

  if (interior.autoPoles !== false) {
    const pole = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.022, 0.022, bodyHeight * 0.72, 8), poleMaterial);
    pole.position.set(0, bodyCenterY - bodyHeight * 0.1, 0);
    group.add(pole);
  }
}

--- vehicles/blueprint/sections.ts ---
import * as THREE from 'three';
import type { VehicleBlueprintConfig, VehicleSectionConfig, VehicleWindowConfig } from './VehicleBlueprintConfig';
import { resolveMaterial } from './materials';

export type SectionBuildResult = {
  group: THREE.Group;
  passengerShells: THREE.Mesh[];
  cabShells: THREE.Mesh[];
  windowBands: THREE.Mesh[];
};

// Builds one articulated-car section: skirt, body shell, roof cap, side window
// band with strip windows, and an optional chamfered cab face. All geometry is
// local to the section's own center (z=0 at the section midpoint), since the
// runtime overwrites each car's world position every frame using centerOffsetM.
export function buildBlueprintSection(
  section: VehicleSectionConfig,
  sectionIndex: number,
  config: VehicleBlueprintConfig,
  materials: Record<string, THREE.MeshStandardMaterial>,
  windows: VehicleWindowConfig[]
): SectionBuildResult {
  const group = new THREE.Group();
  group.name = `Articulated car ${config.displayName} section ${sectionIndex}`;
  group.userData.centerOffsetM = section.center;

  const passengerShells: THREE.Mesh[] = [];
  const cabShells: THREE.Mesh[] = [];
  const windowBands: THREE.Mesh[] = [];

  const widthM = section.widthM ?? config.dimensions.widthM;
  const heightM = section.heightM ?? config.dimensions.heightM;
  const floorHeightM = config.dimensions.floorHeightM ?? heightM * 0.28;
  const bodyMaterial = resolveMaterial(materials, section.bodyMaterial, 'body');
  const accentMaterial = resolveMaterial(materials, section.accentMaterial, 'accent');
  const glassMaterial = resolveMaterial(materials, section.glassMaterial, 'glass');
  const darkMaterial = resolveMaterial(materials, section.darkMaterial, 'dark');
  const roofMaterial = resolveMaterial(materials, section.roofMaterial, 'roof');

  const bodyHeight = heightM - floorHeightM * 0.35;
  const bodyCenterY = floorHeightM * 0.35 + bodyHeight / 2;

  const skirt = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(widthM * 0.98, floorHeightM * 0.5, section.length - 0.1), accentMaterial);
  skirt.position.y = floorHeightM * 0.25;
  group.add(skirt);
  passengerShells.push(skirt);

  const body = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(widthM * 0.94, bodyHeight, section.length), bodyMaterial);
  body.position.y = bodyCenterY;
  body.castShadow = true;
  group.add(body);
  passengerShells.push(body);

  const roof = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(widthM * 0.84, heightM * 0.06, section.length - 0.1), roofMaterial);
  roof.position.y = bodyCenterY + bodyHeight / 2 + heightM * 0.03;
  group.add(roof);
  passengerShells.push(roof);

  const windowCenterY = bodyCenterY + bodyHeight * 0.14;
  const windowHeight = bodyHeight * 0.42;
  const sectionWindows = windows.filter((window) => window.section === sectionIndex);
  for (const side of [-1, 1] as const) {
    const band = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.08, windowHeight + 0.14, section.length - 0.2), darkMaterial);
    band.position.set(side * widthM * 0.5, windowCenterY, 0);
    group.add(band);
    windowBands.push(band);

    const sideLabel = side < 0 ? 'left' : 'right';
    const sideWindows = sectionWindows.filter((window) => window.side === sideLabel || window.side === 'both');
    const placedWindows: { z: number; widthM?: number; heightM?: number; material?: string }[] =
      sideWindows.length > 0 ? sideWindows : autoPlaceWindows(section);
    for (const window of placedWindows) {
      const windowMaterial = window.material ? resolveMaterial(materials, window.material, 'glass') : glassMaterial;
      const pane = new THREE.Mesh(
        new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.085, window.heightM ?? windowHeight, window.widthM ?? section.length * 0.16),
        windowMaterial
      );
      pane.position.set(side * widthM * 0.505, windowCenterY, window.z);
      group.add(pane);
    }
  }

  if (section.cab === 'front' || section.cab === 'rear') {
    const cabDirection = section.cab === 'front' ? -1 : 1;
    const cabZ = cabDirection * (section.length / 2);
    const nose = makeCabNose(widthM, bodyHeight, bodyMaterial);
    nose.position.set(0, bodyCenterY, cabZ);
    if (cabDirection > 0) nose.rotation.y = Math.PI;
    group.add(nose);
    cabShells.push(nose);

    const face = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(widthM * 0.9, bodyHeight * 0.78, 0.1), darkMaterial);
    face.position.set(0, bodyCenterY + bodyHeight * 0.1, cabZ + cabDirection * 0.14);
    group.add(face);
    cabShells.push(face);

    const windscreen = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(widthM * 0.76, bodyHeight * 0.32, 0.07), glassMaterial);
    windscreen.position.set(0, bodyCenterY + bodyHeight * 0.24, cabZ + cabDirection * 0.19);
    group.add(windscreen);
  }

  return { group, passengerShells, cabShells, windowBands };
}

function autoPlaceWindows(section: VehicleSectionConfig): { z: number; widthM?: number; heightM?: number; material?: string }[] {
  const usableLength = section.length - 1.2;
  if (usableLength <= 0) return [];
  const count = Math.max(1, Math.round(usableLength / 1.6));
  const step = usableLength / count;
  const start = -usableLength / 2 + step / 2;
  return Array.from({ length: count }, (_, index) => ({
    z: start + index * step,
    widthM: step * 0.62
  }));
}

function makeCabNose(widthM: number, heightM: number, material: THREE.Material): THREE.Mesh {
  const halfWidth = widthM * 0.47;
  const halfHeight = heightM * 0.5;
  const shape = new THREE.Shape();
  shape.moveTo(-halfWidth, -halfHeight);
  shape.lineTo(halfWidth, -halfHeight);
  shape.lineTo(halfWidth * 0.88, halfHeight * 0.82);
  shape.lineTo(halfWidth * 0.52, halfHeight);
  shape.lineTo(-halfWidth * 0.52, halfHeight);
  shape.lineTo(-halfWidth * 0.88, halfHeight * 0.82);
  shape.lineTo(-halfWidth, -halfHeight);
  const geometry = new THREE.ExtrudeGeometry(shape, { depth: widthM * 0.4, bevelEnabled: true, bevelThickness: 0.06, bevelSize: 0.06, bevelSegments: 2 });
  geometry.center();
  geometry.rotateX(Math.PI / 2);
  return new THREE.Mesh(geometry, material);
}

--- vehicles/blueprint/validateVehicleBlueprint.ts ---
import type {
  VehicleBlueprintConfig,
  VehicleBlueprintKind,
  VehicleDestinationDisplayConfig,
  VehicleDoorConfig,
  VehicleWindowConfig
} from './VehicleBlueprintConfig';

const HEX_COLOR_PATTERN = /^#(?:[0-9a-fA-F]{3}|[0-9a-fA-F]{6})$/;
const VALID_KINDS: VehicleBlueprintKind[] = ['stadtbahn', 'bus', 's-bahn', 'zacke', 'seilbahn', 'ferry', 'tram', 'schwebebahn'];

// Extreme-value caps prevent malformed uploaded configs from producing broken/degenerate geometry.
const MAX_LENGTH_M = 120;
const MAX_WIDTH_M = 6;
const MAX_HEIGHT_M = 8;

export type VehicleBlueprintValidationResult =
  | { valid: true; config: VehicleBlueprintConfig; warnings: string[] }
  | { valid: false; errors: string[] };

export function validateVehicleBlueprint(raw: unknown): VehicleBlueprintValidationResult {
  const errors: string[] = [];
  const warnings: string[] = [];

  if (typeof raw !== 'object' || raw === null) {
    return { valid: false, errors: ['Vehicle blueprint must be a JSON object.'] };
  }
  const value = raw as Record<string, unknown>;

  if (typeof value.id !== 'string' || !value.id.trim()) errors.push('"id" is required and must be a non-empty string.');
  if (typeof value.displayName !== 'string' || !value.displayName.trim()) errors.push('"displayName" is required and must be a non-empty string.');
  if (typeof value.kind !== 'string' || !VALID_KINDS.includes(value.kind as VehicleBlueprintKind)) {
    errors.push(`"kind" is required and must be one of: ${VALID_KINDS.join(', ')}.`);
  }

  const dimensions = value.dimensions as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
  if (typeof dimensions !== 'object' || dimensions === null) {
    errors.push('"dimensions" is required and must be an object.');
  } else {
    for (const key of ['lengthM', 'widthM', 'heightM'] as const) {
      const dimensionValue = dimensions[key];
      if (typeof dimensionValue !== 'number' || !Number.isFinite(dimensionValue) || dimensionValue <= 0) {
        errors.push(`"dimensions.${key}" must be a positive number.`);
      }
    }
  }

  const materials = value.materials as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
  if (typeof materials !== 'object' || materials === null) {
    errors.push('"materials" is required and must be an object of named material configs.');
  } else {
    for (const [name, materialConfig] of Object.entries(materials)) {
      if (typeof materialConfig !== 'object' || materialConfig === null) {
        errors.push(`Material "${name}" must be an object.`);
        continue;
      }
      const color = (materialConfig as Record<string, unknown>).color;
      if (typeof color !== 'string' || !HEX_COLOR_PATTERN.test(color)) {
        errors.push(`Material "${name}".color must be a valid hex color string (e.g. "#f2f2f2").`);
      }
    }
  }
  const materialNames = new Set(typeof materials === 'object' && materials ? Object.keys(materials) : []);

  const sections = value.sections;
  if (!Array.isArray(sections) || sections.length === 0) {
    errors.push('"sections" is required and must be a non-empty array.');
  } else {
    sections.forEach((section, index) => {
      if (typeof section !== 'object' || section === null) {
        errors.push(`sections[${index}] must be an object.`);
        return;
      }
      const sectionValue = section as Record<string, unknown>;
      if (typeof sectionValue.center !== 'number' || !Number.isFinite(sectionValue.center)) {
        errors.push(`sections[${index}].center must be a finite number.`);
      }
      if (typeof sectionValue.length !== 'number' || !Number.isFinite(sectionValue.length) || sectionValue.length <= 0) {
        errors.push(`sections[${index}].length must be a positive number.`);
      }
      for (const materialKey of ['bodyMaterial', 'accentMaterial', 'glassMaterial', 'darkMaterial', 'roofMaterial'] as const) {
        const reference = sectionValue[materialKey];
        if (typeof reference === 'string' && !materialNames.has(reference)) {
          errors.push(`sections[${index}].${materialKey} references unknown material "${reference}".`);
        }
      }
    });
  }
  const sectionCount = Array.isArray(sections) ? sections.length : 0;

  validateSectionReferencingArray(value.doors as VehicleDoorConfig[] | undefined, 'doors', sectionCount, materialNames, errors);
  validateSectionReferencingArray(value.windows as VehicleWindowConfig[] | undefined, 'windows', sectionCount, materialNames, errors);
  validateSectionReferencingArray(value.destinationDisplays as VehicleDestinationDisplayConfig[] | undefined, 'destinationDisplays', sectionCount, materialNames, errors);

  if (errors.length > 0) return { valid: false, errors };

  const config = clampExtremeValues(raw as unknown as VehicleBlueprintConfig, warnings);
  return { valid: true, config, warnings };
}

function validateSectionReferencingArray(
  entries: Array<{ section?: number; material?: string; glassMaterial?: string; darkMaterial?: string }> | undefined,
  label: string,
  sectionCount: number,
  materialNames: Set<string>,
  errors: string[]
): void {
  if (entries === undefined) return;
  if (!Array.isArray(entries)) {
    errors.push(`"${label}" must be an array when present.`);
    return;
  }
  entries.forEach((entry, index) => {
    if (typeof entry !== 'object' || entry === null) {
      errors.push(`${label}[${index}] must be an object.`);
      return;
    }
    if (typeof entry.section !== 'number' || entry.section < 0 || entry.section >= sectionCount) {
      errors.push(`${label}[${index}].section (${entry.section}) does not reference an existing section index.`);
    }
    for (const materialKey of ['material', 'glassMaterial', 'darkMaterial'] as const) {
      const reference = entry[materialKey];
      if (typeof reference === 'string' && !materialNames.has(reference)) {
        errors.push(`${label}[${index}].${materialKey} references unknown material "${reference}".`);
      }
    }
  });
}

// Caps runaway numeric values from untrusted configs while leaving well-formed configs untouched.
function clampExtremeValues(config: VehicleBlueprintConfig, warnings: string[]): VehicleBlueprintConfig {
  const clamped: VehicleBlueprintConfig = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(config));
  const clamp = (label: string, value: number, max: number): number => {
    if (value > max) {
      warnings.push(`${label} (${value}) exceeded the maximum of ${max} and was clamped.`);
      return max;
    }
    return value;
  };
  clamped.dimensions.lengthM = clamp('dimensions.lengthM', clamped.dimensions.lengthM, MAX_LENGTH_M);
  clamped.dimensions.widthM = clamp('dimensions.widthM', clamped.dimensions.widthM, MAX_WIDTH_M);
  clamped.dimensions.heightM = clamp('dimensions.heightM', clamped.dimensions.heightM, MAX_HEIGHT_M);
  clamped.sections = clamped.sections.map((section, index) => ({
    ...section,
    length: clamp(`sections[${index}].length`, section.length, MAX_LENGTH_M)
  }));
  return clamped;
}

--- vehicles/blueprint/sbahn/defaultSBahnBlueprint.ts ---
import rawConfig from '../../configs/db-br430-sbahn.json';
import { parseSBahnBlueprint, type SBahnBlueprintConfig } from './SBahnBlueprintConfig';

// The built-in DB BR 430 config ships with the bundle, so validation can only
// fail on a bad code change. Surface that loudly in dev but keep the S-Bahn
// working by trusting the raw JSON rather than crashing at import time.
const parsed = parseSBahnBlueprint(rawConfig);
if (!parsed.ok) {
  console.error('Built-in S-Bahn blueprint "db-br430-sbahn" failed validation:', parsed.errors);
}

export const defaultSBahnBlueprint: SBahnBlueprintConfig = parsed.ok ? parsed.config : (rawConfig as unknown as SBahnBlueprintConfig);

--- vehicles/blueprint/sbahn/buildSBahnFromBlueprint.ts ---
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { makeLabelTexture } from '../../../labels';
import type { ResolvedVehicleBuildContext, VehicleInstance } from '../../types';
import {
  addCabinLight,
  addExteriorLightThrow,
  addOpaqueTransitInterior,
  addPantograph,
  addSlidingVehicleDoor,
  makeExteriorLightRig,
  makeSetExteriorLightsOn,
  type ExteriorLightRig
} from '../../parts';
import { makeStandardMaterial } from '../standardMaterial';
import type { SBahnBlueprintConfig, SBahnMaterialRole } from './SBahnBlueprintConfig';

// DB BR 430-family S-Bahn renderer, driven by an SBahnBlueprintConfig.
// The config decides livery, formation, layout, logo and display colors; the
// constants below are fixed BR 430 styling (body cross-section, nose profile,
// cab detailing, ...). Geometry is an unchanged extraction of the former
// procedural S-Bahn builder — keep it visually byte-identical unless the task
// is about changing S-Bahn visuals.

type SBahnMaterials = Record<SBahnMaterialRole, THREE.MeshStandardMaterial>;

export function buildSBahnVehicle(config: SBahnBlueprintConfig, context: ResolvedVehicleBuildContext): VehicleInstance {
  const root = new THREE.Group();
  const cars: THREE.Group[] = [];
  const doors: VehicleInstance['doors'] = [];
  const windowBands: THREE.Mesh[] = [];
  const cabShells: THREE.Mesh[] = [];
  const passengerShells: THREE.Mesh[] = [];
  const cabDisplays: THREE.Mesh[] = [];
  const rig: ExteriorLightRig = makeExteriorLightRig();

  const m = makeSBahnMaterials(config.materials);
  const displayMaterial = context.makeInteriorDisplayMaterial();
  const interior = makeSBahnInteriorMaterials(m);
  rig.headLampMaterials.push(m.headlight);
  rig.tailLampMaterials.push(m.tailLamp);

  const { carCenters } = config.formation;
  const { doorZs, windowZs, seatRowZs, seatXs, poleZs, bogieZs } = config.layout;

  carCenters.forEach((center, index) => {
    const car = new THREE.Group();
    car.name = `${config.formation.sectionNamePrefix} ${index + 1}`;
    car.userData.centerOffsetM = center;
    const body = new THREE.Mesh(makeSBahn430Body(16.15), m.shell);
    body.castShadow = true;
    car.add(body);
    passengerShells.push(body);

    // The BR 430 roof rolls inward above the window band; a low central HVAC
    // pod leaves the curved shoulders visible instead of reading as a box.
    const roofPod = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(1.68, 0.18, 4.8), m.metal);
    roofPod.position.set(0, 3.77, index % 2 === 0 ? 0.65 : -0.65);
    roofPod.castShadow = true;
    car.add(roofPod);
    passengerShells.push(roofPod);
    for (let slat = -7; slat <= 7; slat++) {
      const grille = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(1.46, 0.025, 0.055), m.dark);
      grille.position.set(0, 3.875, roofPod.position.z + slat * 0.28);
      car.add(grille);
    }
    const lowerRed = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(3.08, 0.28, 16.22), m.accent);
    lowerRed.position.y = 0.62;
    car.add(lowerRed);
    passengerShells.push(lowerRed);
    const belt = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(3.09, 0.18, 16.23), m.accent);
    belt.position.y = 1.72;
    car.add(belt);
    passengerShells.push(belt);
    for (const side of [-1, 1]) {
      const band = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.07, 1.08, 14.5), m.dark);
      band.position.set(side * 1.535, 2.43, 0);
      car.add(band);
      windowBands.push(band);
      for (const z of windowZs) {
        const window = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.08, 0.88, 1.08), m.glass);
        window.position.set(side * 1.55, 2.43, z);
        car.add(window);
      }
      for (const z of doorZs) {
        addSlidingVehicleDoor(doors, car, side * 1.55, z, m.accent, m.glass, m.dark, 1.72, 2.05);
      }
    }
    if (index === 0 || index === carCenters.length - 1) {
      // BR 430 front: flat, slightly raked red nose with a wide black
      // windscreen band, vertical LED light bars and a top destination display.
      const cabEnd = (index === 0 ? -1 : 1) as -1 | 1;
      const faceTilt = -cabEnd * 0.197;
      const nose = new THREE.Mesh(makeSBahn430Nose(), m.accent);
      nose.position.z = cabEnd * 8.0;
      if (cabEnd < 0) nose.rotation.y = Math.PI;
      nose.castShadow = true;
      car.add(nose);
      cabShells.push(nose);

      const mask = new THREE.Mesh(makeSBahn430Mask(), m.dark);
      mask.position.set(0, 2.58, cabEnd * 8.76);
      mask.rotation.x = faceTilt;
      car.add(mask);
      cabShells.push(mask);

      const windscreen = new THREE.Mesh(makeSBahn430Windscreen(), m.glass);
      windscreen.position.set(0, 2.5, cabEnd * 8.82);
      windscreen.rotation.x = faceTilt;
      car.add(windscreen);

      for (const side of [-1, 1] as const) {
        const housing = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.2, 0.78, 0.07), m.dark);
        housing.position.set(side * 1.12, 1.32, cabEnd * 8.88);
        car.add(housing);
        cabShells.push(housing);
        const headBar = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.12, 0.42, 0.055), m.headlight);
        headBar.position.set(side * 1.12, 1.18, cabEnd * 8.92);
        car.add(headBar);
        const tailDot = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.12, 0.16, 0.055), m.tailLamp);
        tailDot.position.set(side * 1.12, 1.56, cabEnd * 8.92);
        car.add(tailDot);
        cabShells.push(headBar, tailDot);

        const cabWindow = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.08, 0.85, 1.0), m.glass);
        cabWindow.position.set(side * 1.55, 2.43, cabEnd * 7.35);
        car.add(cabWindow);
      }

      const frontSkirt = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(2.5, 0.3, 0.16), m.dark);
      frontSkirt.position.set(0, 0.52, cabEnd * 8.72);
      car.add(frontSkirt);
      cabShells.push(frontSkirt);

      const logo = new THREE.Mesh(
        new THREE.PlaneGeometry(0.4, 0.28),
        new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({
          map: makeLabelTexture(config.logo.text, { width: 160, height: 112, bg: config.logo.background, fg: config.logo.foreground }),
          side: THREE.DoubleSide
        })
      );
      logo.position.set(0, 1.5, cabEnd * 8.9);
      if (cabEnd < 0) logo.rotation.y = Math.PI;
      car.add(logo);
      cabShells.push(logo);

      const displayAssembly = new THREE.Group();
      displayAssembly.name = `S-Bahn cab route display assembly ${cabEnd}`;
      displayAssembly.position.set(0, 3.06, cabEnd * 8.76);
      displayAssembly.rotation.y = cabEnd < 0 ? Math.PI : 0;
      const tilted = new THREE.Group();
      tilted.rotation.x = -0.197;
      displayAssembly.add(tilted);
      const recess = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(2.0, 0.4, 0.03), m.dark);
      tilted.add(recess);
      cabShells.push(recess);
      const display = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(1.9, 0.32), new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({
        map: makeLabelTexture(`${context.route.line} ${context.route.destination}`, {
          width: 700,
          height: 128,
          bg: config.destinationDisplay.background,
          fg: config.destinationDisplay.foreground
        }),
        side: THREE.DoubleSide,
        depthWrite: false,
        polygonOffset: true,
        polygonOffsetFactor: -4,
        polygonOffsetUnits: -4
      }));
      display.position.z = 0.025;
      display.renderOrder = 20;
      tilted.add(display);
      car.add(displayAssembly);
      cabDisplays.push(display);
    }
    const floor = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(3.0, 0.12, 15.5), interior.floor);
    floor.position.y = 1.12;
    car.add(floor);
    const aisle = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(1.02, 0.018, 15.24), interior.aisle);
    aisle.position.y = 1.19;
    car.add(aisle);
    for (const z of doorZs) {
      const vestibuleFloor = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(2.82, 0.02, 1.72), interior.vestibuleFloor);
      vestibuleFloor.position.set(0, 1.2, z);
      car.add(vestibuleFloor);
    }
    const ceiling = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(2.68, 0.08, 15.35), m.interior);
    ceiling.position.y = 3.32;
    car.add(ceiling);
    addSBahnCeiling(car, interior);
    addCabinLight(rig, car, 3.04, -4.5);
    addCabinLight(rig, car, 3.04, 4.5);
    addOpaqueTransitInterior(car, 15.35, 1.4, m.interior, () => doorZs);
    addSBahnVestibulePartitions(car, doorZs, interior);
    addSBahnLuggageRacks(car, interior);
    seatRowZs.forEach((z, rowIndex) => {
      for (const x of seatXs) {
        addSBahnSeat(car, x, z, rowIndex % 2 === 0 ? 1 : -1, interior);
      }
    });
    for (const z of poleZs) {
      const pole = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.025, 0.025, 2, 8), m.metal);
      pole.position.set(0, 2.1, z);
      car.add(pole);
    }
    addSBahnHandrails(car, doorZs, m.metal);
    addSBahnVestibuleMarkings(car, doorZs, interior.floorMarking);
    const monitorHousing = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(2.28, 0.68, 0.12), interior.displayHousing);
    monitorHousing.position.set(0, 2.96, 0.15);
    car.add(monitorHousing);
    const monitor = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(2.04, 0.48), displayMaterial);
    monitor.position.set(0, 2.96, 0.082);
    monitor.rotation.y = Math.PI;
    monitor.renderOrder = 18;
    car.add(monitor);
    const monitorBracket = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.024, 0.024, 0.27, 8), m.metal);
    monitorBracket.position.set(0, 3.18, 0.15);
    car.add(monitorBracket);
    for (const z of bogieZs) {
      const bogie = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(2.7, 0.42, 1.8), m.dark);
      bogie.position.set(0, 0.48, z);
      car.add(bogie);
    }
    cars.push(car);
    root.add(car);
  });

  const trainHalfLengthM = config.formation.halfLengthM;
  addPantograph(root, new THREE.Vector3(config.pantograph.x, config.pantograph.y, config.pantograph.z), m.dark);
  addExteriorLightThrow(root, rig, trainHalfLengthM, config.headlightBeamHeightM);

  return {
    root,
    cars,
    doors,
    windowBands,
    cabShells,
    passengerShells,
    cabDisplays,
    glassMaterial: m.glass,
    trainHalfLengthM,
    setExteriorLightsOn: makeSetExteriorLightsOn(rig),
    dispose: () => disposeSBahnVehicle(root, displayMaterial)
  };
}

function makeSBahnMaterials(configMaterials: SBahnBlueprintConfig['materials']): SBahnMaterials {
  const materials = {} as SBahnMaterials;
  for (const role of Object.keys(configMaterials) as SBahnMaterialRole[]) {
    materials[role] = makeStandardMaterial(configMaterials[role]);
  }
  return materials;
}

type SBahnInteriorMaterials = {
  floor: THREE.MeshStandardMaterial;
  aisle: THREE.MeshStandardMaterial;
  vestibuleFloor: THREE.MeshStandardMaterial;
  ceilingVent: THREE.MeshStandardMaterial;
  ceilingLight: THREE.MeshStandardMaterial;
  displayHousing: THREE.MeshStandardMaterial;
  floorMarking: THREE.MeshBasicMaterial;
  seatFabric: THREE.MeshStandardMaterial;
  seatHeadrest: THREE.MeshStandardMaterial;
  seatTrim: THREE.MeshStandardMaterial;
  partitionGlass: THREE.MeshStandardMaterial;
};

function makeSBahnInteriorMaterials(m: SBahnMaterials): SBahnInteriorMaterials {
  const seatFabric = m.seat;
  seatFabric.map = makeSBahnSeatFabricTexture();
  seatFabric.needsUpdate = true;

  const floor = m.floor;
  floor.map = makeSBahnFloorTexture();
  floor.needsUpdate = true;

  const partitionGlass = m.glass.clone();
  partitionGlass.color.set('#dff2f5');
  partitionGlass.transparent = true;
  partitionGlass.opacity = 0.24;
  partitionGlass.depthWrite = false;
  partitionGlass.needsUpdate = true;

  return {
    floor,
    aisle: new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#4f585c', roughness: 0.93 }),
    vestibuleFloor: new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#3e484d', roughness: 0.92 }),
    ceilingVent: new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#6f7a7d', roughness: 0.58, metalness: 0.28 }),
    ceilingLight: new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
      color: '#fffdf4',
      emissive: '#fff8dc',
      emissiveIntensity: 0.75,
      roughness: 0.42
    }),
    displayHousing: new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#182126', roughness: 0.36, metalness: 0.32 }),
    floorMarking: new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: '#e9edeb', transparent: true, opacity: 0.82, side: THREE.DoubleSide }),
    seatFabric,
    seatHeadrest: new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#111d31', roughness: 0.78 }),
    seatTrim: new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#d9dfe0', roughness: 0.5, metalness: 0.12 }),
    partitionGlass
  };
}

function makeSBahnSeatFabricTexture(): THREE.DataTexture {
  const size = 24;
  const pixels = new Uint8Array(size * size * 4);
  for (let y = 0; y < size; y++) {
    for (let x = 0; x < size; x++) {
      const index = (y * size + x) * 4;
      const brightDot = (x === 4 || x === 16) && (y === 4 || y === 16);
      const smallDot = (x === 10 || x === 22) && (y === 10 || y === 22);
      const weave = (x + y) % 5 === 0;
      pixels[index] = brightDot ? 150 : smallDot ? 110 : weave ? 76 : 255;
      pixels[index + 1] = brightDot ? 232 : smallDot ? 186 : weave ? 100 : 255;
      pixels[index + 2] = brightDot ? 255 : smallDot ? 246 : weave ? 128 : 255;
      pixels[index + 3] = 255;
    }
  }
  const texture = new THREE.DataTexture(pixels, size, size, THREE.RGBAFormat);
  texture.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
  texture.wrapS = THREE.RepeatWrapping;
  texture.wrapT = THREE.RepeatWrapping;
  texture.repeat.set(5, 7);
  texture.magFilter = THREE.NearestFilter;
  texture.minFilter = THREE.NearestFilter;
  texture.needsUpdate = true;
  return texture;
}

function makeSBahnFloorTexture(): THREE.DataTexture {
  const size = 32;
  const pixels = new Uint8Array(size * size * 4);
  for (let y = 0; y < size; y++) {
    for (let x = 0; x < size; x++) {
      const index = (y * size + x) * 4;
      const seed = (x * 17 + y * 31 + x * y * 7) % 29;
      const tone = seed < 3 ? 184 : seed > 25 ? 232 : 207;
      pixels[index] = tone;
      pixels[index + 1] = tone + 2;
      pixels[index + 2] = tone + 2;
      pixels[index + 3] = 255;
    }
  }
  const texture = new THREE.DataTexture(pixels, size, size, THREE.RGBAFormat);
  texture.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
  texture.wrapS = THREE.RepeatWrapping;
  texture.wrapT = THREE.RepeatWrapping;
  texture.repeat.set(8, 28);
  texture.magFilter = THREE.NearestFilter;
  texture.minFilter = THREE.LinearFilter;
  texture.needsUpdate = true;
  return texture;
}

function addSBahnCeiling(car: THREE.Group, m: SBahnInteriorMaterials): void {
  const vent = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.84, 0.024, 14.7), m.ceilingVent);
  vent.position.set(0, 3.265, 0);
  car.add(vent);
  for (const x of [-0.93, 0.93]) {
    const lightStrip = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.27, 0.028, 14.7), m.ceilingLight);
    lightStrip.position.set(x, 3.27, 0);
    car.add(lightStrip);
  }
  for (let z = -7.05; z <= 7.05; z += 0.34) {
    const grille = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.7, 0.018, 0.035), m.seatTrim);
    grille.position.set(0, 3.245, z);
    car.add(grille);
  }
}

function addSBahnSeat(
  car: THREE.Group,
  x: number,
  z: number,
  facing: -1 | 1,
  m: SBahnInteriorMaterials
): void {
  const seat = new THREE.Group();
  const cushion = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.42, 0.14, 0.56), m.seatFabric);
  cushion.position.set(0, 1.39, 0);
  seat.add(cushion);

  const back = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.42, 0.54, 0.12), m.seatFabric);
  back.position.set(0, 1.7, -facing * 0.23);
  seat.add(back);
  const headrest = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.42, 0.16, 0.14), m.seatHeadrest);
  headrest.position.set(0, 2.04, -facing * 0.25);
  seat.add(headrest);

  for (const side of [-1, 1]) {
    const edge = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.045, 0.55, 0.58), m.seatTrim);
    edge.position.set(side * 0.22, 1.64, -facing * 0.01);
    seat.add(edge);
  }
  const base = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.38, 0.18, 0.47), m.displayHousing);
  base.position.set(0, 1.23, 0);
  seat.add(base);
  seat.position.set(x, 0, z);
  car.add(seat);
}

function addSBahnVestibulePartitions(car: THREE.Group, doorZs: number[], m: SBahnInteriorMaterials): void {
  for (const doorZ of doorZs) {
    for (const side of [-1, 1] as const) {
      for (const offset of [-0.65, 0.65]) {
        const z = doorZ + offset;
        const panel = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.025, 1.72, 0.58), m.partitionGlass);
        panel.position.set(side * 1.18, 2.03, z);
        car.add(panel);
        const post = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.025, 0.025, 1.82, 8), m.seatTrim);
        post.position.set(side * 1.19, 2.03, z + Math.sign(offset) * 0.3);
        car.add(post);
        const lowerRail = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.05, 0.11, 0.62), m.seatTrim);
        lowerRail.position.set(side * 1.19, 1.28, z);
        car.add(lowerRail);
        for (let y = 1.62; y <= 2.35; y += 0.18) {
          const dot = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.035, 0.05, 0.035), m.seatTrim);
          dot.position.set(side * 1.155, y, z);
          car.add(dot);
        }
      }
    }
  }
}

function addSBahnLuggageRacks(car: THREE.Group, m: SBahnInteriorMaterials): void {
  for (const side of [-1, 1] as const) {
    const rackX = side * 1.15;
    const shelf = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.5, 0.045, 14.2), m.seatTrim);
    shelf.position.set(rackX, 2.91, 0);
    car.add(shelf);

    const guard = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.028, 0.23, 14.0), m.seatTrim);
    guard.position.set(side * 0.93, 3.04, 0);
    car.add(guard);

    for (let z = -6.5; z <= 6.5; z += 1.3) {
      addCylinderBetween(
        car,
        new THREE.Vector3(side * 1.36, 2.57, z),
        new THREE.Vector3(side * 0.96, 3.0, z),
        m.seatTrim
      );
    }
  }
}

function addSBahnVestibuleMarkings(car: THREE.Group, doorZs: number[], material: THREE.Material): void {
  for (const doorZ of doorZs) {
    for (const side of [-1, 1] as const) {
      const centre = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.RingGeometry(0.15, 0.19, 20), material);
      centre.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;
      centre.position.set(side * 0.73, 1.215, doorZ);
      car.add(centre);

      const spoke = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(0.025, 0.38), material);
      spoke.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;
      spoke.position.set(side * 0.73, 1.216, doorZ);
      car.add(spoke);
    }
  }
}

function addSBahnHandrails(car: THREE.Group, doorZs: number[], material: THREE.Material): void {
  for (const side of [-1, 1] as const) {
    addCylinderBetween(car, new THREE.Vector3(side * 1.12, 2.76, -7.0), new THREE.Vector3(side * 1.12, 2.76, 7.0), material);
  }
  for (const doorZ of doorZs) {
    for (const side of [-1, 1] as const) {
      addCylinderBetween(
        car,
        new THREE.Vector3(side * 1.16, 1.24, doorZ - 0.56),
        new THREE.Vector3(side * 0.74, 2.78, doorZ - 0.12),
        material
      );
      addCylinderBetween(
        car,
        new THREE.Vector3(side * 1.16, 1.24, doorZ + 0.56),
        new THREE.Vector3(side * 0.74, 2.78, doorZ + 0.12),
        material
      );
    }
  }
}

function addCylinderBetween(car: THREE.Group, start: THREE.Vector3, end: THREE.Vector3, material: THREE.Material): void {
  const direction = end.clone().sub(start);
  const rail = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.026, 0.026, direction.length(), 8), material);
  rail.position.copy(start).add(end).multiplyScalar(0.5);
  rail.quaternion.setFromUnitVectors(new THREE.Vector3(0, 1, 0), direction.normalize());
  car.add(rail);
}

function disposeSBahnVehicle(root: THREE.Group, sharedDisplayMaterial: THREE.Material): void {
  const materials = new Set<THREE.Material>();
  root.traverse((object) => {
    if (!(object instanceof THREE.Mesh)) return;
    object.geometry.dispose();
    const meshMaterials = Array.isArray(object.material) ? object.material : [object.material];
    for (const material of meshMaterials) {
      if (material !== sharedDisplayMaterial) materials.add(material);
    }
  });
  for (const material of materials) {
    if (material instanceof THREE.MeshStandardMaterial) material.map?.dispose();
    material.dispose();
  }
}

function makeSBahn430Body(lengthM: number): THREE.ExtrudeGeometry {
  // Aluminium body profile: nearly vertical sides up to the cant rail, then
  // broad curved shoulders and a shallow crown around the rooftop equipment.
  const crossSection = new THREE.Shape();
  crossSection.moveTo(-1.51, 0.4);
  crossSection.lineTo(1.51, 0.4);
  crossSection.lineTo(1.51, 3.02);
  crossSection.quadraticCurveTo(1.49, 3.25, 1.35, 3.43);
  crossSection.quadraticCurveTo(1.16, 3.64, 0.72, 3.69);
  crossSection.quadraticCurveTo(0.34, 3.73, 0, 3.73);
  crossSection.quadraticCurveTo(-0.34, 3.73, -0.72, 3.69);
  crossSection.quadraticCurveTo(-1.16, 3.64, -1.35, 3.43);
  crossSection.quadraticCurveTo(-1.49, 3.25, -1.51, 3.02);
  crossSection.closePath();

  const geometry = new THREE.ExtrudeGeometry(crossSection, {
    depth: lengthM,
    bevelEnabled: true,
    bevelThickness: 0.025,
    bevelSize: 0.025,
    bevelSegments: 2,
    curveSegments: 8
  });
  geometry.translate(0, 0, -lengthM / 2);
  return geometry;
}

function makeSBahn430Nose(): THREE.ExtrudeGeometry {
  // Side profile of the flat, slightly raked BR 430 front, extruded across the width.
  const profile = new THREE.Shape();
  profile.moveTo(0, 0.42);
  profile.lineTo(0.55, 0.42);
  profile.lineTo(0.72, 0.62);
  profile.lineTo(0.78, 1.9);
  profile.lineTo(0.52, 3.2);
  profile.quadraticCurveTo(0.42, 3.5, 0.12, 3.6);
  profile.lineTo(0, 3.6);
  profile.closePath();
  const geometry = new THREE.ExtrudeGeometry(profile, {
    depth: 2.7,
    bevelEnabled: true,
    bevelThickness: 0.09,
    bevelSize: 0.09,
    bevelSegments: 3
  });
  geometry.translate(0, 0, -1.35);
  geometry.rotateY(-Math.PI / 2);
  return geometry;
}

function makeSBahn430Mask(): THREE.ExtrudeGeometry {
  const shape = new THREE.Shape();
  shape.moveTo(-1.3, -0.66);
  shape.lineTo(1.3, -0.66);
  shape.lineTo(1.06, 0.56);
  shape.quadraticCurveTo(0.98, 0.66, 0.82, 0.68);
  shape.lineTo(-0.82, 0.68);
  shape.quadraticCurveTo(-0.98, 0.66, -1.06, 0.56);
  shape.closePath();
  const geometry = new THREE.ExtrudeGeometry(shape, { depth: 0.06, bevelEnabled: true, bevelSize: 0.02, bevelThickness: 0.02, bevelSegments: 2 });
  geometry.translate(0, 0, -0.03);
  return geometry;
}

function makeSBahn430Windscreen(): THREE.ExtrudeGeometry {
  const shape = new THREE.Shape();
  shape.moveTo(-1.18, -0.56);
  shape.lineTo(1.18, -0.56);
  shape.lineTo(0.92, 0.44);
  shape.quadraticCurveTo(0.84, 0.54, 0.68, 0.56);
  shape.lineTo(-0.68, 0.56);
  shape.quadraticCurveTo(-0.84, 0.54, -0.92, 0.44);
  shape.closePath();
  const geometry = new THREE.ExtrudeGeometry(shape, { depth: 0.05, bevelEnabled: true, bevelSize: 0.02, bevelThickness: 0.015, bevelSegments: 2 });
  geometry.translate(0, 0, -0.025);
  return geometry;
}

--- vehicles/blueprint/sbahn/SBahnBlueprintConfig.ts ---
import { collectMaterialConfigErrors, type BlueprintStandardMaterialConfig } from '../standardMaterial';

// Declarative configuration for the BR 430-family S-Bahn/metro renderer. The
// config owns livery materials, car formation, door/window/seat layout, the
// end logo and display colors. Fixed BR 430 styling (body cross-section, nose
// profile, cab detailing, ...) lives in buildSBahnFromBlueprint.ts.

export const SBAHN_MATERIAL_ROLES = [
  'shell',
  'accent',
  'dark',
  'glass',
  'interior',
  'floor',
  'seat',
  'metal',
  'headlight',
  'tailLamp'
] as const;

export type SBahnMaterialRole = (typeof SBAHN_MATERIAL_ROLES)[number];

export type SBahnBlueprintConfig = {
  id: string;
  kind: 's-bahn';
  displayName: string;
  materials: Record<SBahnMaterialRole, BlueprintStandardMaterialConfig>;
  // Operator logo shown on both cab ends.
  logo: {
    text: string;
    background: string;
    foreground: string;
  };
  destinationDisplay: {
    background: string;
    foreground: string;
  };
  formation: {
    // Car centre offsets along the train; first/last cars get cab ends.
    carCenters: number[];
    halfLengthM: number;
    sectionNamePrefix: string;
  };
  // Per-car layout, all z positions in car-local metres.
  layout: {
    doorZs: number[];
    windowZs: number[];
    seatRowZs: number[];
    seatXs: number[];
    poleZs: number[];
    bogieZs: number[];
  };
  pantograph: {
    x: number;
    y: number;
    z: number;
  };
  headlightBeamHeightM: number;
};

export type SBahnBlueprintParseResult =
  | { ok: true; config: SBahnBlueprintConfig }
  | { ok: false; errors: string[] };

// Narrows an untrusted value (e.g. a user-edited JSON file) into an
// SBahnBlueprintConfig. Never throws; malformed input is reported as errors.
export function parseSBahnBlueprint(raw: unknown): SBahnBlueprintParseResult {
  const errors: string[] = [];
  const root = asRecord(raw, 'config', errors);
  if (!root) return { ok: false, errors };

  requireString(root, 'id', errors);
  requireString(root, 'displayName', errors);
  if (root.kind !== 's-bahn') errors.push('kind must be "s-bahn"');

  const materials = asRecord(root.materials, 'materials', errors);
  if (materials) {
    for (const role of SBAHN_MATERIAL_ROLES) {
      collectMaterialConfigErrors(materials[role], `materials.${role}`, errors);
    }
  }

  for (const [key, fields] of [
    ['logo', ['text', 'background', 'foreground']],
    ['destinationDisplay', ['background', 'foreground']]
  ] as const) {
    const record = asRecord(root[key], key, errors);
    if (record) for (const field of fields) requireString(record, field, errors, `${key}.`);
  }

  const formation = asRecord(root.formation, 'formation', errors);
  if (formation) {
    requireNumber(formation, 'halfLengthM', errors, 'formation.');
    requireString(formation, 'sectionNamePrefix', errors, 'formation.');
    if (!isNumberArray(formation.carCenters) || formation.carCenters.length < 2) {
      errors.push('formation.carCenters must be an array of at least 2 numbers');
    }
  }

  const layout = asRecord(root.layout, 'layout', errors);
  if (layout) {
    for (const key of ['doorZs', 'windowZs', 'seatRowZs', 'seatXs', 'poleZs', 'bogieZs'] as const) {
      if (!isNumberArray(layout[key])) errors.push(`layout.${key} must be an array of numbers`);
    }
  }

  const pantograph = asRecord(root.pantograph, 'pantograph', errors);
  if (pantograph) {
    for (const key of ['x', 'y', 'z'] as const) requireNumber(pantograph, key, errors, 'pantograph.');
  }

  requireNumber(root, 'headlightBeamHeightM', errors);

  if (errors.length > 0) return { ok: false, errors };
  return { ok: true, config: raw as SBahnBlueprintConfig };
}

function asRecord(value: unknown, path: string, errors: string[]): Record<string, unknown> | null {
  if (value && typeof value === 'object' && !Array.isArray(value)) return value as Record<string, unknown>;
  errors.push(`${path} must be an object`);
  return null;
}

function requireString(record: Record<string, unknown>, key: string, errors: string[], prefix = ''): void {
  if (typeof record[key] !== 'string' || record[key] === '') errors.push(`${prefix}${key} must be a non-empty string`);
}

function requireNumber(record: Record<string, unknown>, key: string, errors: string[], prefix = ''): void {
  if (typeof record[key] !== 'number' || !Number.isFinite(record[key])) errors.push(`${prefix}${key} must be a finite number`);
}

function isNumberArray(value: unknown): value is number[] {
  return Array.isArray(value) && value.every((entry) => typeof entry === 'number' && Number.isFinite(entry));
}

--- engine/core/ResourceScope.ts ---
/**
 * Tracks disposables (geometries, materials, textures, render targets, controls,
 * layers, event bindings wrapped as `{ dispose }`) so an owner can release every
 * resource it created in one call.
 *
 * Each system and layer gets its own scope, giving every Three.js resource a
 * single explicit owner and a deterministic disposal path.
 */
export interface Disposable {
  dispose(): void;
}

export class ResourceScope implements Disposable {
  private readonly disposables = new Set<Disposable>();
  private disposed = false;

  /** Registers a disposable and returns it for convenient inline use. */
  track<T extends Disposable>(value: T): T {
    if (this.disposed) {
      // A scope that already disposed must not adopt new resources — that would
      // silently leak them. Callers racing async loads should check the scope.
      value.dispose();
      return value;
    }
    this.disposables.add(value);
    return value;
  }

  /** Stops tracking a value without disposing it (ownership handed elsewhere). */
  release(value: Disposable): void {
    this.disposables.delete(value);
  }

  /** True once {@link dispose} has run; async loaders should bail if set. */
  get isDisposed(): boolean {
    return this.disposed;
  }

  dispose(): void {
    this.disposed = true;
    for (const value of this.disposables) {
      value.dispose();
    }
    this.disposables.clear();
  }
}

--- engine/core/TransitEngine.test.ts ---
import { describe, it, expect, vi } from 'vitest';
import type * as THREE from 'three';
import { TransitEngine } from './TransitEngine';
import type { EngineSystem } from './EngineSystem';

function makeEngine() {
  const render = vi.fn();
  const scene = {} as THREE.Scene;
  const camera = {} as THREE.PerspectiveCamera;
  const renderer = { render } as unknown as THREE.WebGLRenderer;
  return { engine: new TransitEngine(scene, camera, renderer), render, scene, camera };
}

/** Records lifecycle calls with a shared log so ordering can be asserted. */
function recordingSystem(name: string, log: string[]): EngineSystem {
  return {
    fixedUpdate: () => log.push(`${name}.fixed`),
    update: () => log.push(`${name}.update`),
    dispose: () => log.push(`${name}.dispose`)
  };
}

describe('TransitEngine', () => {
  it('updates systems in registration order, then renders once', () => {
    const { engine, render, scene, camera } = makeEngine();
    const log: string[] = [];
    engine.register(recordingSystem('a', log));
    engine.register(recordingSystem('b', log));
    engine.update(0.016, 1);
    expect(log).toEqual(['a.update', 'b.update']);
    expect(render).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(render).toHaveBeenCalledWith(scene, camera);
  });

  it('runs fixedUpdate for every system without rendering', () => {
    const { engine, render } = makeEngine();
    const log: string[] = [];
    engine.register(recordingSystem('a', log));
    engine.register(recordingSystem('b', log));
    engine.fixedUpdate(1 / 60);
    expect(log).toEqual(['a.fixed', 'b.fixed']);
    expect(render).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('disposes systems in reverse registration order', () => {
    const { engine } = makeEngine();
    const log: string[] = [];
    engine.register(recordingSystem('a', log));
    engine.register(recordingSystem('b', log));
    engine.register(recordingSystem('c', log));
    engine.dispose();
    expect(log).toEqual(['c.dispose', 'b.dispose', 'a.dispose']);
  });

  it('tolerates systems that implement only some hooks', () => {
    const { engine, render } = makeEngine();
    const dispose = vi.fn();
    engine.register({ dispose }); // no update/fixedUpdate
    expect(() => engine.update(0.016, 1)).not.toThrow();
    expect(() => engine.fixedUpdate(0.016)).not.toThrow();
    expect(render).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    engine.dispose();
    expect(dispose).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it('does not re-dispose systems after a dispose', () => {
    const { engine } = makeEngine();
    const dispose = vi.fn();
    engine.register({ dispose });
    engine.dispose();
    engine.dispose();
    expect(dispose).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });
});

--- engine/core/LegacyWorldSystem.ts ---
import type { TransitWorld } from '../../world';
import type { EngineSystem } from './EngineSystem';

/**
 * Adapter that lets the existing {@link TransitWorld} live inside the engine's
 * system list while it is decomposed into real systems (camera, active vehicle,
 * lighting, environment, …). Rendering is owned by the engine now; this adapter
 * only ties world disposal into the engine's disposal so ownership is explicit.
 *
 * The world's per-frame simulation binding (updateTrain) is still driven
 * directly by SimulatorRuntime and will migrate into ActiveVehicleSystem.
 */
export class LegacyWorldSystem implements EngineSystem {
  constructor(private readonly world: TransitWorld) {}

  dispose(): void {
    this.world.dispose();
  }
}

--- engine/core/EngineSystem.ts ---
/**
 * A unit of engine behavior with an explicit lifecycle. Systems are registered
 * on {@link TransitEngine} and driven by its loop.
 *
 * - `fixedUpdate` runs on the fixed simulation step (deterministic work).
 * - `update` runs once per rendered frame with an interpolation `alpha`
 *   (visual/camera/lighting work).
 * - `dispose` releases every Three.js resource the system owns.
 */
export interface EngineSystem {
  initialize?(): void | Promise<void>;
  fixedUpdate?(dt: number): void;
  update?(dt: number, alpha: number): void;
  dispose(): void;
}

--- engine/core/ResourceScope.test.ts ---
import { describe, it, expect, vi } from 'vitest';
import { ResourceScope, type Disposable } from './ResourceScope';

const disposable = (): Disposable & { dispose: ReturnType<typeof vi.fn> } => ({ dispose: vi.fn() });

describe('ResourceScope', () => {
  it('disposes every tracked resource once', () => {
    const scope = new ResourceScope();
    const a = disposable();
    const b = disposable();
    scope.track(a);
    scope.track(b);
    scope.dispose();
    expect(a.dispose).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(b.dispose).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it('returns the tracked value for inline use', () => {
    const scope = new ResourceScope();
    const a = disposable();
    expect(scope.track(a)).toBe(a);
  });

  it('does not dispose a released resource', () => {
    const scope = new ResourceScope();
    const a = disposable();
    scope.track(a);
    scope.release(a);
    scope.dispose();
    expect(a.dispose).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('immediately disposes resources tracked after disposal (no leak)', () => {
    const scope = new ResourceScope();
    scope.dispose();
    expect(scope.isDisposed).toBe(true);
    const late = disposable();
    scope.track(late);
    expect(late.dispose).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it('clears its set so a second dispose does not re-dispose', () => {
    const scope = new ResourceScope();
    const a = disposable();
    scope.track(a);
    scope.dispose();
    scope.dispose();
    expect(a.dispose).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });
});

--- engine/core/TransitEngine.ts ---
import type * as THREE from 'three';
import type { EngineSystem } from './EngineSystem';

/**
 * Owns the scene graph render and the ordered set of {@link EngineSystem}s.
 *
 * Incremental adoption: today the scene/camera/renderer are still created and
 * owned by the legacy `TransitWorld` and injected here, and systems are
 * extracted from the world one at a time (starting with a LegacyWorldSystem
 * adapter). The renderer is therefore *not* disposed here yet — its current
 * owner (the world) disposes it — so we don't double-dispose. Once
 * EnvironmentSystem and friends own creation, the engine will own the renderer
 * outright per the target architecture.
 */
export class TransitEngine {
  private readonly systems: EngineSystem[] = [];

  constructor(
    readonly scene: THREE.Scene,
    readonly camera: THREE.PerspectiveCamera,
    readonly renderer: THREE.WebGLRenderer
  ) {}

  register(system: EngineSystem): void {
    this.systems.push(system);
  }

  /** Fixed-step pass for deterministic systems. */
  fixedUpdate(dt: number): void {
    for (const system of this.systems) {
      system.fixedUpdate?.(dt);
    }
  }

  /** Per-frame pass: update visual systems, then render once. */
  update(dt: number, alpha: number): void {
    for (const system of this.systems) {
      system.update?.(dt, alpha);
    }
    this.renderer.render(this.scene, this.camera);
  }

  /** Disposes systems in reverse registration order (LIFO ownership). */
  dispose(): void {
    for (const system of [...this.systems].reverse()) {
      system.dispose();
    }
    this.systems.length = 0;
  }
}

--- engine/builders/BuilderRegistry.test.ts ---
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { BuilderRegistry } from './BuilderRegistry';

describe('BuilderRegistry', () => {
  it('registers and resolves builders by key', () => {
    const r = new BuilderRegistry<string, () => string>();
    r.register('bus', () => 'stop').register('rail', () => 'platform');
    expect(r.resolve('bus')()).toBe('stop');
    expect(r.resolve('rail')()).toBe('platform');
  });

  it('has() reports registration', () => {
    const r = new BuilderRegistry<string, number>();
    r.register('a', 1);
    expect(r.has('a')).toBe(true);
    expect(r.has('b')).toBe(false);
  });

  it('resolveOptional returns undefined for an unregistered key', () => {
    const r = new BuilderRegistry<string, number>();
    expect(r.resolveOptional('missing')).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it('resolve throws for an unregistered key', () => {
    const r = new BuilderRegistry<string, number>();
    expect(() => r.resolve('missing')).toThrow(/No builder registered for missing/);
  });

  it('re-registering a key overrides the previous builder', () => {
    const r = new BuilderRegistry<string, number>();
    r.register('a', 1).register('a', 2);
    expect(r.resolve('a')).toBe(2);
  });
});

--- engine/builders/BuilderRegistry.ts ---
/**
 * A keyed lookup of builder strategies, replacing large `if (kind === …)`
 * chains. Register a builder per key; resolve it by key at build time. New
 * variants register without editing existing branch logic.
 */
export class BuilderRegistry<K, B> {
  private readonly builders = new Map<K, B>();

  register(key: K, builder: B): this {
    this.builders.set(key, builder);
    return this;
  }

  has(key: K): boolean {
    return this.builders.has(key);
  }

  /** Returns the builder for `key`, or undefined if none is registered. */
  resolveOptional(key: K): B | undefined {
    return this.builders.get(key);
  }

  /** Returns the builder for `key`, throwing if none is registered. */
  resolve(key: K): B {
    const builder = this.builders.get(key);
    if (!builder) throw new Error(`No builder registered for ${String(key)}`);
    return builder;
  }
}

--- engine/workspace/Workspace.ts ---
import type { EngineInputEvent } from './EngineInputEvent';

/**
 * A mode of interaction with the engine (simulation, photo, and — later —
 * vehicle constructor). Only simulation and photo exist today; new workspaces
 * plug into the {@link WorkspaceManager} without touching existing ones.
 */
export interface Workspace {
  readonly id: string;

  enter(): void | Promise<void>;
  exit(): void | Promise<void>;

  /** Returns true if the event was consumed. */
  handleInput(event: EngineInputEvent): boolean;

  fixedUpdate?(dt: number): void;
  update?(dt: number): void;
}

/**
 * The narrow slice of runtime commands workspaces act on. `SimulatorRuntime`
 * satisfies this structurally, so workspaces stay decoupled from it (and from
 * React/Three.js) and are unit-testable with a plain fake.
 */
export interface WorkspaceTarget {
  readonly input: {
    setThrottle(value: number): void;
    setBrake(value: number): void;
    setLook(left: boolean, right: boolean): void;
    reset(): void;
  };
  adjustZoom(delta: number): void;
  enterPhotoMode(): void;
  exitPhotoMode(): void;
  cyclePhotoPreset(): void;
}

--- engine/workspace/WorkspaceManager.test.ts ---
import { describe, it, expect, vi } from 'vitest';
import { WorkspaceManager } from './WorkspaceManager';
import { SimulationWorkspace } from './SimulationWorkspace';
import { PhotoWorkspace } from './PhotoWorkspace';
import type { Workspace, WorkspaceTarget } from './Workspace';

function makeTarget() {
  return {
    input: { setThrottle: vi.fn(), setBrake: vi.fn(), setLook: vi.fn(), reset: vi.fn() },
    adjustZoom: vi.fn(),
    enterPhotoMode: vi.fn(),
    exitPhotoMode: vi.fn(),
    cyclePhotoPreset: vi.fn()
  } satisfies WorkspaceTarget;
}

function recordingWorkspace(id: string, log: string[]): Workspace {
  return {
    id,
    enter: vi.fn(() => void log.push(`${id}.enter`)),
    exit: vi.fn(() => void log.push(`${id}.exit`)),
    handleInput: () => false
  };
}

describe('WorkspaceManager', () => {
  it('enters the first workspace and reports the active id', async () => {
    const log: string[] = [];
    const manager = new WorkspaceManager();
    await manager.activate(recordingWorkspace('a', log));
    expect(log).toEqual(['a.enter']);
    expect(manager.activeId).toBe('a');
  });

  it('exits the previous workspace before entering the next', async () => {
    const log: string[] = [];
    const manager = new WorkspaceManager();
    await manager.activate(recordingWorkspace('a', log));
    await manager.activate(recordingWorkspace('b', log));
    expect(log).toEqual(['a.enter', 'a.exit', 'b.enter']);
  });

  it('activating the same workspace twice is a no-op', async () => {
    const log: string[] = [];
    const manager = new WorkspaceManager();
    const a = recordingWorkspace('a', log);
    await manager.activate(a);
    await manager.activate(a);
    expect(log).toEqual(['a.enter']);
  });

  it('routes input to the active workspace only', async () => {
    const manager = new WorkspaceManager();
    const consumed: Workspace = { id: 'x', enter: () => {}, exit: () => {}, handleInput: () => true };
    await manager.activate(consumed);
    expect(manager.handleInput({ type: 'zoom', delta: 1 })).toBe(true);
  });

  it('returns false when there is no active workspace', () => {
    const manager = new WorkspaceManager();
    expect(manager.handleInput({ type: 'photo-exit' })).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe('SimulationWorkspace', () => {
  it('routes driving input, zoom, and photo-toggle', () => {
    const target = makeTarget();
    const ws = new SimulationWorkspace(target);
    expect(ws.handleInput({ type: 'throttle', value: 1 })).toBe(true);
    expect(target.input.setThrottle).toHaveBeenCalledWith(1);
    ws.handleInput({ type: 'brake', value: 0.5 });
    expect(target.input.setBrake).toHaveBeenCalledWith(0.5);
    ws.handleInput({ type: 'look', left: true, right: false });
    expect(target.input.setLook).toHaveBeenCalledWith(true, false);
    ws.handleInput({ type: 'zoom', delta: -1 });
    expect(target.adjustZoom).toHaveBeenCalledWith(-1);
    ws.handleInput({ type: 'photo-toggle' });
    expect(target.enterPhotoMode).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it('releases held input on exit', () => {
    const target = makeTarget();
    new SimulationWorkspace(target).exit();
    expect(target.input.reset).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it('ignores photo-exit/cycle', () => {
    const target = makeTarget();
    const ws = new SimulationWorkspace(target);
    expect(ws.handleInput({ type: 'photo-exit' })).toBe(false);
    expect(ws.handleInput({ type: 'photo-cycle' })).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe('PhotoWorkspace', () => {
  it('resets input on enter and exits on photo-exit/toggle', () => {
    const target = makeTarget();
    const ws = new PhotoWorkspace(target);
    ws.enter();
    expect(target.input.reset).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(ws.handleInput({ type: 'photo-exit' })).toBe(true);
    expect(target.exitPhotoMode).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    ws.handleInput({ type: 'photo-toggle' });
    expect(target.exitPhotoMode).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
  });

  it('cycles preset and zooms', () => {
    const target = makeTarget();
    const ws = new PhotoWorkspace(target);
    ws.handleInput({ type: 'photo-cycle' });
    expect(target.cyclePhotoPreset).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    ws.handleInput({ type: 'zoom', delta: 2 });
    expect(target.adjustZoom).toHaveBeenCalledWith(2);
  });

  it('swallows driving input so it never reaches the simulator', () => {
    const target = makeTarget();
    const ws = new PhotoWorkspace(target);
    expect(ws.handleInput({ type: 'throttle', value: 1 })).toBe(true);
    expect(target.input.setThrottle).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});

--- engine/workspace/EngineInputEvent.ts ---
/**
 * Semantic input the InputController translates physical events into. The
 * controller does not decide which subsystem handles an event — it emits these
 * and the active {@link Workspace} interprets them.
 */
export type EngineInputEvent =
  | { type: 'throttle'; value: number }
  | { type: 'brake'; value: number }
  | { type: 'look'; left: boolean; right: boolean }
  | { type: 'zoom'; delta: number }
  | { type: 'photo-toggle' }
  | { type: 'photo-exit' }
  | { type: 'photo-cycle' };

--- engine/workspace/WorkspaceManager.ts ---
import type { Workspace } from './Workspace';
import type { EngineInputEvent } from './EngineInputEvent';

/**
 * Holds the active {@link Workspace} and routes input to it. Activating a
 * workspace exits the previous one first, so enter/exit are always balanced.
 */
export class WorkspaceManager {
  private active: Workspace | null = null;

  get activeId(): string | null {
    return this.active?.id ?? null;
  }

  async activate(next: Workspace): Promise<void> {
    if (this.active === next) return;
    await this.active?.exit();
    this.active = next;
    await next.enter();
  }

  handleInput(event: EngineInputEvent): boolean {
    return this.active?.handleInput(event) ?? false;
  }

  fixedUpdate(dt: number): void {
    this.active?.fixedUpdate?.(dt);
  }

  update(dt: number): void {
    this.active?.update?.(dt);
  }
}

--- engine/workspace/SimulationWorkspace.ts ---
import type { Workspace, WorkspaceTarget } from './Workspace';
import type { EngineInputEvent } from './EngineInputEvent';

/**
 * Default driving workspace: throttle/brake/look feed the runtime input, zoom
 * adjusts the camera, and photo-toggle enters cinematic mode. Held input is
 * released on exit so leaving the workspace can't leave a control latched.
 */
export class SimulationWorkspace implements Workspace {
  readonly id = 'simulation';

  constructor(private readonly target: WorkspaceTarget) {}

  enter(): void {}

  exit(): void {
    this.target.input.reset();
  }

  handleInput(event: EngineInputEvent): boolean {
    switch (event.type) {
      case 'throttle':
        this.target.input.setThrottle(event.value);
        return true;
      case 'brake':
        this.target.input.setBrake(event.value);
        return true;
      case 'look':
        this.target.input.setLook(event.left, event.right);
        return true;
      case 'zoom':
        this.target.adjustZoom(event.delta);
        return true;
      case 'photo-toggle':
        this.target.enterPhotoMode();
        return true;
      default:
        return false;
    }
  }
}

--- engine/workspace/PhotoWorkspace.ts ---
import type { Workspace, WorkspaceTarget } from './Workspace';
import type { EngineInputEvent } from './EngineInputEvent';

/**
 * Cinematic presentation workspace. Driving input (throttle/brake/look) is
 * ignored here; zoom still works, photo-cycle changes the preset, and
 * photo-exit/toggle returns to the simulation workspace.
 */
export class PhotoWorkspace implements Workspace {
  readonly id = 'photo';

  constructor(private readonly target: WorkspaceTarget) {}

  enter(): void {
    // Release any held driving input on entry so it can't leak into photo mode.
    this.target.input.reset();
  }

  exit(): void {}

  handleInput(event: EngineInputEvent): boolean {
    switch (event.type) {
      case 'photo-exit':
      case 'photo-toggle':
        this.target.exitPhotoMode();
        return true;
      case 'photo-cycle':
        this.target.cyclePhotoPreset();
        return true;
      case 'zoom':
        this.target.adjustZoom(event.delta);
        return true;
      default:
        // Swallow driving input so it doesn't reach the simulator in photo mode.
        return true;
    }
  }
}

--- engine/runtime/worldKey.ts ---
import { routeMode } from '../../route';
import type { RouteSession } from '../../simulator/SimulatorRuntime';

/**
 * Structural identity of a world. When this key changes the world must be fully
 * rebuilt (terrain, scenery, vehicle); when only other session/presentation
 * fields change, existing systems are updated in place instead.
 */
export function worldKey(session: RouteSession): string {
  return [
    session.city.id,
    session.route.id,
    session.route.shapeId ?? '',
    session.route.vehicleDesignId ?? '',
    routeMode(session.route)
  ].join(':');
}

--- engine/runtime/InputState.test.ts ---
import { describe, it, expect, vi } from 'vitest';
import { InputState } from './InputState';

describe('InputState', () => {
  it('clamps throttle and brake to [0, 1]', () => {
    const input = new InputState();
    input.setThrottle(2);
    input.setBrake(-1);
    expect(input.throttle).toBe(1);
    expect(input.brake).toBe(0);
  });

  it('maps look keys to a signed axis and cancels opposing/matching keys', () => {
    const input = new InputState();
    input.setLook(true, false);
    expect(input.lookX).toBe(-1);
    input.setLook(false, true);
    expect(input.lookX).toBe(1);
    input.setLook(true, true);
    expect(input.lookX).toBe(0);
    input.setLook(false, false);
    expect(input.lookX).toBe(0);
  });

  it('reset clears all values', () => {
    const input = new InputState();
    input.setThrottle(1);
    input.setBrake(1);
    input.setLook(false, true);
    input.reset();
    expect(input.throttle).toBe(0);
    expect(input.brake).toBe(0);
    expect(input.lookX).toBe(0);
  });

  it('notifies the change listener on every mutation', () => {
    const input = new InputState();
    const listener = vi.fn();
    input.onChange(listener);
    input.setThrottle(0.5);
    input.setBrake(0.5);
    input.setLook(true, false);
    input.reset();
    expect(listener).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(4);
    expect(listener).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith(input);
  });

  it('stops notifying after the listener is cleared', () => {
    const input = new InputState();
    const listener = vi.fn();
    input.onChange(listener);
    input.onChange(null);
    input.setThrottle(1);
    expect(listener).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});

--- engine/runtime/RuntimeReconciler.test.ts ---
