import { describe, it, expect, vi } from 'vitest';
import { RuntimeReconciler, type ReconcilerRuntime } from './RuntimeReconciler';
import type { DesiredRuntimeState } from './DesiredRuntimeState';
import type { RouteSession } from '../../simulator/SimulatorRuntime';

function makeRuntime() {
  return {
    loadRoute: vi.fn(),
    setStartStation: vi.fn(),
    setRideMode: vi.fn(),
    setSeason: vi.fn(),
    setTime: vi.fn(),
    setCameraMode: vi.fn(),
    setPaused: vi.fn()
  } satisfies ReconcilerRuntime;
}

/** Minimal session — only the fields worldKey/reconciler read matter. */
function session(overrides: Partial<{ cityId: string; routeId: string; transportMode: string; mode: 'ride' | 'driver'; startStationIndex: number }> = {}): RouteSession {
  const { cityId = 'stuttgart', routeId = 'u12', transportMode = 'stadtbahn', mode = 'ride', startStationIndex = 0 } = overrides;
  return {
    route: { id: routeId, transportMode } as unknown as RouteSession['route'],
    networkRoutes: [],
    city: { id: cityId } as unknown as RouteSession['city'],
    mode,
    startStationIndex,
    season: 'summer',
    time: 'auto',
    cameraMode: 'chase'
  } as unknown as RouteSession;
}

function desired(overrides: Partial<DesiredRuntimeState['presentation']> & { session?: RouteSession | null; paused?: boolean } = {}): DesiredRuntimeState {
  return {
    session: 'session' in overrides ? overrides.session! : session(),
    simulation: { paused: overrides.paused ?? false },
    presentation: {
      season: overrides.season ?? 'summer',
      time: overrides.time ?? 'auto',
      cameraMode: overrides.cameraMode ?? 'chase'
    }
  };
}

describe('RuntimeReconciler', () => {
  it('loads the route on first apply, then does nothing for identical state', () => {
    const runtime = makeRuntime();
    const r = new RuntimeReconciler(runtime);
    const state = desired();
    r.apply(state);
    expect(runtime.loadRoute).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    r.apply(desired()); // structurally identical
    expect(runtime.loadRoute).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(runtime.setStartStation).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(runtime.setRideMode).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('rebuilds the world exactly once on a route change', () => {
    const runtime = makeRuntime();
    const r = new RuntimeReconciler(runtime);
    r.apply(desired());
    r.apply(desired({ session: session({ routeId: 'u6' }) }));
    expect(runtime.loadRoute).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
  });

  it('rebuilds the world on a vehicle-design/structural change', () => {
    const runtime = makeRuntime();
    const r = new RuntimeReconciler(runtime);
    r.apply(desired());
    r.apply(desired({ session: session({ cityId: 'ulm' }) }));
    expect(runtime.loadRoute).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
  });

  it('season change calls only setSeason', () => {
    const runtime = makeRuntime();
    const r = new RuntimeReconciler(runtime);
    r.apply(desired());
    Object.values(runtime).forEach((fn) => fn.mockClear());
    r.apply(desired({ season: 'winter' }));
    expect(runtime.setSeason).toHaveBeenCalledWith('winter');
    expect(runtime.loadRoute).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(runtime.setStartStation).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(runtime.setCameraMode).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('start-station change updates the world without rebuilding it', () => {
    const runtime = makeRuntime();
    const r = new RuntimeReconciler(runtime);
    r.apply(desired());
    Object.values(runtime).forEach((fn) => fn.mockClear());
    r.apply(desired({ session: session({ startStationIndex: 3 }) }));
    expect(runtime.setStartStation).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(runtime.loadRoute).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('camera change calls only setCameraMode (no world rebuild)', () => {
    const runtime = makeRuntime();
    const r = new RuntimeReconciler(runtime);
    r.apply(desired());
    Object.values(runtime).forEach((fn) => fn.mockClear());
    r.apply(desired({ cameraMode: 'cab' }));
    expect(runtime.setCameraMode).toHaveBeenCalledWith('cab');
    expect(runtime.loadRoute).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('ride-mode change calls only setRideMode', () => {
    const runtime = makeRuntime();
    const r = new RuntimeReconciler(runtime);
    r.apply(desired());
    Object.values(runtime).forEach((fn) => fn.mockClear());
    r.apply(desired({ session: session({ mode: 'driver' }) }));
    expect(runtime.setRideMode).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(runtime.loadRoute).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('pause toggles setPaused only when it changes', () => {
    const runtime = makeRuntime();
    const r = new RuntimeReconciler(runtime);
    r.apply(desired({ paused: false }));
    expect(runtime.setPaused).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    r.apply(desired({ paused: false }));
    expect(runtime.setPaused).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    r.apply(desired({ paused: true }));
    expect(runtime.setPaused).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith(true);
  });

  it('does nothing structural when session is null', () => {
    const runtime = makeRuntime();
    const r = new RuntimeReconciler(runtime);
    r.apply(desired({ session: null }));
    expect(runtime.loadRoute).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(runtime.setPaused).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});

--- engine/runtime/DesiredRuntimeState.ts ---
import type { CameraMode } from '../../world/CameraController';
import type { Season } from '../../app/types';
import type { RouteSession } from '../../simulator/SimulatorRuntime';

/**
 * The complete state React *wants* the runtime to be in. React produces this
 * declaratively; the {@link RuntimeReconciler} diffs it against the previous
 * value and issues the minimal set of imperative commands — replacing the old
 * one-effect-per-command chain in AppShell.
 *
 * Photo/workspace activation is intentionally not here yet: it stays
 * runtime-authoritative (Phase 1) until the WorkspaceManager lands (Phase 9).
 */
export type DesiredRuntimeState = {
  session: RouteSession | null;
  simulation: {
    paused: boolean;
  };
  presentation: {
    season: Season;
    time: 'auto' | number;
    cameraMode: CameraMode;
  };
};

--- engine/runtime/RuntimeReconciler.ts ---
import type { CameraMode } from '../../world/CameraController';
import type { Season } from '../../app/types';
import type { RouteSession } from '../../simulator/SimulatorRuntime';
import type { DesiredRuntimeState } from './DesiredRuntimeState';
import { worldKey } from './worldKey';

/** The subset of runtime commands the reconciler drives (kept narrow for testing). */
export interface ReconcilerRuntime {
  loadRoute(session: RouteSession): void;
  setStartStation(session: RouteSession): void;
  setRideMode(session: RouteSession): void;
  setSeason(season: Season): void;
  setTime(time: 'auto' | number): void;
  setCameraMode(mode: CameraMode): void;
  setPaused(paused: boolean): void;
}

/**
 * Diffs successive {@link DesiredRuntimeState} values and issues only the
 * commands whose inputs changed:
 *
 * - A change to the structural {@link worldKey} rebuilds the world once.
 * - A start-station or ride-mode change updates the existing world.
 * - Season/time/camera/pause changes update presentation without rebuilding.
 *
 * Applying the same desired state twice is a no-op.
 */
export class RuntimeReconciler {
  private previous: DesiredRuntimeState | null = null;

  constructor(private readonly runtime: ReconcilerRuntime) {}

  apply(next: DesiredRuntimeState): void {
    const previous = this.previous;

    if (!next.session) {
      this.previous = next;
      return;
    }

    if (!previous?.session || worldKey(previous.session) !== worldKey(next.session)) {
      this.runtime.loadRoute(next.session);
    } else {
      if (previous.session.startStationIndex !== next.session.startStationIndex) {
        this.runtime.setStartStation(next.session);
      }
      if (previous.session.mode !== next.session.mode) {
        this.runtime.setRideMode(next.session);
      }
    }

    if (previous?.presentation.season !== next.presentation.season) {
      this.runtime.setSeason(next.presentation.season);
    }
    if (previous?.presentation.time !== next.presentation.time) {
      this.runtime.setTime(next.presentation.time);
    }
    if (previous?.presentation.cameraMode !== next.presentation.cameraMode) {
      this.runtime.setCameraMode(next.presentation.cameraMode);
    }
    if (previous?.simulation.paused !== next.simulation.paused) {
      this.runtime.setPaused(next.simulation.paused);
    }

    this.previous = next;
  }
}

--- engine/runtime/RuntimeMachine.ts ---
/**
 * Runtime lifecycle as an explicit state machine.
 *
 * Replaces the ad-hoc `loaded` / `disposed` / terminal-timer booleans that used
 * to be scattered across the runtime. Pure and framework-free so it can be unit
 * tested without React or Three.js. Invalid transitions are ignored (the
 * reducer returns the same reference), which is what guards against e.g. a
 * terminal turnaround firing twice.
 */
export type RuntimeState =
  | { value: 'idle' }
  | { value: 'loading-engine' }
  | { value: 'ready' }
  | { value: 'loading-world'; sessionKey: string }
  | { value: 'running' }
  | { value: 'paused' }
  | { value: 'terminal-dwell'; enteredAt: number }
  | { value: 'failed'; error: Error }
  | { value: 'disposed' };

export type RuntimeStateValue = RuntimeState['value'];

export type RuntimeEvent =
  | { type: 'LOAD_ENGINE' }
  | { type: 'ENGINE_READY' }
  | { type: 'LOAD_WORLD'; sessionKey: string }
  | { type: 'WORLD_READY' }
  | { type: 'PAUSE' }
  | { type: 'RESUME' }
  | { type: 'TERMINAL_REACHED'; at: number }
  | { type: 'TURNAROUND_COMPLETE' }
  | { type: 'FAIL'; error: Error }
  | { type: 'DISPOSE' };

export function runtimeInitialState(): RuntimeState {
  return { value: 'idle' };
}

/**
 * Pure transition function. Returns the *same* state reference when a transition
 * is not allowed so callers can cheaply detect ignored (invalid) events.
 */
export function runtimeTransition(state: RuntimeState, event: RuntimeEvent): RuntimeState {
  // Terminal states accept nothing further.
  if (state.value === 'disposed') return state;

  // DISPOSE and FAIL are legal from any non-terminal state.
  if (event.type === 'DISPOSE') return { value: 'disposed' };
  if (event.type === 'FAIL') return { value: 'failed', error: event.error };

  switch (event.type) {
    case 'LOAD_ENGINE':
      return state.value === 'idle' ? { value: 'loading-engine' } : state;
    case 'ENGINE_READY':
      return state.value === 'loading-engine' ? { value: 'ready' } : state;
    case 'LOAD_WORLD':
      return state.value === 'ready' ||
        state.value === 'running' ||
        state.value === 'paused' ||
        state.value === 'terminal-dwell'
        ? { value: 'loading-world', sessionKey: event.sessionKey }
        : state;
    case 'WORLD_READY':
      return state.value === 'loading-world' ? { value: 'running' } : state;
    case 'PAUSE':
      return state.value === 'running' ? { value: 'paused' } : state;
    case 'RESUME':
      return state.value === 'paused' ? { value: 'running' } : state;
    case 'TERMINAL_REACHED':
      // Only reachable once from 'running'; ignored while already dwelling so the
      // turnaround cannot fire twice.
      return state.value === 'running' ? { value: 'terminal-dwell', enteredAt: event.at } : state;
    case 'TURNAROUND_COMPLETE':
      return state.value === 'terminal-dwell' ? { value: 'running' } : state;
    default:
      return state;
  }
}

/** Thin stateful wrapper around {@link runtimeTransition} with change notification. */
export class RuntimeMachine {
  private state: RuntimeState = runtimeInitialState();

  constructor(private readonly onChange?: (state: RuntimeState) => void) {}

  get current(): RuntimeState {
    return this.state;
  }

  matches(value: RuntimeStateValue): boolean {
    return this.state.value === value;
  }

  /** True once the engine has finished loading and is not failed/disposed. */
  get engineReady(): boolean {
    return (
      this.state.value === 'ready' ||
      this.state.value === 'loading-world' ||
      this.state.value === 'running' ||
      this.state.value === 'paused' ||
      this.state.value === 'terminal-dwell'
    );
  }

  /** Applies an event; notifies subscribers only when the state actually changed. */
  send(event: RuntimeEvent): RuntimeState {
    const next = runtimeTransition(this.state, event);
    if (next !== this.state) {
      this.state = next;
      this.onChange?.(next);
    }
    return this.state;
  }
}

--- engine/runtime/InputState.ts ---
/**
 * Transient driver/look input, owned by the engine rather than React.
 *
 * Holding a control (throttle, brake, look) must never travel through React
 * state, otherwise every held frame would re-render the shell. The runtime owns
 * one instance and forwards changes to the simulator/world via {@link onChange}.
 *
 * This class has no React or Three.js dependency and is unit-tested directly.
 */
export type InputStateListener = (input: InputState) => void;

export class InputState {
  throttle = 0;
  brake = 0;
  lookX: -1 | 0 | 1 = 0;

  private listener: InputStateListener | null = null;

  /** Registers (or clears) the sink that applies input to the simulator/world. */
  onChange(listener: InputStateListener | null): void {
    this.listener = listener;
  }

  setThrottle(value: number): void {
    this.throttle = clamp01(value);
    this.emit();
  }

  setBrake(value: number): void {
    this.brake = clamp01(value);
    this.emit();
  }

  setLook(left: boolean, right: boolean): void {
    // Opposing or matching keys cancel out; otherwise -1 looks left, +1 right.
    this.lookX = left === right ? 0 : left ? -1 : 1;
    this.emit();
  }

  reset(): void {
    this.throttle = 0;
    this.brake = 0;
    this.lookX = 0;
    this.emit();
  }

  private emit(): void {
    this.listener?.(this);
  }
}

function clamp01(value: number): number {
  return value < 0 ? 0 : value > 1 ? 1 : value;
}

--- engine/runtime/RuntimeMachine.test.ts ---
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import {
  RuntimeMachine,
  runtimeInitialState,
  runtimeTransition,
  type RuntimeState
} from './RuntimeMachine';

const engineReady = (): RuntimeState => {
  let s = runtimeInitialState();
  s = runtimeTransition(s, { type: 'LOAD_ENGINE' });
  s = runtimeTransition(s, { type: 'ENGINE_READY' });
  return s;
};

const running = (): RuntimeState => {
  let s = runtimeTransition(engineReady(), { type: 'LOAD_WORLD', sessionKey: 'a' });
  s = runtimeTransition(s, { type: 'WORLD_READY' });
  return s;
};

describe('runtimeTransition — valid transitions', () => {
  it('walks idle → loading-engine → ready → loading-world → running', () => {
    let s = runtimeInitialState();
    expect(s.value).toBe('idle');
    s = runtimeTransition(s, { type: 'LOAD_ENGINE' });
    expect(s.value).toBe('loading-engine');
    s = runtimeTransition(s, { type: 'ENGINE_READY' });
    expect(s.value).toBe('ready');
    s = runtimeTransition(s, { type: 'LOAD_WORLD', sessionKey: 'k' });
    expect(s).toEqual({ value: 'loading-world', sessionKey: 'k' });
    s = runtimeTransition(s, { type: 'WORLD_READY' });
    expect(s.value).toBe('running');
  });

  it('pauses and resumes', () => {
    let s = running();
    s = runtimeTransition(s, { type: 'PAUSE' });
    expect(s.value).toBe('paused');
    s = runtimeTransition(s, { type: 'RESUME' });
    expect(s.value).toBe('running');
  });

  it('reloads the world from running/paused/terminal-dwell', () => {
    expect(runtimeTransition(running(), { type: 'LOAD_WORLD', sessionKey: 'b' }).value).toBe('loading-world');
    const paused = runtimeTransition(running(), { type: 'PAUSE' });
    expect(runtimeTransition(paused, { type: 'LOAD_WORLD', sessionKey: 'b' }).value).toBe('loading-world');
  });
});

describe('runtimeTransition — invalid transitions are ignored', () => {
  it('ignores ENGINE_READY before LOAD_ENGINE', () => {
    const s = runtimeInitialState();
    expect(runtimeTransition(s, { type: 'ENGINE_READY' })).toBe(s);
  });

  it('ignores PAUSE unless running', () => {
    const ready = engineReady();
    expect(runtimeTransition(ready, { type: 'PAUSE' })).toBe(ready);
  });

  it('ignores RESUME unless paused', () => {
    const r = running();
    expect(runtimeTransition(r, { type: 'RESUME' })).toBe(r);
  });
});

describe('runtimeTransition — terminal dwell fires once', () => {
  it('enters terminal-dwell from running and ignores repeats', () => {
    const r = running();
    const dwell = runtimeTransition(r, { type: 'TERMINAL_REACHED', at: 100 });
    expect(dwell).toEqual({ value: 'terminal-dwell', enteredAt: 100 });
    // A second TERMINAL_REACHED while dwelling is ignored (no double turnaround).
    const again = runtimeTransition(dwell, { type: 'TERMINAL_REACHED', at: 200 });
    expect(again).toBe(dwell);
  });

  it('returns to running on TURNAROUND_COMPLETE', () => {
    const dwell = runtimeTransition(running(), { type: 'TERMINAL_REACHED', at: 1 });
    expect(runtimeTransition(dwell, { type: 'TURNAROUND_COMPLETE' }).value).toBe('running');
  });
});

describe('runtimeTransition — failure and disposal', () => {
  const states: RuntimeState[] = [
    runtimeInitialState(),
    runtimeTransition(runtimeInitialState(), { type: 'LOAD_ENGINE' }),
    engineReady(),
    running(),
    runtimeTransition(running(), { type: 'PAUSE' })
  ];

  it('can fail from any non-disposed state', () => {
    for (const s of states) {
      const failed = runtimeTransition(s, { type: 'FAIL', error: new Error('x') });
      expect(failed.value).toBe('failed');
    }
  });

  it('can dispose from any state', () => {
    for (const s of states) {
      expect(runtimeTransition(s, { type: 'DISPOSE' }).value).toBe('disposed');
    }
  });

  it('disposed is terminal — accepts nothing further', () => {
    const disposed = runtimeTransition(running(), { type: 'DISPOSE' });
    expect(runtimeTransition(disposed, { type: 'LOAD_ENGINE' })).toBe(disposed);
    expect(runtimeTransition(disposed, { type: 'FAIL', error: new Error('y') })).toBe(disposed);
  });
});

describe('RuntimeMachine wrapper', () => {
  it('notifies only on real changes', () => {
    const changes: RuntimeState[] = [];
    const m = new RuntimeMachine((s) => changes.push(s));
    m.send({ type: 'ENGINE_READY' }); // invalid from idle → no change
    m.send({ type: 'LOAD_ENGINE' });
    m.send({ type: 'ENGINE_READY' });
    expect(changes.map((s) => s.value)).toEqual(['loading-engine', 'ready']);
  });

  it('reports engineReady correctly', () => {
    const m = new RuntimeMachine();
    expect(m.engineReady).toBe(false);
    m.send({ type: 'LOAD_ENGINE' });
    expect(m.engineReady).toBe(false);
    m.send({ type: 'ENGINE_READY' });
    expect(m.engineReady).toBe(true);
    m.send({ type: 'DISPOSE' });
    expect(m.engineReady).toBe(false);
  });
});

--- engine/world/WorldLayer.ts ---
import type * as THREE from 'three';

/**
 * A self-contained piece of the world (terrain, guideway, stations, landmarks,
 * opposing traffic, …).
 *
 * Contract — each layer:
 *   1. owns exactly one {@link THREE.Group} (`root`);
 *   2. adds all of its meshes beneath that group (never straight to the scene);
 *   3. owns its geometries, materials, textures, and listeners;
 *   4. removes its root and releases resources on {@link dispose};
 *   5. aborts unfinished async loading when the provided signal fires.
 *
 * The raw scene is intentionally not handed to layers; the world adds each
 * layer's `root` once.
 */
export interface WorldLayer {
  readonly root: THREE.Group;

  build(signal: AbortSignal): void | Promise<void>;
  setVisible(visible: boolean): void;
  dispose(): void;
}

--- engine/cameras/CameraRig.ts ---
import type * as THREE from 'three';
import type { CameraMode, CameraUpdateInput } from '../../world/CameraController';

/** A read-only camera target for the active vehicle at the current frame. */
export type CameraPose = {
  position: THREE.Vector3;
  target: THREE.Vector3;
  up: THREE.Vector3;
};

/** Per-frame follow input a rig reads (never the full world). */
export type CameraRigContext = CameraUpdateInput;

/**
 * Computes the desired camera pose for one view (chase, passenger, cab, …).
 *
 * The shared per-frame smoothing (position lerp, side-look yaw) lives in the
 * controller and reads the rig's own smoothing constants, so each mode keeps its
 * exact feel while new rigs can be registered without touching existing ones.
 */
export interface CameraRig {
  readonly id: CameraMode;
  /** Lerp factor applied to the camera position toward the pose. */
  readonly positionSmoothing: number;
  /** Lerp factor for the side-look yaw. */
  readonly lookYawFactor: number;
  /** Maximum side-look yaw (radians) at full look input. */
  readonly lookYawAmount: number;

  pose(input: CameraRigContext): CameraPose;
}

--- engine/cameras/CabCameraRig.ts ---
import * as THREE from 'three';
import type { CameraMode } from '../../world/CameraController';
import type { CameraPose, CameraRig, CameraRigContext } from './CameraRig';

/** Driver cab view: just behind the vehicle nose looking far forward. */
export class CabCameraRig implements CameraRig {
  readonly id: CameraMode = 'cab';
  readonly positionSmoothing = 0.32;
  readonly lookYawFactor = 0.12;
  readonly lookYawAmount = Math.PI * 0.34;

  pose(input: CameraRigContext): CameraPose {
    return {
      position: input.position.clone()
        .add(input.tangent.clone().multiplyScalar(input.trainHalfLengthM - 1.75)) // just behind the vehicle nose.
        .add(new THREE.Vector3(0, 2.65, 0)), // cab eye height.
      target: input.position.clone()
        .add(input.tangent.clone().multiplyScalar(120)) // long forward target for driving view.
        .add(new THREE.Vector3(0, 2.2, 0)), // slightly lower than cab eye height.
      up: new THREE.Vector3(0, 1, 0)
    };
  }
}

--- engine/cameras/ChaseCameraRig.ts ---
import * as THREE from 'three';
import type { CameraMode } from '../../world/CameraController';
import type { CameraPose, CameraRig, CameraRigContext } from './CameraRig';

/**
 * Exterior chase camera, with a lower/closer variant while underground so the
 * camera stays inside tunnel headroom. Chase gets a gentler side-look and
 * smoother follow than interior views.
 */
export class ChaseCameraRig implements CameraRig {
  readonly id: CameraMode = 'chase';
  readonly positionSmoothing = 0.12;
  readonly lookYawFactor = 0.18;
  readonly lookYawAmount = Math.PI * 0.18;

  pose(input: CameraRigContext): CameraPose {
    const up = new THREE.Vector3(0, 1, 0);
    if (input.underground) {
      return {
        position: input.position.clone()
          .add(input.tangent.clone().multiplyScalar(-(input.trainHalfLengthM + 22))) // closer chase camera in tunnels.
          .add(new THREE.Vector3(0, 4.6, 0)), // low ceiling-safe camera height.
        target: input.position.clone()
          .add(input.tangent.clone().multiplyScalar(12)) // look slightly ahead of train.
          .add(new THREE.Vector3(0, 1.8, 0)), // target near vehicle body height.
        up
      };
    }
    return {
      position: input.position.clone()
        .add(input.tangent.clone().multiplyScalar(-(input.trainHalfLengthM + 14))) // exterior chase distance behind train.
        .add(new THREE.Vector3(0, 16, 0)), // elevated outdoor chase height.
      target: input.position.clone().add(new THREE.Vector3(0, 2, 0)), // target near vehicle center mass.
      up
    };
  }
}

--- engine/cameras/PassengerCameraRig.ts ---
import * as THREE from 'three';
import type { CameraMode } from '../../world/CameraController';
import type { CameraPose, CameraRig, CameraRigContext } from './CameraRig';

/**
 * Passenger interior view. For vehicles with a detailed interior (bus/s-bahn/
 * tram profiles) the camera anchors to a car mesh so articulated vehicles turn
 * correctly; otherwise it falls back to a seated eye-height position.
 */
export class PassengerCameraRig implements CameraRig {
  readonly id: CameraMode = 'passenger';
  readonly positionSmoothing = 0.32;
  readonly lookYawFactor = 0.12;
  readonly lookYawAmount = Math.PI * 0.34;

  pose(input: CameraRigContext): CameraPose {
    const normal = new THREE.Vector3(input.tangent.z, 0, -input.tangent.x).normalize();
    const anchoredInterior =
      input.interiorCameraProfile === 'bus' ||
      input.interiorCameraProfile === 's-bahn' ||
      input.interiorCameraProfile === 'tram';

    if (anchoredInterior) {
      // Anchor interior cameras to a car mesh so articulated vehicles turn correctly.
      input.train.updateMatrixWorld(true);

      const midCar = input.trainCars[2] ?? input.trainCars[Math.floor(input.trainCars.length / 2)] ?? input.trainCars[0];
      const passengerCar = input.interiorCameraProfile === 's-bahn'
        ? midCar // middle S-Bahn car gives a stable passenger view.
        : input.interiorCameraProfile === 'tram'
          ? midCar // middle tram section avoids front cab obstruction; falls back if fewer than 3 cars.
          : input.trainCars[0]; // bus has one body group.

      const customCameraLocal = passengerCar.userData.passengerCameraLocal as [number, number, number] | undefined;
      const customLookAtLocal = passengerCar.userData.passengerLookAtLocal as [number, number, number] | undefined;
      const localZ = input.interiorCameraProfile === 's-bahn'
        ? -2 // S-Bahn passenger seat position within selected car.
        : input.interiorCameraProfile === 'tram'
          ? -0.6 // tram interior camera near articulated center section.
          : -4.4; // bus passenger camera further back from the driver area.

      const position = passengerCar.localToWorld(new THREE.Vector3(
        customCameraLocal?.[0] ?? 0.16,
        customCameraLocal?.[1] ?? 2.3,
        customCameraLocal?.[2] ?? localZ
      ));
      const target = passengerCar.localToWorld(new THREE.Vector3(
        customLookAtLocal?.[0] ?? 0,
        customLookAtLocal?.[1] ?? 2.25,
        customLookAtLocal?.[2] ?? (input.interiorCameraProfile === 'tram' ? 26 : 70)
      ));
      const up = new THREE.Vector3(0, 1, 0)
        .applyQuaternion(passengerCar.getWorldQuaternion(new THREE.Quaternion()))
        .normalize();
      return { position, target, up };
    }

    // Fallback passenger camera for vehicles without a detailed anchored interior.
    return {
      position: input.position.clone()
        .add(input.tangent.clone().multiplyScalar(input.passengerOffsetM))
        .add(normal.clone().multiplyScalar(0.08)) // tiny side offset to avoid exact centerline.
        .add(new THREE.Vector3(0, 2.3, 0)), // passenger eye height.
      target: input.position.clone()
        .add(input.tangent.clone().multiplyScalar(85)) // far forward look target.
        .add(normal.clone().multiplyScalar(1.5)) // small side bias so the view feels seated.
        .add(new THREE.Vector3(0, 2.25, 0)), // passenger eye-level target.
      up: new THREE.Vector3(0, 1, 0)
    };
  }
}

--- engine/systems/LightingSystem.ts ---
import * as THREE from 'three';
import type { Season } from '../../app/types';
import { LightingController } from '../../world/LightingController';
import type { EngineSystem } from '../core/EngineSystem';

/** Per-season sky tint and fog distances. */
const SKY: Record<Season | 'default', { color: number; near: number; far: number }> = {
  default: { color: 0xaac8df, near: 900, far: 2800 },
  spring: { color: 0xb9d6ed, near: 900, far: 2800 },
  summer: { color: 0xa7d3ff, near: 1100, far: 3200 },
  autumn: { color: 0xcab38f, near: 760, far: 2300 },
  winter: { color: 0xd8e5ee, near: 620, far: 1800 }
};

/**
 * Owns sun/ambient/hemisphere lighting, time-of-day transitions, season-based
 * lighting, and the sky/fog presentation. Seasonal decorative props
 * (leaves/snow) are scene decorations and remain with the world/EnvironmentSystem.
 */
export class LightingSystem implements EngineSystem {
  private readonly hemisphere = new THREE.HemisphereLight();
  private readonly sun = new THREE.DirectionalLight();
  private readonly visibilityFill = new THREE.AmbientLight();
  private readonly controller: LightingController;

  constructor(private readonly scene: THREE.Scene, renderer: THREE.WebGLRenderer) {
    this.controller = new LightingController(scene, renderer, this.hemisphere, this.sun, this.visibilityFill);
  }

  /** Adds lights and applies the default sky before a season is chosen. */
  initialize(): void {
    this.applySky('default');
    this.controller.initialize();
  }

  setSeason(season: Season): void {
    this.applySky(season);
    this.controller.setSeason(season);
  }

  setTimeOfDay(hour: number): void {
    this.controller.setTimeOfDay(hour);
  }

  /** 0 (night) … 1 (full day); used to switch vehicle exterior lamps. */
  daylightFactor(): number {
    return this.controller.daylightFactor();
  }

  private applySky(key: Season | 'default'): void {
    const sky = SKY[key];
    this.scene.background = new THREE.Color(sky.color);
    this.scene.fog = new THREE.Fog(sky.color, sky.near, sky.far);
  }

  dispose(): void {
    this.scene.remove(this.hemisphere, this.sun, this.visibilityFill);
    this.sun.shadow.map?.dispose();
    this.hemisphere.dispose();
    this.sun.dispose();
    this.visibilityFill.dispose();
  }
}

--- engine/systems/CameraSystem.ts ---
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { CameraController, type CameraMode } from '../../world/CameraController';
import type { CameraRig } from '../cameras/CameraRig';
import type { EngineSystem } from '../core/EngineSystem';

/** Per-frame follow input for the active vehicle (mirrors CameraController.update). */
export type CameraFollowInput = Parameters<CameraController['update']>[0];

/**
 * Owns the perspective camera and its chase/passenger/cab rig, plus zoom, side
 * look, viewport resizing, and pose smoothing.
 *
 * It deliberately does NOT own route simulation, vehicle creation, or the
 * cinematic (photo) camera — those belong to ActiveVehicleSystem and
 * PhotoSystem. The world feeds it a follow input each frame.
 */
export class CameraSystem implements EngineSystem {
  readonly camera: THREE.PerspectiveCamera;
  private readonly controller: CameraController;
  private readonly onResizeBound = () => this.resize();

  constructor() {
    this.camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(62, window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight, 0.1, 5000);
    this.controller = new CameraController(this.camera);
    window.addEventListener('resize', this.onResizeBound);
  }

  /**
   * Places the camera just behind the route start so cities with a regional
   * coordinate origin don't fly in over empty terrain on the first frame.
   */
  placeAtStart(startPoint: THREE.Vector3, startTangent: THREE.Vector3): void {
    this.camera.position.set(
      startPoint.x - startTangent.x * 60,
      startPoint.y + 16,
      startPoint.z - startTangent.z * 60
    );
  }

  /** Smooths the camera toward the active-vehicle pose for the current mode. */
  follow(input: CameraFollowInput): void {
    this.controller.update(input);
  }

  /** Registers a new camera rig; existing rigs and this class stay untouched. */
  registerRig(rig: CameraRig): void {
    this.controller.registerRig(rig);
  }

  cycle(): CameraMode {
    return this.controller.cycle();
  }

  setMode(mode: CameraMode): CameraMode {
    return this.controller.setMode(mode);
  }

  currentMode(): CameraMode {
    return this.controller.currentMode();
  }

  setLookInput(leftHeld: boolean, rightHeld: boolean): void {
    this.controller.setLookInput(leftHeld, rightHeld);
  }

  adjustZoom(delta: number): void {
    this.controller.adjustZoom(delta);
  }

  private resize(): void {
    this.camera.aspect = window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight;
    this.camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
  }

  dispose(): void {
    window.removeEventListener('resize', this.onResizeBound);
  }
}

--- engine/systems/EnvironmentSystem.ts ---
import * as THREE from 'three';
import type { DemoRoute } from '../../route';
import type { CityDefinition } from '../../cities';
import type { GuidewayKind } from '../../domain/transport';
import type { GeoPoint, LandmarkZone } from '../../landmarks';
import { TerrainSampler } from '../../terrain';
import { OsmSceneStreamer } from '../../osm-scene';
import { LandmarkLayer } from '../../world/layers/LandmarkLayer';
import type { WorldLayerContext } from '../../world/layers/WorldLayerContext';
import type { EngineSystem } from '../core/EngineSystem';

export type EnvironmentContext = {
  scene: THREE.Scene;
  route: DemoRoute;
  city?: CityDefinition;
  guidewayKind: GuidewayKind;
  routeGeo: GeoPoint[];
  projectGeo(lat: number, lon: number): THREE.Vector3;
  surfaceHeightAt(t: number): number;
  nearestSelectedGeoPoint(lat: number, lon: number, points: GeoPoint[]): { distanceM: number; t: number };
};

/**
 * Owns terrain sampling, curated landmarks, streamed OSM scenery, and the
 * ground/underground visibility of those layers.
 *
 * Built in two phases to preserve the world's original construction order:
 * the constructor creates terrain + landmarks (which the static layers may need
 * to sample against); {@link buildScenery} later starts OSM streaming (or a
 * static landmark layer) once the ground mesh exists. The scenery-height blend
 * that keeps streamed buildings sitting on the route corridor lives here too.
 */
export class EnvironmentSystem implements EngineSystem {
  private readonly terrain: TerrainSampler | null;
  private readonly landmarkGroup: THREE.Group;
  private readonly landmarkExclusions: LandmarkZone[];
  private osmScene: OsmSceneStreamer | null = null;
  private ground: THREE.Mesh | null = null;

  constructor(private readonly ctx: EnvironmentContext) {
    this.terrain = ctx.route.elevationDatumM !== undefined ? new TerrainSampler(ctx.route.elevationDatumM) : null;
    const landmarkLayer = ctx.city?.landmarkFactory?.(
      ctx.routeGeo,
      (lat, lon) => ctx.projectGeo(lat, lon),
      (lat, lon) => this.terrain?.sample(lat, lon) ?? Promise.resolve(0)
    );
    this.landmarkGroup = landmarkLayer?.group ?? new THREE.Group();
    this.landmarkExclusions = landmarkLayer?.zones ?? [];
    ctx.scene.add(this.landmarkGroup);
  }

  /**
   * Starts OSM building streaming (or a static landmark layer for routes without
   * geo points), remembering the ground mesh for underground toggling.
   *
   * Ferry routes are on open water — streaming land buildings from surrounding
   * hills at their real elevation produces floating boxes, so they are skipped.
   */
  buildScenery(layerContext: WorldLayerContext, ground: THREE.Mesh | null, initialGeoPoint: GeoPoint | undefined): void {
    this.ground = ground;
    const osmEnabled = this.ctx.guidewayKind !== 'ferry' && this.ctx.routeGeo.length >= 2;
    if (osmEnabled) {
      this.osmScene = new OsmSceneStreamer(
        this.ctx.scene,
        (lat, lon) => this.ctx.projectGeo(lat, lon),
        (lat, lon) => this.scenerySurfaceHeight(lat, lon),
        this.landmarkExclusions
      );
      this.osmScene.update(initialGeoPoint);
    } else if (this.ctx.guidewayKind !== 'ferry') {
      const landmarkLayer = new LandmarkLayer(layerContext);
      landmarkLayer.build();
      this.ctx.scene.add(landmarkLayer.root);
    }
  }

  /** Streams new tiles around the current position (no-op when OSM is disabled). */
  updateStreaming(geoPoint: GeoPoint | undefined): void {
    this.osmScene?.update(geoPoint);
  }

  /** Hides ground/scenery/landmarks while the route is in a tunnel. */
  setUnderground(underground: boolean): void {
    if (this.ground) this.ground.visible = !underground;
    if (this.osmScene) this.osmScene.group.visible = !underground;
    this.landmarkGroup.visible = !underground;
  }

  /**
   * Blends sampled terrain toward the route's own surface height near the
   * corridor so streamed buildings meet the track instead of floating/sinking.
   */
  private async scenerySurfaceHeight(lat: number, lon: number): Promise<number> {
    const sampledHeight = await (this.terrain?.sample(lat, lon) ?? Promise.resolve(0));
    if (this.ctx.routeGeo.length < 2) return sampledHeight;
    const nearest = this.ctx.nearestSelectedGeoPoint(lat, lon, this.ctx.routeGeo);
    const innerCorridorM = this.ctx.guidewayKind === 's-bahn' ? 28 : this.ctx.guidewayKind === 'bus' ? 20 : 12;
    const outerCorridorM = this.ctx.guidewayKind === 's-bahn' ? 110 : this.ctx.guidewayKind === 'bus' ? 80 : 52;
    if (nearest.distanceM >= outerCorridorM) return sampledHeight;
    const routeSurfaceHeight = this.ctx.surfaceHeightAt(nearest.t);
    const blend = THREE.MathUtils.smoothstep(nearest.distanceM, innerCorridorM, outerCorridorM);
    return THREE.MathUtils.lerp(routeSurfaceHeight, sampledHeight, blend);
  }

  dispose(): void {
    this.osmScene?.dispose();
    this.osmScene = null;
    this.ground = null;
  }
}

--- engine/systems/PhotoSystem.ts ---
import * as THREE from 'three';
import type { EngineSystem } from '../core/EngineSystem';

export type CinematicPreset =
  | 'platform-fly-by'
  | 'front-tracking-shot'
  | 'side-tracking-shot'
  | 'high-drone-shot'
  | 'cab-approach'
  | 'station-departure';

const CINEMATIC_PRESETS: Array<{ id: CinematicPreset; label: string }> = [
  { id: 'platform-fly-by', label: 'Platform fly-by' },
  { id: 'front-tracking-shot', label: 'Front tracking shot' },
  { id: 'side-tracking-shot', label: 'Side tracking shot' },
  { id: 'high-drone-shot', label: 'High drone shot' },
  { id: 'cab-approach', label: 'Cab approach' },
  { id: 'station-departure', label: 'Station departure' }
];

/** Everything the cinematic camera needs per frame, supplied by the world. */
export type CinematicCameraContext = {
  camera: THREE.PerspectiveCamera;
  distanceM: number;
  routeLengthM: number;
  trainPosition: THREE.Vector3;
  tangent: THREE.Vector3;
  trainHalfLengthM: number;
  activeLaneOffsetM: number;
  normalAt(t: number): THREE.Vector3;
  nearestStations(distanceM: number): { previous?: { distanceM: number }; next?: { distanceM: number } };
  routePointAtDistance(distanceM: number, laneOffsetM: number): THREE.Vector3;
  routeTangentAtDistance(distanceM: number): THREE.Vector3;
};

/**
 * Owns the cinematic (photo) presentation: enter/exit, preset cycling, the
 * preset label, and the smoothed cinematic camera positioning. It drives the
 * camera the CameraSystem owns; it does not create its own.
 */
export class PhotoSystem implements EngineSystem {
  private active = false;
  private presetIndex = 0;

  setActive(active: boolean): { active: boolean; label: string } {
    this.active = active;
    return { active: this.active, label: this.currentLabel() };
  }

  toggle(): { active: boolean; label: string } {
    return this.setActive(!this.active);
  }

  isActive(): boolean {
    return this.active;
  }

  cyclePreset(): { preset: CinematicPreset; label: string } {
    this.presetIndex = (this.presetIndex + 1) % CINEMATIC_PRESETS.length;
    return { preset: this.currentPreset(), label: this.currentLabel() };
  }

  currentPreset(): CinematicPreset {
    return CINEMATIC_PRESETS[this.presetIndex]?.id ?? 'platform-fly-by';
  }

  currentLabel(): string {
    return CINEMATIC_PRESETS[this.presetIndex]?.label ?? 'Platform fly-by';
  }

  /** Smooths the camera toward the current cinematic preset pose. */
  updateCamera(ctx: CinematicCameraContext): void {
    const { camera, trainPosition, tangent, routeLengthM, distanceM } = ctx;
    const routeT = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(distanceM / routeLengthM, 0, 1);
    const normal = ctx.normalAt(routeT);
    const halfLength = ctx.trainHalfLengthM;
    const { previous, next } = ctx.nearestStations(distanceM);
    let cameraPosition = camera.position.clone();
    let lookAt = trainPosition.clone().add(new THREE.Vector3(0, 2.5, 0));

    switch (this.currentPreset()) {
      case 'platform-fly-by': {
        const stationDistanceM = next?.distanceM ?? Math.min(routeLengthM, distanceM + 120);
        const anchor = ctx.routePointAtDistance(stationDistanceM, ctx.activeLaneOffsetM);
        const anchorTangent = ctx.routeTangentAtDistance(stationDistanceM);
        const anchorNormal = new THREE.Vector3(anchorTangent.z, 0, -anchorTangent.x).normalize();
        cameraPosition = anchor
          .add(anchorNormal.multiplyScalar(12))
          .add(anchorTangent.multiplyScalar(-14))
          .add(new THREE.Vector3(0, 3.2, 0));
        break;
      }
      case 'front-tracking-shot':
        cameraPosition = trainPosition.clone()
          .add(tangent.clone().multiplyScalar(halfLength + 22))
          .add(normal.clone().multiplyScalar(1.8))
          .add(new THREE.Vector3(0, 4.8, 0));
        lookAt = trainPosition.clone().add(tangent.clone().multiplyScalar(halfLength * 0.2)).add(new THREE.Vector3(0, 2.5, 0));
        break;
      case 'side-tracking-shot':
        cameraPosition = trainPosition.clone()
          .add(normal.clone().multiplyScalar(14))
          .add(tangent.clone().multiplyScalar(-3.5))
          .add(new THREE.Vector3(0, 5.4, 0));
        break;
      case 'high-drone-shot':
        cameraPosition = trainPosition.clone()
          .add(normal.clone().multiplyScalar(10))
          .add(tangent.clone().multiplyScalar(-16))
          .add(new THREE.Vector3(0, 52, 0));
        lookAt = trainPosition.clone().add(new THREE.Vector3(0, 3.5, 0));
        break;
      case 'cab-approach':
        cameraPosition = trainPosition.clone()
          .add(tangent.clone().multiplyScalar(halfLength + 34))
          .add(normal.clone().multiplyScalar(0.8))
          .add(new THREE.Vector3(0, 3.4, 0));
        lookAt = trainPosition.clone().add(tangent.clone().multiplyScalar(halfLength * 0.3)).add(new THREE.Vector3(0, 2.6, 0));
        break;
      case 'station-departure': {
        const stationDistanceM = previous?.distanceM ?? Math.max(0, distanceM - 90);
        const anchor = ctx.routePointAtDistance(stationDistanceM, ctx.activeLaneOffsetM);
        const anchorTangent = ctx.routeTangentAtDistance(stationDistanceM);
        const anchorNormal = new THREE.Vector3(anchorTangent.z, 0, -anchorTangent.x).normalize();
        cameraPosition = anchor
          .add(anchorNormal.multiplyScalar(12))
          .add(anchorTangent.multiplyScalar(-26))
          .add(new THREE.Vector3(0, 6.4, 0));
        break;
      }
    }

    camera.up.lerp(new THREE.Vector3(0, 1, 0), 0.4).normalize();
    camera.position.lerp(cameraPosition, 0.11);
    camera.lookAt(lookAt);
  }

  dispose(): void {
    this.active = false;
  }
}

--- engine/systems/ActiveVehicleSystem.ts ---
import * as THREE from 'three';
import type { DemoRoute } from '../../route';
import type { CityDefinition } from '../../cities';
import { fallbackTransportModeDefinition, type TransportModeDefinition } from '../../domain/transport';
import type { CameraMode } from '../../world/CameraController';
import { resolveRouteVehicleDefinition } from '../../vehicles/VehicleDefinition';
import type { VehicleVisualController } from '../../vehicles/VehicleVisualController';
import { vehicleVisualFactory } from '../../vehicles/VehicleVisualFactory';
import type { EngineSystem } from '../core/EngineSystem';

/** Route-geometry placement helper owned by the world (also used by opposing traffic). */
export type ArticulatedPlacer = (
  root: THREE.Group,
  cars: THREE.Group[],
  distanceM: number,
  direction: 1 | -1,
  trackOffsetM: number
) => void;

export type ActiveVehicleContext = {
  scene: THREE.Scene;
  route: DemoRoute;
  city?: CityDefinition;
  modeDefinition?: TransportModeDefinition;
  activeLaneOffsetM: number;
  placeArticulated: ArticulatedPlacer;
};

/**
 * Owns the active {@link VehicleInstance}: builds it, places its articulated
 * sections along the route, animates doors, drives the interior destination
 * display, and toggles interior/cab visibility and exterior lamps. It exposes
 * the vehicle transform (root/cars/half-length) to the camera and photo systems.
 *
 * It does NOT own route positioning math, terrain, camera movement, rendering,
 * OSM streaming, or station building — placement math is supplied by the world.
 */
export class ActiveVehicleSystem implements EngineSystem {
  // Shared presentation (doors, interior display, visibility, lamps); placement
  // stays here because the simulator drives it by route distance.
  private readonly visual: VehicleVisualController;

  constructor(private readonly ctx: ActiveVehicleContext) {
    // Resolve the shared definition and build through the shared factory; the real
    // route/city/mode are still forwarded so route-specific builder reads (e.g.
    // funicular orientation) keep working.
    const definition = resolveRouteVehicleDefinition(ctx.route, ctx.city, ctx.modeDefinition ?? fallbackTransportModeDefinition);
    this.visual = vehicleVisualFactory.create(definition, {
      route: ctx.route,
      city: ctx.city,
      modeDefinition: ctx.modeDefinition
    });
    ctx.scene.add(this.visual.root);
    // Initial board from the real schedule (first stop), not just the destination.
    this.visual.updateInteriorDisplay(ctx.route.stations[1]?.name ?? ctx.route.destination, false);
  }

  /** The vehicle root group (identity of the active train in the scene). */
  get root(): THREE.Group {
    return this.visual.root;
  }

  get cars(): THREE.Group[] {
    return this.visual.cars;
  }

  get trainHalfLengthM(): number {
    return this.visual.trainHalfLengthM;
  }

  /** Places the articulated sections at the given route distance (forward). */
  place(distanceM: number): void {
    this.ctx.placeArticulated(this.visual.root, this.visual.cars, distanceM, 1, this.ctx.activeLaneOffsetM);
  }

  updateDoors(open: boolean, deltaSeconds: number): void {
    this.visual.updateDoors(open, deltaSeconds);
  }

  /** Applies interior/cab window transparency and shell visibility per view. */
  setPresentation(mode: CameraMode, cinematicActive: boolean): void {
    this.visual.setPresentation(mode, cinematicActive);
  }

  /** Head/tail lamps come on in low light (night, dawn, dusk). */
  updateExteriorLights(daylightFactor: number): void {
    this.visual.updateExteriorLights(daylightFactor);
  }

  updateInteriorDisplay(nextStation: string, doorsOpen: boolean): void {
    this.visual.updateInteriorDisplay(nextStation, doorsOpen);
  }

  dispose(): void {
    this.visual.dispose();
  }
}

--- domain/transport.ts ---
// City-specific transport mode id, kept as string so new cities can define custom modes.
export type TransportMode = string;

// Canonical set of transport-mode ids known across cities. Centralized here so
// URL parsing and other validators don't each hard-code their own list.
export const KNOWN_TRANSPORT_MODES = [
  'stadtbahn',
  'tram',
  'bus',
  's-bahn',
  'zacke',
  'seilbahn',
  'ferry',
  'rail',
  'trolleybus',
  'schwebebahn'
] as const;

const KNOWN_TRANSPORT_MODE_SET = new Set<string>(KNOWN_TRANSPORT_MODES);

// True when a raw string matches a known transport-mode id.
export function isKnownTransportMode(value: string): value is TransportMode {
  return KNOWN_TRANSPORT_MODE_SET.has(value);
}

// Vehicle renderer family used by TransitWorld.
export type VehicleKind = 'stadtbahn' | 'bus' | 's-bahn' | 'zacke' | 'seilbahn' | 'ferry' | 'schwebebahn';

// Guideway/track behavior used for route rendering and lane offsets.
export type GuidewayKind = 'stadtbahn' | 'bus' | 's-bahn' | 'zacke' | 'seilbahn' | 'ferry' | 'schwebebahn';

// Interior camera placement profile; not always one-to-one with vehicle kind.
export type InteriorCameraProfile = 'rail' | 'bus' | 's-bahn' | 'tram';

// Per-city mode definition connecting UI labels to vehicle, guideway, and camera behavior.
export type TransportModeDefinition = {
  id: TransportMode;
  label: string;
  available: boolean;
  vehicleFamily: string;
  vehicleKind: VehicleKind;
  guidewayKind: GuidewayKind;
  interiorCameraProfile: InteriorCameraProfile;
  passengerOffsetM?: number; // route-forward offset for fallback passenger camera placement
  lineAccentColor?: string; // CSS/canvas color used for vehicle displays and route accents
  vehicleDesignId?: string; // optional explicit vehicle registry id, overrides vehicleFamily/vehicleKind resolution
  stationStyleId?: string; // optional station style provider id (e.g. 'vvs-stadtbahn'); falls back to guideway station builders
};

// Default Stuttgart Stadtbahn behavior used when older route data has no transportMode.
export const fallbackTransportModeDefinition: TransportModeDefinition = {
  id: 'stadtbahn',
  label: 'Stadtbahn',
  available: true,
  vehicleFamily: 'Light rail vehicle',
  vehicleKind: 'stadtbahn',
  guidewayKind: 'stadtbahn',
  interiorCameraProfile: 'rail',
  passengerOffsetM: 4.2, // seated passenger camera offset in meters for rail fallback view
  lineAccentColor: '#ffd800', // SSB-like yellow accent
  stationStyleId: 'vvs-stadtbahn'
};

// Normalizes optional/legacy route mode values to a usable transport mode id.
export function routeTransportMode(value: string | undefined): TransportMode {
  return value?.trim() || fallbackTransportModeDefinition.id;
}

// Finds a city mode definition, falling back to a safe Stadtbahn-like definition with the requested id.
export function resolveTransportModeDefinition(
  mode: TransportMode,
  definitions: TransportModeDefinition[]
): TransportModeDefinition {
  return definitions.find((definition) => definition.id === mode) ?? {
    ...fallbackTransportModeDefinition,
    id: mode,
    label: mode
  };
}

// Returns the display label for a mode id.
export function transportModeLabel(mode: TransportMode, definitions: TransportModeDefinition[]): string {
  return resolveTransportModeDefinition(mode, definitions).label;
}

--- world.ts ---
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { makeLabelTexture } from './labels';
import type { DemoRoute } from './route';
import type { GuidewayKind, TransportModeDefinition } from './domain/transport';
import type { Season } from './app/types';
import type { CityDefinition } from './cities';
import type { CameraMode } from './world/CameraController';
import { CameraSystem } from './engine/systems/CameraSystem';
import { LightingSystem } from './engine/systems/LightingSystem';
import { PhotoSystem, type CinematicPreset } from './engine/systems/PhotoSystem';

export type { CinematicPreset };
import { ActiveVehicleSystem } from './engine/systems/ActiveVehicleSystem';
import { EnvironmentSystem } from './engine/systems/EnvironmentSystem';
import type { WorldLayerContext } from './world/layers/WorldLayerContext';
import { RouteLayer } from './world/layers/RouteLayer';
import { GuidewayLayer } from './world/layers/GuidewayLayer';
import { StationLayer } from './world/layers/StationLayer';
import { OpposingTrafficLayer } from './world/layers/OpposingTrafficLayer';
type GeoPoint = { lat: number; lon: number };

const EARTH_M_PER_DEG_LAT = 111_320;
const TRACK_GAUGE_M = 2.1;
const TRACK_CENTER_SPACING_M = 4.2;
const ACTIVE_TRACK_OFFSET_M = -TRACK_CENTER_SPACING_M / 2;
const OPPOSITE_TRACK_OFFSET_M = TRACK_CENTER_SPACING_M / 2;
export class TransitWorld {
  readonly scene = new THREE.Scene();
  // Camera + chase/passenger/cab rig, zoom, look, and resize are owned here.
  private readonly cameraSystem = new CameraSystem();
  readonly renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ antialias: true, preserveDrawingBuffer: true });
  readonly curve: THREE.CatmullRomCurve3;
  private readonly stationMarkers: THREE.Object3D[] = [];
  private readonly seasonalObjects = new THREE.Group();
  // Oncoming services rendered on the opposite track lane.
  private opposingLayer: OpposingTrafficLayer | null = null;
  // The active vehicle (placement, doors, interior display, visibility, lamps).
  private activeVehicle!: ActiveVehicleSystem;
  // Terrain sampling, curated landmarks, OSM streaming, and scenery visibility.
  private environment!: EnvironmentSystem;
  private readonly routeGeo: GeoPoint[];
  private readonly routeGeoDistancesM: number[];
  // Sun/ambient/hemisphere lighting, time-of-day, season lighting, sky + fog.
  private readonly lightingSystem = new LightingSystem(this.scene, this.renderer);
  private currentSeason: Season = 'summer';
  // Cinematic (photo) presentation: enter/exit, preset cycling, camera math.
  private readonly photoSystem = new PhotoSystem();
  // Renderer sizing stays with the world (it owns the renderer); CameraSystem
  // owns the camera aspect side of the resize.
  private readonly onRendererResizeBound = () => this.renderer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);

  /** The active camera, owned by CameraSystem; exposed for the engine renderer. */
  get camera(): THREE.PerspectiveCamera {
    return this.cameraSystem.camera;
  }

  constructor(
    curve: THREE.CatmullRomCurve3,
    private readonly route: DemoRoute,
    private readonly networkRoutes: DemoRoute[] = [route],
    private readonly city?: CityDefinition,
    private readonly modeDefinition?: TransportModeDefinition
  ) {
    this.curve = curve;
    this.routeGeo = (route.geoPoints ?? []).map(([lat, lon]) => ({ lat, lon }));
    this.routeGeoDistancesM = this.routeGeo.reduce<number[]>((distances, point, index) => {
      if (index === 0) return [0];
      const previous = this.routeGeo[index - 1];
      const lonScale = Math.cos((point.lat * Math.PI) / 180) * EARTH_M_PER_DEG_LAT;
      return [...distances, distances[index - 1] + Math.hypot(
        (point.lat - previous.lat) * EARTH_M_PER_DEG_LAT,
        (point.lon - previous.lon) * lonScale
      )];
    }, []);
    this.renderer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);
    this.renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio, 2));
    this.renderer.shadowMap.enabled = true;
    this.renderer.toneMapping = THREE.ACESFilmicToneMapping;
    this.renderer.setAnimationLoop(null);

    // Terrain sampling, curated landmarks, and OSM streaming/visibility.
    this.environment = new EnvironmentSystem({
      scene: this.scene,
      route: this.route,
      city: this.city,
      guidewayKind: this.guidewayKind,
      routeGeo: this.routeGeo,
      projectGeo: (lat, lon) => this.projectGeo(lat, lon),
      surfaceHeightAt: (t) => this.surfaceHeightAt(t),
      nearestSelectedGeoPoint: (lat, lon, points) => this.nearestSelectedGeoPoint(lat, lon, points)
    });

    // Place the camera near the route start so cities that use a regional
    // coordinate origin (e.g. Bodensee) don't produce a long fly-in over
    // empty terrain before the first frame.
    const startPoint = this.curve.getPointAt(0);
    const startTangent = this.curve.getTangentAt(0).normalize();
    this.cameraSystem.placeAtStart(startPoint, startTangent);
    this.lightingSystem.initialize();

    // Scene layers build their meshes from shared route geometry; TransitWorld
    // coordinates them and owns the vehicle, scenery streaming, and frame loop.
    const layerContext = this.createLayerContext();
    const routeLayer = new RouteLayer(layerContext);
    const ground = routeLayer.build();
    const guidewayLayer = new GuidewayLayer(layerContext);
    guidewayLayer.build();
    const stationLayer = new StationLayer(layerContext);
    stationLayer.build();
    this.scene.add(routeLayer.root, guidewayLayer.root, stationLayer.root);
    this.activeVehicle = new ActiveVehicleSystem({
      scene: this.scene,
      route: this.route,
      city: this.city,
      modeDefinition: this.modeDefinition,
      activeLaneOffsetM: this.activeLaneOffsetM,
      placeArticulated: (root, cars, distanceM, direction, trackOffsetM) =>
        this.updateArticulatedVehicle(root, cars, distanceM, direction, trackOffsetM)
    });
    this.activeVehicle.setPresentation(this.cameraSystem.currentMode(), this.photoSystem.isActive());
    this.activeVehicle.updateExteriorLights(this.lightingSystem.daylightFactor());
    this.opposingLayer = new OpposingTrafficLayer(layerContext);
    this.opposingLayer.build(this.activeVehicle.root);
    this.scene.add(this.opposingLayer.root);
    this.environment.buildScenery(layerContext, ground, this.geoPointAt(0));
    this.scene.add(this.seasonalObjects);

    window.addEventListener('resize', this.onRendererResizeBound);
  }

  mount(target: HTMLElement): void {
    target.appendChild(this.renderer.domElement);
  }

  setSeason(season: Season): void {
    this.currentSeason = season;
    this.lightingSystem.setSeason(season);
    // Decorative seasonal props are scene decorations owned by the world.
    this.seasonalObjects.clear();
    if (season === 'autumn') this.addLeaves();
    else if (season === 'winter') this.addSnow();
  }

  setTimeOfDay(hour: number): void {
    this.lightingSystem.setTimeOfDay(hour);
    this.activeVehicle.updateExteriorLights(this.lightingSystem.daylightFactor());
  }

  /** Reapplies interior/cab window + shell visibility for the current view. */
  private applyVehiclePresentation(): void {
    this.activeVehicle.setPresentation(this.cameraSystem.currentMode(), this.photoSystem.isActive());
  }

  cycleCamera(): CameraMode {
    this.cameraSystem.cycle();
    this.applyVehiclePresentation();
    return this.cameraSystem.currentMode();
  }

  currentCameraMode(): CameraMode {
    return this.cameraSystem.currentMode();
  }

  setCameraMode(mode: CameraMode): CameraMode {
    this.cameraSystem.setMode(mode);
    this.applyVehiclePresentation();
    return this.cameraSystem.currentMode();
  }

  setInteriorLookInput(leftHeld: boolean, rightHeld: boolean): void {
    this.cameraSystem.setLookInput(leftHeld, rightHeld);
  }

  adjustCameraZoom(delta: number): void {
    this.cameraSystem.adjustZoom(delta);
  }

  toggleCinematicMode(): { active: boolean; label: string } {
    const result = this.photoSystem.toggle();
    this.applyVehiclePresentation();
    return result;
  }

  setCinematicMode(active: boolean): { active: boolean; label: string } {
    const result = this.photoSystem.setActive(active);
    this.applyVehiclePresentation();
    return result;
  }

  cinematicModeActive(): boolean {
    return this.photoSystem.isActive();
  }

  cycleCinematicPreset(): { preset: CinematicPreset; label: string } {
    return this.photoSystem.cyclePreset();
  }

  currentCinematicPreset(): CinematicPreset {
    return this.photoSystem.currentPreset();
  }

  currentCinematicPresetLabel(): string {
    return this.photoSystem.currentLabel();
  }

  updateTrain(distanceM: number, doorsOpen = false, deltaSeconds = 1 / 60, nextStation = ''): void {
    const t = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(distanceM / this.route.routeLengthM, 0, 1);
    const underground = this.stationIsUnderground(t);
    const tangent = this.curve.getTangentAt(t).normalize();
    const normal = this.normalAt(t);
    const position = this.curve.getPointAt(t).add(normal.clone().multiplyScalar(this.activeLaneOffsetM));

    this.environment.updateStreaming(this.geoPointAt(t));
    this.environment.setUnderground(underground);
    this.activeVehicle.updateDoors(doorsOpen, deltaSeconds);
    this.activeVehicle.updateInteriorDisplay(nextStation, doorsOpen);
    this.activeVehicle.place(distanceM);
    this.opposingLayer?.update(distanceM);
    this.cameraSystem.follow({
      position,
      tangent,
      underground,
      interiorCameraProfile: this.modeDefinition?.interiorCameraProfile ?? 'rail',
      passengerOffsetM: this.modeDefinition?.passengerOffsetM ?? 0,
      trainHalfLengthM: this.activeVehicle.trainHalfLengthM,
      train: this.activeVehicle.root,
      trainCars: this.activeVehicle.cars
    });
    if (this.photoSystem.isActive()) {
      this.photoSystem.updateCamera({
        camera: this.camera,
        distanceM,
        routeLengthM: this.route.routeLengthM,
        trainPosition: position,
        tangent,
        trainHalfLengthM: this.activeVehicle.trainHalfLengthM,
        activeLaneOffsetM: this.activeLaneOffsetM,
        normalAt: (t) => this.normalAt(t),
        nearestStations: (d) => this.nearestStations(d),
        routePointAtDistance: (d, offset) => this.routePointAtDistance(d, offset),
        routeTangentAtDistance: (d) => this.routeTangentAtDistance(d)
      });
    }
  }

  render(): void {
    this.renderer.render(this.scene, this.camera);
  }

  dispose(): void {
    window.removeEventListener('resize', this.onRendererResizeBound);
    this.cameraSystem.dispose();
    this.lightingSystem.dispose();
    this.photoSystem.dispose();
    this.environment.dispose();
    this.activeVehicle.dispose();
    // Free geometries/materials/textures built by the scene layers so switching
    // routes does not leak GPU resources.
    this.disposeSceneGraph();
    this.opposingLayer = null;
    this.renderer.domElement.remove();
    this.renderer.dispose();
  }

  /** Recursively disposes geometries and materials of every mesh in the scene. */
  private disposeSceneGraph(): void {
    this.scene.traverse((object) => {
      const mesh = object as Partial<THREE.Mesh> & { material?: THREE.Material | THREE.Material[] };
      mesh.geometry?.dispose?.();
      const material = mesh.material;
      if (Array.isArray(material)) for (const entry of material) this.disposeMaterial(entry);
      else if (material) this.disposeMaterial(material);
    });
    this.scene.clear();
  }

  private disposeMaterial(material: THREE.Material): void {
    for (const value of Object.values(material)) {
      if (value instanceof THREE.Texture) value.dispose();
    }
    material.dispose();
  }

  private get guidewayKind(): GuidewayKind {
    return this.modeDefinition?.guidewayKind ?? 'stadtbahn';
  }

  private get activeLaneOffsetM(): number {
    if (this.guidewayKind === 'bus') return -1.65;
    if (this.guidewayKind === 'schwebebahn') return -1.45;
    if (this.guidewayKind === 'zacke' || this.guidewayKind === 'seilbahn' || this.guidewayKind === 'ferry') return 0;
    return this.guidewayKind === 's-bahn' ? -2.35 : ACTIVE_TRACK_OFFSET_M;
  }

  private get oppositeLaneOffsetM(): number {
    if (this.guidewayKind === 'bus') return 1.65;
    if (this.guidewayKind === 'schwebebahn') return 1.45;
    if (this.guidewayKind === 'zacke' || this.guidewayKind === 'seilbahn' || this.guidewayKind === 'ferry') return 0;
    return this.guidewayKind === 's-bahn' ? 2.35 : OPPOSITE_TRACK_OFFSET_M;
  }

  private get railGaugeM(): number {
    return this.guidewayKind === 's-bahn' ? 1.435 : TRACK_GAUGE_M;
  }

  /** True when the route is in tunnel/underground at normalized progress t. */
  private stationIsUnderground(t: number): boolean {
    if (this.guidewayKind === 'bus' || this.guidewayKind === 'ferry') return false;
    if (this.route.tunnelRanges?.length) {
      const distanceM = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(t, 0, 1) * this.route.routeLengthM;
      return this.route.tunnelRanges.some((range) => distanceM >= range.startM && distanceM <= range.endM);
    }
    const mask = this.route.tunnelMask;
    if (!mask?.length) return false;
    const { index, fraction } = this.geoSegmentAt(t);
    return Boolean(mask[Math.min(mask.length - 1, Math.round(index + fraction))]);
  }

  /** Bundles shared route geometry/sampling helpers for the scene layers. */
  private createLayerContext(): WorldLayerContext {
    return {
      curve: this.curve,
      route: this.route,
      guidewayKind: this.guidewayKind,
      stationStyleId: this.modeDefinition?.stationStyleId,
      activeLaneOffsetM: this.activeLaneOffsetM,
      oppositeLaneOffsetM: this.oppositeLaneOffsetM,
      railGaugeM: this.railGaugeM,
      stationMarkers: this.stationMarkers,
      stationIsUnderground: (t) => this.stationIsUnderground(t),
      normalAt: (t) => this.normalAt(t),
      offsetCurvePoints: (offsetM, segments, heightM) => this.offsetCurvePoints(offsetM, segments, heightM),
      surfaceHeightAt: (t) => this.surfaceHeightAt(t),
      makeRouteStrip: (offsetM, widthM, startT, endT, segments, heightM) =>
        this.makeRouteStrip(offsetM, widthM, startT, endT, segments, heightM),
      makeTrackStrip: (offsetM, widthM, segments) => this.makeTrackStrip(offsetM, widthM, segments),
      maskRanges: (mask) => this.maskRanges(mask),
      routeBounds: () => this.routeBounds(),
      routeGeoPoints: () => this.routeGeoPoints(),
      updateArticulatedVehicle: (root, cars, distanceM, direction, trackOffsetM) =>
        this.updateArticulatedVehicle(root, cars, distanceM, direction, trackOffsetM),
      wrapDistance: (distanceM) => this.wrapDistance(distanceM)
    };
  }

  private routePointAtDistance(distanceM: number, laneOffsetM = 0): THREE.Vector3 {
    const t = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(distanceM / this.route.routeLengthM, 0, 1);
    return this.curve.getPointAt(t).add(this.normalAt(t).multiplyScalar(laneOffsetM));
  }

  private routeTangentAtDistance(distanceM: number): THREE.Vector3 {
    const t = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(distanceM / this.route.routeLengthM, 0, 1);
    return this.curve.getTangentAt(t).normalize();
  }

  private nearestStations(distanceM: number): { previous: { distanceM: number } | undefined; next: { distanceM: number } | undefined } {
    let previous: { distanceM: number } | undefined;
    let next: { distanceM: number } | undefined;
    for (const station of this.route.stations) {
      if (station.distanceM <= distanceM) previous = station;
      if (station.distanceM > distanceM) {
        next = station;
        break;
      }
    }
    return { previous, next };
  }

  private nearestSelectedGeoPoint(lat: number, lon: number, points: GeoPoint[]): { distanceM: number; t: number } {
    const lonScale = Math.cos((lat * Math.PI) / 180) * EARTH_M_PER_DEG_LAT;
    let bestDistance = Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
    let bestRouteDistanceM = 0;
    for (let index = 1; index < points.length; index++) {
      const a = points[index - 1];
      const b = points[index];
      const ax = (a.lon - lon) * lonScale;
      const az = (a.lat - lat) * EARTH_M_PER_DEG_LAT;
      const bx = (b.lon - lon) * lonScale;
      const bz = (b.lat - lat) * EARTH_M_PER_DEG_LAT;
      const vx = bx - ax;
      const vz = bz - az;
      const fraction = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(-(ax * vx + az * vz) / (vx * vx + vz * vz || 1), 0, 1);
      const distance = Math.hypot(ax + vx * fraction, az + vz * fraction);
      if (distance < bestDistance) {
        bestDistance = distance;
        bestRouteDistanceM = THREE.MathUtils.lerp(
          this.routeGeoDistancesM[index - 1] ?? 0,
          this.routeGeoDistancesM[index] ?? 0,
          fraction
        );
      }
    }
    const totalDistanceM = this.routeGeoDistancesM.at(-1) ?? 0;
    return {
      distanceM: bestDistance,
      t: totalDistanceM > 0 ? bestRouteDistanceM / totalDistanceM : 0
    };
  }

  private updateArticulatedVehicle(
    root: THREE.Group,
    cars: THREE.Group[],
    distanceM: number,
    direction: 1 | -1,
    trackOffsetM: number
  ): void {
    const centerPose = this.trackPose(distanceM, direction, trackOffsetM);
    root.position.copy(centerPose.position);
    root.quaternion.copy(centerPose.quaternion);
    const inverseRoot = centerPose.quaternion.clone().invert();

    for (const car of cars) {
      const centerOffsetM = Number(car.userData.centerOffsetM ?? 0);
      const pose = this.trackPose(distanceM + centerOffsetM * direction, direction, trackOffsetM);
      car.position.copy(pose.position).sub(centerPose.position).applyQuaternion(inverseRoot);
      car.quaternion.copy(inverseRoot).multiply(pose.quaternion);
    }
  }

  private trackPose(distanceM: number, direction: 1 | -1, trackOffsetM: number): { position: THREE.Vector3; quaternion: THREE.Quaternion } {
    const clampedDistance = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(distanceM, 0, this.route.routeLengthM);
    const t = clampedDistance / this.route.routeLengthM;
    const routeTangent = this.curve.getTangentAt(t).normalize();
    const position = this.curve.getPointAt(t);
    if (distanceM < 0) position.add(routeTangent.clone().multiplyScalar(distanceM));
    if (distanceM > this.route.routeLengthM) {
      position.add(routeTangent.clone().multiplyScalar(distanceM - this.route.routeLengthM));
    }
    position
      .add(this.normalAt(t).multiplyScalar(trackOffsetM))
      .add(new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.2, 0));
    const tangent = routeTangent.multiplyScalar(direction);
    // Build a level track frame instead of using the shortest rotation from
    // forward to the tangent. The latter can introduce visible roll whenever
    // a steep route, such as the Zacke, changes both heading and elevation.
    const worldUp = new THREE.Vector3(0, 1, 0);
    const right = worldUp.clone().cross(tangent).normalize();
    const up = tangent.clone().cross(right).normalize();
    const quaternion = new THREE.Quaternion().setFromRotationMatrix(
      new THREE.Matrix4().makeBasis(right, up, tangent)
    );
    return { position, quaternion };
  }

  private wrapDistance(distanceM: number): number {
    const wrapped = distanceM % this.route.routeLengthM;
    return wrapped < 0 ? wrapped + this.route.routeLengthM : wrapped;
  }

  private addSnow(): void {
    const bounds = this.routeBounds();
    const mat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xffffff });
    for (let i = 0; i < 260; i++) {
      const snow = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.18 + Math.random() * 0.15, 8, 8), mat);
      snow.position.set(
        bounds.min.x + Math.random() * bounds.size.x,
        2 + Math.random() * 70,
        bounds.min.z + Math.random() * bounds.size.z
      );
      this.seasonalObjects.add(snow);
    }
  }

  private addLeaves(): void {
    const bounds = this.routeBounds();
    const mat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xb96b2a, side: THREE.DoubleSide });
    for (let i = 0; i < 160; i++) {
      const leaf = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(0.8, 0.28), mat);
      leaf.position.set(
        bounds.min.x + Math.random() * bounds.size.x,
        0.06,
        bounds.min.z + Math.random() * bounds.size.z
      );
      leaf.rotation.y = Math.random() * Math.PI;
      this.seasonalObjects.add(leaf);
    }
  }

  private routeBounds(): { min: THREE.Vector3; max: THREE.Vector3; center: THREE.Vector3; size: THREE.Vector3 } {
    const points = this.curve.getSpacedPoints(120);
    const box = new THREE.Box3().setFromPoints(points);
    const padding = new THREE.Vector3(320, 0, 320);
    box.min.sub(padding);
    box.max.add(padding);
    const center = new THREE.Vector3();
    const size = new THREE.Vector3();
    box.getCenter(center);
    box.getSize(size);
    return { min: box.min, max: box.max, center, size };
  }

  private normalAt(t: number): THREE.Vector3 {
    const tangent = this.curve.getTangentAt(t).normalize();
    return new THREE.Vector3(tangent.z, 0, -tangent.x).normalize();
  }

  private offsetCurvePoints(offsetM: number, segments: number, heightM: number): THREE.Vector3[] {
    return this.curve.getSpacedPoints(segments).map((p, i, arr) => {
      const t = i / Math.max(arr.length - 1, 1);
      return p.clone().add(this.normalAt(t).multiplyScalar(offsetM)).add(new THREE.Vector3(0, heightM, 0));
    });
  }

  private surfaceHeightAt(t: number): number {
    const heights = this.route.surfaceHeightsM;
    if (!heights?.length) return this.curve.getPointAt(t).y;
    const { index, fraction } = this.geoSegmentAt(t);
    const next = Math.min(index + 1, heights.length - 1);
    return THREE.MathUtils.lerp(heights[index] ?? heights.at(-1) ?? 0, heights[next] ?? heights.at(-1) ?? 0, fraction);
  }

  private makeRouteStrip(
    offsetM: number,
    widthM: number,
    startT: number,
    endT: number,
    segments: number,
    heightM: number
  ): THREE.BufferGeometry {
    const vertices: number[] = [];
    const uvs: number[] = [];
    const indices: number[] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i <= segments; i++) {
      const t = THREE.MathUtils.lerp(startT, endT, i / segments);
      const center = this.curve.getPointAt(t).add(this.normalAt(t).multiplyScalar(offsetM)).add(new THREE.Vector3(0, heightM, 0));
      const halfWidth = this.normalAt(t).multiplyScalar(widthM / 2);
      const left = center.clone().sub(halfWidth);
      const right = center.clone().add(halfWidth);
      vertices.push(left.x, left.y, left.z, right.x, right.y, right.z);
      uvs.push(0, i / segments, 1, i / segments);
      if (i < segments) {
        const base = i * 2;
        indices.push(base, base + 2, base + 1, base + 1, base + 2, base + 3);
      }
    }
    const geometry = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
    geometry.setAttribute('position', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(vertices, 3));
    geometry.setAttribute('uv', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(uvs, 2));
    geometry.setIndex(indices);
    geometry.computeVertexNormals();
    return geometry;
  }

  private makeTrackStrip(offsetM: number, widthM: number, segments: number): THREE.BufferGeometry {
    return this.makeRouteStrip(offsetM, widthM, 0, 1, segments, 0.035);
  }

  private maskRanges(mask: boolean[]): [number, number][] {
    const ranges: [number, number][] = [];
    for (let index = 0; index < mask.length;) {
      if (!mask[index]) {
        index++;
        continue;
      }
      const start = index;
      while (index + 1 < mask.length && mask[index + 1]) index++;
      ranges.push([start, index]);
      index++;
    }
    return ranges;
  }

  private routeGeoPoints(): GeoPoint[] {
    return this.routeGeo;
  }

  private geoPointAt(t: number): GeoPoint | undefined {
    const points = this.routeGeo;
    if (points.length === 0) return undefined;
    if (points.length === 1) return points[0];
    const { index, fraction } = this.geoSegmentAt(t);
    return {
      lat: THREE.MathUtils.lerp(points[index].lat, points[index + 1].lat, fraction),
      lon: THREE.MathUtils.lerp(points[index].lon, points[index + 1].lon, fraction)
    };
  }

  private geoSegmentAt(t: number): { index: number; fraction: number } {
    if (this.routeGeoDistancesM.length < 2) return { index: 0, fraction: 0 };
    const totalDistanceM = this.routeGeoDistancesM.at(-1) ?? 0;
    const targetDistanceM = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(t, 0, 1) * totalDistanceM;
    let low = 0;
    let high = this.routeGeoDistancesM.length - 1;
    while (low + 1 < high) {
      const middle = Math.floor((low + high) / 2);
      if (this.routeGeoDistancesM[middle] <= targetDistanceM) low = middle;
      else high = middle;
    }
    const segmentLengthM = this.routeGeoDistancesM[high] - this.routeGeoDistancesM[low];
    return {
      index: Math.min(low, this.routeGeo.length - 2),
      fraction: segmentLengthM > 0 ? (targetDistanceM - this.routeGeoDistancesM[low]) / segmentLengthM : 0
    };
  }

  private projectGeo(lat: number, lon: number): THREE.Vector3 {
    const origin = this.routeGeoPoints()[0];
    if (!origin) return new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, 0);
    const lonScale = Math.cos((origin.lat * Math.PI) / 180) * EARTH_M_PER_DEG_LAT;
    return new THREE.Vector3(-(lon - origin.lon) * lonScale, 0, (lat - origin.lat) * EARTH_M_PER_DEG_LAT);
  }
}

--- labels.ts ---
import * as THREE from 'three';

/** Creates a canvas-backed texture for station signs, vehicle displays, and POI labels. */
export function makeLabelTexture(
  text: string,
  options: { width?: number; height?: number; bg?: string; fg?: string } = {}
): THREE.CanvasTexture {
  // Default texture size balances text clarity with moderate GPU memory use.
  const width = options.width ?? 512;
  const height = options.height ?? 160;

  const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
  canvas.width = width;
  canvas.height = height;

  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
  if (!ctx) throw new Error('Canvas 2D context not available');

  ctx.fillStyle = options.bg ?? '#f7d900'; // SSB-like yellow fallback background.
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);

  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.16)'; // subtle lower shadow band for label depth.
  ctx.fillRect(0, height - 24, width, 24); // 24 px footer band.

  ctx.fillStyle = options.fg ?? '#111111'; // near-black fallback text color.
  ctx.font = 'bold 54px Inter, Arial, sans-serif'; // large readable sign text.
  ctx.textAlign = 'center';
  ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';

  // Slight upward offset visually centers capital-heavy station names.
  ctx.fillText(text, width / 2, height / 2 - 4);

  const texture = new THREE.CanvasTexture(canvas);

  // Treat canvas colors as sRGB so labels match CSS-like colors in Three.js.
  texture.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;

  return texture;
}
